# Bible Historical and Archaeological Evidence Index | 圣经史料与考古旁证索引

> This index records what external evidence can and cannot confirm. It supports the Bible timeline without treating archaeology as a substitute for theological interpretation.<br>
> 本索引记录“外部材料能确认什么、不能确认什么”。它服务于圣经时间线，不把考古当作神学结论的替代品。
>
> **Language status:** Navigation, usage notes, all 205 entries, and research tables are paired in English and Chinese. All remain Draft and require human review.<br>
> **语言状态：** 导航、使用说明、全部205项正文及研究表已中英配对；均为Draft，仍需人工审阅。

## How to Use This Index | 使用说明

- **Dates** are approximate dates for artifacts, events described in inscriptions, or their conventional historical placement.
  **年代**是器物、铭文所述事件或通行历史定位的约数。

- **Related Scripture** means that two sources may be read alongside each other; it does not mean that every detail agrees.
  **对应经文**表示可相互参照，不表示两份材料对每个细节都一致。

- **Evidence grades** follow the A–D scale in the [Research Master Index](./00_研究总索引与路线图.md).
  **证据等级**沿用[研究总索引](./00_研究总索引与路线图.md)中的 A–D 分级。

- To work backward from a reading week or biblical book, use the [205 Evidence Anchors—52 Weeks—66 Books Crosswalk](./旁证锚点_52周与66卷对应表.md).
  若从读经周次或书卷反查材料，请使用[205 个旁证锚点—52 周—66 卷对应表](./旁证锚点_52周与66卷对应表.md)。

- Links prioritize museums, antiquities authorities, and primary collection pages; peer-reviewed studies are added for major disputes.
  优先链接博物馆、文物主管机构和原始藏品页面；关键争议后续再补同行评议论文。

- This page was started on 2026-07-26; later additions should record their review date.
  本页初建于 2026-07-26，后续新增材料时应记录查阅日期。

- Genesis through Deuteronomy is expanded in [The Pentateuch: Historical Context and Limits of Evidence](./摩西五经_历史背景与证据边界.md).
  创世记至申命记的材料已展开为[《摩西五经：历史背景与证据边界》](./摩西五经_历史背景与证据边界.md)。

- The entry into Canaan through the formation of the monarchy is expanded in [Joshua to the Formation of the Monarchy: Archaeological Models and Limits of Evidence](./约书亚至王国形成_考古模型与证据边界.md).
  进入迦南至王国形成的材料已展开为[《约书亚至王国形成：考古模型与证据边界》](./约书亚至王国形成_考古模型与证据边界.md)。

- The divided kingdom through the Persian restoration is expanded in [Divided Kingdom to Restoration: Imperial Sources and Event-by-Event Comparison](./分裂王国至归回_帝国史料与逐事件对照.md).
  分裂王国至波斯归回的材料已展开为[《分裂王国至归回：帝国史料与逐事件对照》](./分裂王国至归回_帝国史料与逐事件对照.md)。

- The period between the Testaments through the Gospels is expanded in [Between the Testaments to the Gospels: Second Temple, Hellenistic, and Roman Worlds](./两约之间至四福音_第二圣殿与罗马背景.md).
  两约之间至四福音的材料已展开为[《两约之间至四福音：第二圣殿、希腊化与罗马世界》](./两约之间至四福音_第二圣殿与罗马背景.md)。

- The journeys, chronology, and cities of Acts and the Pauline Epistles are expanded in [Acts and the Pauline Epistles: Journeys, Chronology, and Urban Evidence](./使徒行传与保罗书信_旅程年代与城市史料对照.md).
  使徒行传与保罗书信的旅程、年代和城市材料已展开为[《使徒行传与保罗书信：旅程、年代与城市史料对照》](./使徒行传与保罗书信_旅程年代与城市史料对照.md)。

- Later New Testament material for Hebrews, the General Epistles, and Revelation is expanded in [Hebrews to Revelation: Later New Testament, General Epistles, and the Seven Churches](./希伯来书至启示录_晚期新约与七教会背景.md).
  希伯来书、普通书信与启示录的晚期新约材料已展开为[《希伯来书至启示录：晚期新约、普通书信与七教会背景》](./希伯来书至启示录_晚期新约与七教会背景.md)。

<a id="batch-1"></a>

## Batch 1: Initial Historical Anchors | 第一批历史锚点

<a id="evidence-001"></a>

### 1. Merneptah Stele: a Group Called Israel in Canaan | 梅尔内普塔碑：迦南地已有名为“以色列”的群体

- **Date:** Around 1208 BCE, during Pharaoh Merneptah's reign.
  **年代**：约主前 1208 年（法老梅尔内普塔统治时期）。
- **Pending verification / 待核实（A1）：** The cited museum text supports the fifth regnal year, but the exact conversion to 1208 BCE still needs a dated chronological source. / 所引馆藏说明支持第五年纪年，换算为主前 1208 年仍需补充可核对的年代学来源。
- **Collection / identifier:** Egyptian Museum, Cairo; JE 31408 / CG 34025.
  **馆藏／编号**：开罗埃及博物馆，JE 31408／CG 34025。
- **Material:** Egyptian victory stele naming Israel in Canaan, commonly regarded as the earliest extrabiblical occurrence of that name.
  **材料**：埃及胜利碑；铭文提到迦南地区的“以色列”，通常被视为圣经以外最早的以色列之名。
- **Related Scripture:** The transition depicted from Exodus through Judges.
  **对应经文**：出埃及记—士师记所呈现的时代过渡。
- **Supports:** By the late thirteenth century BCE, Canaan contained a population called Israel by Egyptians.
  **能支持**：到主前 13 世纪末，迦南已有一个被埃及人称作“以色列”的人口群体。
- **Does not establish:** It narrates neither Exodus, wilderness wandering, nor Joshua's conquest and cannot independently decide the Exodus date.
  **不能支持**：它没有叙述出埃及、旷野漂流或约书亚征服，也不能单独决定出埃及年代。
- **Grade:** A for Israel's name and broad region; not direct evidence for Exodus routes or conquest models.
  **等级**：A（“以色列”名称与大致地域）；对出埃及路线与征服模式不构成直接证明。
- **Sources:** [Global Egyptian Museum: JE 31408](https://www.globalegyptianmuseum.org/record.aspx?id=15349&lan=E); [BASOR: Israel in the Merneptah Stela](https://www.journals.uchicago.edu/doi/10.2307/1357179)
  **来源**：[Global Egyptian Museum：JE 31408](https://www.globalegyptianmuseum.org/record.aspx?id=15349&lan=E)；[BASOR：Israel in the Merneptah Stela](https://www.journals.uchicago.edu/doi/10.2307/1357179)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-002"></a>

### 2. Tel Dan Stele: the Dynastic Title House of David | 特尔但碑：“大卫之家”的王朝称谓

- **Date:** Usually ninth century BCE.
  **年代**：多定于主前 9 世纪。
- **Collection / identifier:** Israel Antiquities Authority collection, 1996-125 and 1993-3162; permanent display at the Israel Museum, as stated in the original text and qualified by pending item A2 below.
  **馆藏／编号**：以色列文物局藏品，1996-125、1993-3162；以色列博物馆常设展出。
- **Pending verification / 待核实（A2）：** The linked loan-exhibition page confirms inventory numbers, not current permanent-display status; confirm that status with the holding institution. / 所链借展页支持编号，不能确认当前常设展陈状态；该状态需向馆藏机构资料核对。
- **Material:** Aramaic victory-stele fragments; most researchers read the key wording as `ביתדוד` / House of David.
  **材料**：亚兰文胜利碑残片；多数研究者把关键字读作“ביתדוד / 大卫之家”。
- **Related Scripture:** 2 Samuel, the Davidic covenant, and Judah's dynasty in Kings.
  **对应经文**：撒母耳记下、大卫之约、列王纪中的犹大王朝。
- **Supports:** If the mainstream reading stands, a neighboring kingdom called Judah's dynasty House of David over a century after David: direct evidence for dynastic memory.
  **能支持**：若主流释读成立，邻国在大卫之后约一个多世纪把犹大王朝称为“大卫之家”，是王朝记忆的直接外证。
- **Does not establish:** Not every detail of David's life in Samuel, nor independently the united kingdom's territory or scale.
  **不能支持**：不能由此推出撒母耳记中大卫生平的全部细节，也不能单独判断联合王国的疆域和规模。
- **Debate:** Damage leaves battle reconstruction, persons, and some letters disputed. House of David is mainstream, but its reading should still be explained.
  **争议**：残片破损，具体战事、人物复原及个别字读法仍有讨论；“大卫之家”是主流读法，但应保留释读说明。
- **Grade:** A− for the dynastic title; C for reconstructing events from damaged lines.
  **等级**：A−（王朝称谓）；涉及残缺行文的事件重建为 C。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [BASOR: The Stela from Dan — epigraphic and historical discussion](https://www.journals.uchicago.edu/doi/10.2307/1357180)
    [BASOR：The Stela from Dan——铭文学与历史讨论](https://www.journals.uchicago.edu/doi/10.2307/1357180)
  - [Jewish Museum / Israel Museum loan exhibition: Tel Dan Stele](https://thejewishmuseum.org/exhibitions/tel-dan-stele/)
    [犹太博物馆／以色列博物馆借展说明：Tel Dan Stele](https://thejewishmuseum.org/exhibitions/tel-dan-stele/)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-003"></a>

### 3. Mesha Stele: Moab, the Omride Dynasty, and Israel | 米沙碑：摩押、暗利王朝与以色列之间的冲突

- **Date:** Ninth century BCE; Louvre catalogue around 830–805 BCE, with the inscription carved after 842 BCE.
  **年代**：主前 9 世纪；卢浮宫目录定为约主前 830–805 年，并说明铭文在主前 842 年后刻成。
- **Collection / identifier:** Louvre AO 5066; also KAI 181.
  **馆藏／编号**：卢浮宫 AO 5066；另编号 KAI 181。
- **Material:** Moabite king Mesha's Moabite-language victory stele, naming Omri, Israel, and conflict with Moab.
  **材料**：摩押王米沙的摩押文胜利碑，提及暗利、以色列及摩押与其冲突。
- **Related Scripture:** 2 Kings 3.
  **对应经文**：列王纪下 3 章。
- **Supports:** Mesha's historicity and prolonged political/military conflict between Moab and Omride Israel.
  **能支持**：米沙是历史人物；摩押与暗利王朝治下的以色列确有长期政治和军事冲突。
- **Does not establish:** The stele and Kings are war narratives from opposing standpoints. Neither side's victory propaganda is automatically a neutral battle report.
  **不能支持**：碑文与列王纪是双方立场不同的战争叙事，不能把其中一方的胜利宣传直接当作中立战报。
- **Debate:** Readings such as another House of David in damaged text remain disputed and outside this entry's core conclusion.
  **争议**：碑文残损处是否另有“大卫之家”等读法仍有争论，不纳入本条核心结论。
- **Grade:** A for Mesha, Omri, Israel, and conflict; C for damaged-letter reconstruction.
  **等级**：A（米沙、暗利、以色列及冲突）；残损字复原为 C。
- **Sources:** [Louvre collection: Mesha Stele](https://collections.louvre.fr/en/ark:/53355/cl010120339)
  **来源**：[卢浮宫藏品页：米沙碑](https://collections.louvre.fr/en/ark:/53355/cl010120339)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-004"></a>

### 4. Kurkh Monolith: Ahab and the 853 BCE Battle of Qarqar | 库尔赫碑：亚哈与主前 853 年夸夸之战

- **Date:** Stele around 852 BCE, recounting the 853 BCE campaign.
  **年代**：碑约主前 852 年，记述主前 853 年战役。
- **Collection / identifier:** British Museum BM 118884; registration 1863,0619.2.
  **馆藏／编号**：大英博物馆 BM 118884；登记号 1863,0619.2。
- **Material:** Shalmaneser III's royal inscription; the war list is commonly read as naming Ahab the Israelite.
  **材料**：亚述王撒缦以色三世的王室铭文；战争名单通常读到“以色列人亚哈”。
- **Related Scripture:** Ahab's era in 1 Kings 16–22; this battle itself is not narrated in the Bible.
  **对应经文**：列王纪上 16–22 章的亚哈时代；这场战役本身未见于圣经。
- **Supports:** Ahab and northern Israel within Assyria's western campaigns, supplying an external fixed point for royal chronology.
  **能支持**：亚哈与北国以色列处于亚述西征的真实国际格局中，也为列王年代提供外部固定点。
- **Does not establish:** Not all biblical Ahab stories. Assyrian victory claims require evaluation as royal propaganda.
  **不能支持**：不能证明亚哈的所有圣经事迹；亚述王宣称大胜也必须按王室宣传文类谨慎评估。
- **Grade:** A.
  **等级**：A。
- **Sources:** [British Museum collection: Kurkh Stela, BM 118884](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1863-0619-2)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆藏品页：Kurkh Stela，BM 118884](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1863-0619-2)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-005"></a>

### 5. Black Obelisk: Jehu's Tribute to Assyria | 黑色方尖碑：耶户向亚述进贡

- **Date:** Made around 825 BCE; Jehu's tribute is usually dated to 841 BCE.
  **年代**：方尖碑约主前 825 年制成，所记耶户进贡通常定于主前 841 年。
- **Collection / identifier:** British Museum BM 118885; registration 1848,1104.1.
  **馆藏／编号**：大英博物馆 BM 118885；登记号 1848,1104.1。
- **Material:** Shalmaneser III's black-limestone obelisk. Images/captions associate the tribute bearer with the House of Omri; the museum identifies him as Israelite king Jehu.
  **材料**：撒缦以色三世的黑色石灰岩方尖碑；图像与题记把进贡者联系到“暗利之家”，博物馆说明为以色列王耶户。
- **Related Scripture:** 2 Kings 9–10.
  **对应经文**：列王纪下 9–10 章。
- **Supports:** Jehu as an Israelite ruler in diplomatic/tributary relations with Assyria; continued Assyrian use of House of Omri for the region/kingdom.
  **能支持**：耶户是与亚述发生外交/朝贡关系的以色列君王；亚述仍以“暗利之家”称呼这一地区或王国。
- **Does not establish:** The Bible does not record this tribute, and the monument does not assess the theology of Jehu's reforms.
  **不能支持**：圣经没有记载这次进贡，方尖碑也不评价耶户宗教改革的神学意义。
- **Grade:** A.
  **等级**：A。
- **Sources:** [British Museum collection: Black Obelisk, BM 118885](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1848-1104-1)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆藏品页：Black Obelisk，BM 118885](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1848-1104-1)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-006"></a>

### 6. Lachish Reliefs and Sennacherib's Prism: Assyria Attacks Judah in 701 BCE | 拉吉浮雕与西拿基立棱柱：主前 701 年亚述进攻犹大

- **Date:** Reliefs around 700–692 BCE; Taylor Prism around 691 BCE, recounting 701 BCE.
  **年代**：拉吉浮雕约主前 700–692 年制成；泰勒棱柱约主前 691 年制成，回述主前 701 年战役。
- **Collection / identifier:** Taylor/Sennacherib Prism BM 91032, registration 1855,1003.1; British Museum Lachish relief series.
  **馆藏／编号**：泰勒／西拿基立棱柱 BM 91032，登记号 1855,1003.1；浮雕为大英博物馆拉吉系列藏品。
- **Material:** Palace reliefs from Nineveh depicting Lachish's siege and Sennacherib's six-sided royal-inscription prism.
  **材料**：尼尼微宫殿中的拉吉围城浮雕，以及西拿基立的六棱柱王室铭文。
- **Related Scripture:** 2 Kings 18–19; 2 Chronicles 32; Isaiah 36–37.
  **对应经文**：列王纪下 18–19 章；历代志下 32 章；以赛亚书 36–37 章。
- **Supports:** A shared historical core: Sennacherib attacks Judah, takes Lachish, and receives Hezekiah's tribute. The prism names Judah's king Hezekiah.
  **能支持**：西拿基立攻打犹大、攻取拉吉、向希西家收取贡物，是多种来源共享的历史核心；棱柱直接提到犹大王希西家。
- **Comparative value:** Assyrian accounts stress conquest, tribute, and confining Hezekiah in Jerusalem; the Bible stresses Jerusalem's survival and its theological explanation. Neither says Sennacherib captured Jerusalem.
  **互读价值**：亚述材料强调征服、贡物和把希西家困在耶路撒冷；圣经强调耶路撒冷最终未被攻取及其神学原因。两者视角不同，却都没有说西拿基立攻陷了耶路撒冷。
- **Does not establish:** Archaeology/Assyrian inscriptions cannot test Kings' theological explanation of withdrawal.
  **不能支持**：考古与亚述铭文不能检验列王纪对亚述退兵之神学解释。
- **Grade:** A for war, Lachish, Hezekiah, tribute; withdrawal causes need distinct historical and faith interpretation.
  **等级**：A（战争、拉吉、希西家、贡物）；具体退兵原因需分别按史料与信仰解释讨论。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [British Museum: relief of Sennacherib viewing the fall of Lachish](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1856-0909-14_7)
    [大英博物馆：西拿基立观看拉吉陷落的浮雕](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1856-0909-14_7)
  - [British Museum: Taylor / Sennacherib Prism, BM 91032](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1855-1003-1)
    [大英博物馆：泰勒／西拿基立棱柱，BM 91032](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1855-1003-1)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-007"></a>

### 7. Babylonian Chronicle: Jerusalem Falls in 597 BCE | 巴比伦编年史：主前 597 年耶路撒冷陷落

- **Date:** Tablet covers 605–594 BCE; surviving copy around 550–400 BCE.
  **年代**：泥版记载主前 605–594 年，现存抄本约制于主前 550–400 年。
- **Collection / identifier:** British Museum BM 21946; registration 1896,0409.51.
  **馆藏／编号**：大英博物馆 BM 21946；登记号 1896,0409.51。
- **Material:** Chronicle tablet BM 21946 recounting Nebuchadnezzar's first capture of Jerusalem.
  **材料**：巴比伦编年史泥版 BM 21946，记述尼布甲尼撒首次攻取耶路撒冷的战役。
- **Related Scripture:** 2 Kings 24:10–17; 2 Chronicles 36:9–10; Jeremiah/Ezekiel chronology.
  **对应经文**：列王纪下 24:10–17；历代志下 36:9–10；耶利米书与以西结书的年代背景。
- **Supports:** Nebuchadnezzar's 597 BCE capture: one of the exile chronology's strongest external anchors.
  **能支持**：主前 597 年尼布甲尼撒攻取耶路撒冷，是被掳时间线最可靠的外部锚点之一。
- **Does not establish:** Not the whole deportee experience or a replacement for independent study of the second destruction in 586 BCE.
  **不能支持**：该泥版没有涵盖被掳群体全部经历，也不能代替对主前 586 年第二次毁城的独立研究。
- **Grade:** A.
  **等级**：A。
- **Sources:** [British Museum collection: Babylonian Chronicle, BM 21946](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1896-0409-51)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆藏品页：Babylonian Chronicle，BM 21946](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1896-0409-51)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-008"></a>

### 8. Cyrus Cylinder: Imperial Background to Return | 古列圆柱：归回政策的帝国背景

- **Date:** After Babylon's capture in 539 BCE.
  **年代**：主前 539 年攻取巴比伦后。
- **Collection / identifier:** British Museum BM 90920; registration 1880,0617.1941.
  **馆藏／编号**：大英博物馆 BM 90920；登记号 1880,0617.1941。
- **Material:** Cyrus' royal inscription in Babylonian literary form, describing restored shrines and returned divine images/displaced populations.
  **材料**：以巴比伦文体写成的古列王室铭文，记述恢复部分神庙、归还神像及被迁移人口。
- **Related Scripture:** Ezra 1; 2 Chronicles 36:22–23.
  **对应经文**：以斯拉记 1 章；历代志下 36:22–23。
- **Supports:** Return of displaced communities and restoration of local religious centers fit Cyrus' Babylonian governing background.
  **能支持**：允许被迁群体归回、恢复地方宗教中心，符合古列治理巴比伦的政策背景。
- **Does not establish:** It names neither Jews, Jerusalem, nor reconstruction of Jerusalem's temple. It is not the archaeological original of Ezra's decree.
  **不能支持**：圆柱没有直接提到犹太人、耶路撒冷或重建耶路撒冷圣殿，因此不能称为“以斯拉记诏书的考古原件”。
- **Reading caution:** A royal legitimation text; calling it the first human-rights charter is modern packaging rather than the artifact's original meaning.
  **解读提醒**：它是古列合法化统治的王室宣传文本；“世界上第一份人权宪章”是现代包装，不是文物原意。
- **Grade:** B for Ezra's administrative background, not word-for-word grade-A proof of Ezra 1.
  **等级**：B（以斯拉记的政策背景），不是对以斯拉记 1 章逐字的 A 级证明。
- **Sources:** [British Museum collection: Cyrus Cylinder, BM 90920](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1880-0617-1941)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆藏品页：Cyrus Cylinder，BM 90920](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1880-0617-1941)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-009"></a>

### 9. Dead Sea Scrolls: Second Temple Copying of Scripture | 死海古卷：第二圣殿时期的经文传抄

- **Date:** The collection generally spans several centuries BCE to the first century CE; individual manuscripts need individual dates.
  **年代**：古卷群通常跨主前数世纪至主后 1 世纪；具体卷本需逐件定年。
- **Collection / identifier:** Dispersed collections; representative here: Great Isaiah Scroll 1QIsaᵃ.
  **馆藏／编号**：古卷分藏多处；本条代表性卷本为大以赛亚书卷 1QIsaᵃ。
- **Material:** Hebrew, Aramaic, and Greek manuscripts from Qumran/Judean Desert, including biblical texts, interpretation, and community writings.
  **材料**：昆兰及犹大旷野发现的大量希伯来文、亚兰文和希腊文抄本；包括圣经文本、释经和群体文献。
- **Related Scripture:** Old Testament textual traditions, particularly Isaiah.
  **对应经文**：旧约文本传统；尤其《以赛亚书》等。
- **Supports:** Many biblical books were copied/read long before medieval Masoretic manuscripts. The Great Isaiah Scroll is the earliest surviving complete single biblical-book manuscript.
  **能支持**：在比中世纪马所拉抄本早得多的时期，许多圣经书卷已被传抄和阅读；大以赛亚书卷是现存最早的完整圣经单卷抄本。
- **Does not establish:** Not the books' autographs or proof of every narrated event. Variants show Second Temple textual diversity; do not claim every letter has always been identical.
  **不能支持**：古卷不是旧约各书的原稿，也不证明卷中所述每件历史事件；古卷还显示第二圣殿时期存在文本异文，不能简化为“每个字从未有差异”。
- **Grade:** A for ancient texts' existence/transmission; internal events are another question.
  **等级**：A（古代文本的存在与传抄史）；对卷内事件真实性属于另一问题。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [Israel Antiquities Authority: Leon Levy Dead Sea Scrolls Digital Library](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/home?locale=en_US)
    [以色列文物局：Leon Levy Dead Sea Scrolls Digital Library](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/home?locale=en_US)
  - [Israel Museum: Digital Dead Sea Scrolls](https://dss.collections.imj.org.il/)
    [以色列博物馆：Digital Dead Sea Scrolls](https://dss.collections.imj.org.il/)
  - [Israel Museum: Great Isaiah Scroll exhibition](https://www.imj.org.il/en/exhibitions/voice-desert)
    [以色列博物馆：大以赛亚书卷展览说明](https://www.imj.org.il/en/exhibitions/voice-desert)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-010"></a>

### 10. Pilate Inscription: Pontius Pilate, Roman Official in Judea | 彼拉多铭文：罗马犹太行省的总督本丢·彼拉多

- **Date:** First century CE, during Pilate's administration.
  **年代**：主后 1 世纪，彼拉多任内。
- **Collection / identifier:** Pilate Stone, excavated at Caesarea; Israel Antiquities Authority collection, displayed at Israel Museum.
  **馆藏／编号**：通称 Pilate Stone；凯撒利亚出土，以色列文物局藏、以色列博物馆展出。
- **Material:** Latin inscription from Caesarea preserving Pontius Pilate's name and title information.
  **材料**：凯撒利亚发现的拉丁文铭文，保留本丢·彼拉多的名字和职衔信息。
- **Related Scripture:** The four Gospels' trial accounts; the historical placement retained in the Apostles' Creed.
  **对应经文**：四福音对耶稣受审的记载；使徒信经所保留的历史定位。
- **Supports:** Pilate as a real Roman official, placing Gospel narratives within Tiberian Judean administration.
  **能支持**：本丢·彼拉多是真实的罗马官员，并把福音书叙事放进提庇留时期的犹太行省行政背景。
- **Does not establish:** The inscription does not mention Jesus or the trial.
  **不能支持**：铭文本身不提耶稣或审判事件。
- **Grade:** A for person/office; indirect background for trial details.
  **等级**：A（人物与官职）；对耶稣受审的具体叙事为间接背景。
- **Sources:** [Israel Museum: Cradle of Christianity exhibition](https://www.imj.org.il/en/exhibitions/cradle-christianity)
  **来源**：[以色列博物馆“基督教的摇篮”展览说明](https://www.imj.org.il/en/exhibitions/cradle-christianity)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-011"></a>

### 11. Caiaphas Ossuary: a Possible Physical Witness to a High-Priestly Family | “该亚法”骨棺：大祭司家族的可能实物见证

- **Date:** First century CE.
  **年代**：主后 1 世纪。
- **Material:** Jerusalem burial ossuary bearing names associated with Joseph Caiaphas; Israel Museum connects it with the New Testament high priest.
  **材料**：耶路撒冷墓葬中发现、刻有“约瑟·该亚法”相关名字的骨棺；以色列博物馆将其与新约中的大祭司该亚法联系。
- **Related Scripture:** Matthew 26; John 11, 18; Luke 3:2; Acts 4:6.
  **对应经文**：马太福音 26 章；约翰福音 11、18 章；路加福音 3:2；使徒行传 4:6。
- **Supports:** Names, priestly-family context, and elite Jerusalem burial customs compatible with the New Testament setting.
  **能支持**：名字、祭司家族和当时耶路撒冷精英的葬俗与新约背景相符。
- **Does not establish:** Namesake/family identification requires inscription, tomb assemblage, and historical inference. The ossuary does not record Jesus' trial.
  **不能支持**：同名与家族认定仍需借助铭文、墓葬组合及历史推断；骨棺不记载耶稣受审。
- **Grade:** B+ overall; A for inscribed names/burial customs, with weaker certainty for identifying the Gospel's particular Caiaphas.
  **等级**：B+；若只就铭文中的姓名和葬俗则为 A，对“就是福音书那位该亚法”的身份认定稍弱。
- **Sources:** [Israel Museum: Ossuary of Caiaphas](https://www.imj.org.il/en/exhibitions/ossuary-caiaphas-high-priest-18-36-ce); [Cradle of Christianity exhibition](https://www.imj.org.il/en/exhibitions/cradle-christianity)
  **来源**：[以色列博物馆：Ossuary of Caiaphas](https://www.imj.org.il/en/exhibitions/ossuary-caiaphas-high-priest-18-36-ce)；[“基督教的摇篮”展览说明](https://www.imj.org.il/en/exhibitions/cradle-christianity)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="batch-2"></a>

## Batch 2: Canaan to the Formation of the Monarchy | 第二批扩展锚点：进入迦南至王国形成

> 本节只保留速查结论；遗址分期、解释模型、经文注释和完整来源见[专题正文](./约书亚至王国形成_考古模型与证据边界.md)。

<a id="evidence-012"></a>

### 12. Jericho / Tell es-Sultan: Dating the Famous Burning | 耶利哥／Tell es-Sultan：著名火毁的年代难题

- **Date:** The major fortified-city destruction belongs to the Middle Bronze end; radiocarbon places it in the late seventeenth or sixteenth century BCE.
  **年代**：最显著的设防城火毁属于中青铜时代末，碳十四研究置于主前 17 世纪末或 16 世纪。
- **Related Scripture:** Joshua 2, 6.
  **对应经文**：约书亚记 2、6 章。
- **Supports:** Ancient Jericho's identity, long occupation, major Middle Bronze defenses, and intense burning.
  **能支持**：古耶利哥身份、长期居住史、中青铜时代大型城防及猛烈火毁。
- **Does not establish:** No equation with Joshua's campaign without chronological comparison.
  **不能支持**：不能不经年代比较就把该毁坏层等同约书亚的战役。
- **Debate:** Scale of Late Bronze activity/defenses and its relationship with early/late Canaan-entry models.
  **争议**：晚青铜时代活动的规模、城防状态和与早／晚进入迦南年代的关系。
- **Grade:** A for site/Middle Bronze destruction; C for direct correspondence with Joshua 6.
  **等级**：A（遗址和中青铜毁坏）；与书 6 的直接对应为 C。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [UNESCO：Ancient Jericho / Tell es-Sultan](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1687/)
  - [Radiocarbon: dating Jericho’s end-Middle Bronze destruction](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/radiocarbon/article/tell-essultan-jericho-radiocarbon-results-of-shortlived-cereal-and-multiyear-charcoal-samples-from-the-end-of-the-middle-bronze-age/49BF732173E7890A2B0EC1B21CB6A817)
    [Radiocarbon：耶利哥中青铜时代末毁坏年代](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/radiocarbon/article/tell-essultan-jericho-radiocarbon-results-of-shortlived-cereal-and-multiyear-charcoal-samples-from-the-end-of-the-middle-bronze-age/49BF732173E7890A2B0EC1B21CB6A817)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-013"></a>

### 13. Ai / et-Tell: a Long Occupation Gap | 艾城／et-Tell：长期居住中断

- **Date:** Major Early Bronze fortified city, then a long interruption; a smaller village in Iron I.
  **年代**：早青铜时代有大型设防城；此后长期中断；铁器时代 I 出现较小村落。
- **Related Scripture:** Joshua 7–8.
  **对应经文**：约书亚记 7–8 章。
- **Supports:** Major occupation sequence, Early Bronze destruction, and Iron I resettlement at et-Tell.
  **能支持**：et-Tell 的主要居住序列、早青铜毁坏和铁器 I 重居。
- **Does not establish:** If et-Tell is biblical Ai, usual phasing lacks a Late Bronze fortified city at common conquest dates.
  **不能支持**：若 et-Tell 就是经文艾城，现有通行分期没有常见征服年代所期待的晚青铜设防城。
- **Debate:** Possible misidentification of et-Tell; alternatives including Khirbet el-Maqatir/Khirbet Nisya lack broad consensus.
  **争议**：et-Tell 是否认定错误；Khirbet el-Maqatir、Khirbet Nisya 等替代地点尚无广泛共识。
- **Grade:** A for main strata; B/C for biblical-site identification and alternatives.
  **等级**：A（主要地层）；遗址等同经文艾城及替代地点均为 B/C。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [Biblical Archaeologist：Excavating Ai (Et-Tell)](https://www.journals.uchicago.edu/doi/10.2307/3209413)
  - [University of Pennsylvania: et-Tell radiocarbon samples and main periods](https://resolve.cambridge.org/core/services/aop-cambridge-core/content/view/8AB039BCBE1F826FEE240542C2F562F5/S0033822200003532a.pdf/university-of-pennsylvania-radiocarbon-dates-xix.pdf)
    [宾夕法尼亚大学：et-Tell 碳十四样本与主要时期](https://resolve.cambridge.org/core/services/aop-cambridge-core/content/view/8AB039BCBE1F826FEE240542C2F562F5/S0033822200003532a.pdf/university-of-pennsylvania-radiocarbon-dates-xix.pdf)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-014"></a>

### 14. Hazor: Major Burning at the Late Bronze End | 夏琐／Tel Hazor：晚青铜时代末的大火

- **Date:** Late Bronze end, around the thirteenth–twelfth-century BCE transition.
  **年代**：晚青铜时代末，约主前 13–12 世纪转折附近。
- **Related Scripture:** Joshua 11; Judges 4–5; 1 Kings 9:15.
  **对应经文**：约书亚记 11 章；士师记 4–5 章；列王纪上 9:15。
- **Supports:** A major regional city and intense Late Bronze-end burning.
  **能支持**：夏琐是区域大型城市，并在晚青铜时代末遭受剧烈火毁。
- **Does not establish:** Burnt soil alone cannot identify Joshua's army, internal rebels, or other forces as destroyers.
  **不能支持**：烧土本身不能告诉我们毁城者是约书亚军、内部反叛者或其他势力。
- **Grade:** A for city/destruction; C for identifying Joshua 11's particular event.
  **等级**：A（城市与毁坏）；与书 11 的具体事件认定为 C。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [Hebrew University: Tel Hazor](https://archaeology.huji.ac.il/tel-hazor)
    [希伯来大学：Tel Hazor](https://archaeology.huji.ac.il/tel-hazor)
  - [Hebrew University: Fire at the Gate of Hazor](https://cris.huji.ac.il/en/publications/fire-at-the-gate-of-hazor-a-micro-geoarchaeological-study-of-the-)
    [希伯来大学：Fire at the Gate of Hazor](https://cris.huji.ac.il/en/publications/fire-at-the-gate-of-hazor-a-micro-geoarchaeological-study-of-the-)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-015"></a>

### 15. Iron I Central Highland Villages: Social Setting for Early Israel's Formation | 铁器时代 I 中央山地村落：早期以色列形成的社会基地

- **Date:** Around 1200–1000 BCE.
  **年代**：约主前 1200–1000 年。
- **Material:** Over 250 small farming/herding villages recorded through intensive surveys/excavation.
  **材料**：密集地表调查与发掘所见 250 多个小型农牧村落。
- **Related Scripture:** Later Joshua; Judges; Ruth; early 1 Samuel.
  **对应经文**：约书亚记后半、士师记、路得记、撒母耳记上早期。
- **Supports:** Major central-highland population/settlement changes after the Late Bronze city-state system.
  **能支持**：晚青铜城邦体系之后，中央山地人口与定居方式发生显著改变。
- **Does not establish:** Houses, pottery, or pig bones alone cannot identify each inhabitant's ethnicity or belief.
  **不能支持**：房屋、陶器或猪骨不能单独认定每名居民的族群和信仰。
- **Grade:** A for settlement change; B/C for equating all villagers with twelve tribes.
  **等级**：A（聚落变化）；把全部村民等同十二支派为 B/C。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [Cambridge University Press: The New Society (c. 1150–1050)](https://www.cambridge.org/core/books/abs/israels-history-and-the-history-of-israel/new-society-c-11501050/46B4CA61957F15CB994630FBBC42D13E)
    [剑桥大学出版社：The New Society (c. 1150–1050)](https://www.cambridge.org/core/books/abs/israels-history-and-the-history-of-israel/new-society-c-11501050/46B4CA61957F15CB994630FBBC42D13E)
  - [Near Eastern Archaeology：A Landscape Comes to Life](https://www.journals.uchicago.edu/doi/10.2307/3210703)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-016"></a>

### 16. Medinet Habu and Ashkelon Ancient DNA: Philistine Formation | 麦地那哈布与亚实基伦古 DNA：非利士形成

- **Date:** Twelfth century BCE and subsequent early Iron Age.
  **年代**：主前 12 世纪及其后的铁器时代初期。
- **Material:** Ramesses III temple images/inscriptions of Peleset/Sea Peoples; Bronze–Iron Ashkelon human genomes.
  **材料**：拉美西斯三世神庙的 Peleset／“海上民族”图像与铭文；亚实基伦青铜—铁器时代人类遗骸基因组。
- **Related Scripture:** Judges 13–16; 1 Samuel.
  **对应经文**：士师记 13–16 章；撒母耳记上。
- **Supports:** Eastern Mediterranean connections in Philistine cultural formation. Early Iron Ashkelon samples show temporary European-related genetic input, supporting at least some actual migration.
  **能支持**：非利士文化形成包含东地中海联系；亚实基伦早期铁器时代样本显示短暂欧洲相关遗传输入，因此至少有部分真实人口迁移。
- **Does not establish:** Not all Philistines as a pure European population from one place, nor Samson/Goliath as individuals.
  **不能支持**：不能把所有非利士人描述成来自同一地点的“纯欧洲族群”，也不能证明参孙、歌利亚等个人。
- **Grade:** A/B for texts, samples, migration; C for extension to all Philistines.
  **等级**：A/B（文本、样本与迁移）；外推全部非利士人口为 C。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [University of Chicago: published Medinet Habu reliefs and inscriptions](https://oi.uchicago.edu/sites/default/files/uploads/shared/docs/saoc12.pdf)
    [芝加哥大学：麦地那哈布浮雕与铭文正式出版](https://oi.uchicago.edu/sites/default/files/uploads/shared/docs/saoc12.pdf)
  - [Science Advances / PMC: ancient DNA from Ashkelon](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC6609216/)
    [Science Advances／PMC：亚实基伦古 DNA](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC6609216/)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-017"></a>

### 17. Khirbet Qeiyafa: a Fortified Center Around 1000 BCE | 基伯特·凯雅法：约主前 1000 年的设防中心

- **Date:** Late eleventh–early tenth century BCE.
  **年代**：约主前 11 世纪末至 10 世纪初。
- **Material:** Planned walls, wall-adjacent houses, two gates, inscriptions, and radiocarbon sequences from charred olive pits.
  **材料**：规划式城墙、邻墙房屋、两座城门、铭文与烧焦橄榄核碳十四序列。
- **Related Scripture:** Saul/David's era; the Elah Valley setting of 1 Samuel 17.
  **对应经文**：扫罗、大卫时代；撒母耳记上 17 章的以拉谷地理背景。
- **Supports:** A fortified Shephelah center capable of organizing major works around 1000 BCE.
  **能支持**：约主前 1000 年，犹大低地已有能够组织大型工程的设防中心。
- **Does not establish:** Date/geography alone cannot establish direct Davidic rule or undisputed identification with Shaaraim.
  **不能支持**：不能仅凭年代和地理认定为大卫直接管辖，或无争议地等同沙拉音。
- **Grade:** A for fortified city/date; B/C for allegiance/biblical place-name.
  **等级**：A（设防城和年代）；政治归属与经文地名为 B/C。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [Hebrew University: second radiocarbon study](https://cris.huji.ac.il/en/publications/king-davids-city-at-khirbet-qeiyafa-results-of-the-second-radioca/)
    [希伯来大学：第二轮碳十四研究](https://cris.huji.ac.il/en/publications/king-davids-city-at-khirbet-qeiyafa-results-of-the-second-radioca/)
  - [Radiocarbon: Qeiyafa and Judah state formation](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/radiocarbon/article/state-formation-in-judah-biblical-tradition-modern-historical-theories-and-radiometric-dates-at-khirbet-qeiyafa/0F8B4D6E883C99098BF8070BE472719D)
    [Radiocarbon：凯雅法与犹大国家形成](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/radiocarbon/article/state-formation-in-judah-biblical-tradition-modern-historical-theories-and-radiometric-dates-at-khirbet-qeiyafa/0F8B4D6E883C99098BF8070BE472719D)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-018"></a>

### 18. Sheshonq I's Karnak Place List: an Early Divided-Kingdom Anchor | 舍顺克一世卡纳克地名表：王国分裂初期的外部锚点

- **Date:** Around 925 BCE.
  **年代**：约主前 925 年。
- **Material:** Egyptian pharaoh Sheshonq I's Canaan-campaign relief/place list, usually identified with biblical Shishak.
  **材料**：埃及法老舍顺克一世的迦南战役浮雕与地名表；通常与圣经“示撒”认同。
- **Related Scripture:** 1 Kings 14:25–26; 2 Chronicles 12.
  **对应经文**：列王纪上 14:25–26；历代志下 12 章。
- **Supports:** A late-tenth-century Egyptian campaign in the southern Levant; pharaoh's name/date compatible with Shishak.
  **能支持**：10 世纪末埃及确在南黎凡特发动战役，法老姓名和年代与示撒相合。
- **Does not establish:** No undisputed Jerusalem in the extant list; a place-name does not mean every city was burned.
  **不能支持**：现存表中没有无争议的“耶路撒冷”；地名也不等于每一城均被焚毁。
- **Grade:** A for pharaoh/regional campaign; B for Jerusalem details.
  **等级**：A（法老和区域战役）；与耶路撒冷细节对应为 B。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [British Museum: Sheshonq I](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/term/BIOG55871)
    [大英博物馆：Sheshonq I](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/term/BIOG55871)
  - [PLOS ONE: Gezer chronology and the Sheshonq campaign](https://journals.plos.org/plosone/article?id=10.1371%2Fjournal.pone.0293119)
    [PLOS ONE：基色年代与舍顺克战役讨论](https://journals.plos.org/plosone/article?id=10.1371%2Fjournal.pone.0293119)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="batch-3"></a>

## Batch 3: Divided Kingdom to the Persian Restoration | 第三批扩展锚点：分裂王国至波斯归回

> 本节新增速查材料，不重复前文已有的米沙碑、库尔赫碑、黑色方尖碑、主前 701 年战役、597 年编年史和古列圆柱。完整事件链、先知书位置、经文注释及年代争议见[专题正文](./分裂王国至归回_帝国史料与逐事件对照.md)。

<a id="evidence-019"></a>

### 19. Tiglath-pileser III: Menahem's Tribute to Assyria | 提革拉毗列色三世铭文：米拿现向亚述进贡

- **Date:** Around 738 BCE.
  **年代**：约主前 738 年。
- **Material:** Assyrian royal inscriptions list Menahem of Samaria among western tribute payers.
  **材料**：亚述王室铭文在西方贡赋名单中列出“撒马利亚的米拿现”。
- **Related Scripture:** 2 Kings 15:17–22.
  **对应经文**：列王纪下 15:17–22。
- **Supports:** Menahem as northern king, paying tribute for Assyrian recognition and a secure throne.
  **能支持**：米拿现是北国君王；他以贡赋换取亚述承认和王位稳固。
- **Does not establish:** No record of how he extracted silver from Israel's wealthy households or assessment of the social cost.
  **不能支持**：铭文没有记录米拿现怎样向以色列富户征收银子，也不评价这项政策的社会代价。
- **Grade:** A.
  **等级**：A。
- **Sources:** [ORACC / RINAP 1: Tiglath-pileser III 32, Menahem of Samaria](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/rinap/Q003445); [RINAP 1: Tiglath-pileser III 35, the same tribute list](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/rinap/rinap1/Q003448)
  **来源**：[ORACC／RINAP 1：Tiglath-pileser III 32，列出 Menahem of Samaria](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/rinap/Q003445)；[RINAP 1：Tiglath-pileser III 35，同一贡赋名单](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/rinap/rinap1/Q003448)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-020"></a>

### 20. Tiglath-pileser III: Pekah Killed, Hoshea Installed | 提革拉毗列色三世铭文：比加被杀、何细亚被立

- **Date:** Around 732 BCE.
  **年代**：主前 732 年前后。
- **Material:** Fragmentary Assyrian inscriptions record annexation of northern territory, Pekah's death, and the Assyrian king's installation of Hoshea and receipt of tribute.
  **材料**：残存亚述铭文记北国领土遭并吞、比加被杀，并称亚述王立何细亚为王、收取贡物。
- **Related Scripture:** 2 Kings 15:29–30; 16; Isaiah 7–8.
  **对应经文**：列王纪下 15:29–30；16 章；以赛亚书 7–8 章。
- **Supports:** Direct Assyrian intervention in northern cities, population, and rule during the Syro-Ephraimite crisis; external attestation of Pekah/Hoshea.
  **能支持**：亚兰—以法莲危机期间，北国城市、人口和政权确受亚述直接干预；比加与何细亚均有外证。
- **Does not establish:** Scripture credits Hoshea with the coup; the Assyrian king claims its result. These may describe internal/external layers, but damaged inscriptions do not supply the whole mechanism.
  **不能支持**：经文把政变归给何细亚，亚述王把结果归功自己；两者可能是政变内外层面，不能假装残缺铭文已交代全部执行过程。
- **Grade:** A for people, regime change, annexation; B/C for the coup mechanism.
  **等级**：A（人物、政权更替与领土并吞）；具体政变机制为 B/C。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [ORACC / RINAP 1: Tiglath-pileser III 42, Pekah and Hoshea](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/rinap/rinap1/Q003455)
    [ORACC／RINAP 1：Tiglath-pileser III 42，比加与何细亚](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/rinap/rinap1/Q003455)
  - [ORACC / RINAP 1: Tiglath-pileser III 44, the overthrow of Pekah](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/rinap/rinap1/Q003457.10)
    [ORACC／RINAP 1：Tiglath-pileser III 44，北国推翻比加](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/rinap/rinap1/Q003457.10)
  - [University of Pennsylvania: Israel, the House of Omri](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/saao/aebp/essentials/countries/israel/)
    [宾夕法尼亚大学：Israel, the House of Omri](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/saao/aebp/essentials/countries/israel/)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-021"></a>

### 21. Sargon II: Samaria's Fall and Deportation | 撒珥根二世铭文：撒马利亚陷落与人口迁徙

- **Date:** 722/720 BCE.
  **年代**：主前 722/720 年。
- **Material:** Sargon's inscriptions claim Samaria's capture, about 27,290 deportees, recruitment of 50 chariots, a governor, and restored tribute.
  **材料**：撒珥根二世铭文声称攻取撒马利亚、迁走约 27,290 名居民、征编 50 辆战车、设省长并恢复贡赋。
- **Related Scripture:** 2 Kings 17:1–24; 18:9–12.
  **对应经文**：列王纪下 17:1–24；18:9–12。
- **Supports:** Assyrian annexation of the north, partial deportation, absorption of military capacity, and provincialization.
  **能支持**：北国被亚述吞并、部分人口被迁、军力被吸收、领土行省化。
- **Does not establish:** Sources assign actions to Sargon's accession year or renewed suppression in 720 BCE; Kings names Shalmaneser as starting a three-year siege. Do not compress all accounts into one instant.
  **不能支持**：不同文本把行动归入撒珥根登基年或主前 720 年再镇压；王下又称撒缦以色发动三年围城，所以不能把所有材料压成单一瞬间。
- **Reading caution:** 722 BCE is a useful shorthand for the northern fall; detailed chronology should use 722/720.
  **解读提醒**：“主前 722 年北国灭亡”可作速记；精细年表写“722/720”。
- **Grade:** A for conquest, deportation, provincialization; B for who completed the final capture.
  **等级**：A（征服、迁徙与行省化）；最后攻城完成者为 B。
- **Sources:** [ORACC / RINAP 2: Sargon II takes Samaria and deports 27,290](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/rinap/rinap2/Q006488.194.7); [University of Pennsylvania: Israel, the House of Omri](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/saao/aebp/essentials/countries/israel/)
  **来源**：[ORACC／RINAP 2：撒珥根二世征服撒马利亚并迁走 27,290 人](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/rinap/rinap2/Q006488.194.7)；[宾夕法尼亚大学：Israel, the House of Omri](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/saao/aebp/essentials/countries/israel/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-022"></a>

### 22. Siloam Tunnel and Inscription: Engineering Behind Hezekiah's Siege Preparations | 西罗亚水道与铭文：希西家围城预备的工程背景

- **Date:** Iron II, commonly associated with the late eighth century BCE.
  **年代**：铁器时代 II 期；常与主前 8 世纪末相连。
- **Material:** Water tunnel from Gihon toward the city; ancient Hebrew inscription describing two teams meeting while cutting through.
  **材料**：从基训泉向城内引水的隧道，以及描述两组工人相向凿通的古希伯来文铭文。
- **Related Scripture:** 2 Kings 20:20; 2 Chronicles 32:2–4, 30.
  **对应经文**：列王纪下 20:20；历代志下 32:2–4、30。
- **Supports:** Large-scale water engineering, organization, and technical writing in Jerusalem amid Assyrian threat; radiometric work supports an Iron Age date.
  **能支持**：耶路撒冷在亚述威胁时期拥有大型引水工程、组织能力和工程书写传统；放射性研究支持铁器时代年代。
- **Does not establish:** The inscription does not name Hezekiah. Construction phases and scriptural details need study; the engineering does not establish why Assyria withdrew.
  **不能支持**：铭文本身没有希西家姓名；水道施工各阶段和经文细节仍需研究，工程不能证明亚述退兵原因。
- **Grade:** A for engineering/Iron Age date; B+ for Hezekiah attribution.
  **等级**：A（工程与铁器时代年代）；归给希西家为 B+。
- **Sources:** [Nature: radiometric dating of the Siloam Tunnel](https://www.nature.com/articles/nature01875)
  **来源**：[Nature：西罗亚水道放射性年代](https://www.nature.com/articles/nature01875)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-023"></a>

### 23. Assyrian Vassal List: Manasseh, King of Judah | 亚述附庸名单：“犹大王玛拿西”

- **Date:** Mid-seventh century BCE, under Ashurbanipal.
  **年代**：主前 7 世纪中叶，亚述巴尼拔时期。
- **Material:** Royal inscription listing Manasseh king of Judah among 22 western vassal kings.
  **材料**：亚述王室铭文把“犹大王玛拿西”列入西方 22 位附庸王。
- **Related Scripture:** 2 Kings 21; 2 Chronicles 33.
  **对应经文**：列王纪下 21 章；历代志下 33 章。
- **Supports:** Manasseh's historicity and Judah's place in Assyrian tribute/vassal structures.
  **能支持**：玛拿西是历史人物，犹大处在亚述贡赋与附庸体系中。
- **Does not establish:** This inscription does not recount his removal to Babylon, prayer, return, or reforms.
  **不能支持**：现有该条铭文没有记录他被带到巴比伦、祷告、归回和改革。
- **Grade:** A for person, royal title, vassal status.
  **等级**：A（人物、王衔、附庸地位）。
- **Sources:** [ORACC / RINAP: Manasseh in Ashurbanipal's inscription](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/rinap/rinap5/Q003705.538.3)
  **来源**：[ORACC／RINAP：亚述巴尼拔铭文中的玛拿西](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/rinap/rinap5/Q003705.538.3)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-024"></a>

### 24. Fall of Nineveh Chronicle: the Assyrian Capital Falls in 612 BCE | 尼尼微陷落编年史：亚述首都于主前 612 年陷落

- **Date:** Tablet covers 616–609 BCE; surviving copy around 550–400 BCE.
  **年代**：泥版覆盖主前 616–609 年；现存抄本约主前 550–400 年制作。
- **Material:** Babylonian Chronicle BM 21901 recording Nineveh's fall.
  **材料**：巴比伦编年史 BM 21901，记述尼尼微陷落。
- **Related Scripture:** Nahum; Zephaniah 2:13–15; late Kings international setting.
  **对应经文**：那鸿书；西番雅书 2:13–15；列王纪下末期的国际背景。
- **Supports:** The Babylonian–Median alliance overcomes Assyria's capital; Nineveh was not impregnable.
  **能支持**：巴比伦—玛代联盟终结亚述首都，尼尼微并非永不可破。
- **Does not establish:** No proof of Nahum's revelatory source or substitute for reading poetic symbolism/theology.
  **不能支持**：编年史不能证明那鸿神谕的启示来源，也不能代替对诗歌象征和神学的阅读。
- **Grade:** A.
  **等级**：A。
- **Sources:** [British Museum: BM 21901](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1896-0409-6)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆：BM 21901](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1896-0409-6)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-025"></a>

### 25. Lachish Letters: Military Correspondence in Judah's Final Years | 拉吉书信：犹大末期军事通信

- **Date:** Late seventh–early sixth century BCE, usually before Babylon's attack.
  **年代**：主前 7 世纪末至 6 世纪初，通常置于巴比伦进攻前夕。
- **Material:** Ancient Hebrew ink-on-pottery military/administrative letters concerning officials, orders, prophets, and signal fires.
  **材料**：以古希伯来文墨写在陶片上的军事和行政书信，涉及官员、命令、先知与信号火。
- **Related Scripture:** Jeremiah 34:7; the siege world of chapters 37–39.
  **对应经文**：耶利米书 34:7；37–39 章的围城世界。
- **Supports:** A late Judah local military communications network; Lachish and Azekah under crisis.
  **能支持**：犹大末期存在地方军事通信网络；拉吉和亚西加处在危机中。
- **Does not establish:** Failure to see Azekah's signals in one letter alone does not establish that city's fall; namesakes are not automatically Jeremiah's people.
  **不能支持**：某封信看不见亚西加信号，不足以单独证明该城当时已陷落；同名者也不能自动等同耶利米人物。
- **Grade:** A for administration/crisis setting; B for precise alignment with Jeremiah 34.
  **等级**：A（行政和危机背景）；与耶 34 的具体时刻对应为 B。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [British Museum: Lachish Letter II](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1959-0711-2)
    [大英博物馆：拉吉书信 II](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1959-0711-2)
  - [British Museum: Lachish letters bibliography](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/term/BIB996)
    [大英博物馆：拉吉书信专题书目](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/term/BIB996)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-026"></a>

### 26. Arad Ostraca: Military-Administrative Writing Beyond Judah's Capital | 亚拉得陶片：末期犹大的军政识字网络

- **Date:** Around 600 BCE.
  **年代**：约主前 600 年。
- **Material:** Numerous Hebrew ink ostraca from Arad fortress recording rations, troops, orders, names, and links among forts.
  **材料**：亚拉得要塞出土的大量希伯来文墨书陶片，记录配给、军队、命令、人名和堡垒联系。
- **Related Scripture:** The writing society after Josiah, around Jeremiah/Baruch.
  **对应经文**：约西亚以后、耶利米与巴录的书写社会背景。
- **Supports:** Practical writing in military administration beyond the capital; multiple writers in the studied samples.
  **能支持**：实用书写进入首都之外的军事行政；一批样本显示多名不同书写者。
- **Does not establish:** Eighteen ostraca cannot calculate national literacy directly or establish a biblical book's completion then.
  **不能支持**：不能由 18 件陶片直接计算全国识字率，也不能证明某卷圣经在此时完成。
- **Grade:** A for documents/multiple writers; C for national-literacy extrapolation.
  **等级**：A（文书与多名书写者）；全国识字率外推为 C。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [PLOS ONE: Arad writers study](https://journals.plos.org/plosone/article?id=10.1371/journal.pone.0237962)
    [PLOS ONE：亚拉得书写者研究](https://journals.plos.org/plosone/article?id=10.1371/journal.pone.0237962)
  - [PLOS ONE: multispectral imaging reveals unseen text](https://journals.plos.org/plosone/article?id=10.1371/journal.pone.0178400)
    [PLOS ONE：多光谱成像发现未见文字](https://journals.plos.org/plosone/article?id=10.1371/journal.pone.0178400)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-027"></a>

### 27. Jerusalem Burn Layers: Urban Catastrophe in 587/586 BCE | 耶路撒冷大火毁坏层：主前 587/586 年城市灾变

- **Date:** Late Iron Age, traditionally 586 BCE, also commonly converted to 587.
  **年代**：铁器时代末，传统常记主前 586 年；绝对纪年也常写 587 年。
- **Material:** Burning, collapse, pottery, seals, and archaeomagnetic evidence in major elite/public buildings.
  **材料**：耶路撒冷大型精英／公共建筑的焚烧、坍塌、陶器、印记与古地磁资料。
- **Related Scripture:** 2 Kings 25:8–10; Jeremiah 39, 52; Lamentations.
  **对应经文**：列王纪下 25:8–10；耶利米书 39、52 章；耶利米哀歌。
- **Supports:** Large, intense late-kingdom destruction in Jerusalem connected with Babylon's attack.
  **能支持**：耶路撒冷在犹大王国末期发生大规模、猛烈的火毁，与巴比伦毁城历史相连。
- **Does not establish:** One burn layer cannot establish that every building burned the same day or test Lamentations' spiritual interpretation.
  **不能支持**：一处烧层不能确认每栋建筑同日燃烧，也不能检验哀歌对灾难的属灵解释。
- **Grade:** A/B+.
  **等级**：A/B+。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [Hebrew University / Journal of Archaeological Science: reconstructing fire destruction](https://cris.huji.ac.il/en/publications/destruction-by-fire-reconstructing-the-evidence-of-the-586-bce-ba/)
    [希伯来大学／Journal of Archaeological Science：火毁重建](https://cris.huji.ac.il/en/publications/destruction-by-fire-reconstructing-the-evidence-of-the-586-bce-ba/)
  - [PLOS ONE: archaeomagnetic study of Babylonian destruction](https://journals.plos.org/plosone/article?id=10.1371/journal.pone.0237029)
    [PLOS ONE：巴比伦毁灭层古地磁研究](https://journals.plos.org/plosone/article?id=10.1371/journal.pone.0237029)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-028"></a>

### 28. Nabu-sharrussu-ukin Tablet: a Senior Babylonian Official in Jeremiah | 尼布撒西金泥版：耶利米书中的巴比伦高级官员

- **Date:** Under Nebuchadnezzar II.
  **年代**：尼布甲尼撒二世时期。
- **Material:** Administrative tablet BM 114789 naming Nabu-sharrussu-ukin and the senior title `rab ša-rēši`.
  **材料**：巴比伦行政泥版 BM 114789，记 Nabu-sharrussu-ukin 和 “rab ša-rēši” 高级官衔。
- **Related Scripture:** Jeremiah 39:3.
  **对应经文**：耶利米书 39:3。
- **Supports:** Strong consonantal-name/title correspondence with biblical Nebo-sarsekim / rab-saris; Jeremiah preserves specific Babylonian court terminology.
  **能支持**：姓名辅音和高级官衔与经文尼布撒西金／拉撒力高度吻合；《耶利米书》保存了具体巴比伦宫廷术语。
- **Does not establish:** The tablet records a temple gold delivery, not Jerusalem's siege or every action of the official there.
  **不能支持**：泥版记录神庙交金事务，不是耶路撒冷围城战报，不能证明该官员在城中的每项行动。
- **Grade:** A−/B+.
  **等级**：A−／B+。
- **Sources:** [British Museum: BM 114789](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1920-1213-81)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆：BM 114789](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1920-1213-81)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-029"></a>

### 29. Jehoiachin Ration Documents: the Deposed Judean King in Babylon | 约雅斤配给文书：被废的犹大王在巴比伦

- **Date:** Sixth century BCE.
  **年代**：主前 6 世纪。
- **Material:** Oil-ration list VAT 16378 mentioning the king of Judah and attendants among captives.
  **材料**：配给清单 VAT 16378，记录给俘虏发放油，并提到“犹大王”及其随从。
- **Related Scripture:** 2 Kings 24:8–17; 25:27–30.
  **对应经文**：列王纪下 24:8–17；25:27–30。
- **Supports:** Jehoiachin/attendants within Babylonian palace provision, still identified by Judah's royal title.
  **能支持**：约雅斤及随从处于巴比伦宫廷供给体系，他仍以犹大王身份被辨认。
- **Does not establish:** Not an archive from the release day in 2 Kings 25 or a picture of every exile's living conditions.
  **不能支持**：该泥版不是王下 25 所记获释当天的档案，也不能代表所有被掳者生活状况。
- **Grade:** A.
  **等级**：A。
- **Sources:** [German Digital Library / Vorderasiatisches Museum Berlin: VAT 16378](https://www.deutsche-digitale-bibliothek.de/item/ZTR54HLR23HFPLZKCSDKCYF7P5UCBS4K)
  **来源**：[德国数字图书馆／柏林西亚博物馆：VAT 16378](https://www.deutsche-digitale-bibliothek.de/item/ZTR54HLR23HFPLZKCSDKCYF7P5UCBS4K)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-030"></a>

### 30. Al-Yahudu Documents: Judean Exiles' Rural Life | 亚胡杜文书：犹大被掳者的乡村生活

- **Date:** Mainly sixth–fifth centuries BCE; related material roughly 572–474 BCE.
  **年代**：主要跨主前 6–5 世纪；相关材料可覆盖约主前 572–474 年。
- **Material:** Cuneiform legal/administrative archives from Al-Yahudu and neighboring settlements.
  **材料**：亚胡杜及周边聚落的楔形文字法律和行政档案。
- **Related Scripture:** Jeremiah 29:4–7; Ezekiel's Babylonian exile setting.
  **对应经文**：耶利米书 29:4–7；以西结书的巴比伦被掳背景。
- **Supports:** Judean-origin residents farming, serving, borrowing, trading, forming families, and using imperial law in rural Babylonia.
  **能支持**：犹大来源居民在巴比伦乡村耕作、服役、借贷、买卖、成家并使用帝国法务系统。
- **Does not establish:** They were not wholly free modern farmers; legal contracts cannot test the inner grief of Psalm 137.
  **不能支持**：不能把他们描写成完全自由的现代农民，也不能用法律合同检验诗篇 137 的内心哀痛。
- **Grade:** A/B+.
  **等级**：A/B+。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [University of Pennsylvania: Judean exiles, 572–474 BCE](https://anch.sas.upenn.edu/news/0419-laurie-pearce-uc-berkeley-evidence-judean-exiles-babylonia-572%E2%80%93474-bce)
    [宾夕法尼亚大学：犹大被掳者主前 572–474 年材料](https://anch.sas.upenn.edu/news/0419-laurie-pearce-uc-berkeley-evidence-judean-exiles-babylonia-572%E2%80%93474-bce)
  - [ORACC: Al-Yahudu legal-text catalogue](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/ctij/withatf)
    [ORACC：亚胡杜法律文本目录](https://oracc.museum.upenn.edu/ctij/withatf)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-031"></a>

### 31. Nabonidus Chronicle: Babylon Passes to Persia in 539 BCE | 拿波尼度编年史：主前 539 年巴比伦归波斯

- **Date:** Surviving tablet possibly third–second century BCE, recounting sixth-century events.
  **年代**：现存泥版可能为主前 3–2 世纪抄本，所记事件属于主前 6 世纪。
- **Material:** Nabonidus Chronicle BM 35382.
  **材料**：拿波尼度编年史 BM 35382。
- **Related Scripture:** The imperial transition in Daniel 5–6; Isaiah 44–45; Ezra 1's setting.
  **对应经文**：但以理书 5–6 章的帝国过渡；以赛亚书 44–45 章；以斯拉记 1 章背景。
- **Supports:** Cyrus' acquisition of Babylon in 539 BCE, with a framework for Nabonidus' reign, Persian expansion, and entry.
  **能支持**：主前 539 年古列取得巴比伦，并给出拿波尼度统治、波斯扩张和入城过程的年表框架。
- **Does not establish:** Not Daniel 5's banquet, writing, or miracles; the chronicle also has a Babylonian priestly standpoint.
  **不能支持**：不能证明但 5 的宴席、墙上文字或神迹；编年史也有巴比伦祭司立场。
- **Grade:** A for imperial succession.
  **等级**：A（政权更替）。
- **Sources:** [British Museum: Nabonidus Chronicle, BM 35382](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_Sp-II-964)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆：拿波尼度编年史 BM 35382](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_Sp-II-964)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-032"></a>

### 32. Bisotun Inscription: Darius' Early Imperial Reorganization | 贝希斯敦铭文：大流士初年的帝国重整

- **Date:** 521–520 BCE.
  **年代**：主前 521–520 年。
- **Material:** Darius I's large trilingual inscription/relief recounting suppression of rival claimants and revolts.
  **材料**：大流士一世下令制作的三语巨型铭文和浮雕，记述他镇压各地王位竞争者和叛乱。
- **Related Scripture:** Ezra 5–6; Haggai; Zechariah 1–8.
  **对应经文**：以斯拉记 5–6 章；哈该书；撒迦利亚书 1–8 章。
- **Supports:** Widespread early-reign disorder and restored central control, supplying the setting for temple work resuming in 520 BCE.
  **能支持**：大流士即位初年帝国确有广泛动荡，随后重新建立中央控制；这为主前 520 年圣殿工程重启提供政治背景。
- **Does not establish:** No Jerusalem, Zerubbabel, Haggai, or temple. Calling opponents liars is also royal legitimation language.
  **不能支持**：铭文没有提耶路撒冷、所罗巴伯、哈该或圣殿；大流士称对手为“说谎者”也属王室合法化语言。
- **Grade:** A for Darius/imperial events; B for temple setting.
  **等级**：A（大流士与帝国事件）；对应圣殿背景为 B。
- **Sources:** [UNESCO: Bisotun](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1222/)
  **来源**：[UNESCO：贝希斯敦](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1222/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-033"></a>

### 33. YHD/YHWD Bullae, Stamps, and Coins: Persian Yehud | YHD／YHWD 封泥、印记与钱币：波斯犹大行省

- **Date:** Late sixth–fourth centuries BCE.
  **年代**：主前 6 世纪末至 4 世纪。
- **Material:** Bullae, jar-handle stamps, and coins bearing the provincial name, officials, or governor titles.
  **材料**：写有犹大行省名称、官员或省长称号的封泥、罐柄印记和钱币。
- **Related Scripture:** Ezra/Nehemiah's Persian provincial/governor system.
  **对应经文**：以斯拉记、尼希米记的波斯行省与总督制度。
- **Supports:** Post-exilic Judah as Yehud province, with local administration, writing, and limited minting.
  **能支持**：被掳后的犹大以 Yehud 行省形式存在，拥有本地行政、文书和有限铸币活动。
- **Does not establish:** A provincial name cannot establish a particular Ezra/Nehemiah reform; unprovenanced antiquities-market material needs downgrading.
  **不能支持**：一个行省名不能证明以斯拉或尼希米某项具体改革；来源不明的古董市场材料须降级。
- **Grade:** A for provincial name/administration.
  **等级**：A（行省名称与行政）。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [Hebrew University: post-exilic Judah archive bullae](https://archaeology.huji.ac.il/publications/qedem-4)
    [希伯来大学：被掳后犹大档案封泥](https://archaeology.huji.ac.il/publications/qedem-4)
  - [Hebrew University: Yehud stamps and coins overview](https://archaeology.huji.ac.il/city-david)
    [希伯来大学：Yehud 印记与钱币概述](https://archaeology.huji.ac.il/city-david)
  - [BASOR: stratified Ramat Raḥel Yehud stamp catalogue](https://www.journals.uchicago.edu/doi/10.1086/710530)
    [BASOR：有考古语境的 Ramat Raḥel Yehud 印记总表](https://www.journals.uchicago.edu/doi/10.1086/710530)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26. Some Qedem 4 material is accidental discovery without precise excavation context. This entry's A grade principally rests on stratified stamps, coins, and administrative evidence.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。Qedem 4 所收部分材料为偶然发现、缺少精确出土语境；本条的 A 级判断主要依靠有考古语境的印记、钱币与行政材料。

<a id="evidence-034"></a>

### 34. Susa's Palace: Esther's Imperial Location | 书珊王宫：以斯帖记的帝国地点背景

- **Date:** Achaemenid period; Esther's story is usually set under Xerxes I, 486–465 BCE.
  **年代**：阿契美尼德时期；《以斯帖记》叙事通常放在薛西斯一世统治期，主前 486–465 年。
- **Material:** Darius' palace, audience hall, administrative/residential buildings at Susa. Ahasuerus is usually linked with the Old Persian name Xerxes.
  **材料**：书珊遗址的大流士王宫、接见大厅、行政和居住建筑；亚哈随鲁之名通常与古波斯薛西斯之名相连。
- **Related Scripture:** Esther.
  **对应经文**：以斯帖记。
- **Supports:** Susa as a major Persian capital/court center, providing reliable location, royal-name, and administrative background.
  **能支持**：书珊是波斯帝国重要首都和宫廷中心；书卷的地点、王名与帝国行政世界有可靠背景。
- **Does not establish:** No contemporary external confirmation of Esther, Mordecai, Vashti, or Haman; later tomb traditions are not fifth-century BCE evidence.
  **不能支持**：尚无同期外部文献确认以斯帖、末底改、瓦实提或哈曼；后世墓葬传统不是主前 5 世纪证明。
- **Grade:** B for setting; no external personal attestation.
  **等级**：B（背景）；人物外证缺失。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [UNESCO: Susa](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1455/)
    [UNESCO：书珊遗址](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1455/)
  - [Encyclopaedia Iranica: Ahasuerus / Xerxes](https://www.iranicaonline.org/articles/ahasureus/)
    [Encyclopaedia Iranica：亚哈随鲁／薛西斯](https://www.iranicaonline.org/articles/ahasureus/)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-035"></a>

### 35. Elephantine Aramaic Papyri: Persian-Period Jewish Diaspora | 象岛亚兰文纸草：波斯时期犹太侨民

- **Date:** Fifth century BCE.
  **年代**：主前 5 世纪。
- **Material:** Marriage, property, loans, receipts, release, and family-law documents from Elephantine's Jewish community in Egypt.
  **材料**：埃及象岛犹太社群的婚姻、财产、借贷、收据、获释与家庭法律文书。
- **Related Scripture:** Esther's diaspora theme; Ezra/Nehemiah's Persian world.
  **对应经文**：以斯帖记的散居主题；以斯拉记、尼希米记所处的波斯帝国世界。
- **Supports:** Jewish communities continuing elsewhere in the empire after return, using Aramaic/imperial law and maintaining family/religious identity.
  **能支持**：归回以后仍有犹太群体长期居住帝国其他地区，使用亚兰文和帝国法律制度，维持家庭与宗教身份。
- **Does not establish:** Elephantine does not represent all Persian Jews; local shrines/practices cannot be projected as Jerusalem's uniform situation.
  **不能支持**：象岛不能代表所有波斯犹太人，其地方圣所和宗教实践也不可倒推成耶路撒冷群体的统一状况。
- **Grade:** A/B+.
  **等级**：A/B+。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [Brooklyn Museum: Elephantine Aramaic papyri collection](https://opencollection.brooklynmuseum.org/geographicallocations/7436305)
    [布鲁克林博物馆：象岛亚兰文纸草藏品群](https://opencollection.brooklynmuseum.org/geographicallocations/7436305)
  - [Metropolitan Museum of Art: Late Period Egypt and Elephantine's Jewish community](https://www.metmuseum.org/de/essays/egypt-in-the-late-period-ca-712-332-b-c)
    [大都会艺术博物馆：埃及晚期与象岛犹太社群](https://www.metmuseum.org/de/essays/egypt-in-the-late-period-ca-712-332-b-c)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="batch-4"></a>

## Batch 4: Between the Testaments to the Gospels | 第四批扩展锚点：两约之间至四福音

> 本节保留速查结论；政权更替、犹太宗派、四福音年代难题与逐段注释见[专题正文](./两约之间至四福音_第二圣殿与罗马背景.md)。

<a id="evidence-036"></a>

### 36. Rylands Greek P 458: Pre-Christian Greek Deuteronomy | Rylands Greek P 458：基督教以前的希腊文申命记

- **Date:** Second century BCE.
  **年代**：主前 2 世纪。
- **Material / collection identifier:** Eight fragments of a Deuteronomy papyrus roll; John Rylands Library, Greek P 458.
  **材料／馆藏号**：申命记纸草卷的八个残片；John Rylands Library, Greek P 458。
- **Supports:** Copying/use of the Greek Pentateuch before Christianity.
  **能支持**：摩西五经的古希腊译本在基督教形成前已经被抄写和使用。
- **Does not establish:** Not the Septuagint autograph or evidence that one team translated the whole Old Testament simultaneously.
  **不能支持**：不是七十士译本原稿，也不表示全部旧约在同一时间由同一团队翻完。
- **Grade:** A.
  **等级**：A。
- **Sources:** [University of Manchester collection](https://www.digitalcollections.manchester.ac.uk/view/MS-GREEK-P-00458)
  **来源**：[曼彻斯特大学藏品页](https://www.digitalcollections.manchester.ac.uk/view/MS-GREEK-P-00458)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-037"></a>

### 37. Heliodorus Stele: Seleucid Royal and Local Sanctuary Administration | 赫利奥多罗斯碑：塞琉古王室与地方圣所行政

- **Date:** Under Seleucus IV, first half of the second century BCE.
  **年代**：塞琉古四世时期，主前 2 世纪前半。
- **Supports:** Heliodorus as a real senior official, with royal intervention in regional sanctuary administration including Judea, supplying independent background for 2 Maccabees 3.
  **能支持**：赫利奥多罗斯是真实高官，王室介入包括犹太地在内区域的圣所行政，为《马加比二书》3 章提供独立背景。
- **Does not establish:** No account of that chapter's miracle or proof of every narrative detail.
  **不能支持**：碑文不记载该章神迹，也不证明叙事全部细节。
- **Grade:** A for person/institutions; B for the particular narrative.
  **等级**：A（人物与制度）；对具体叙事为 B。
- **Sources:** [Israel Museum](https://www.imj.org.il/en/exhibitions/heliodorus-stele)
  **来源**：[以色列博物馆](https://www.imj.org.il/en/exhibitions/heliodorus-stele)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-038"></a>

### 38. Antiochus IV Coins: Hellenistic Kingship's Public Image | 安条克四世钱币：希腊化王权的公开形象

- **Date:** Reign 175–164 BCE; listed silver coin around 173–169 BCE.
  **年代**：安条克四世在位为主前 175–164 年；所列银币约铸于主前 173–169 年。
- **Material / collection identifier:** Silver coin minted at Antioch; British Museum RPK,p180A.5.AntIV.
  **材料／馆藏号**：安条克铸银币；British Museum RPK,p180A.5.AntIV。
- **Supports:** Antiochus IV's identity, royal titles, minting date, and divine images.
  **能支持**：安条克四世身份、王号、铸币年代和神祇图像。
- **Does not establish:** One coin cannot reconstruct the whole Jerusalem persecution.
  **不能支持**：单枚钱币不能重建耶路撒冷迫害全过程。
- **Grade:** A.
  **等级**：A。
- **Sources:** [British Museum collection](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/C_RPK-p180A-5-AntIV?selectedImageId=143669001)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆藏品页](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/C_RPK-p180A-5-AntIV?selectedImageId=143669001)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-039"></a>

### 39. John Hyrcanus I Coins: Hasmonean Autonomy and Priestly Rule | 约翰·许尔堪一世钱币：哈斯摩尼自治与祭司政权

- **Date:** Around 135–104 BCE.
  **年代**：约主前 135–104 年。
- **Material / collection identifier:** Copper-alloy prutah; British Museum 1907,0107.7.
  **材料／馆藏号**：铜合金 prutah；British Museum 1907,0107.7。
- **Supports:** Local Hasmonean rule, priestly title, Hebrew expression of authority, and local minting.
  **能支持**：哈斯摩尼本土统治、祭司称号、希伯来文政权表达和地方铸币。
- **Does not establish:** Official coinage does not establish every subject's agreement with rule/expansion.
  **不能支持**：政权钱币不能证明所有臣民认同其王权或扩张政策。
- **Grade:** A.
  **等级**：A。
- **Sources:** [British Museum collection](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/C_1907-0107-7)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆藏品页](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/C_1907-0107-7)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-040"></a>

### 40. Mount Gerizim Sanctuary: a Samaritan Worship Center | 基利心山圣区：撒玛利亚敬拜中心

- **Date:** Persian–Hellenistic periods.
  **年代**：波斯—希腊化时期。
- **Supports:** A real large Samaritan sanctuary/settlement, supplying material setting for John 4's sanctuary dispute.
  **能支持**：大型撒玛利亚圣区和聚落真实存在，为约翰福音 4 章的圣所争论提供物质背景。
- **Does not establish:** The site cannot decide Jerusalem's versus Gerizim's theological legitimacy.
  **不能支持**：遗址不能裁决耶路撒冷或基利心山何者具有神学正统性。
- **Grade:** A for site/community setting; B for John 4.
  **等级**：A（遗址与群体背景）；对约 4 为 B。
- **Sources:** [Israel Nature and Parks Authority](https://en.parks.org.il/reserve-park/mount-gerizim/)
  **来源**：[以色列自然与公园管理局](https://en.parks.org.il/reserve-park/mount-gerizim/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26. The authority describes two major sanctuary construction phases, late Persian and Hellenistic. These alone cannot date the origins of Samaritan Pentateuch or sanctuary traditions.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。管理局所述圣区有波斯时期末与希腊化时期两个主要建设阶段；这一分期不能单独决定撒玛利亚五经或圣所传统的起源年代。

<a id="evidence-041"></a>

### 41. Herodian Buildings: Material Expression of Roman-Recognized Kingship | 希律建筑群：罗马认可王权的物质表达

- **Date:** Herod the Great's actual rule, 37–4 BCE.
  **年代**：大希律实际统治期，主前 37–4 年。
- **Material:** Caesarea, Masada, Herodium, and Jerusalem temple expansion, among others.
  **材料**：凯撒利亚、马萨达、赫罗狄翁及耶路撒冷圣殿扩建等。
- **Supports:** Herod as a major builder combining defense, cities, shipping, temple, and Roman loyalty.
  **能支持**：希律是大规模建造者，其政权把防御、城市、海运、圣殿与罗马忠诚结合。
- **Does not establish:** Buildings are not direct evidence for Matthew 2 or particular Jesus events; identifying Herodium's tomb as Herod's remains contested.
  **不能支持**：建筑遗址不是太 2 或某件耶稣事迹的直接证物；赫罗狄翁陵墓是否即希律墓仍有异议。
- **Grade:** A for construction/date; B/C for particular identifications.
  **等级**：A（工程和年代）；个别建筑认定为 B/C。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [UNESCO：Masada](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1040/)
  - [Hebrew University: Herodium report](https://heb.archaeology.huji.ac.il/sites/default/files/archaeology/files/qedem13.pdf)
    [希伯来大学：Herodium 报告](https://heb.archaeology.huji.ac.il/sites/default/files/archaeology/files/qedem13.pdf)
  - [Hebrew University: Caesarea report](https://heb.archaeology.huji.ac.il/sites/default/files/archaeology/files/qedem21.pdf)
    [希伯来大学：Caesarea 报告](https://heb.archaeology.huji.ac.il/sites/default/files/archaeology/files/qedem21.pdf)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-042"></a>

### 42. Temple Warning Inscription: Original, Replica, and the Gentile Boundary | 圣殿警告碑原件与复制品：外邦人进入内院的界线

- **Date:** Original first century BCE–first century CE; the British Museum object is a twentieth-century fiberglass replica.
  **年代**：原碑为主前 1 世纪至主后 1 世纪；大英博物馆所列物件是 20 世纪玻璃纤维复制品。
- **Material / collection identifier:** Original Istanbul Archaeological Museums 2196T; British Museum replica C.328.
  **材料／馆藏号**：原件现藏伊斯坦布尔考古博物馆，编号 2196T；大英博物馆复制品 C.328。
- **Supports:** An explicit ritual/ethnic boundary in temple space and public warning in Greek, illuminating Acts 21's accusation.
  **能支持**：圣殿空间存在明确礼仪—族群界线，公共警告使用希腊语；可帮助理解徒 21 的指控背景。
- **Does not establish:** No Paul; Ephesians 2's whole theology cannot be reduced to one stone barrier. The British Museum web object is a replica, not the original.
  **不能支持**：碑文不提保罗，也不能把弗 2 的全部神学简化为一块石栏；大英博物馆网页展示的是复制品，不可误写成馆藏原碑。
- **Grade:** A.
  **等级**：A。
- **Sources:** [British Museum C.328](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_C-328); the page also records discovery history and the original's present location.
  **来源**：[大英博物馆 C.328 藏品说明](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_C-328)；该页同时记录原件的发现史与现藏位置。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-043"></a>

### 43. Theodotus Inscription: Synagogue Functions Before 70 CE | Theodotus 铭文：主后 70 年前会堂的功能

- **Date:** Usually late Second Temple; precise date disputed.
  **年代**：通常置于晚期第二圣殿时代，精确年代仍有讨论。
- **Supports:** A Greek-speaking Jerusalem synagogue for law reading, commandment teaching, and hospitality to visitors.
  **能支持**：耶路撒冷希腊语会堂用于诵读律法、教导诫命和接待外来者。
- **Does not establish:** One synagogue's functions are not a universal regional liturgy.
  **不能支持**：不能把一所会堂的功能当成所有地区的统一礼仪。
- **Grade:** A for wording; B/C for dating/generalization.
  **等级**：A（铭文内容）；年代和普遍化为 B/C。
- **Sources:** [University of Michigan image database](https://image-database.nes.lsa.umich.edu/items/show/196)
  **来源**：[密歇根大学图像资料库](https://image-database.nes.lsa.umich.edu/items/show/196)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26. The database dates its script to the Herodian period. This project retains a wider late Second Temple range because precise phasing is debated.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。该资料库依字体把铭文置于希律时期；项目仍保留更宽的晚期第二圣殿年代范围，因为精确分期存在讨论。

<a id="evidence-044"></a>

### 44. Magdala Synagogue: Galilean Assembly in the Gospel Era | 马格达拉会堂：福音时代的加利利会堂

- **Date:** Early Roman, used before 70 CE.
  **年代**：早期罗马时期，主后 70 年以前使用。
- **Supports:** An actual Galilean synagogue building in Jesus' era, linking local assembly with Jerusalem temple imagery.
  **能支持**：加利利在耶稣时代已有实际会堂建筑，地方聚会与耶路撒冷圣殿图像相连。
- **Does not establish:** No inscription establishes Jesus' entry into this excavated building.
  **不能支持**：没有铭文证明耶稣曾进入已发掘的这一座建筑。
- **Grade:** A for building/date; B for specific Gospel events.
  **等级**：A（建筑与年代）；对具体福音事件为 B。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: preliminary excavation report](https://publications.iaa.org.il/atiqot/vol90/iss1/7/)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局发掘初报](https://publications.iaa.org.il/atiqot/vol90/iss1/7/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26. Excavation divides Early Roman strata into the mid-/late first century BCE and first century CE through 67, supporting the pre-70 wording.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。发掘报告把早期罗马层细分为主前 1 世纪中后期及主后 1 世纪至 67 年，支持“主后 70 年前”的表述。

<a id="evidence-045"></a>

### 45. Ancient Galilee Boat: Fishing and Lake Transport | 加利利湖古船：渔业与湖上交通

- **Date:** Radiocarbon around 100 BCE–100 CE.
  **年代**：碳十四年代约主前 100 至主后 100 年。
- **Supports:** Gospel-era Galilee boat size, construction, and repair technology.
  **能支持**：福音时代加利利湖船只的规模、造船和修补技术。
- **Does not establish:** No ownership inscription; it is not identified as Jesus' or Peter's boat.
  **不能支持**：没有所有者铭文，不能称为耶稣或彼得的船。
- **Grade:** A for object/date; B for Gospel setting.
  **等级**：A（器物与年代）；对福音叙事为 B。
- **Sources:** [Yigal Allon Centre](https://en.yigal-allon-centre.org.il/the-ancient-boat/)
  **来源**：[Yigal Allon Centre](https://en.yigal-allon-centre.org.il/the-ancient-boat/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-046"></a>

### 46. Pilate Coins: Local Administration Under a Roman Governor | 彼拉多钱币：罗马长官的地方行政

- **Date:** Listed coin dated 30–31 CE.
  **年代**：所列藏品为主后 30–31 年。
- **Material / collection identifier:** Copper-alloy prutah; British Museum 1908,0110.522.
  **材料／馆藏号**：铜合金 prutah；British Museum 1908,0110.522。
- **Supports:** Jerusalem minting under Pilate, with Tiberian dating and Roman religious imagery.
  **能支持**：彼拉多任内在耶路撒冷铸币，并使用提庇留纪年与罗马宗教图像。
- **Does not establish:** No account of Jesus' trial or crucifixion.
  **不能支持**：钱币不记载耶稣受审或十字架事件。
- **Grade:** A.
  **等级**：A。
- **Sources:** [British Museum collection](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/C_1908-0110-522)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆藏品页](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/C_1908-0110-522)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-047"></a>

### 47. Caiaphas Ossuary: Priestly Elites, Names, and Burial | “该亚法”骨棺：祭司精英、姓名与葬俗

- **Date:** First century CE.
  **年代**：主后 1 世纪。
- **Material / inscribed name:** Limestone ossuary from a southern Jerusalem family tomb, inscription commonly read Joseph bar Caiaphas.
  **材料／铭名**：耶路撒冷南部家族墓所出石灰岩骨棺，铭名通常读作“约瑟·巴·该亚法”（Joseph bar Caiaphas）。
- **Supports:** Caiaphas-related names, Jerusalem priestly elite families, and ossuary burial.
  **能支持**：该亚法相关姓名、耶路撒冷祭司精英家族和骨棺葬俗。
- **Does not establish:** No Jesus trial record; identification with the Gospel high priest still involves historical inference.
  **不能支持**：骨棺不记载耶稣受审；与福音书大祭司的具体身份等同仍含历史推断。
- **Grade:** A for object/name/burial; B+ for personal identification.
  **等级**：A（器物、铭名和葬俗）；身份等同为 B+。
- **Sources:** [Israel Museum](https://www.imj.org.il/en/exhibitions/ossuary-caiaphas-high-priest-18-36-ce)
  **来源**：[以色列博物馆](https://www.imj.org.il/en/exhibitions/ossuary-caiaphas-high-priest-18-36-ce)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-048"></a>

### 48. Yehohanan's Crucified Remains: Roman Punishment and Jewish Burial | 约哈难被钉者遗骸：罗马刑罚与犹太安葬

- **Date:** Herodian period, first century CE.
  **年代**：希律时期，主后 1 世纪。
- **Material:** Ossuary with a Yehohanan-related inscription and a right heel bone still pierced transversely by an iron nail.
  **材料**：刻有“约哈难／Yehohanan”相关铭名的骨棺，以及仍被铁钉横向穿过的右跟骨。
- **Supports:** Roman crucifixion in Judea and at least one crucified person's later Jewish tomb/ossuary burial.
  **能支持**：罗马在犹太地使用钉十字架；至少一名被钉者后来进入犹太墓葬并以骨棺收殓。
- **Does not establish:** Not Jesus' remains, a fixed model for all crucifixion, or resurrection evidence. The 1985 reassessment rejected the old one-long-nail-through-both-heels reconstruction; surviving bones cannot establish whether arms were nailed or tied.
  **不能支持**：不是耶稣遗骸，不能固定所有十字架刑方式，也不涉及复活。1985 年复核否定了“一枚长钉同时贯穿两脚跟”的旧重建；现存骨骼也不能证明双臂究竟被钉还是被绑。
- **Grade:** A for this punishment case; B for Jesus' passion details.
  **等级**：A（刑罚个案）；对耶稣受难细节为 B。
- **Sources:** see the linked sources below.
  **来源**：
  - [Google Arts & Culture / Israel Museum](https://artsandculture.google.com/asset/ossuary-of-yehohanan-son-of-hagkol-with-heel-bone-and-nail-unknown/8gEPRIAlblWlqw)
    [Google Arts & Culture / 以色列博物馆](https://artsandculture.google.com/asset/ossuary-of-yehohanan-son-of-hagkol-with-heel-bone-and-nail-unknown/8gEPRIAlblWlqw)
  - [Israel Exploration Journal 35 (1985): Zias and Sekeles reassessment](https://www.jstor.org/stable/27925968)
    [Israel Exploration Journal 35（1985）：Zias 与 Sekeles 复核](https://www.jstor.org/stable/27925968)
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-049"></a>

### 49. Arch of Titus: Roman Commemoration of the Temple's 70 CE Destruction | 提多凯旋门：主后 70 年圣殿被毁的罗马纪念

- **Date:** After 81 CE.
  **年代**：主后 81 年以后。
- **Carrier / identifier:** Pentelic-marble single arch; original dedication CIL VI 945.
  **载体／编号**：潘泰利克大理石单拱门；原始奉献铭文为 CIL VI 945。
- **Supports:** Public Roman celebration of victory in the Jewish War, depicting temple spoils including the seven-branched lampstand.
  **能支持**：罗马公开庆祝犹太战争胜利，浮雕展示从耶路撒冷圣殿取得的七枝灯台等战利品。
- **Does not establish:** An imperial victory monument, not a neutral battle report.
  **不能支持**：凯旋门是帝国胜利宣传，不是中立战报。
- **Grade:** A.
  **等级**：A。
- **Sources:** [Colosseum Archaeological Park: Arch of Titus](https://colosseo.it/en/marvels/arch-of-titus/); [Same institution: architecture, inscription, and restoration](https://colosseo.it/en/restauri/arch-of-titus/)
  **来源**：[罗马斗兽场考古公园：提多凯旋门](https://colosseo.it/en/marvels/arch-of-titus/)；[同机构：建筑、铭文与修复资料](https://colosseo.it/en/restauri/arch-of-titus/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="batch-5"></a>

## Batch 5: Acts and the Pauline Epistles | 第五批扩展锚点：使徒行传与保罗书信

> 本节只列速查结论；宣教旅程、书信相对年代、作者争议、逐段经文注释和完整来源见[专题正文](./使徒行传与保罗书信_旅程年代与城市史料对照.md)。

<a id="evidence-050"></a>

### 50. Agrippa I Coins: Royal Background to Acts 12 | 亚基帕一世钱币：使徒行传 12 章的王权背景

- **Date:** Reign 37–44 CE; listed coin from year eight, 43–44 CE.
  **年代**：亚基帕一世统治期为主后 37–44 年；所列钱币为在位第八年，即主后 43–44 年。
- **Material / collection:** Copper-alloy coin, British Museum BM 1908,0110.395, minted at Caesarea.
  **材质／馆藏**：铜合金币；大英博物馆 BM 1908,0110.395，铸于凯撒利亚。
- **Related Scripture:** Acts 12.
  **对应经文**：使徒行传 12 章。
- **Supports:** Herod Agrippa I as a Roman-recognized ruler under Claudius, exercising Judean kingship and minting coins.
  **能支持**：希律·亚基帕一世是革老丢时代受罗马承认、在犹太拥有王权并发行钱币的统治者。
- **Does not establish:** No James' death, Peter's imprisonment, or Agrippa's death.
  **不能支持**：钱币不记雅各被杀、彼得被囚或亚基帕之死。
- **Grade:** A for person/title/rule; B for Acts 12's particular events.
  **等级**：A（人物、称号、统治背景）；与徒 12 具体事件关系为 B。
- **Sources:** [British Museum: BM 1908,0110.395](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/C_1908-0110-395)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆，BM 1908,0110.395](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/C_1908-0110-395)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-051"></a>

### 51. Paphos: Cyprus' Provincial Capital on Journey One | 帕福斯遗址：第一次旅程中的居比路省会

- **Date:** Hellenistic–Roman periods.
  **年代**：希腊化至罗马时期。
- **Related Scripture:** Acts 13:4–12.
  **对应经文**：使徒行传 13:4–12。
- **Supports:** Roman Cyprus' capital with prominent Aphrodite worship, supplying the urban setting for proconsul, religious specialist, and mission.
  **能支持**：帕福斯是罗马时期的居比路首府，并具有显著的阿佛洛狄忒敬拜传统，为方伯、宗教专家与宣教相遇提供城市背景。
- **Does not establish:** No proof of Sergius Paulus' conversion or identification of Elymas.
  **不能支持**：遗址不证明士求·保罗归信，也没有确认以吕马个人。
- **Grade:** A for city/period; B for narrative setting.
  **等级**：A（城市与时代）；B（叙事背景）。
- **Sources:** [UNESCO: Paphos](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/79/); [University of Cyprus: Paphos Harbour and the Roman proconsul's seat](https://www.ucy.ac.cy/marecyprium/case-studies/paphos-harbour/)
  **来源**：[UNESCO：Paphos](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/79/)；[居比路大学：Paphos Harbour 与罗马方伯驻地](https://www.ucy.ac.cy/marecyprium/case-studies/paphos-harbour/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-052"></a>

### 52. Philippi and Via Egnatia: Roman Colonial Identity | 腓立比与 Via Egnatia：罗马殖民城的身份世界

- **Date:** Roman colonial phase, especially after 42 BCE.
  **年代**：罗马殖民城阶段，尤其主前 42 年以后。
- **Related Scripture:** Acts 16; Philippians.
  **对应经文**：使徒行传 16 章；腓立比书。
- **Supports:** Colonial public space, road links, tribunal, and identity culture, illuminating language about Roman identity and heavenly citizenship.
  **能支持**：腓立比具有罗马殖民地公共空间、交通道路、法庭和身份文化，“我们是罗马人”与“天上国民”等语言可放在这一环境中理解。
- **Does not establish:** Existing buildings cannot identify Lydia's house, the jailer's home, or the supposed Paul prison.
  **不能支持**：现存建筑不能确定吕底亚的家、禁卒的住处或所谓“保罗监牢”。
- **Grade:** A for city identity/site; D for particular personal locations.
  **等级**：A（城市身份与遗址）；具体人物地点为 D。
- **Sources:** [UNESCO: Archaeological Site of Philippi](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1517/)
  **来源**：[UNESCO：Archaeological Site of Philippi](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1517/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-053"></a>

### 53. Thessalonian Politarch Inscription: Local Office Terminology | 帖撒罗尼迦“政治官”铭文：地方官职用语

- **Date:** Surviving block second century CE.
  **年代**：现存石块为主后 2 世纪。
- **Material / collection:** Marble gate block; British Museum BM 1877,0511.1; IG X 126.
  **材质／馆藏**：大理石城门建筑块；大英博物馆 BM 1877,0511.1，铭文出版号 IG X 126。
- **Related Scripture:** Acts 17:6, 8.
  **对应经文**：使徒行传 17:6、8。
- **Supports:** Politarch as an actual Macedonian, especially Thessalonian office, showing Acts' local terminology.
  **能支持**：politarch 是马其顿、特别是帖撒罗尼迦真实使用的地方官职，说明《使徒行传》的术语有地方色彩。
- **Does not establish:** The later block does not name the officials who arrested Jason.
  **不能支持**：石块晚于保罗时代，没有列出抓耶孙的那批官员。
- **Grade:** A for title use; B for Acts 17 setting.
  **等级**：A（职称使用）；B（徒 17 背景）。
- **Sources:** [British Museum: BM 1877,0511.1](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/G_1877-0511-1)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆，BM 1877,0511.1](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/G_1877-0511-1)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-054"></a>

### 54. Areopagus and Unknown Gods: Athens' Religious Setting | 亚略巴古与“未知诸神”：雅典演说的宗教环境

- **Date:** Classical–Roman; Pausanias' account composed in the second century CE.
  **年代**：古典至罗马时期；保萨尼亚斯记载成书于主后 2 世纪。
- **Related Scripture:** Acts 17:16–34.
  **对应经文**：使徒行传 17:16–34。
- **Supports:** Areopagus as both place and public council; ancient authors knew Athenian altar traditions for unknown deities.
  **能支持**：亚略巴古既是地点也是具有公共职能的议事机关；古代作者确知雅典存在献给未知神灵的祭坛传统。
- **Does not establish:** No identified altar demonstrably seen by Paul with the singular unknown-god inscription; classical accounts are not verbatim sermon transcripts.
  **不能支持**：尚未找到可确认是保罗所见、写着单数“未识之神”的那座祭坛；古典文献也不是保罗讲章逐字记录。
- **Grade:** B.
  **等级**：B。
- **Sources:** [Acropolis Museum: Areopagus Council](https://www.theacropolismuseum.gr/en/node/1007); [Scaife／Perseus: Pausanias 1.1.4](https://scaife.perseus.org/reader/urn%3Acts%3AgreekLit%3Atlg0525.tlg001.perseus-eng2%3A1.1.4/?right=perseus-grc2)
  **来源**：[雅典卫城博物馆：Areopagus Council](https://www.theacropolismuseum.gr/en/node/1007)；[Scaife／Perseus：Pausanias 1.1.4](https://scaife.perseus.org/reader/urn%3Acts%3AgreekLit%3Atlg0525.tlg001.perseus-eng2%3A1.1.4/?right=perseus-grc2)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-055"></a>

### 55. Suetonius and Claudius: Background to Aquila and Priscilla Leaving Rome | 苏埃托尼乌斯与革老丢措施：亚居拉、百基拉离开罗马的背景

- **Date:** Common reconstruction around 49 CE; Claudius reigns 41–54 CE.
  **年代**：常见重建约主后 49 年；革老丢在位为主后 41–54 年。
- **Related Scripture:** Acts 18:1–3; Romans 16:3–5.
  **对应经文**：使徒行传 18:1–3；罗马书 16:3–5。
- **Supports:** Suetonius records Claudius expelling Jews over unrest associated with Chrestus, comparable with Aquila/Priscilla's arrival from Italy in Corinth.
  **能支持**：罗马作者苏埃托尼乌斯记有革老丢因与 “Chrestus” 有关的骚乱而驱逐犹太人的行动，与亚居拉、百基拉从意大利到哥林多的背景可互相参照。
- **Does not establish:** No names of the couple; Chrestus' identity, scope, and exact year remain discussed.
  **不能支持**：材料没有点名这对夫妇；“Chrestus”所指、措施范围和精确年份仍有讨论。
- **Grade:** B+.
  **等级**：B+。
- **Sources:** [Perseus: Suetonius, Claudius 25.4](https://www.perseus.tufts.edu/hopper/text?doc=Perseus%3Atext%3A1999.02.0061%3Alife%3Dcl.%3Achapter%3D25)
  **来源**：[Perseus：Suetonius, Claudius 25.4](https://www.perseus.tufts.edu/hopper/text?doc=Perseus%3Atext%3A1999.02.0061%3Alife%3Dcl.%3Achapter%3D25)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-056"></a>

### 56. Delphi Gallio Inscription: a Strong Pauline Chronological Synchronism | 德尔斐迦流铭文：保罗年代线最强同步点

- **Date:** Claudius' Delphi decree around 52 CE; Gallio's Achaean proconsulship usually reconstructed around 51–52 CE.
  **年代**：革老丢致德尔斐的诏令约主后 52 年；迦流任亚该亚省方伯通常重建在主后 51–52 年附近。
- **Carrier / identifier:** Nine Greek limestone fragments preserving over eleven lines; common edition SIG II 801d.
  **载体／编号**：九块希腊文石灰岩残片，共保存十一行以上；常用出版编号 SIG II 801d。
- **Related Scripture:** Acts 18:12–17.
  **对应经文**：使徒行传 18:12–17。
- **Supports:** Gallio explicitly as Achaean proconsul; imperial title combinations and Gallio's report to the emperor constrain his office to a narrow window, informing Paul's Corinth phase and relative letter dates.
  **能支持**：诏令称迦流为亚该亚 proconsul；皇帝头衔组合和迦流向皇帝报告的关系为其任期提供窄年代窗，由此可回推保罗在哥林多和早期书信的相对年代。
- **Does not establish:** No Paul, synagogue dispute, or ruling; decree dating cannot fix Gallio's office to one precise month.
  **不能支持**：铭文不提保罗、会堂争议或迦流的裁决；诏令日期也不能把迦流任期精确到某一个月。
- **Grade:** A for official/title/synchronism; B for Paul's event.
  **等级**：A（官员、职衔、年代同步）；与保罗事件关系为 B。
- **Sources:** [Archaeological Museum of Delphi](https://archaeologicalmuseums.culture.gov.gr/en/museum/5df34af3deca5e2d79e8c17a/archaeological-museum-of-delphi); [K. C. Hanson: inscription, Greek text, English translation](https://www.kchanson.com/ANCDOCS/greek/gallio.html); [Persée: Gallio inscription study](https://www.persee.fr/doc/reg_0035-2039_1967_num_80_379_3963)
