  **来源**：[德尔斐考古博物馆](https://archaeologicalmuseums.culture.gov.gr/en/museum/5df34af3deca5e2d79e8c17a/archaeological-museum-of-delphi)；[K. C. Hanson：铭文资料、希腊文与英译](https://www.kchanson.com/ANCDOCS/greek/gallio.html)；[Persée：迦流铭文研究](https://www.persee.fr/doc/reg_0035-2039_1967_num_80_379_3963)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-057"></a>

### 57. Corinth Bema: Public Judicial Space in a Provincial Capital | 哥林多 bema：省会的公开司法空间

- **Date:** Roman period.
  **年代**：罗马时期。
- **Related Scripture:** Acts 18:12–17.
  **对应经文**：使徒行传 18:12–17。
- **Supports:** A public forum platform compatible with officials' open hearings in a provincial capital.
  **能支持**：哥林多广场确有公开讲台／裁判平台，符合省会中官员公开听讼的空间背景。
- **Does not establish:** No exact step on which Paul stood, and no supposed Paul footprints.
  **不能支持**：不能确认保罗站在现存平台的哪一级，更没有所谓“保罗脚印”。
- **Grade:** A for architecture/function; B for particular narrative scene.
  **等级**：A（建筑与功能）；B（具体叙事场景）。
- **Sources:** [American School of Classical Studies: Corinth bema excavation report](https://www.ascsa.edu.gr/uploads/media/hesperia/146969.pdf)
  **来源**：[美国古典研究学院：Corinth bema 发掘报告](https://www.ascsa.edu.gr/uploads/media/hesperia/146969.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-058"></a>

### 58. Erastus Pavement Inscription: a Namesake Official, Not a Settled Identity | 以拉都铺路铭文：同名城市官员，不等于已经确认身份

- **Date:** Roman; exact date/construction phase debated.
  **年代**：罗马时期；精确年代与建筑阶段仍有讨论。
- **Publication identifier:** *Corinth* VIII.3, no. 232, also IKorinthKent 232.
  **出版编号**：*Corinth* VIII.3, no. 232（亦写 IKorinthKent 232）。
- **Related Scripture:** Romans 16:23; Acts 19:22; 2 Timothy 4:20.
  **对应经文**：罗马书 16:23；使徒行传 19:22；提摩太后书 4:20。
- **Supports:** A wealthy public official named Erastus paying for Corinthian paving, illuminating civic patronage/office.
  **能支持**：哥林多有一位名叫 Erastus、具财富和公共职位的人自费铺路，说明城市赞助和官员世界。
- **Does not establish:** Shared name/city cannot establish Paul's coworker; equivalence of Latin aedile and Greek oikonomos is disputed.
  **不能支持**：名字与城市相同不足以证明他就是保罗同工；拉丁 aedile 与希腊 oikonomos 是否对应也有争论。
- **Grade:** B for civic setting; C for personal identification.
  **等级**：B（城市背景）；C（人物等同）。
- **Sources:** [American School of Classical Studies: Corinth VIII.3, Latin inscription 232](https://www.ascsa.edu.gr/uploads/media/oa_ebooks/oa_corinth/Corinth_VIII_3.pdf); [PHI: Corinth 8.3, no. 232 text](https://epigraphy.packhum.org/text/209961); [Cambridge New Testament Studies: identity and office debate](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/new-testament-studies/article/erastus-of-corinth-romans-1623-responding-to-recent-proposals-on-his-rank-status-and-faith/B5585E1CDE28E82C95A0CA9D2413E52A)
  **来源**：[美国古典研究学院：*Corinth* VIII.3 拉丁铭文，第 232 号](https://www.ascsa.edu.gr/uploads/media/oa_ebooks/oa_corinth/Corinth_VIII_3.pdf)；[PHI：Corinth 8.3, no. 232 文本](https://epigraphy.packhum.org/text/209961)；[Cambridge *New Testament Studies*：身份与职位争论](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/new-testament-studies/article/erastus-of-corinth-romans-1623-responding-to-recent-proposals-on-his-rank-status-and-faith/B5585E1CDE28E82C95A0CA9D2413E52A)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-059"></a>

### 59. Ephesus and Artemis: Faith, Honor, and Economy | 以弗所城市与亚底米圣所：信仰、荣誉和经济互相牵动

- **Date:** Hellenistic–Roman; Paul's activity concentrated in the mid-first century CE.
  **年代**：希腊化至罗马时期，保罗活动集中在主后 1 世纪中叶。
- **Related Scripture:** Acts 18–20; 1 Corinthians 15:32, 16:8–9.
  **对应经文**：使徒行传 18–20 章；哥林多前书 15:32、16:8–9。
- **Supports:** Theater, Artemis sanctuary, administration, festivals, craft economy, and inscriptions illuminate the social dynamics of Acts 19's riot.
  **能支持**：大型剧场、亚底米圣所、城市行政、节庆、工匠经济与大量铭文共同说明徒 19 暴动的社会机制。
- **Does not establish:** No archaeological identification of silversmith Demetrius or proof of every crowd shout.
  **不能支持**：考古没有确认银匠底米丢本人，也不能证明群众每一句呼喊。
- **Grade:** A for city/sanctuary/institutions; B for narrative background.
  **等级**：A（城市、圣所与制度）；B（叙事背景）。
- **Sources:** [UNESCO: Ephesus](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1018/); [Austrian Archaeological Institute: Ephesus inscriptions](https://www.oeaw.ac.at/en/oeai/research/classical-studies/epigraphy/the-inscriptions-of-asia-minor)
  **来源**：[UNESCO：Ephesus](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1018/)；[奥地利科学院考古研究院：Ephesus inscriptions](https://www.oeaw.ac.at/en/oeai/research/classical-studies/epigraphy/the-inscriptions-of-asia-minor)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-060"></a>

### 60. Felix and Festus Coins: Administration During the Caesarean Imprisonment | 腓力斯与非斯都钱币：凯撒利亚监禁的行政背景

- **Date:** Felix coin around 54–55 CE; British Museum Festus coin catalogued to Nero's fifth year, 58–59 CE.
  **年代**：腓力斯钱币约主后 54–55 年；大英博物馆所藏非斯都钱币编年为尼禄第五年、主后 58–59 年。
- **Material / collection:** Both Jerusalem-minted copper-alloy prutahs: Felix BM 1908,0110.569; Festus BM 1908,0110.577.
  **材质／馆藏**：两枚均为耶路撒冷铸造的铜合金 prutah；腓力斯币 BM 1908,0110.569，非斯都币 BM 1908,0110.577。
- **Related Scripture:** Acts 23–26.
  **对应经文**：使徒行传 23–26 章。
- **Supports:** Both Roman Judean governors connected with local coinage and Neronian rule.
  **能支持**：两位罗马犹太行政长官都与地方铸币和尼禄时期政权相连。
- **Does not establish:** No Pauline trial. Festus' exact accession requires Josephus and other chronology; one catalogue date cannot end the debate.
  **不能支持**：钱币不提保罗审讯；非斯都准确就任年仍需结合约瑟夫斯和其他年代资料讨论，不能由一枚目录年代终结争议。
- **Grade:** A for officials/coins; C for Festus' precise accession.
  **等级**：A（官员和钱币）；C（非斯都精确就任年）。
- **Sources:** [British Museum: Antonius Felix coin, BM 1908,0110.569](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/C_1908-0110-569); [British Museum: Porcius Festus coin, BM 1908,0110.577](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/C_1908-0110-577)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆：Antonius Felix coin，BM 1908,0110.569](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/C_1908-0110-569)；[大英博物馆：Porcius Festus coin，BM 1908,0110.577](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/C_1908-0110-577)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-061"></a>

### 61. P46: a Pauline Collection Around 200 CE | P46：约主后 200 年前后的保罗书信集

- **Date:** Common catalogues: late second–early third century, around 200 CE, a paleographic approximation rather than exact production year; Michigan describes the hand as third century.
  **年代**：通行目录多写主后 2 世纪末至 3 世纪初、约主后 200 年；这是古文字学约数，不是精确制作年，密歇根大学将其书体描述为 3 世纪。
- **Material / collection:** Greek papyrus codex, 86 surviving leaves: 56 at Chester Beatty Library, Dublin (CBL BP II), 30 at Michigan (Inv. 6238).
  **材质／馆藏**：希腊文纸草 codex，现存 86 叶；56 叶藏都柏林 Chester Beatty Library（CBL BP II），30 叶藏密歇根大学（Inv. 6238）。
- **Related Scripture:** Early transmission of Pauline letters and Hebrews.
  **对应经文**：保罗书信集及希伯来书的早期传抄史。
- **Supports:** On conventional dating, multiple Pauline letters copied in one codex by the late second/early third century, with an order differing from modern Bibles.
  **能支持**：按通行年代，多封保罗书信在主后 2 世纪末至 3 世纪初已被抄在同一纸草 codex 中；书卷次序与现代圣经不完全相同。
- **Does not establish:** Not Paul's autograph or an exact paleographic year. Including Hebrews cannot prove Pauline authorship. Missing final leaves mean surviving contents cannot establish that Pastorals were never in the original codex.
  **不能支持**：P46 不是保罗原稿；古文字学不能给出精确年份；也不能因希伯来书被收在书信集中就证明其作者是保罗。末叶缺失，因此不能仅凭现存内容断定教牧书信从未在原 codex 中。
- **Grade:** A for manuscript/text/collection history.
  **等级**：A（抄本、文本和文集史）。
- **Sources:** [CSNTM: P46 digital manuscript and catalogue](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/Manuscript/Group/GA_P46); [University of Michigan: introduction to P46](https://apps.lib.umich.edu/files/collections/papyrus/k12/reading/Paul/index.html); [University of Michigan: P46 paleography](https://apps.lib.umich.edu/files/collections/papyrus/exhibits/reading/Paul/paleography.html); [Chester Beatty Library: P46 publication](https://chesterbeatty.ie/assets/uploads/2018/11/Biblical-Papyri-Descriptions-and-Texts-of-Twelve-Manuscripts-on-Payprus-of-the-Greek-Bible-III-text-Opt.pdf)
  **来源**：[CSNTM：P46 数字抄本与目录](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/Manuscript/Group/GA_P46)；[密歇根大学：P46 导论](https://apps.lib.umich.edu/files/collections/papyrus/k12/reading/Paul/index.html)；[密歇根大学：P46 古文字学说明](https://apps.lib.umich.edu/files/collections/papyrus/exhibits/reading/Paul/paleography.html)；[Chester Beatty Library：P46 专刊](https://chesterbeatty.ie/assets/uploads/2018/11/Biblical-Papyri-Descriptions-and-Texts-of-Twelve-Manuscripts-on-Payprus-of-the-Greek-Bible-III-text-Opt.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="batch-6"></a>

## Batch 6: General Epistles and Revelation | 第六批扩展锚点：普通书信与启示录

> 本节保留速查结论；作者／年代矩阵、小普林尼书信、逐卷注释、七城背景、帝国崇拜和启示录解释边界见[专题正文](./希伯来书至启示录_晚期新约与七教会背景.md)。

<a id="evidence-062"></a>

### 62. Josephus and James' Death: a Jerusalem Anchor Around 62 CE | 约瑟夫斯与雅各之死：主后 62 年附近的耶路撒冷锚点

- **Date:** Around 62 CE.
  **年代**：主后 62 年附近。
- **Text identifier:** Antiquities 20.200, traditionally 20.9.1.
  **文本标识**：约瑟夫斯《犹太古史》20.200（传统章节点 20.9.1）。
- **Related Scripture:** James' authorship setting; Acts 15, 21; Galatians 2:9.
  **对应经文**：雅各书作者背景；使徒行传 15、21 章；加拉太书 2:9。
- **Supports:** Antiquities 20.200 places James, brother of Jesus called Christ, in a high-priestly abuse-of-authority episode between Festus' death and Albinus' arrival.
  **能支持**：《犹太古史》20.200 把名为雅各、被称为基督之耶稣的兄弟，放在非斯都死后与亚尔比努到任前的大祭司越权事件中。
- **Does not establish:** No mention of the letter James, so no independent proof of its author/year. A large majority accept authenticity, with a minority interpolation proposal.
  **不能支持**：材料不提雅各书，不能单独证明该书作者或写作年。该段真实性获绝大多数学者接受，但仍有少数学者主张插入说。
- **Grade:** A/B.
  **等级**：A/B。
- **Sources:** [Perseus: Josephus, Antiquities 20.9.1](https://www.perseus.tufts.edu/hopper/text?doc=J.+AJ+20.9.1); [Oxford: study of Antiquities 20.200](https://academic.oup.com/book/60034/chapter/513641505)
  **来源**：[Perseus：Josephus, Antiquities 20.9.1](https://www.perseus.tufts.edu/hopper/text?doc=J.+AJ+20.9.1)；[Oxford：Antiquities 20.200 研究](https://academic.oup.com/book/60034/chapter/513641505)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-063"></a>

### 63. Pliny and Trajan: Christians in Pontus–Bithynia | 小普林尼与图拉真书信：本都—比提尼亚的基督徒

- **Date:** Around 111–113 CE.
  **年代**：约主后 111–113 年。
- **Text identifier:** Pliny the Younger, Letters 10.96; Trajan's reply 10.97.
  **文本标识**：小普林尼《书信》10.96；图拉真复函 10.97。
- **Related Scripture:** The regional/later suffering setting of 1 Peter 1:1, 4:12–16.
  **对应经文**：彼得前书 1:1、4:12–16 的地域与受苦后景。
- **Supports:** Christians across social ranks in towns/countryside, singing to Christ as a god, sharing ethical commitments, and facing interrogation for refusing sacrifice or to curse Christ.
  **能支持**：本都—比提尼亚城乡已有跨阶层基督徒；他们向基督唱诗如向神、共同守伦理承诺，并因拒绝祭祀和咒诅基督面临审讯。
- **Does not establish:** Later than most proposed 1 Peter dates, so no proof that it was written in 112 or concerned the same people. Trajan rejects active searching and anonymous accusations: local hearings, not an emperor's empire-wide persecution decree.
  **不能支持**：书信比彼得前书多数候选日期晚，不能证明彼前写于 112 年或两者为同一批人。图拉真要求不主动搜捕并拒绝匿名控告，材料显示地方审理在运作，却不是皇帝发动全国统一大迫害的诏令。
- **Grade:** A for documents/early-second-century setting; B for 1 Peter's later regional background.
  **等级**：A（文献与二世纪初处境）；B（彼前稍后地域背景）。
- **Sources:** [Perseus: Pliny, Letters 10.96](https://www.perseus.tufts.edu/hopper/text?doc=Perseus%3Atext%3A1999.02.0139%3Abook%3D10%3Aletter%3D96); [Dickinson College: Latin text and notes on 10.96](https://dcc.dickinson.edu/pliny-letters/10-96); [Fordham University: English translation of 10.96–97](https://sourcebooks.web.fordham.edu/source/pliny1.asp)
  **来源**：[Perseus：Pliny, Letters 10.96](https://www.perseus.tufts.edu/hopper/text?doc=Perseus%3Atext%3A1999.02.0139%3Abook%3D10%3Aletter%3D96)；[Dickinson College：10.96 拉丁文与注释](https://dcc.dickinson.edu/pliny-letters/10-96)；[Fordham University：10.96–97 英译](https://sourcebooks.web.fordham.edu/source/pliny1.asp)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-064"></a>

### 64. P72: Early Collection Witness to 1–2 Peter and Jude | P72：彼得前后书与犹大书的早期合订见证

- **Date:** Third–fourth centuries CE.
  **年代**：主后 3–4 世纪。
- **Material / collection:** Greek papyrus codex, Gregory–Aland P72, Bodmer VII–VIII. Petrine Pap.Bodmer.VIII is at Vatican Apostolic Library; related portions at Fondation Martin Bodmer.
  **材质／馆藏**：希腊文纸草 codex；Gregory–Aland P72，Bodmer VII–VIII。彼得书信部分 Pap.Bodmer.VIII 藏梵蒂冈宗座图书馆，相关部分亦藏 Fondation Martin Bodmer。
- **Related Scripture:** 1 Peter, 2 Peter, Jude.
  **对应经文**：彼得前书、彼得后书、犹大书。
- **Supports:** These books copied within a papyrus collection preserving important early text; other Christian works also share the collection.
  **能支持**：三书已被抄在纸草 codex 文集中，保存重要早期文本；合订本也包含其他基督教作品。
- **Does not establish:** Not autographs or a decision on 2 Peter/Jude's priority, authorship, or initial date. Binding works together does not give them equal canonical status.
  **不能支持**：不是作者亲笔，不能决定彼后与犹大的先后、作者或原作日期；与其他作品合订不等于全部具有同一正典地位。
- **Grade:** A for manuscript/reception.
  **等级**：A（抄本和接收史）。
- **Sources:** [CSNTM: P72](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/view/GA_P72); [Vatican Apostolic Library: Pap.Bodmer.VIII digital images](https://digi.vatlib.it/view/MSS_Pap.Bodmer.VIII); [Cambridge New Testament Studies: P72 and the Bodmer Miscellaneous Codex](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/new-testament-studies/article/papyrus-72-and-the-bodmer-miscellaneous-codex/BE92FCBAEAFA92E43C3EFCBF555BE1CE)
  **来源**：[CSNTM：P72](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/view/GA_P72)；[梵蒂冈宗座图书馆：Pap.Bodmer.VIII 数字图像](https://digi.vatlib.it/view/MSS_Pap.Bodmer.VIII)；[Cambridge *New Testament Studies*：P72 与 Bodmer Miscellaneous Codex](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/new-testament-studies/article/papyrus-72-and-the-bodmer-miscellaneous-codex/BE92FCBAEAFA92E43C3EFCBF555BE1CE)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-065"></a>

### 65. Patmos Cave and Monastery: Pilgrimage Memory, Not Excavated Proof of Visions | 拔摩洞穴与修道院：朝圣记忆，不是异象出土证明

- **Date:** Existing monastery principally from 1088 CE; the cave tradition becomes a sustained medieval pilgrimage center.
  **年代**：现存修道院主要始于主后 1088 年；洞穴传统在中世纪形成持续朝圣中心。
- **Related Scripture:** Revelation 1:9.
  **对应经文**：启示录 1:9。
- **Supports:** A long Patmian commemoration of John/Revelation, with real millennial Christian tradition and architectural heritage.
  **能支持**：拔摩长期纪念约翰与启示录，形成真实的千年基督教传统和建筑遗产。
- **Does not establish:** Existing buildings/cave arrangements cannot identify a first-century vision's precise location or establish Revelation's author as the Fourth Gospel's author.
  **不能支持**：现存建筑和洞穴布置不能确认一世纪异象的具体位置，也不能证明启示录作者就是第四福音作者。
- **Grade:** A for tradition/architecture; D as direct first-century evidence.
  **等级**：A（传统与建筑史）；D（作为一世纪直接证据）。
- **Sources:** [UNESCO: Patmos](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/942/)
  **来源**：[UNESCO：Patmos](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/942/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-066"></a>

### 66. Smyrna Agora: Public Space in a Roman Port | 士每拿广场：罗马港城的公共空间

- **Date:** Roman period and subsequent rebuilding.
  **年代**：罗马时期及后续重建阶段。
- **Related Scripture:** Revelation 2:8–11.
  **对应经文**：启示录 2:8–11。
- **Supports:** A real port city with agora, trade, public religion, and civic honor systems.
  **能支持**：士每拿是具有广场、贸易、公共宗教和城市荣誉体系的真实罗马港城。
- **Does not establish:** No equation of ten days with ten years or exclusive derivation of life's crown from one local custom.
  **不能支持**：遗址不能把“十日”锁成十年或确认“生命冠冕”只来自某项本地习俗。
- **Grade:** A for city; C for specific symbolism.
  **等级**：A（城市）；C（具体象征来源）。
- **Sources:** [Turkish Ministry of Culture and Tourism: Smyrna Agora Archaeological Site](https://muze.gov.tr/muze-detay?DistId=MRK&SectionId=AGR01); [Izmir Culture and Tourism Directorate: Smyrna excavations](https://izmir.ktb.gov.tr/TR-77163/smyrna-bayrakli-hoyugu-kazisi--izmir.html)
  **来源**：[土耳其文化与旅游部：Smyrna Agora Archaeological Site](https://muze.gov.tr/muze-detay?DistId=MRK&SectionId=AGR01)；[伊兹密尔文化与旅游局：Smyrna 发掘](https://izmir.ktb.gov.tr/TR-77163/smyrna-bayrakli-hoyugu-kazisi--izmir.html)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-067"></a>

### 67. Pergamon Acropolis and Sanctuaries: Multiple Settings for Satan's Throne | 别迦摩卫城与圣所：“撒但座位”的多重候选背景

- **Date:** Hellenistic–Roman.
  **年代**：希腊化至罗马时期。
- **Related Scripture:** Revelation 2:12–17.
  **对应经文**：启示录 2:12–17。
- **Supports:** Monumental altars, temples, Asclepieion, Roman administration, and imperial worship, supplying strong background for religious/political power imagery.
  **能支持**：别迦摩集中宏大祭坛、神庙、亚斯克勒比翁、罗马行政和帝国崇拜，为政治—宗教权势图像提供强背景。
- **Does not establish:** Archaeology cannot uniquely identify Satan's throne as the Zeus altar, healing sanctuary, or one imperial temple.
  **不能支持**：“撒但座位”不能被考古唯一等同宙斯祭坛、医治圣所或某一座帝国神庙。
- **Grade:** A for city/sanctuaries; C for one symbolic identification.
  **等级**：A（城市与圣所）；C（单一象征认定）。
- **Sources:** [UNESCO: Pergamon](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1457); [German Archaeological Institute: Pergamon and imperial worship](https://publications.dainst.org/journals/istmitt/article/view/4701)
  **来源**：[UNESCO：Pergamon](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1457)；[德国考古研究院：Pergamon 与帝国崇拜](https://publications.dainst.org/journals/istmitt/article/view/4701)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-068"></a>

### 68. Thyatira: Textiles, Roads, and Commerce | 推雅推喇遗址：纺织、交通与商业背景

- **Date:** Roman remains chiefly colonnaded street, buildings, and coins.
  **年代**：罗马时期可见遗存主要包括柱廊街、建筑和钱币。
- **Related Scripture:** Revelation 2:18–29; Acts 16:14.
  **对应经文**：启示录 2:18–29；使徒行传 16:14。
- **Supports:** Thyatira at Lydia–Mysia road junctions, with inscriptions/excavation attesting weaving, dyeing, pottery, leather associations, and flourishing Roman trade.
  **能支持**：推雅推喇位于吕底亚—每西亚道路交汇处；铭文和发掘资料显示织造、染色、制陶、皮革等行业组织与罗马时期贸易繁荣。
- **Does not establish:** No identified teacher Jezebel or her guild; existing later buildings are not first-century meeting places.
  **不能支持**：没有找到名为“耶洗别”的教师或她所属行会；现存后期建筑也不是一世纪聚会处。
- **Grade:** A/B.
  **等级**：A/B。
- **Sources:** [Turkish culture/tourism authority: Thyateira](https://manisa.ktb.gov.tr/TR-73011/tepe-mezarligi-oren-yeri--akhisar.html); [Dokuz Eylül University Archaeology: Thyateira and Hastane Mound excavations](https://arkeoloji.deu.edu.tr/?page_id=163&lang=en)
  **来源**：[土耳其文化与旅游部门：Thyateira](https://manisa.ktb.gov.tr/TR-73011/tepe-mezarligi-oren-yeri--akhisar.html)；[Dokuz Eylül University 考古系：Thyateira 与 Hastane Mound 发掘](https://arkeoloji.deu.edu.tr/?page_id=163&lang=en)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-069"></a>

### 69. Sardis: Wealth, Imperial Worship, and a Later Synagogue | 撒狄遗址：财富记忆、帝国崇拜与较晚会堂

- **Date:** Lydian, Roman, and late-antique layers; the large synagogue chiefly fourth–fifth centuries CE.
  **年代**：吕底亚至罗马、晚期古代多层遗存；大型会堂主要为主后 4–5 世纪。
- **Related Scripture:** Revelation 3:1–6.
  **对应经文**：启示录 3:1–6。
- **Supports:** A city renowned for wealth, acropolis, and coinage, with Roman public buildings/imperial worship and a significant later Jewish community.
  **能支持**：撒狄曾以财富、卫城和铸币著名，罗马时期有公共建筑与帝国崇拜；后期仍有重要犹太群体。
- **Does not establish:** The later synagogue is not Revelation's conflict scene. White clothing and thief imagery cannot be explained solely by local industries or capture stories.
  **不能支持**：后期会堂不能当作启示录收信时冲突现场；白衣和“如贼”也不能只靠本地产业／城陷故事解释。
- **Grade:** A for city history; C for specific symbols.
  **等级**：A（城市史）；C（具体象征）。
- **Sources:** [UNESCO: Sardis](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1731/); [Harvard/Cornell Sardis Expedition: city overview](https://sardisexpedition.org/en/essays/about-sardis); [Same excavation project: synagogue phasing](https://www.sardisexpedition.org/en/essays/about-synagogue)
  **来源**：[UNESCO：Sardis](https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1731/)；[Harvard/Cornell Sardis Expedition：城市概览](https://sardisexpedition.org/en/essays/about-sardis)；[同发掘项目：会堂分期](https://www.sardisexpedition.org/en/essays/about-synagogue)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-070"></a>

### 70. Philadelphia: Earthquakes and a Later St. John Church | 非拉铁非遗址：地震城市与后期圣约翰教堂

- **Date:** Roman city; prominent visible church remains sixth century CE.
  **年代**：罗马城市；今日显著教堂遗存为主后 6 世纪。
- **Related Scripture:** Revelation 3:7–13.
  **对应经文**：启示录 3:7–13。
- **Supports:** Long occupation in an earthquake region and a later important Christian center.
  **能支持**：城市处于地震区并长期有人居住，后期成为重要基督教中心。
- **Does not establish:** The extant church postdates Revelation by roughly 500 years; one site cannot fully explain pillars, open door, and new name.
  **不能支持**：现存教堂晚于启示录约五百年；柱子、敞开之门和新名不能被一座遗址完全解释。
- **Grade:** A for site dating; D as a first-century recipient building.
  **等级**：A（遗址年代）；D（认作一世纪收信建筑）。
- **Sources:** [Turkish Ministry of Culture and Tourism: Alaşehir / Philadelphia Archaeological Site](https://muze.gov.tr/muze-detay?distId=MRK&sectionId=ALS01); [Turkish cultural heritage authority: St. Jean Archaeological Site](https://kvmgm.ktb.gov.tr/Eklenti/91480%2Cmanisa-alasehir-archaeological-sitepdf.pdf?0=)
  **来源**：[土耳其文化与旅游部：Alaşehir / Philadelphia Archaeological Site](https://muze.gov.tr/muze-detay?distId=MRK&sectionId=ALS01)；[土耳其文化遗产部门：St. Jean Archaeological Site](https://kvmgm.ktb.gov.tr/Eklenti/91480%2Cmanisa-alasehir-archaeological-sitepdf.pdf?0=)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-071"></a>

### 71. Laodicea and Water: Wealth and Lukewarm Imagery | 老底嘉城市与供水：富足和“不冷不热”的背景

- **Date:** Roman urban peak; later churches from the fourth century CE.
  **年代**：罗马时期为城市发展高峰；后期教堂为主后 4 世纪以后。
- **Related Scripture:** Revelation 3:14–22; Colossians 4:13–16.
  **对应经文**：启示录 3:14–22；歌罗西书 4:13–16。
- **Supports:** Commerce, textiles, earthquake destruction/rebuilding, pipes from springs about eight kilometers south, and two major distribution terminals; real urban setting for wealth, clothing, and self-sufficiency language.
  **能支持**：老底嘉具有商业、纺织、地震毁坏与重建，以及从城南约八公里外水源引水的管道和两座大型配水终端；富足、衣服与自足语言可放在真实城市环境中阅读。
- **Does not establish:** The popular Hierapolis-hot/Colossae-cold/Laodicea-lukewarm three-city supply diagram lacks matching pipeline evidence. A 2023 hydrological/archaeological reassessment further argues temperature imagery cannot be derived from Laodicea's water system. The text first interprets it as a community claiming wealth while actually poor, blind, and naked.
  **不能支持**：流行的“希拉波立热水—歌罗西冷水—老底嘉温水”三城输水图没有相应管线证据。2023 年水文—考古复核进一步主张温度比喻不能由老底嘉供水系统推出；比喻首先由经文解释为群体自认富足、实际贫穷、瞎眼、赤身。
- **Grade:** A for city/water infrastructure; C for deriving the temperature metaphor specifically from local supply.
  **等级**：A（城市与供水设施）；C（把温度比喻具体还原为当地供水）。
- **Sources:** [UNESCO: Archaeological Site of Laodikeia](https://whc.unesco.org/en/tentativelists/5823/); [Mark Wilson: Laodicean hydrology and temperature-metaphor reassessment](https://doi.org/10.54577/lycus.1381310); [Cambridge New Testament Studies: debate on Laodicea's local context](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/new-testament-studies/article/abs/message-to-laodicea-and-the-problem-of-its-local-context-a-study-of-the-imagery-in-rev-31422/E67AB80ED60928E5A9D45F70D5F12EB2)
  **来源**：[UNESCO：Archaeological Site of Laodikeia](https://whc.unesco.org/en/tentativelists/5823/)；[Mark Wilson：老底嘉水文与温度比喻复核](https://doi.org/10.54577/lycus.1381310)；[Cambridge *New Testament Studies*：老底嘉信息的地方背景争论](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/new-testament-studies/article/abs/message-to-laodicea-and-the-problem-of-its-local-context-a-study-of-the-imagery-in-rev-31422/E67AB80ED60928E5A9D45F70D5F12EB2)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-072"></a>

### 72. P115 and 616: an Early Beast-Number Variant | P115 与 616：启示录兽数的早期异读

- **Date:** Late third–early fourth century CE.
  **年代**：主后 3 世纪末至 4 世纪初。
- **Material / collection:** Twenty-six Greek papyrus codex fragments; P.Oxy. 4499, Oxford Sackler Library.
  **材质／馆藏**：希腊文纸草 codex 的 26 块残片；P.Oxy. 4499，现藏牛津 Sackler Library。
- **Related Scripture:** Revelation 13:18.
  **对应经文**：启示录 13:18。
- **Supports:** Fragments across Revelation 2–15, including 616. Irenaeus already knew this variant in the late second century; it did not arise after modern discovery.
  **能支持**：P.Oxy. 4499 保存启示录 2–15 章的多处片段，并在兽数处见证 616；爱任纽在主后 2 世纪末也已经知道这一异读，说明它不是现代发现后才产生。
- **Does not establish:** One manuscript cannot establish 616 as original or uniquely identify the beast. 666 has broader manuscript support. 616 often figures in differing Nero/Neron Caesar spelling models, but the variant itself does not reveal the scribe's reason.
  **不能支持**：不能单凭一份抄本断定原文一定是 616，也不能把兽数唯一锁定一人；666 具有更广的抄本支持。616 常被纳入 “Nero / Neron Caesar” 不同拼法模型，但异文本身没有说明抄写员为何采用该数字。
- **Grade:** A for manuscript variant; B/C for historical decoding.
  **等级**：A（抄本异读）；B/C（历史解码）。
- **Sources:** [CSNTM: P115](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/View/GA_P115); [Cambridge *New Testament Studies*: P115 / P.Oxy. 4499](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/new-testament-studies/article/new-oxyrhynchus-papyrus-of-revelation-p115-p-oxy-4499/3CDD00D19F8FB101DC33C39E80A7156B); [Cambridge Journal of Ecclesiastical History: early 616 witnesses and reception](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/journal-of-ecclesiastical-history/article/abs/apocalyptic-number-616-and-the-donatist-church/0775DE1C87F329E3270E5A34AF356069)
  **来源**：[CSNTM：P115](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/View/GA_P115)；[Cambridge *New Testament Studies*：P115 / P.Oxy. 4499](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/new-testament-studies/article/new-oxyrhynchus-papyrus-of-revelation-p115-p-oxy-4499/3CDD00D19F8FB101DC33C39E80A7156B)；[Cambridge *Journal of Ecclesiastical History*：616 的早期见证与接收史](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/journal-of-ecclesiastical-history/article/abs/apocalyptic-number-616-and-the-donatist-church/0775DE1C87F329E3270E5A34AF356069)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="batch-7"></a>

## Batch 7: Research Updates That Change Earlier Site Identifications | 第七批维护更新：新研究改变旧遗址认定

<a id="evidence-073"></a>

### 73. Tel Dan Sacred Precinct Rephased: Not Every Monument Is Iron Age | 特尔但祭祀区重新分期：不是所有宏大遗构都属铁器时代

- **Date:** Cult activity from Iron Age to Roman periods; revised stratigraphic study published in 2025.
  **年代**：遗址包含铁器时代至罗马时期的多层祭祀活动；重新分层研究发表于 2025 年。
- **Related Scripture:** 1 Kings 12:25–33; 2 Kings 10:29; Amos 7:10–17.
  **对应经文**：列王纪上 12:25–33；列王纪下 10:29；阿摩司书 7:10–17。
- **Supports:** A long cult tradition at Tel Dan, situating an Iron Age northern sanctuary in a real place/regional religious network.
  **能支持**：特尔但确有长期祭祀传统；铁器时代北方圣所处在真实地点与区域宗教网络中。
- **Revision details:** The 2025 study argues from stratigraphy, architecture, and material culture that many large constructions previously assigned to the tenth–eighth centuries BCE belong instead to Persian, Hellenistic, or Roman phases.
  **修订内容**：2025 年研究根据分层关系、建筑和物质文化，主张过去被归给主前 10–8 世纪的不少宏大构筑应改归波斯、希腊化或罗马时期。
- **Does not establish:** Visible monumental platforms cannot directly be labeled Jeroboam's altar; the revised phasing does not conversely disprove historical grounding for 1 Kings 12.
  **不能支持**：不能把今天可见的宏大台基直接称为“耶罗波安祭坛”；新分期也不能反向证明王上 12 章没有历史基础。
- **Grade:** A/B for place, long cult history, and rephasing research; C for assigning one existing building to Jeroboam.
  **等级**：A／B（地点、长期祭祀与重新分层研究）；C（把现存某一建筑署名给耶罗波安）。
- **Sources:** [BASOR／ASOR: The Sacred Precinct of Tel Dan Revisited](https://www.journals.uchicago.edu/doi/10.1086/734979)
  **来源**：[BASOR／ASOR：The Sacred Precinct of Tel Dan Revisited](https://www.journals.uchicago.edu/doi/10.1086/734979)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="batch-8"></a>

## Batch 8: Genesis, the Patriarchs, and the Ancient Near East | 第八批扩展锚点：创世—列祖与古代近东背景

> This group chiefly attests textual, visual, legal-cultural, and migration backgrounds. Unless an entry says otherwise, these are not direct external evidence for Genesis persons or events.<br>
> 本组材料主要确认文本、图像、法律文化和人口流动背景。除非条目另有说明，它们不是创世记人物或事件的直接外证。

<a id="evidence-074"></a>

### 74. Enuma Elish Fragment: Babylonian Creation Tradition | 《埃努玛·埃利什》残片：巴比伦创造传统

- **Date / collection:** British Museum BM 47292 (1881,0830.814), a Late Babylonian fragment of Tablet I. Formation/copying predates this extant copy; precise composition remains disputed.
  **年代／馆藏**：大英博物馆 BM 47292（1881,0830.814），晚期巴比伦泥版残片，内容属于《埃努玛·埃利什》第一块泥版（Tablet I）；作品形成和传抄历史早于该现存抄本，精确成书年代仍有讨论。
- **Related Scripture:** Genesis 1–2.
  **对应经文**：创世记 1–2 章。
- **Supports:** Mesopotamian creation traditions involving primordial waters, cosmic order, gods, and human origins; a setting for comparing Genesis.
  **能支持**：美索不达米亚确有以原初水域、宇宙秩序、神祇和人类起源为主题的创造传统；创世记可放在这一古代思想世界中比较。
- **Does not establish:** Shared themes do not establish Genesis copying this fragment or test divine creation's revelatory claim. Divine genealogy, combat, and human service purposes differ substantially from Genesis.
  **不能支持**：共同主题不能证明创世记直接抄写这件现存残片，也不能用泥版检验神创造的启示宣告。《埃努玛·埃利什》的神祇谱系、神战和人类服役目的与创世记有关键差异。
- **Grade:** A for tablet/text; B for literary-cultural comparison.
  **等级**：A（泥版与文本见证）；B（与创世记的文学—文化比较）。
- **Sources:** [British Museum: BM 47292](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1881-0830-814); [CDLI: a Neo-Babylonian Enuma Elish Tablet I witness](https://cdli.earth/P479346)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆：BM 47292](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1881-0830-814)；[CDLI：一件新巴比伦时期《埃努玛·埃利什》第一泥版见证](https://cdli.earth/P479346)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-075"></a>

### 75. Atrahasis Flood Tablet: an Earlier Babylonian Flood Text | 《阿特拉哈西斯》洪水泥版：较早的巴比伦洪水文本

- **Date / collection:** Around 1635 BCE, Old Babylonian; British Museum BM 78943 (1889,0426.236), from Sippar, the upper portion of one of an original three-tablet set preserving the flood.
  **年代／馆藏**：约主前 1635 年，古巴比伦时期；大英博物馆 BM 78943（1889,0426.236），出自西帕尔，是原三块泥版组中保存洪水故事的一块上半部。
- **Related Scripture:** Genesis 6–9.
  **对应经文**：创世记 6–9 章。
- **Supports:** Early-second-millennium Babylonian transmission of flood, warned survivor, boat, and preservation of family/animals.
  **能支持**：第二千纪前期的巴比伦已经传抄洪水、被警告的幸存者、船只、家人和动物得保存等叙事。
- **Does not establish:** No date, extent, or geological layer for Noah's flood, or proof of Genesis copying this tablet. Atrahasis connects disaster with human noise/divine conflict; Genesis with violence, corruption, and covenant.
  **不能支持**：它不能决定挪亚洪水的日期、范围或地质层，也不能证明创世记直接复制这件泥版。该作把灾难与人口噪音及众神冲突相连，创世记则把审判与人类暴力、败坏和圣约相连。
- **Grade:** A for tablet/date/flood text; B for literary background.
  **等级**：A（泥版、年代与洪水文本）；B（文学背景关系）。
- **Sources:** [British Museum: BM 78943](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1889-0426-236)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆：BM 78943](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1889-0426-236)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-076"></a>

### 76. Gilgamesh Flood Tablet: Tablet XI from Ashurbanipal's Library | 《吉尔伽美什史诗》洪水泥版：亚述巴尼拔图书馆的第十一泥版

- **Date / collection:** Seventh century BCE, Neo-Assyrian; British Museum K.3375, excavated at Kuyunjik/Nineveh, Ashurbanipal's library.
  **年代／馆藏**：主前 7 世纪，新亚述时期；大英博物馆 K.3375，出土于尼尼微库云吉克，属亚述巴尼拔图书馆。
- **Related Scripture:** Genesis 6–9.
  **对应经文**：创世记 6–9 章。
- **Supports:** Boat, flood, landing, birds, and sacrifice structures support literary comparison with Genesis, also showing long copying/rewriting of flood tradition.
  **能支持**：第十一泥版保存船、洪水、停泊、放鸟和献祭等结构，与创世记洪水叙事可作重要文学比较；它也显示洪水传统经过长期抄传和改写。
- **Does not establish:** Extant K.3375 postdates the Atrahasis copy and is not the origin of every flood tradition. Similar sequence alone establishes neither a global flood nor Genesis' direct copying of this seventh-century object.
  **不能支持**：现存 K.3375 比《阿特拉哈西斯》抄本晚，不能被写成所有洪水传统的起点；相似顺序既不能单独证明全球洪水，也不能证明创世记由该件七世纪抄本直接抄成。
- **Grade:** A for tablet/date/text; B for literary background.
  **等级**：A（泥版、年代与文本）；B（文学背景关系）。
- **Sources:** [British Museum: Flood Tablet K.3375](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_K-3375)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆：Flood Tablet K.3375](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_K-3375)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-077"></a>

### 77. Nebuchadnezzar II's Ziggurat Cylinder: Babylon and Borsippa | 尼布甲尼撒二世神塔圆柱：巴比伦与波尔西帕的阶梯神塔

- **Date / collection:** 604–562 BCE; British Museum BM 91142 (1885,0430.1), baked-clay cylinder from Babylon.
  **年代／馆藏**：主前 604–562 年；大英博物馆 BM 91142（1885,0430.1），出自巴比伦的烧制泥圆柱。
- **Related Scripture:** Genesis 11:1–9.
  **对应经文**：创世记 11:1–9。
- **Supports:** Nebuchadnezzar's restoration of temples/ziggurats at Babylon and Borsippa, establishing Mesopotamian brick cities, royal works, and ziggurat traditions.
  **能支持**：铭文记载尼布甲尼撒二世修复巴比伦和波尔西帕的神庙与阶梯神塔，确认美索不达米亚砖造城市、王权工程和神塔传统真实存在。
- **Does not establish:** Later than Genesis' Babel story-world; no direct identification of one ziggurat as the original Tower of Babel or explanation of language dispersion's theological claim.
  **不能支持**：这件圆柱晚于创世记所叙的巴别事件世界，不能把其中某座神塔直接认定为“原巴别塔”；它也不能解释语言分散的神学宣告。
- **Grade:** A for cylinder/king/works; B for Babel's architectural-imperial setting; C for a particular tower.
  **等级**：A（圆柱、君王和工程记载）；B（巴别的建筑—帝国背景）；具体塔址认定为 C。
- **Sources:** [British Museum: BM 91142](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1885-0430-1)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆：BM 91142](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/W_1885-0430-1)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-078"></a>

### 78. Beni Hasan's West Asian Procession: Ancient Painting and a 1931 Facsimile | 贝尼哈桑西亚人队伍：古代墓画与 1931 年临摹

- **Date / carrier:** Original in Khnumhotep II's Beni Hasan tomb BH 3, Twelfth Dynasty, Senwosret II, around 1887–1878 BCE. Metropolitan Museum 33.8.17 is Norman de Garis Davies' life-size paper facsimile made in the tomb in 1931.
  **年代／载体**：原画位于贝尼哈桑赫努姆霍特普二世墓（BH 3），第十二王朝森沃斯雷特二世时期，约主前 1887–1878 年；大都会艺术博物馆 33.8.17 是 Norman de Garis Davies 于 1931 年在墓室制作的等比例纸本临摹。
- **Related Scripture:** Genesis 12:10–20; 37–50.
  **对应经文**：创世记 12:10–20；37–50 章。
- **Supports:** Egyptian art depicts West Asians with distinct dress/hairstyles. Trade, migration, and other mobility make Bronze Age West Asian arrivals historically understandable.
  **能支持**：埃及艺术确实描绘来自西亚、具有不同服饰和发式的人群；贸易、迁徙或其他人口流动使西亚人进入埃及在青铜时代具有历史可理解性。
- **Does not establish:** The Met object is not the ancient original; no inscription identifies Abraham, Jacob, or Joseph's family. The scene may also express Egyptian symbolism concerning outsiders/order.
  **不能支持**：大都会藏品不是古代墓画原件；画中人也没有被铭文认作亚伯拉罕、雅各或约瑟家族。场景还可能包含埃及人关于外族和秩序的象征表达。
- **Grade:** A for facsimile provenance/original location/date; B for patriarchal Egyptian setting; D for personal identification.
  **等级**：A（临摹来源、原画位置和年代）；B（列祖下埃及的社会背景）；D（人物等同）。
- **Sources:** [Metropolitan Museum of Art: Leaders of the Aamu of Shu, 33.8.17](https://www.metmuseum.org/art/collection/search/544548); [Metropolitan Museum of Art: explanatory publication](https://www.metmuseum.org/es/-/media/files/about-the-met/curatorial-departments/egyptian/facsimiles/2016-19_walklikeanegyptian_web.pdf)
  **来源**：[大都会艺术博物馆：Leaders of the Aamu of Shu, 33.8.17](https://www.metmuseum.org/art/collection/search/544548)；[大都会专题说明](https://www.metmuseum.org/es/-/media/files/about-the-met/curatorial-departments/egyptian/facsimiles/2016-19_walklikeanegyptian_web.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-079"></a>

### 79. Brooklyn Papyrus 35.1446: West Asian Servants in an Egyptian Household | 布鲁克林纸草 35.1446：埃及家庭中的西亚仆役

- **Date / collection:** Around 1809–1743 BCE, late Twelfth–Thirteenth Dynasties; Brooklyn Museum 35.1446a–e, ink papyrus.
  **年代／馆藏**：约主前 1809–1743 年，第十二王朝末至第十三王朝；布鲁克林博物馆 35.1446a–e，纸草、墨书。
- **Related Scripture:** Genesis 37–50; earlier social background to Exodus 1.
  **对应经文**：创世记 37–50 章；出埃及记 1 章的较早社会背景。
- **Supports:** A Theban noblewoman's ownership of 95 household servants, including 45 West Asian names: Northwest Semitic people/names in early-second-millennium Egypt.
  **能支持**：文书涉及一名底比斯贵族妇女对 95 名家庭仆役的所有权，其中 45 人的名字显示西亚来源；西北闪语人群及姓名在第二千纪前期埃及确实存在。
- **Does not establish:** Not automatically Israelites; `Šp-ra` cannot be identified as Exodus' Shiphrah. Earlier than most Exodus date models and names neither Joseph nor Moses.
  **不能支持**：这些人不能自动称为以色列人；名单中的 `Šp-ra` 不能确认为出埃及记的施弗拉。文书也早于多数出埃及年代方案，不提约瑟或摩西。
- **Grade:** A for document/servant counts/West Asian names; B for population setting; D for biblical identification.
  **等级**：A（文书、仆役人数与西亚姓名）；B（人口背景）；D（圣经人物等同）。
- **Sources:** [Brooklyn Museum: Portion of a Historical Text, 35.1446](https://www.brooklynmuseum.org/objects/3369)
  **来源**：[布鲁克林博物馆：Portion of a Historical Text, 35.1446](https://www.brooklynmuseum.org/objects/3369)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-080"></a>

### 80. Hammurabi's Law Stele: the Legal World of Patriarchs and Sinai | 汉谟拉比法律碑：列祖与西奈所处的古代法律世界

- **Date / collection:** Around 1750 BCE; Louvre SB 8 / AS 6064, black basalt, about 2.25 meters high, excavated at Susa in 1901–1902.
  **年代／馆藏**：约主前 1750 年；卢浮宫 SB 8／AS 6064，黑色玄武岩碑，高约 2.25 米，1901–1902 年在苏萨出土。
- **Related Scripture:** Genesis' patriarchal family, property, inheritance, and servants; Exodus 21–23; Deuteronomic laws.
  **对应经文**：创世记列祖叙事中的家庭、财产、继承与仆役；出埃及记 21–23 章；申命记法律。
- **Supports:** Developed early-second-millennium Mesopotamian case-law traditions on family, property, commerce, labor, and injury. If–then formulation belongs to a real ancient legal world.
  **能支持**：第二千纪前期美索不达米亚已有成熟的判例传统，处理家庭、财产、商业、劳动和伤害；“若……则……”的案例法形式属于真实古代法律环境。
- **Does not establish:** The Louvre's code is not a complete modern statutory system. Shared legal subjects establish neither direct Mosaic copying nor precise dating of patriarchs/Pentateuch from one custom.
  **不能支持**：卢浮宫所称的“法典”不是现代意义上的完整成文法系统；相似法题不能证明摩西律法直接抄自此碑，也不能用一条相似习俗给列祖或摩西五经精确定年。
- **Grade:** A for stele/date/legal text; B for broad legal culture; C for direct dependence/precise dating.
  **等级**：A（碑、年代和法律文本）；B（广义法律文化背景）；直接依赖或精确定年为 C。
- **Sources:** [Louvre: The Code of Hammurabi](https://www.louvre.fr/en/the-code-of-hammurabi); [Louvre collection: SB 8](https://collections.louvre.fr/en/ark%3A/53355/cl010174436)
  **来源**：[卢浮宫：The Code of Hammurabi](https://www.louvre.fr/en/the-code-of-hammurabi)；[卢浮宫藏品页：SB 8](https://collections.louvre.fr/en/ark%3A/53355/cl010174436)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="batch-9"></a>

## Batch 9: Exodus, Sinai, and the Wilderness | 第九批扩展锚点：出埃及—西奈—旷野背景

> This group separates cities, labor images, treaties, protective silver plaques, and divine-name/seer traditions. They reconstruct cultural settings, not an archaeologically proven Exodus route.<br>
> 本组把城市、劳动图像、条约、护身银片与神名／先见传统分开处理。它们能重建经文所在的文化世界，却不能拼成一条已经由考古直接证实的出埃及路线。

<a id="evidence-081"></a>

### 81. Qantir–Pi-Ramesse: Ramesside Capital and Industrial Center | 坎提尔—皮兰塞：拉美西斯王城与工业中心

- **Date / place:** New Kingdom Ramesside Egypt, especially thirteenth century BCE; Qantir area, eastern Nile Delta.
  **年代／地点**：埃及新王国拉美西斯时期，重点为主前 13 世纪；遗址位于尼罗河三角洲东部坎提尔一带。
- **Related Scripture:** Exodus 1:11's store-city Rameses.
  **对应经文**：出埃及记 1:11 的“兰塞”积货城。
- **Supports:** A real Ramesside capital, with bronze casting, glass production, palace/temple buildings, and major stables: royal construction and industry. Rameses is not a later invented name.
  **能支持**：皮兰塞是拉美西斯王朝真实存在的首都；考古项目发现青铜铸造、玻璃生产、宫殿／神庙建筑以及大型马厩等设施，显示这是一座王室工程与工业中心。“兰塞”并非后世凭空虚构的地名。
- **Does not establish:** No Israel, Moses, or plagues. A city name alone cannot identify the Exodus pharaoh/date; a later copyist may update an older geographic tradition to a current name.
  **不能支持**：遗址没有点名以色列、摩西或十灾；单凭城名不能确定出埃及法老或年代。地名也可能是较晚抄写者用当时通行名称更新较早地理传统。
- **Grade:** A for city/date/industry; B for Exodus 1:11's name/construction setting; C for fixing Exodus date/pharaoh.
  **等级**：A（城市、年代与工业设施）；B（出 1:11 的地名和工程背景）；C（由此确定出埃及年代或法老）。
- **Sources:** [International Association of Egyptologists: Qantir–Pi-Ramesse Project](https://www.iae-egyptology.org/archives/696); [University College London: Qantir–Piramesses glass industry](https://www.ucl.ac.uk/news/2005/jun/mystery-egyptian-glass-making-becomes-clear); [UCL Discovery: Late Bronze glass-production study](https://discovery.ucl.ac.uk/id/eprint/93697/)
  **来源**：[国际埃及学家协会：Qantir–Pi-Ramesse Project](https://www.iae-egyptology.org/archives/696)；[伦敦大学学院：Qantir–Piramesses 玻璃工业](https://www.ucl.ac.uk/news/2005/jun/mystery-egyptian-glass-making-becomes-clear)；[UCL Discovery：晚青铜时代玻璃生产研究](https://discovery.ucl.ac.uk/id/eprint/93697/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-082"></a>

### 82. Rekhmire Tomb Brickmakers: Ancient Painting and a 1930 Paper Facsimile | 雷克米拉墓制砖图：古代原画与 1930 年纸本摹本

- **Date / carrier:** Original Theban TT 100, Eighteenth Dynasty, Thutmose III–early Amenhotep II, around 1479–1425 BCE. Metropolitan Museum 30.4.77 is Nina de Garis Davies' full-size tempera-on-paper facsimile accessioned in 1930.
  **年代／载体**：原画位于底比斯雷克米拉墓 TT 100，十八王朝图特摩斯三世至阿蒙霍特普二世早期，约主前 1479–1425 年；大都会艺术博物馆 30.4.77 是 Nina de Garis Davies 制作、1930 年入藏的 1:1 纸本蛋彩摹本。
- **Related Scripture:** Exodus 5's mud, brickmaking, overseers, and construction labor.
  **对应经文**：出埃及记 5 章的和泥、制砖、监工与工程劳动。
- **Supports:** Large-scale New Kingdom mudbrick manufacture: original painting shows gathering mud, moving materials, molding bricks, and supervision, supplying real Egyptian labor context.
  **能支持**：新王国埃及确有成规模泥砖制造；原画呈现取泥、运料、塑砖及监督的劳动流程，使出 5 所述劳动类型具有真实埃及背景。
- **Does not establish:** Museum object is not an ancient wall painting; workers are not inscribed as Hebrews. A fifteenth-century picture alone cannot establish an early Exodus model.
  **不能支持**：博物馆藏品不是古代墙画原件；图中工人没有被铭文认作希伯来人。画面属于主前 15 世纪，也不能单凭它证明“早期出埃及”年代方案。
- **Grade:** A for original location/date/facsimile type/technology; B for Exodus 5 labor; D for identifying workers as Hebrews.
  **等级**：A（原画位置、年代、摹本性质和劳动技术）；B（出 5 的劳动背景）；D（工人等同希伯来人）。
- **Sources:** [Metropolitan Museum of Art: Brickmakers, Tomb of Rekhmire, 30.4.77](https://www.metmuseum.org/art/collection/search/544649)
  **来源**：[大都会艺术博物馆：Brickmakers, Tomb of Rekhmire，30.4.77](https://www.metmuseum.org/art/collection/search/544649)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-083"></a>

### 83. Esarhaddon's Tayinat Succession Treaty: Loyalty, Deposition, and Curses | 特尔泰纳特以撒哈顿继承条约：忠诚誓约、存放与咒诅

- **Date / carrier:** 672 BCE, Neo-Assyrian cuneiform tablet from Tell Tayinat Temple XVI; Hatay Archaeological Museum, Turkey; CDLI/RINAP P500551.
  **年代／载体**：主前 672 年，新亚述楔形文字泥版；出土于特尔泰纳特 Temple XVI，现藏土耳其哈塔伊考古博物馆；CDLI／RINAP 标识 P500551。
- **Related Scripture:** Exodus 19–24; Deuteronomy, especially 27–30's covenant, reading, loyalty, blessings/curses.
  **对应经文**：出埃及记 19–24 章；申命记，尤其 27–30 章的立约、宣读、忠诚与祝福／咒诅。
- **Supports:** Esarhaddon requires the governor/local officials of Kunalia to swear loyalty to crown prince Ashurbanipal. Extensive breach curses and deposition in a local temple physically attest first-millennium treaties' loyalty, public texts, and sanctions.
  **能支持**：以撒哈顿要求基纳利亚省长和地方官员宣誓效忠王储亚述巴尼拔；泥版包含漫长的违约咒诅，并被放置在地方神庙中。它为第一千纪政治条约怎样塑造忠诚、公开文本和制裁提供实物见证。
- **Does not establish:** Centuries later than Mosaic candidate dates; formal resemblance establishes neither direct Deuteronomic copying nor composition date. Broader comparison includes second-millennium Hittite treaties.
  **不能支持**：这件泥版比摩西传统候选年代晚数百年；形式相似不能证明申命记直接抄自该版条约，也不能单独确定申命记成书年代。申命记还需与第二千纪赫人条约等更广传统比较。
- **Grade:** A for tablet/672 BCE/findspot/oath; B for covenant genre; C for direct dependence/precise dating.
  **等级**：A（泥版、主前 672 年、出土地点与誓约内容）；B（圣约文体背景）；C（直接依赖或据此精确定年）。
- **Sources:** [University of Toronto: Tayinat Archaeological Project](https://tayinat.artsci.utoronto.ca/the-toronto-expedition/the-esarhaddon-succession-treaty/); [CDLI: Lauinger's 2012 edition and P500551](https://cdli.earth/publications/1769646)
  **来源**：[多伦多大学 Tayinat Archaeological Project](https://tayinat.artsci.utoronto.ca/the-toronto-expedition/the-esarhaddon-succession-treaty/)；[CDLI：Lauinger 2012 正式出版与 P500551](https://cdli.earth/publications/1769646)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-084"></a>

### 84. Ketef Hinnom Silver Amulets: Early Priestly-Blessing Wording | 基特夫欣嫩银护身片：祭司祝福的早期文本见证

- **Date / collection:** Usually late seventh–early sixth century BCE; KH 1/KH 2, IAA 1980-1495/1980-1496, Israel Museum. A minority proposes a later, early-return date.
  **年代／馆藏**：通行分期为主前 7 世纪末至 6 世纪初；两件刻文银护身片 KH 1／KH 2，IAA 1980-1495／1980-1496，现藏以色列博物馆。也有少数研究提出较晚、归回初期的年代。
- **Related Scripture:** Numbers 6:24–26.
  **对应经文**：民数记 6:24–26。
- **Supports:** Parallel priestly-blessing wording circulating by the First Temple's end or early return, usable in personal amulet/blessing settings.
  **能支持**：两件银片保存与祭司祝福平行的措辞，说明这一祝福传统至迟在第一圣殿末期或归回初期已经流传，并可用于个人护身／祝福语境。
- **Does not establish:** Rolled wearable amulets, not Pentateuch scrolls or Moses' handwriting. Three blessing verses cannot establish Numbers' author, initial writing, or final compilation date.
  **不能支持**：它们是卷起佩戴的护身片，不是摩西五经卷轴，更不是摩西亲笔；不能由三节祝福语确定民数记全书的作者、最初写作时间或最终编定时间。
- **Grade:** A for objects/inscriptions/IDs/parallels; A−/B for exact dates given objections; C for dating all Numbers from them.
  **等级**：A（器物、刻文、编号与祝福平行）；A−／B（精确年代，因仍有异议）；C（由此推定民数记全书年代）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Museum: two silver amulets and priestly blessing](https://www.imj.org.il/en/exhibitions/i-will-lift-mine-eyes); [BASOR: 2004 revised reading and assessment](https://www.journals.uchicago.edu/doi/10.2307/4150106); [USC West Semitic Research Project: KH 1/KH 2 and IAA identifiers](https://dornsife.usc.edu/wsrp-temp/wp-content/uploads/sites/155/2023/05/Northwest-Semitic-Amulets-and-Incantations.pdf); [Nadav Na’aman: objection favoring a later date](https://www.jstor.org/stable/23214240)
  **来源**：[以色列博物馆：两件银片与祭司祝福](https://www.imj.org.il/en/exhibitions/i-will-lift-mine-eyes)；[BASOR：2004 年新版释读与评估](https://www.journals.uchicago.edu/doi/10.2307/4150106)；[USC West Semitic Research Project：KH 1／KH 2 与 IAA 编号](https://dornsife.usc.edu/wsrp-temp/wp-content/uploads/sites/155/2023/05/Northwest-Semitic-Amulets-and-Incantations.pdf)；[Nadav Na’aman：较晚年代的异议](https://www.jstor.org/stable/23214240)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-085"></a>

### 85. Deir ʿAlla Plaster Inscription: Balaam Son of Beor in Transjordan | 德尔阿拉石灰墙墨书：“比珥之子巴兰”的跨约旦传统

- **Date / carrier:** Late ninth–eighth century BCE, commonly around 800; red/black ink plaster fragments found in 1967 at Tell Deir ʿAlla, Jordan Valley; KAI 312, Jordan Archaeological Museum.
  **年代／载体**：约主前 9 世纪末至 8 世纪，常置于约主前 800 年附近；1967 年在约旦河谷 Tell Deir ʿAlla 发现的红、黑墨石灰墙残片，传统出版号 KAI 312，现藏约旦考古博物馆。
- **Related Scripture:** Numbers 22–24.
  **对应经文**：民数记 22–24 章。
- **Supports:** Opening commonly read Balaam son of Beor, a seer of the gods: an Iron Age Transjordanian tradition sharing Numbers' name/patronymic.
  **能支持**：残片开头通常释读为“比珥之子巴兰”，并称其为众神的先见；它显示铁器时代约旦河东地区流传一个与民数记同名、同父名的先见传统。
- **Does not establish:** Centuries after all Mosaic date models, not contemporary event records, and a different divine world. Joins, damaged readings, dialect, and date remain discussed; not every reconstructed line is certain.
  **不能支持**：铭文晚于所有摩西年代方案数百年，不是民数记事件的同期记录；其神明世界与民数记不同。残片组合、受损字句、方言分类和具体日期仍有讨论，不能把复原文本每一行都视为确定读法。
- **Grade:** A− for inscription/name/seer title; B for shared tradition; C for particular historical relation to Numbers.
  **等级**：A−（铭文、名称和先见称号）；B（共享巴兰传统）；C（与民数记具体事件的历史关系）。
- **Sources:** [ASOR / The Biblical Archaeologist: Balaam tradition study](https://www.journals.uchicago.edu/doi/10.2307/3210015); [Brill: *The Balaam Text from Deir ʿAlla Re-Evaluated*](https://brill.com/edcollchap/book/9789004666351/B9789004666351_s006.xml); [Tell Deir ʿAlla cult-space study](https://www.mdpi.com/2077-1444/10/3/145); [American Center of Research: Jordan Archaeological Museum display archive](https://archives.acorjordan.org/s/acor/item/88230)
  **来源**：[ASOR／*The Biblical Archaeologist*：巴兰传统研究](https://www.journals.uchicago.edu/doi/10.2307/3210015)；[Brill：*The Balaam Text from Deir ʿAlla Re-Evaluated*](https://brill.com/edcollchap/book/9789004666351/B9789004666351_s006.xml)；[Tell Deir ʿAlla 祭祀空间研究](https://www.mdpi.com/2077-1444/10/3/145)；[American Center of Research：约旦考古博物馆展陈档案](https://archives.acorjordan.org/s/acor/item/88230)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-086"></a>

### 86. Soleb and Amara West Lists: `yhwꜣ` of Shasu-Land | 索莱布与阿玛拉西地名表：Shasu-land 的 `yhwꜣ`

- **Date / carrier:** Soleb under Amenhotep III, about 1390–1352 BCE; Amara West parallel under Ramesses II, about 1279–1213. Monumental place/population lists in Egyptian temples in Nubia.
  **年代／载体**：索莱布例属阿蒙霍特普三世时期，约主前 1390–1352 年；阿玛拉西平行例属拉美西斯二世时期，约主前 1279–1213 年。两者都是努比亚埃及神庙中的纪念性地名／族群表。
- **Related Scripture:** Exodus 3:13–15; Deuteronomy 33:2; Judges 5:4–5 and southern-wilderness appearance traditions.
  **对应经文**：出埃及记 3:13–15；申命记 33:2；士师记 5:4–5 等把耶和华显现与南方旷野相联的传统。
- **Supports:** New Kingdom records of a Shasu-land-associated name commonly transcribed `yhwꜣ`, essential material for early YHWH-name history.
  **能支持**：新王国埃及文献确实记录与 Shasu-land 相联、通常转写为 `yhwꜣ` 的名称；它是讨论 YHWH 神名早期历史时不能绕过的材料。
- **Does not establish:** These lists first map geography/populations; `yhwꜣ` may designate a place, region, or group association. Even cognacy with YHWH would not equate Shasu with Israel or prove Moses, burning bush, or Exodus.
  **不能支持**：埃及表列首先是在制作地理—族群地图，`yhwꜣ` 可能是地名、地区或群体关联名称；即使它与 YHWH 同源，也不能自动把 Shasu 等同以色列人，更不能证明摩西、燃烧荆棘或出埃及事件。
- **Grade:** A for inscriptions/dates/name; C for specific relations to Israel's divine name, population, or Mosaic tradition.
  **等级**：A（两处铭文、年代与 `yhwꜣ` 名称）；C（与以色列神名、群体及摩西传统的具体关系）。
- **Sources:** [Daniel E. Fleming, “Yhwʒ of Shasu-Land,” *Yahweh before Israel*, Cambridge University Press](https://www.cambridge.org/core/books/abs/yahweh-before-israel/yhw-of-shasuland/119116DC7806AE299828B5D58EA06539)
  **来源**：[Daniel E. Fleming, “Yhwʒ of Shasu-Land,” *Yahweh before Israel*, Cambridge University Press](https://www.cambridge.org/core/books/abs/yahweh-before-israel/yhw-of-shasuland/119116DC7806AE299828B5D58EA06539)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="batch-10"></a>

## Batch 10: Leviticus, Sacrifice, Sanctuary, and Textual Transmission | 第十批扩展锚点：利未记的祭祀、圣所与文本流传

> This group separates Late Bronze ritual texts, Iron Age Judah sanctuaries, and later Leviticus manuscripts. The first two illuminate ritual worlds; the third text transmission. No one item proves Moses’ tabernacle, ritual origins, and biblical events together.<br>
> 本组把晚青铜时代祭祀文本、铁器时代犹大圣所和后期《利未记》抄本分成三个证据层。前两类只能照明礼仪世界，后者只能说明文本流传；任何一项都不能同时证明摩西会幕、礼仪起源和经文事件。

<a id="evidence-087"></a>

### 87. Ugaritic KTU 1.41: Late Bronze Offerings and Festival Scheduling | 乌加列祭祀表 KTU 1.41：晚青铜时代的祭物与节期安排

- **Date / collection:** Late Bronze II, around 1400–1200 BCE; found in 1929 in Room 1 of the High Priest's House, Ras Shamra acropolis. RS 1.003 and RS 2.[005] join as KTU 1.41; Louvre AO 11996.
  **年代／馆藏**：晚青铜时代 II，约主前 1400–1200 年；1929 年出土于拉斯沙姆拉卫城“大祭司之家”1 室。两块泥版残片 RS 1.003 与 RS 2.[005] 拼合为 KTU 1.41，现藏卢浮宫 AO 11996。
- **Related Scripture:** Leviticus 1–7 offering categories; chapter 23 festivals/sacrifices.
  **对应经文**：利未记 1–7 章的祭物分类；23 章的节期与献祭安排。
- **Supports:** Ugaritic alphabetic-cuneiform lists of animals, grain, oil, and wine: real Late Bronze Northwest Semitic offering terms, calendars, and public rites.
  **能支持**：乌加列字母楔形文字确有祭祀清单，安排牲畜、谷物、油和酒等供物；祭物术语、历法和公共礼仪属于晚青铜时代西北闪语世界的真实制度。
- **Does not establish:** Multiple Ugaritic gods and royal ritual differ from Leviticus. Shared offerings may reflect similar agricultural societies, not direct copying or precise Levitical composition dates.
  **不能支持**：文本敬奉多位乌加列神明，也有王室祭仪处境；共同祭物可来自相近农业社会，不能证明利未记直接抄自此版，更不能由现存泥版给利未记精确定年。
- **Grade:** A for tablet/IDs/date/list; B for sacrificial/festival setting; C for direct dependence/precise dating.
  **等级**：A（泥版、编号、年代与祭祀清单）；B（祭祀与节期背景）；C（直接依赖或精确定年）。
- **Sources:** [Louvre collection: AO 11996 / KTU 1.41 (joined fragments)](https://collections.louvre.fr/en/ark%3A/53355/cl010136717)
  **来源**：[卢浮宫藏品页：AO 11996，RS 1.003 + RS 2.[005]，KTU 1.41](https://collections.louvre.fr/en/ark%3A/53355/cl010136717)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-088"></a>

### 88. Emar Zukru Text, Emar VI 373: Cyclical Public Ritual | 埃马 Zukru 节期文本 Emar VI 373：周期性公共礼仪

- **Date / text identifier:** Late Bronze, usually thirteenth century BCE; diviner's archive at Emar/Meskene, Syria; Emar VI 373.
  **年代／文本号**：晚青铜时代，通常置于主前 13 世纪；出自叙利亚埃马／Meskene 的占卜者档案，正式出版号 Emar VI 373。
- **Related Scripture:** Leviticus 23's calendar; limited comparison with chapter 25's cyclical structure.
  **对应经文**：利未记 23 章的节期历法；25 章的周期结构可作有限比较。
- **Supports:** A multi-day Dagan festival with offerings, processions, and civic participation, showing calendars coordinating religion, public life, and food supply in ancient Syria.
  **能支持**：文本描述献给 Dagan 的多日节期，包括供物、巡行和城中群体参与，显示古代叙利亚社会会用历法协调宗教、公共生活与食物供应。
- **Does not establish:** Not an Israelite festival or Levitical theology. Whether every seventh year means a six- or seven-year cycle remains disputed; no direct equation with sabbatical year, Jubilee, or a Leviticus 23 festival.
  **不能支持**：Zukru 不是以色列节期，Dagan 崇拜也不是利未记神学；研究对“每第七年”究竟构成六年还是七年周期仍有讨论，不能把它直接等同安息年、禧年或利未记 23 章某一节期。
- **Grade:** A for text/edition number; B for cyclical public festivals; C for cycle calculation/direct Levitical relationship.
  **等级**：A（文本及正式编号）；B（周期性节期和公共礼仪背景）；C（周期计算与利未制度的直接关系）。
- **Sources:** [Masamichi Yamada, “The zukru Festival in Emar,” *ORIENT* 45](https://www.jstage.jst.go.jp/article/orient/45/0/45_111/_article); [Same author: interpreting Emar VI 373's cycle](https://cir.nii.ac.jp/crid/1390282680479629056?lang=en); [Brill: *The Politics of Ritual Change*](https://brill.com/display/title/39264?language=en)
  **来源**：[Masamichi Yamada, “The zukru Festival in Emar,” *ORIENT* 45](https://www.jstage.jst.go.jp/article/orient/45/0/45_111/_article)；[同作者：Emar VI 373 的周期解释问题](https://cir.nii.ac.jp/crid/1390282680479629056?lang=en)；[Brill：*The Politics of Ritual Change*](https://brill.com/display/title/39264?language=en)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-089"></a>

### 89. Arad Altar Residues: Frankincense, Cannabis, and Judean Sanctuary Practice | 特尔亚拉得两座祭坛残留物：乳香、大麻与犹大圣所实践

- **Date / place:** Eighth century BCE; two limestone altars in southern Judah's Arad fortress shrine, analyzed by two independent laboratories in a 2020 study.
  **年代／地点**：主前 8 世纪；犹大南部特尔亚拉得堡垒圣所内两座石灰石祭坛。2020 年研究在两所独立实验室分析其表面残留物。
- **Related Scripture:** Leviticus 2:1–2, 16; 16:12–13; 24:7 on frankincense/incense.
  **对应经文**：利未记 2:1–2、16；16:12–13；24:7 的乳香与焚香背景。
- **Supports:** Frankincense biomarkers on the larger altar and THC/CBD/CBN on the smaller. Animal fat/dung may respectively aid volatilization/low-temperature heating: direct material evidence for aromatics in an Iron Age Judean shrine.
  **能支持**：较大祭坛检出乳香相关生物标志物，较小祭坛检出 THC、CBD、CBN 等大麻成分；动物脂肪与粪便可能分别帮助挥发和低温加热。这是铁器时代犹大圣所使用芳香物质的直接物质证据。
- **Does not establish:** Centuries after any Mosaic date, not tabernacle/Aaronic altars. Cannabis is absent from biblical incense recipes; residues establish neither Jerusalem temple use nor `qaneh-bosem` as cannabis.
  **不能支持**：亚拉得比任何摩西年代方案都晚数百年，不是会幕或亚伦祭坛；大麻没有出现在圣经香料配方中，残留物也不能证明耶路撒冷圣殿同样使用大麻，更不能据此把 `qaneh-bosem` 确定译为 cannabis。
- **Grade:** A for altars/chemistry; B for Judean incense practice; C/D for extending to tabernacle, Jerusalem, or ingredient semantics.
  **等级**：A（祭坛与化学残留物）；B（铁器时代犹大焚香实践）；C／D（推广至会幕、耶路撒冷或香料词义）。
- **Sources:** [Eran Arie、Baruch Rosen、Dvory Namdar, “Cannabis and Frankincense at the Judahite Shrine of Arad,” *Tel Aviv* 47.1](https://cris.haifa.ac.il/en/publications/cannabis-and-frankincense-at-the-judahite-shrine-of-arad/)
  **来源**：[Eran Arie、Baruch Rosen、Dvory Namdar, “Cannabis and Frankincense at the Judahite Shrine of Arad,” *Tel Aviv* 47.1](https://cris.haifa.ac.il/en/publications/cannabis-and-frankincense-at-the-judahite-shrine-of-arad/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-090"></a>

### 90. Tel Moẓa Temple: a Judean Sanctuary Outside Jerusalem | 特尔摩撒铁器时代神庙：耶路撒冷之外的犹大圣所

- **Date / place:** Iron IIA, initial construction commonly late tenth–early ninth century BCE, about seven kilometers northwest of Jerusalem. IAA salvage excavation found a monumental multi-phase temple complex.
  **年代／地点**：铁器时代 IIA，初建常置于主前 10 世纪末至 9 世纪初；位于耶路撒冷西北约 7 公里的 Tel Moẓa。以色列文物局抢救发掘发现多期延续的纪念性神庙建筑群。
- **Related Scripture:** Leviticus 1–7's altars/offerings; chapter 17's locations; Deuteronomy 12's centralization setting.
  **对应经文**：利未记 1–7 章的祭坛与祭物；17 章的献祭地点；申命记 12 章的敬拜集中化背景。
- **Supports:** East–west axis, courtyard/east entrance, unworked-stone altar, offering table, ash, many animal bones, broken cult vessels, and figurines: enduring local sanctuary practice in Judah outside Jerusalem.
  **能支持**：遗址有东西轴线、院落、向东入口、未凿石祭坛、供桌、灰与大量动物骨骼，以及破碎祭祀器物和小像；显示犹大境内长期存在耶路撒冷之外的地方圣所和复杂实践。
- **Does not establish:** Not an identified tabernacle or Solomon-temple replica. Similar architecture/local sacrifice cannot date or author Leviticus. Figurines caution that actual Judean religion need not match legal norms fully.
  **不能支持**：摩撒神庙不是可确认的会幕，也不能称为所罗门圣殿复制品；建筑相似和地方祭祀不能决定利未记的作者或年代。小像等材料反而提醒我们：实际犹大宗教生活不必完全符合律法规范。
- **Grade:** A for site/phasing/assemblage; B for sanctuary practice/centralization setting; C for direct tabernacle/temple models or composition dates.
  **等级**：A（遗址、分期与祭祀组合）；B（犹大圣所实践及集中化背景）；C（与会幕、圣殿模型或经文成书年代的直接关系）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: Tel Moẓa excavation report, 2020](https://hadashot.iaa.org.il/Report_Detail_Eng.aspx?id=25702); [Shua Kisilevitz, “The Iron IIA Judahite Temple at Tel Moza,” *Tel Aviv* 42](https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1179/0334435515Z.00000000052); [Tel Moẓa Excavation Project](https://www.telmoza.org/)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局发掘报告：Tel Moẓa，2020](https://hadashot.iaa.org.il/Report_Detail_Eng.aspx?id=25702)；[Shua Kisilevitz, “The Iron IIA Judahite Temple at Tel Moza,” *Tel Aviv* 42](https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1179/0334435515Z.00000000052)；[Tel Moẓa Excavation Project](https://www.telmoza.org/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-091"></a>

### 91. 11Q1 Paleo-Hebrew Leviticus: a Second Temple Copy | 11Q1 古希伯来文《利未记》：第二圣殿时期的抄传见证

- **Date / collection information:** Herodian parchment, Cave 11 discovery in 1956; IAA Leon Levy Library 11Q1, paleo-Hebrew script, 104 surviving fragments.
  **年代／馆藏信息**：希律时期，羊皮纸；1956 年发现于昆兰 11 号洞。以色列文物局死海古卷数字图书馆列为 11Q1，古希伯来字体，现存 104 块残片。
- **Related Scripture:** Multiple surviving Leviticus passages: whole-book transmission, not one ritual event.
  **对应经文**：《利未记》多处保存段落；用于全卷文本史，而非某一次礼仪事件。
- **Supports:** Continued copying/preservation as scripture; deliberate use of archaic script in a later age, potentially bearing traditional/identity significance.
  **能支持**：第二圣殿时期《利未记》已被持续抄写并作为经书保存；在较晚时代有抄写者刻意使用古希伯来字体，显示字体本身也可承载传统和身份意义。
- **Does not establish:** Not Moses' autograph; archaic script does not date the text to Moses or First Temple times. Preserved laws inform variants/transmission, not the historicity of every ritual event.
  **不能支持**：11Q1 不是摩西原稿；古体字不等于文本写于摩西或第一圣殿时期。抄本可用于比较异文和流传史，不能因保存了律例就证明所有仪式事件的历史发生。
- **Grade:** A for manuscript/cave/script/material/fragments; C for inferring initial composition from script.
  **等级**：A（抄本、洞穴、字体、材质与残片）；C（由字体反推最初成书年代）。
- **Sources:** [Leon Levy Dead Sea Scrolls Digital Library: 11Q1](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/11Q1-1?locale=en_US); [Israel Antiquities Authority: Cave 11 discovery and paleo-Hebrew Leviticus](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/learn-about-the-scrolls/discovery-sites)
  **来源**：[Leon Levy Dead Sea Scrolls Digital Library：11Q1](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/11Q1-1?locale=en_US)；[以色列文物局：11 号洞发现与古希伯来文《利未记》](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/learn-about-the-scrolls/discovery-sites)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-092"></a>

### 92. En-Gedi Burnt Scroll: Virtually Unwrapped Leviticus 1–2 | 隐基底焦卷：虚拟展开的《利未记》1–2 章

- **Date / discovery:** Charred scroll found in the 1970s among synagogue ark remains; micro-CT/virtual unwrapping reveals two columns. Radiocarbon favors third–fourth century CE; paleography favors late first–early second. Retain the discrepancy.
  **年代／发现**：20 世纪 70 年代在隐基底会堂的约柜遗存中发现炭化卷轴；微型 CT 与“虚拟展开”技术读出两栏文字。碳十四测年最可能落在主后 3–4 世纪，古文字学则偏向主后 1 世纪末至 2 世纪初，两者差异需保留。
- **Related Scripture:** Leviticus 1:1–9; 2:1–11.
  **对应经文**：利未记 1:1–9；2:1–11。
- **Supports:** Readable consonants exactly match the medieval Masoretic text in these passages: a strong local example of stable transmission/synagogue use and nondestructive reading.
  **能支持**：已读取的辅音文本与中世纪马所拉文本在这两段完全一致，提供《利未记》1–2 章长期稳定流传和会堂使用的强实例，也展示无损读取炭化抄本的方法。
- **Does not establish:** Only two short passages are read, not proof of perpetual whole-Bible word-for-word identity. The scroll may contain only Leviticus or multiple Pentateuch books. Its later date cannot establish initial writing or sacrificial events.
  **不能支持**：目前只读出两个短段，不能据此声称整本圣经历来逐字无异；卷轴可能只抄利未记，也可能属于包含数卷摩西五经的卷本。抄本年代远晚于所述礼仪，不能证明经文最初写作时间或献祭事件。
- **Grade:** A for scroll/readable text/unwrapping; A−/B for date given method differences; D for proving events from the copy.
  **等级**：A（卷轴、可读经文与虚拟展开结果）；A−／B（精确年代，因碳十四与古文字学存在差距）；D（由抄本证明事件）。
- **Sources:** [Michael Segal et al., Textus 26: preliminary En-Gedi Leviticus edition](https://openscholar.huji.ac.il/sites/default/files/he_bible_project/files/m._segal1.1.pdf); [Science Advances: virtual-unwrapping method and results](https://doi.org/10.1126/sciadv.1601247); [Hebrew University research record](https://cris.huji.ac.il/en/publications/from-damage-to-discovery-via-virtual-unwrapping-reading-the-scrol/)
  **来源**：[Michael Segal 等，《Textus》26：隐基底《利未记》卷轴初步出版](https://openscholar.huji.ac.il/sites/default/files/he_bible_project/files/m._segal1.1.pdf)；[Science Advances：虚拟展开方法与结果](https://doi.org/10.1126/sciadv.1601247)；[希伯来大学研究资料页](https://cris.huji.ac.il/en/publications/from-damage-to-discovery-via-virtual-unwrapping-reading-the-scrol/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="batch-11"></a>

## Batch 11: Solomon, Temple, Wealth, and Wisdom | 第十一批扩展锚点：所罗门、圣殿、财富与智慧

> This group tests capital chronology, local construction, regional economy, and international wisdom separately rather than seeking a decisive Solomonic object. A building’s century, ruling polity, and individual king are three different questions.<br>
> 本组不寻找一件能“一锤定音”的所罗门文物，而把首都年代、地方城建、区域经济和国际智慧传统分别检验。建筑属于哪个世纪、哪个政权和哪位君王，是三个不同问题。

<a id="evidence-093"></a>

### 93. Iron Age Jerusalem Radiocarbon: the Tenth Century Is Not Blank | 铁器时代耶路撒冷碳十四序列：十世纪并非考古空白

- **Date / research:** 2024 PNAS study, 103 radiocarbon dates from secure contexts in five excavation areas, modeled with microarchaeology/dense stratigraphy.
  **年代／研究**：2024 年 *PNAS* 发表；研究团队从铁器时代耶路撒冷五个发掘区的可靠语境取得 103 个碳十四日期，并用微考古与密集地层序列建立模型。
- **Related Scripture:** 2 Samuel 5–7; 1 Kings 1–11; 1 Chronicles 11–29; 2 Chronicles 1–9.
  **对应经文**：撒母耳记下 5–7 章；列王纪上 1–11 章；历代志上 11–29 章；历代志下 1–9 章。
- **Supports:** Settlement in the twelfth–tenth centuries BCE, westward expansion placed in the ninth, then major building, earthquake damage, repair, and fortification. A wholly blank tenth-century Jerusalem is untenable.
  **能支持**：耶路撒冷在主前 12–10 世纪已有聚落活动；研究把向西扩展放到主前 9 世纪，并显示此后出现大规模建筑、地震破坏、修复和设防。它使“十世纪耶路撒冷完全空白”的说法无法维持。
- **Does not establish:** Samples do not name David, Solomon, palace, or temple. Settlement does not establish a vast imperial capital. No routine comprehensive Temple Mount excavation; calibration plateaus/regional atmospheric offsets require intervals.
  **不能支持**：样本没有写着大卫、所罗门、王宫或圣殿；“十世纪有聚落”不等于“已经证明庞大帝国首都”。圣殿山未作常规全面发掘，碳十四校准平台和区域大气偏移也要求保留年代范围。
- **Grade:** A for samples/strata/urban chronology; B for David–Solomon setting; C for assigning buildings, rulers, or kingdom scale.
  **等级**：A（样本、地层与城市年代序列）；B（大卫—所罗门时代的耶路撒冷背景）；C（具体建筑、君王与王国规模归属）。
- **Sources:** [Regev et al., PNAS 121: Iron Age Jerusalem radiocarbon chronology](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC11087761/); [Weizmann Institute: research record and abstract](https://weizmann.elsevierpure.com/en/publications/radiocarbon-chronology-of-iron-age-jerusalem-reveals-calibration-/)
  **来源**：[Regev 等，*PNAS* 121：铁器时代耶路撒冷碳十四年代](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC11087761/)；[Weizmann Institute 研究记录与摘要](https://weizmann.elsevierpure.com/en/publications/radiocarbon-chronology-of-iron-age-jerusalem-reveals-calibration-/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-094"></a>

### 94. Gezer Stratum 8: Tenth-Century Gate, Walls, and Administration | 基色第 8 层：十世纪城门、城墙与行政建筑

- **Date / site:** Stratum 8/traditional VIII, Iron IIA. The 2023 sequential radiocarbon/Bayesian model places monumental building onset at 998–957 BCE (68.3% highest posterior density; 95.4% interval 1023–942).
  **年代／遗址**：特尔基色第 8 层／传统第 VIII 层，铁器时代 IIA。2023 年连续地层碳十四与贝叶斯模型把纪念性建设开端置于主前 998–957 年（68.3% 最高后验密度；95.4% 范围为 1023–942 年）。
- **Related Scripture:** 1 Kings 9:15–17, Gezer/Pharaoh as father-in-law/Solomon's works; earlier border-conflict setting of 1 Chronicles 20:4.
  **对应经文**：列王纪上 9:15–17 的基色、法老岳父与所罗门城建；历代志上 20:4 的较早边境冲突背景。
- **Supports:** Boundary inscriptions secure Gezer's identification. Six-chamber gate, casemate wall, and major administration establish concentrated early-tenth-century construction/political reorganization.
  **能支持**：基色身份由多块边界铭文稳固确认；第 8 层出现六室城门、城廓式城墙和大型行政建筑，证明主前 10 世纪前半此战略城市发生真实的集中建设与政治重组。
- **Does not establish:** Six-chamber gates are not exclusive to one century/kingdom. Radiocarbon permits chronological compatibility, not a royal signature. Excavators' highland/early Judah expansion connection remains a model; linking destruction with Sheshonq has a decade-to-decades chronological tension.
  **不能支持**：六室门并非某一王国或世纪专用样式；碳十四只能说明年代相容，不能给工程署名。发掘者倾向把该层联系到高地政权／早期犹大扩张，仍属于历史模型；其毁坏与舍顺克一世的对应也有十年至数十年的年代张力。
- **Grade:** A for city/strata/buildings/radiocarbon; B for tenth-century state formation; C for Solomon/Pharaoh/Sheshonq attribution.
  **等级**：A（城市、地层、建筑与碳十四序列）；B（十世纪国家形成背景）；C（所罗门、法老或舍顺克的具体归属）。
- **Sources:** [Webster et al., PLOS ONE 18: Late Bronze–Iron Gezer radiocarbon chronology](https://journals.plos.org/plosone/article?id=10.1371%2Fjournal.pone.0293119)
  **来源**：[Webster 等，*PLOS ONE* 18：基色晚青铜—铁器时代碳十四年代](https://journals.plos.org/plosone/article?id=10.1371%2Fjournal.pone.0293119)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-095"></a>

### 95. Megiddo VA–IVB Rephased: Former Solomonic Palaces in the Omride Era | 米吉多 VA–IVB 层重新分期：昔日“所罗门宫殿”进入暗利时代

- **Date / site:** Megiddo VA–IVB; a 2025 Near Eastern Archaeology synthesis of pottery, radiocarbon, and excavation assigns early ninth-century BCE Omrides.
  **年代／遗址**：米吉多 VA–IVB 层；2025 年 *Near Eastern Archaeology* 综合陶器、碳十四和新发掘资料，把该层放在主前 9 世纪前半、北国暗利王朝时期。
- **Related Scripture:** 1 Kings 9:15's construction; 1 Kings 16–22's Omri–Ahab dynasty.
  **对应经文**：列王纪上 9:15 的米吉多城建；列王纪上 16–22 章的暗利—亚哈王朝。
- **Supports:** Two ashlar palaces, six-chamber gate, and monumental planning, demonstrating strong ninth-century northern building/administrative capacity.
  **能支持**：该层确有两座方石宫殿、六室城门和纪念性城市规划，显示北国在主前 9 世纪具有强大的建筑与行政能力。
- **Does not establish:** If new phasing stands, old Solomonic labels cannot remain direct proof. This is a strong reassessment, not universal three-city agreement; reassigning one Megiddo layer cannot automatically negate different tenth-century Jerusalem/Gezer evidence.
  **不能支持**：这些建筑过去常被标作“所罗门宫殿／城门”，但若新分期成立，就不应继续用旧标签作直接证据。该研究是一项有力重估，不代表三城所有研究者已无异议；米吉多某层改归暗利，也不能自动否定十世纪耶路撒冷或基色的不同资料。
- **Grade:** A for site/monuments; B/C for exact VA–IVB phasing/Omrides; C for Solomonic attribution.
  **等级**：A（遗址与纪念性建筑）；B／C（VA–IVB 的精确分期与暗利归属）；C（所罗门署名）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Finkelstein, “Megiddo of the Omride Dynasty,” *Near Eastern Archaeology* 88.3](https://www.journals.uchicago.edu/doi/10.1086/736851); [University of Chicago: Megiddo excavation publications and exhibition brochure](https://isac.uchicago.edu/sites/default/files/uploads/shared/docs/Museum/ISAC%20Megiddo%20Exhibit%20Brochure.pdf)
  **来源**：[Israel Finkelstein, “Megiddo of the Omride Dynasty,” *Near Eastern Archaeology* 88.3](https://www.journals.uchicago.edu/doi/10.1086/736851)；[芝加哥大学米吉多发掘出版与展览手册](https://isac.uchicago.edu/sites/default/files/uploads/shared/docs/Museum/ISAC%20Megiddo%20Exhibit%20Brochure.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-096"></a>

### 96. Timna Slaves' Hill Copper: Solomon's Era Does Not Establish Solomon's Ownership | 提姆纳“奴隶山”铜业：所罗门时代不等于所罗门矿山

- **Date / site:** Timna Site 34, modern nickname Slaves' Hill; fourteen short-lived radiocarbon samples place peak production in the early tenth century BCE.
  **年代／遗址**：提姆纳谷 Site 34，现代昵称“Slaves’ Hill／奴隶山”；14 个短寿命样本的碳十四日期显示生产高峰在主前 10 世纪前半。
- **Related Scripture:** 1 Kings 7:13–47's bronze; 9:26–28, 10:11–29's trade/wealth; 2 Chronicles 4.
  **对应经文**：列王纪上 7:13–47 的大量铜器；9:26–28、10:11–29 的区域贸易与王室财富；历代志下 4 章。
- **Supports:** Defenses, smelting, slag, pens, and food remains show organized southern Arabah industry/long-distance trade; mobile groups could coordinate complex industry.
  **能支持**：防御体系、冶炼设施、矿渣、畜栏与食物遗存显示南阿拉巴铜业由一个组织严密、参与远距离贸易的复杂社会经营；非定居群体也能形成中央协调的工业体系。
- **Does not establish:** No direct Jerusalem ownership link; researchers more often associate operators with early Edomites. A modern nickname does not establish Solomonic slave labor; era is not ownership.
  **不能支持**：研究不能把矿山直接连接到耶路撒冷；运营者更常被研究者联系到早期以东群体。“奴隶山”只是现代昵称，不能证明劳工是所罗门奴隶；“年代属于所罗门时代”不等于“所有权属于所罗门”。
- **Grade:** A for site/industry/tenth-century peak; B for regional copper/trade/wealth; D for direct Solomon's mines identification.
  **等级**：A（遗址、工业活动与主前 10 世纪高峰）；B（区域铜业、贸易与财富背景）；D（“所罗门矿山”直接认定）。
- **Sources:** [Erez Ben-Yosef, “Back to Solomon’s Era,” *BASOR* 376](https://cris.iucc.ac.il/en/publications/back-to-solomons-era-results-of-the-first-excavations-at-slaves-h/); [EU CORDIS: Central Timna Valley project and early Edomite interpretation](https://cordis.europa.eu/project/id/334274/reporting); [Iron Age Timna copper industry and environment study](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC9492654/)
  **来源**：[Erez Ben-Yosef, “Back to Solomon’s Era,” *BASOR* 376](https://cris.iucc.ac.il/en/publications/back-to-solomons-era-results-of-the-first-excavations-at-slaves-h/)；[欧盟 CORDIS：中央提姆纳谷项目总结与早期以东解释](https://cordis.europa.eu/project/id/334274/reporting)；[铁器时代提姆纳铜业与环境研究](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC9492654/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-097"></a>

### 97. Instruction of Amenemope, EA 10474: Proverbs and International Wisdom | 《阿蒙尼莫普教诲》EA 10474：箴言与国际智慧传统

- **Date / collection:** British Museum EA 10474,2, registration 1888,0515.3.2; Theban hieratic papyrus, provisionally Third Intermediate Period. Instruction on front; calendar of lucky/unlucky days and other texts on back. Neither copy nor composition date should collapse to one year.
  **年代／馆藏**：大英博物馆 EA 10474,2，登记号 1888,0515.3.2；来自底比斯的僧侣体纸草，博物馆目录暂列埃及第三中间期。正面保存《阿蒙尼莫普教诲》，背面有吉凶日历等文本；抄本日期与作品创作日期都不宜压成单一年份。
- **Related Scripture:** Proverbs 22:17–24:22, especially weak/poor people, boundaries, greed, haste, and dining.
  **对应经文**：箴言 22:17–24:22，尤其对贫弱者、边界、贪财、急躁与筵席行为的劝诫。
- **Supports:** Egyptian parental/teacher instruction and dense parallels in sequence, imagery, and wording. Most relevant research sees literary influence while stressing Proverbs' free selection/reordering under reverence for YHWH.
  **能支持**：埃及确有以父／师向子弟训诲为形式的智慧文本；箴言这一段与《教诲》在次序、意象和措辞上有一组密集平行。多数相关研究认为存在文学影响，但也强调箴言自由选择、重排并置于敬畏耶和华的框架中。
- **Does not establish:** No Solomon or proof he translated/copied it. Manuscript dates are not initial composition dates; influence is not whole-book plagiarism or erasure of Proverbs' compilation/theological reshaping.
  **不能支持**：纸草没有提所罗门，也不能证明所罗门本人翻译或抄写；抄本年代不是两部作品最初创作年代。文学影响不等于“整卷箴言抄袭埃及”，更不能消除箴言自身的编集层次和神学重塑。
- **Grade:** A for papyrus/collection/text; B+ for literary relationship; C for transmission route/authors/precise dating.
  **等级**：A（纸草、馆藏与文本）；B+（两部智慧文本的文学关系）；C（具体传播路线、作者与精确定年）。
- **Sources:** [British Museum: EA 10474,2, Instruction of Amenemope](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/Y_EA10474-2); [J. A. Emerton, Vetus Testamentum 51: Proverbs 22–24 and Amenemope](https://doi.org/10.1163/15685330152939488); [Michael V. Fox: editorial reshaping from Amenemope to Proverbs](https://www.degruyterbrill.com/document/doi/10.1515/zaw-2014-0006/html)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆：EA 10474,2，《阿蒙尼莫普教诲》](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/Y_EA10474-2)；[J. A. Emerton, *Vetus Testamentum* 51：箴言 22–24 与《教诲》](https://doi.org/10.1163/15685330152939488)；[Michael V. Fox：从《阿蒙尼莫普教诲》到箴言的编辑重塑](https://www.degruyterbrill.com/document/doi/10.1515/zaw-2014-0006/html)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-098"></a>

### 98. Ludlul bēl nēmeqi: the Pious Sufferer and Limits of the Babylonian Job Label | *Ludlul bēl nēmeqi*：敬虔受苦者与“巴比伦约伯”边界

- **Date / document:** Akkadian wisdom poem, commonly translated I Will Praise the Lord of Wisdom or Poem of the Righteous Sufferer, reconstructed from Assyrian/Babylonian tablets at Nineveh, Assur, Babylon, Sippar, and elsewhere. CDLI P491222 is a digital composite, not one complete original tablet.
  **年代／文献**：阿卡德文智慧诗 *Ludlul bēl nēmeqi*，现代常译《我要赞美智慧之主》或《义人受苦诗》。作品由尼尼微、亚述、巴比伦、锡帕等地的多份亚述与巴比伦泥版重建；CDLI P491222 是数字合成文本，不是一块完整原版泥版。
- **Related Scripture:** Job 1–3, 6–7, 19, 29–31, 38–42.
  **对应经文**：约伯记 1–3、6–7、19、29–31、38–42 章。
- **Supports:** A pious sufferer's illness, lost status, alienated friends, and failed conventional explanations prompt questions about divine intention. Restoration by Marduk supports comparison with Job's suffering, knowledge limits, and ending.
  **能支持**：古代美索不达米亚确有敬虔者在疾病、地位崩落、朋友疏远和常规宗教解释失效时追问神意的智慧传统；诗中人物最后由玛尔杜克恢复，适合与约伯受苦、知识边界和结局恢复作类型比较。
- **Does not establish:** Babylonian Job is a modern nickname, not Job, Uz, or Israelite theology. Copy dates are not initial composition; shared themes establish neither direct copying nor Job's author/date.
  **不能支持**：“巴比伦约伯”只是现代昵称；文本没有约伯、乌斯地或以色列神学。现存泥版年代不等于作品初创年代，相似主题也不能证明《约伯记》直接抄写、确定作者或锁定成书年代。
- **Grade:** A for tablet witnesses/Akkadian text/wide transmission; B for righteous-suffering setting; C for dependence/authorship/routes/precise dating.
  **等级**：A（泥版见证、阿卡德文本与多地传播）；B（古代义人受苦问题背景）；C（直接文学依赖、作者、传播路线与精确定年）。
- **Sources:** [CDLI: Ludlul digital composite P491222](https://cdli.earth/artifacts/491222); [Aino Hätinen, KASKAL 2024: Ludlul manuscripts in Ashurbanipal's library](https://doi.org/10.30687/KASKAL/5235-1939/2024/01/019); [SOAS: Tablet II transcription, translation, and reading](https://www.soas.ac.uk/baplar/recordings/poem-righteous-sufferer-ludlul-bel-nemeqi-tablet-ii-lines-1-55-read-gebhard-j-selz)
  **来源**：[CDLI：*Ludlul* 数字合成文本 P491222](https://cdli.earth/artifacts/491222)；[Aino Hätinen：亚述巴尼拔图书馆中的 *Ludlul* 抄本，*KASKAL* 2024](https://doi.org/10.30687/KASKAL/5235-1939/2024/01/019)；[SOAS：泥版 II 转写、翻译与朗读](https://www.soas.ac.uk/baplar/recordings/poem-righteous-sufferer-ludlul-bel-nemeqi-tablet-ii-lines-1-55-read-gebhard-j-selz)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-099"></a>

### 99. Babylonian Theodicy, BM 034633: Dialogue Between Sufferer and Friend | 《巴比伦神义论》BM 034633：受苦者与朋友的对话诗

- **Date / collection:** A major Akkadian witness is British Museum BM 034633 (CDLI P404896), Neo-Babylonian from Babylon, with others from Nineveh/Assur. Extant form: acrostic of 27 stanzas, eleven lines each. Distinguish copy dates and composition.
  **年代／馆藏**：阿卡德文《巴比伦神义论》的主要见证之一为大英博物馆 BM 034633（CDLI P404896），出自巴比伦，属新巴比伦时期；另有尼尼微、亚述等地见证。作品现有形式为二十七节、每节十一行的离合诗；抄本年代与作品形成时间仍须分开。
- **Related Scripture:** Job 3–31, especially debates with Eliphaz, Bildad, Zophar.
  **对应经文**：约伯记 3–31 章，尤其约伯与以利法、比勒达、琐法的辩论。
- **Supports:** Ancient dialogue poetry addressing poverty, injustice, conventional retribution, and unknowable divine intentions. Dialogue and a friend defending order supply real literary comparison.
  **能支持**：古代美索不达米亚已有用“受苦者—朋友”对话处理贫穷、社会不义、传统报应观和诸神旨意难测的智慧诗；对话体与朋友维护秩序的角色，为理解《约伯记》的文学问题提供真实比较环境。
- **Does not establish:** A polytheistic world with different structure, argument, and ending; no direct prototypes for Job's friends, line-by-line borrowing, or date from shared genre alone.
  **不能支持**：文本处在多神世界，结构、论证和结局也与《约伯记》不同；不能把其中朋友当作约伯朋友的直接原型，不能由共同文类推出逐句借用或给《约伯记》定年。
- **Grade:** A for tablets/Akkadian text/genre; B for wisdom debate/theodicy; C for direct dependence/prototypes/precise routes.
  **等级**：A（泥版、阿卡德文本与文体）；B（智慧辩论和神义问题背景）；C（直接依赖、人物原型与精确传播路线）。
- **Sources:** [CDLI: BM 034633／P404896](https://cdli.earth/artifacts/404896); [CDLI: related witnesses and publication record](https://cdli.earth/publications/164845); [Electronic Babylonian Library: critical text](https://www.ebl.lmu.de/corpus/L/2/1)
  **来源**：[CDLI：BM 034633／P404896](https://cdli.earth/artifacts/404896)；[CDLI：相关见证与出版记录](https://cdli.earth/publications/164845)；[电子巴比伦文库：校勘文本](https://www.ebl.lmu.de/corpus/L/2/1)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-100"></a>

### 100. 11Q10 Aramaic Job: Second Temple Translation and Reception | 11Q10《约伯记亚兰文译本》：第二圣殿时期的翻译与接收

- **Date / manuscript:** Cave 11, 11Q10/11QtgJob, Herodian parchment in square Aramaic script. IAA classifies biblical work/translation; surviving portions chiefly Job's latter half.
  **年代／抄本**：昆兰第 11 洞 11Q10（11QtgJob），希律时期羊皮纸抄本，以方体字写成亚兰文；以色列文物局将其归为“圣经作品／经文译本”，现存部分主要保存《约伯记》后半内容。
- **Related Scripture:** Job, especially chapters 17–42: debates, divine response, ending.
  **对应经文**：约伯记，尤其 17–42 章的辩论、神的回答与结尾。
- **Supports:** Aramaic translation of difficult Job amid continued copying/interpretation/use, permitting comparison of Hebrew poetry/theological terminology with translators' choices.
  **能支持**：第二圣殿时期已有读者把《约伯记》翻成亚兰文，显示这卷艰深智慧书持续被抄写、解释和使用；它也让研究者比较翻译者如何处理希伯来诗歌和神学用语。
- **Does not establish:** A translation, not a Hebrew autograph. Herodian copying cannot fix Job's personal era, narrative historicity, or initial composition; interpretive choices do not replace Hebrew automatically.
  **不能支持**：这是译本而非希伯来原稿；希律时期抄本日期不能反推约伯人物年代、叙事事件真实性或本书最初成书日期。译文中的解释选择也不能自动代替希伯来文本。
- **Grade:** A for copy/cave/material/language/type; B for translation/reception; C for inferring author/precise composition date.
  **等级**：A（抄本、洞穴、材料、语言与文本类型）；B（第二圣殿时期接收与翻译史）；C（由译本反推原作作者或精确定年）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority / Leon Levy Library: 11Q10](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/11Q10-1?locale=en_US); [Israel Antiquities Authority: languages and scripts](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/learn-about-the-scrolls/languages-and-scripts); [PLOS ONE: 11Q10 material reconstruction and unrolling damage](https://doi.org/10.1371/journal.pone.0239831)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局 Leon Levy 死海古卷数字图书馆：11Q10](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/11Q10-1?locale=en_US)；[以色列文物局：语言与字体说明](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/learn-about-the-scrolls/languages-and-scripts)；[PLOS ONE：11Q10 物质重建与展开损伤研究](https://doi.org/10.1371/journal.pone.0239831)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-101"></a>

### 101. 11Q5 Great Psalms Scroll: Different Order, Additional Songs, and David | 11Q5《大诗篇卷》：不同排列、额外诗歌与大卫传统

- **Date / manuscript:** Cave 11, 11Q5/11QPsᵃ, Hebrew Herodian parchment dated 1–68 CE. IAA counts 48 poems, seven absent from standard Masoretic Psalms, plus prose describing David as a prolific song author.
  **年代／抄本**：昆兰第 11 洞 11Q5（11QPsᵃ），主后 1–68 年、希律时期的希伯来文羊皮纸抄本。以色列文物局称其保存四十八首诗，其中七首不见于标准马所拉本，并含一段把大卫描述为大量诗歌作者的散文。
