- **Related Scripture:** Psalms, especially the latter collection and Davidic tradition.
  **对应经文**：诗篇，尤其诗篇后半卷及“大卫诗篇”传统。
- **Supports:** Extensive Second Temple copying/recitation linked with David. Different order/scope physically attests collection formation, worship, and textual diversity.
  **能支持**：诗篇在第二圣殿时期已被大量抄写、诵读并与大卫传统相连；11Q5 的诗歌次序和收录范围与马所拉本不完全相同，是研究诗集编集、敬拜用途和文本多样性的直接物证。
- **Does not establish:** Not David's autograph or a decision on each psalm's author/date. Debate continues over alternative canonical form versus liturgical anthology; one scroll cannot establish absence of canon or universal ordering among all Jewish groups.
  **不能支持**：11Q5 不是大卫亲笔，也不能单独决定每首诗的作者和日期。学界仍讨论它是不同正典形态还是礼仪选集；不能从一卷推断所有犹太群体当时“完全没有正典”或都采用同一排列。
- **Grade:** A for copy/date/language/contents; B for compilation/worship/Davidic tradition; C for universal canon forms or individual authors/dates.
  **等级**：A（抄本、年代范围、语言与现存内容）；B（诗集编集、敬拜使用和大卫传统）；C（代表所有群体的正典形态、单篇作者与日期）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: 11Q5 collection record](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/11Q5-1?locale=en_US); [Israel Antiquities Authority: 11Q5 introduction and content counts](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/featured-scrolls/?locale=en_US)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局：11Q5 馆藏页](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/11Q5-1?locale=en_US)；[以色列文物局：11Q5 专题介绍与内容统计](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/featured-scrolls/?locale=en_US)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-102"></a>

### 102. Great Hymn to the Aten: Egyptian Nature-Hymn Parallels to Psalm 104 | 《阿吞大神颂》：诗篇 104 的埃及自然赞歌平行

- **Date / inscription:** Akhenaten's Eighteenth Dynasty; thirteen hieroglyphic columns on the western court wall of Ay's Amarna tomb. Damage after discovery makes Davies' 1908 copy important.
  **年代／铭文**：埃及第十八王朝阿肯那顿时期，阿玛尔那大臣阿伊墓室庭院西墙的十三列象形文字铭文；原刻文发现后已受损，Davies 1908 摹本是重要记录。
- **Related Scripture:** Psalm 104, especially darkness/lions, sunrise/work, plants, birds, ships, fish, life.
  **对应经文**：诗篇 104，特别是黑夜与狮子、日出与劳作、植物、鸟、船只、鱼和万物得生命的段落。
- **Supports:** A sustained Egyptian solar hymn connecting night, labor, animals, rivers, and life supply. Dense imagery parallels support comparable Egyptian–Levantine nature-hymn language and possible literary contact.
  **能支持**：埃及宗教诗歌确有以太阳运行串联黑夜、日间劳作、动物、江河与生命供应的完整赞歌；它与诗篇 104 的密集图景相似，说明古代埃及—黎凡特之间存在可比较的自然赞歌语言，也容许研究文学接触的可能。
- **Does not establish:** Direction/routes remain disputed. Imagery alone cannot date Psalm 104 or prove copying. Aten/light/royal mediation differs from Psalm 104's YHWH worship and created sun; retain theological distinctions.
  **不能支持**：具体传播方向和路径有争论；相似图景不能单独给诗篇 104 定年或证明直接抄写。《大神颂》把阿吞、光与王室中介相连，诗篇则敬拜耶和华并把太阳置于受造秩序中，不能抹平两者神学差异。
- **Grade:** A for tomb text/Amarna setting; B for Egyptian background/comparison; C for dependence/routes/precise dating.
  **等级**：A（墓室铭文、文本与阿玛尔那处境）；B（诗篇 104 的埃及背景与文学比较）；C（直接依赖、传播路线和精确定年）。
- **Sources:** [UCL Digital Egypt: inscription location, transcription, translation](https://www.ucl.ac.uk/museums-static/digitalegypt/amarna/belief.html); [Oxford Handbook of the Psalms: Egyptian Backgrounds to the Psalms](https://academic.oup.com/edited-volume/35006/chapter-abstract/298739466)
  **来源**：[UCL Digital Egypt：铭文位置、转写与翻译](https://www.ucl.ac.uk/museums-static/digitalegypt/amarna/belief.html)；[Oxford Handbook of the Psalms：Egyptian Backgrounds to the Psalms](https://academic.oup.com/edited-volume/35006/chapter-abstract/298739466)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-103"></a>

### 103. Ugaritic KTU 1.2 / AO 16640: Storm, Sea, and Royal Poetic Language | 乌加列 KTU 1.2／AO 16640：风暴、海洋与王权诗语

- **Date / collection:** Louvre AO 16640, RS 3.367/RS 3.346, KTU 1.2; found in 1931 at Ras Shamra's High Priest's House. Late Bronze II, around 1400–1200 BCE, Ugaritic cuneiform, Baal Cycle.
  **年代／馆藏**：卢浮宫 AO 16640，发掘号 RS 3.367／RS 3.346，目录 KTU 1.2；1931 年出土于拉斯沙姆拉古乌加列“大祭司之家”，属晚青铜时代 II（约主前 1400–1200 年）的乌加列语楔形文字泥版，是巴力循环的一部分。
- **Related Scripture:** Psalms 29, 74:12–17, 89:9–15, 93; Job 26:12–13 comparison.
  **对应经文**：诗篇 29、74:12–17、89:9–15、93 篇；亦可比较约伯记 26:12–13。
- **Supports:** Storm, waters, sea deity, chaos enemy, and divine kingship as Northwest Semitic poetic resources, illuminating Psalm 29's storm geography and Psalms 74/89's sea-monster imagery.
  **能支持**：以风暴、众水、海神、混沌敌手和神圣王权组织诗歌，是以色列周边西北闪族世界已有的语言资源；乌加列文本帮助解释诗篇 29 的雷暴地理，以及诗篇 74、89 中制服海与怪兽的意象。
- **Does not establish:** Shared poetic stock, reuse, and polemical rewriting need poem-specific arguments. No equation of YHWH with Baal, claim that Psalms worship Baal, or automatic direct-tablet copying. Psalms' exclusive YHWH sovereignty supplies a different framework.
  **不能支持**：共同诗歌库存、文学重用和论战性改写需要逐篇论证；不能据此说耶和华就是巴力、诗篇在敬拜巴力，或断言某篇直接抄写某块泥版。诗篇把这些力量置于耶和华独有主权下，神学框架不同。
- **Grade:** A for tablet/collection/language/findspot/Baal text; B for Northwest Semitic poetry; C for direct dependence/routes/original-form reconstruction.
  **等级**：A（泥版、馆藏、语言、出土点与巴力神话文本）；B（西北闪族诗歌背景）；C（某篇直接依赖、传播路线与原始形式重建）。
- **Sources:** [Louvre: AO 16640 / KTU 1.2](https://collections.louvre.fr/ark:/53355/cl010141442); [Joanna Töyräänvuori, Biblische Notizen 186: Psalm 29, Ugaritic texts, and imagery](https://researchportal.helsinki.fi/en/publications/psalm-29-and-methodological-triangulation-what-can-ugaritic-paral/)
  **来源**：[卢浮宫：AO 16640／KTU 1.2](https://collections.louvre.fr/ark:/53355/cl010141442)；[Joanna Töyräänvuori：诗篇 29、乌加列文本与图像比较，*Biblische Notizen* 186](https://researchportal.helsinki.fi/en/publications/psalm-29-and-methodological-triangulation-what-can-ugaritic-paral/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-104"></a>

### 104. 1 Corinthians 15:3–8 and P46: Early Resurrection-Appearance Tradition | 林前 15:3–8 与 P46：早期复活—显现传统

- **Date / text and manuscripts:** 1 Corinthians usually mid-50s CE; 15:3 introduces received/transmitted tradition about Christ's death, burial, resurrection, and appearances to Cephas/others. P46, commonly late second–early third century, preserves the letter/chapter, not Paul's autograph.
  **年代／文本与抄本**：《哥林多前书》通常置于主后 50 年代中期；15:3 以“领受—传递”引入基督受死、埋葬、复活和向矶法等人显现的传统。现存 P46 一般约在主后 2 世纪末至 3 世纪初，保存这封信及其第 15 章，但不是保罗原稿。
- **Related Scripture:** 1 Corinthians 15:1–11, 20–28, 35–58; compare Gospel resurrection narratives and Acts 1.
  **对应经文**：哥林多前书 15:1–11、20–28、35–58；可与四福音复活叙事和使徒行传 1 章并读。
- **Supports:** When writing, Paul treats the proclamation as shared tradition received earlier and passed to Corinth: an early datable Christian death–burial–resurrection–appearance witness. Linguistic/structural research widely recognizes pre-Pauline material; P46 attests its later entry into an early Pauline collection.
  **能支持**：保罗写信时已经把复活宣告当作自己早先领受、又传给哥林多人的共同传统；这使死亡—埋葬—复活—显现链成为现存可较早定年的基督教见证之一。语言和结构研究广泛承认有前保罗材料，P46 则直接见证该文本后来进入早期保罗书信集。
- **Does not establish:** Debate remains over whether the tradition ends at 15:5 or extends through 6–7, its origin place/time, and Paul's editorial words. Exact wording fixed two or three years after the cross is not a manuscript-supplied date. No empty-tomb scene or independently checkable list of 500 people. P46's manufacture dates neither the letter nor resurrection evidence.
  **不能支持**：学界对传统只到 15:5 还是延伸到 15:6–7、何时何地形成及哪些词属于保罗编辑仍有争论；“十字架后两三年已经逐字定型”不是抄本自带日期。保罗没有在此叙述空墓场景，也未提供五百多人名单供独立核查；P46 的制作年代不能代替书信写作年代，更不是复活的考古物证。
- **Grade:** A for text/P46/received-transmitted wording; A/B for Paul's earlier tradition; C for exact wording/place/year; D for calling P46 physical proof of resurrection.
  **等级**：A（林前 15 文本、P46 抄本及“领受—传递”措辞）；A／B（保罗传递较早的复活—显现传统）；C（传统逐词范围、地点与精确形成年份）；D（把 P46 称为复活事件物证）。
- **Sources:** [CSNTM: P46 digital manuscript and catalogue](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/Manuscript/Group/GA_P46); [E. Earle Ellis: *Traditions in I Corinthians*](https://doi.org/10.1017/S002868850001417X); [James Ware: resurrection in the pre-Pauline 1 Corinthians 15:3–5 formula](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0028688514000150)
  **来源**：[CSNTM：P46 数字抄本与目录](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/Manuscript/Group/GA_P46)；[E. Earle Ellis：*Traditions in I Corinthians*](https://doi.org/10.1017/S002868850001417X)；[James Ware：林前 15:3–5 前保罗公式中的复活](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0028688514000150)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-105"></a>

### 105. Sinaiticus and Vaticanus: Mark Ending at 16:8 | 西奈抄本与梵蒂冈抄本：马可福音在 16:8 结束

- **Date / manuscript:** Fourth-century GA 01 has Mark 16:1–8 followed by Luke on one leaf; Vaticanus Vat. gr. 1209/GA 03 has a closing title, blank column, then Luke on the next page. Others have shorter, longer 16:9–20, or both endings. Irenaeus already quotes 16:19 in the late second century, establishing the longer ending's antiquity.
  **年代／抄本**：四世纪希腊文西奈抄本 GA 01 在同一叶保存可 16:1–8，随后开始路加福音；四世纪梵蒂冈抄本 Vat. gr. 1209／GA 03 在可 16:8 后写卷尾题记，留下一栏空白，路加福音从下一页开始。其他见证保存较短结尾、可 16:9–20 的较长结尾或两者并列；爱任纽二世纪末已引用可 16:19，说明较长结尾很古老。
- **Related Scripture:** Mark 16; compare Matthew 28, Luke 24, John 20–21, Acts 1.
  **对应经文**：马可福音 16 章；并读马太福音 28 章、路加福音 24 章、约翰福音 20–21 章、使徒行传 1 章。
- **Supports:** Two crucial fourth-century complete Greek Mark witnesses end at 16:8. The 2021 Editio Critica Maior: Mark and recent criticism still identify 16:8 as the earliest attainable ending. Variation justifies modern textual footnotes on 16:9–20.
  **能支持**：两部最重要的四世纪完整马可福音希腊文见证停在 16:8；2021 年 *Editio Critica Maior: Mark* 及近期文本批判研究仍把 16:8 视为最早可达到的文本结尾。抄本的不同结尾让现代译本给 16:9–20 加文本脚注有充分根据。
- **Does not establish:** The longer ending is not simply medieval fabrication. Its disputed status does not remove Mark's/New Testament resurrection proclamation: 16:1–8 already has empty tomb and resurrection news. Intentional ending versus earlier lost ending remains a literary/textual question.
  **不能支持**：不能把较长结尾简单称为中世纪伪造，也不能因其文本地位有争议就说马可或新约没有复活宣告；可 16:1–8 本身已有空墓与“已经复活”的信息。16:8 是否为马可有意结尾，或更早结尾已经遗失，仍属文学与文本史讨论。
- **Grade:** A for actual pages; A/B for editorial earliest-attainable-text judgment; C for the author's original ending/intention.
  **等级**：A（两部抄本在可 16:8 的实际页况）；A／B（最早可达到文本的编辑判断）；C（作者原始结尾的形成史与意图）。
- **Sources:** [Codex Sinaiticus: Mark 16:1–8 / Luke 1:1–18 on one leaf](https://codexsinaiticus.org/en/manuscript.aspx?book=35); [MARK16: Vaticanus GA 03 ending](https://mr-mark16.sib.swiss/manuscript/GA03); [H. A. G. Houghton: Mark's ending and manuscript context](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0028688522000224); [Irenaeus: Against Heresies, Book III](https://www.ccel.org/ccel/irenaeus/against_heresies_iii.html)
  **来源**：[Codex Sinaiticus：可 16:1–8／路 1:1–18 同页](https://codexsinaiticus.org/en/manuscript.aspx?book=35)；[MARK16：梵蒂冈抄本 GA 03 卷尾](https://mr-mark16.sib.swiss/manuscript/GA03)；[H. A. G. Houghton：马可结尾与抄本语境](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0028688522000224)；[爱任纽《反异端》卷三](https://www.ccel.org/ccel/irenaeus/against_heresies_iii.html)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-106"></a>

### 106. Holy Sepulchre Subsurface Remains: Quarrying, Burials, and Traditional Location | 圣墓教堂地下遗存：采石、丧葬与传统地点的边界

- **Date / site:** Recent Jerusalem excavation identifies prolonged quarrying, then agricultural/burial reuse beneath the church; identifiable large church construction begins under Constantine in the fourth century. The traditional chamber/surroundings underwent Roman, Byzantine, medieval, and modern alterations.
  **年代／遗址**：耶路撒冷圣墓教堂近年发掘确认，现建筑下方首先经历长期采石，随后出现农业和丧葬再利用；四世纪君士坦丁时期才有可明确识别的大规模教堂工程。传统墓室及周边经过罗马、拜占庭、中世纪和近现代多次改建。
- **Related Scripture:** Matthew 27:57–28:10; Mark 15:42–16:8; Luke 23:50–24:12; John 19:38–20:18.
  **对应经文**：马太福音 27:57–28:10；马可福音 15:42–16:8；路加福音 23:50–24:12；约翰福音 19:38–20:18。
- **Supports:** Pre-Christian quarrying/burials compatible with the broad outside-city rock-tomb setting, plus traceable fourth-century/later memorial works; current appearance is not first-century original form.
  **能支持**：地点在基督教建筑以前确有采石与丧葬用途，较早墓地环境与福音书“城外近处岩墓”的基本背景相容；考古也可追踪四世纪纪念建筑及以后改造，而不是把现状误当第一世纪原貌。
- **Does not establish:** Strata cannot uniquely identify Joseph of Arimathea's tomb or fill every pre-third-century traditional link. No undisturbed first-century tomb today. Even a correct traditional location cannot archaeologically test the empty tomb's divine resurrection cause.
  **不能支持**：遗址地层不能唯一认定某一墓就是亚利马太约瑟的新墓，也不能填补三世纪以前每一环传统链；今日所见不是未经扰动的第一世纪墓。即使传统地点认定成立，空墓和复活的神圣原因也不能由建筑遗存直接检验。
- **Grade:** A for quarry/burial reuse/fourth-century works; B for cemetery compatibility; B/C for preserved memory of Jesus' tomb; D for direct resurrection proof.
  **等级**：A（采石、丧葬再利用与四世纪建筑阶段）；B（与早期墓地环境相容）；B／C（地点传统保存耶稣墓记忆）；D（由遗址直接证明复活）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: Holy Sepulchre excavation, 2022–2023](https://hadashot.iaa.org.il/Report_Detail_Eng.aspx?id=26542); [Sapienza University: preliminary excavation report, 2025](https://iris.uniroma1.it/handle/11573/1763575); [Biblical Archaeology Society: site-phase overview](https://www.biblicalarchaeology.org/daily/biblical-sites-places/jerusalem/investigating-the-church-of-the-holy-sepulchre/)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局：2022–2023 年圣墓教堂发掘初报](https://hadashot.iaa.org.il/Report_Detail_Eng.aspx?id=26542)；[Sapienza University：2025 年发掘初报](https://iris.uniroma1.it/handle/11573/1763575)；[Biblical Archaeology Society：遗址阶段概述](https://www.biblicalarchaeology.org/daily/biblical-sites-places/jerusalem/investigating-the-church-of-the-holy-sepulchre/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-107"></a>

### 107. Nazareth Inscription, Froehner.VIII.13: Burial Protection, Not a Resurrection Decree | “拿撒勒敕令” Froehner.VIII.13：墓葬法令而非复活诏书

- **Date / collection and provenance chain:** Bibliothèque nationale de France Froehner.VIII.13, formerly L. Robert 70: white marble, 22 Greek lines, provisionally first century. Collector recorded only sent from Nazareth in 1878, without controlled excavation. A 2020 isotope study favors upper Kos quarries and proposes an Augustan response to damage to tyrant Nikias' tomb.
  **年代／馆藏与来源链**：法国国家图书馆藏 Froehner.VIII.13（旧号 L. Robert 70），白色大理石、二十二行希腊文；馆藏暂列第一世纪。收藏者只记 1878 年“由拿撒勒寄来”，没有现代考古发掘语境。2020 年稳定同位素分析认为石材最可能来自科斯岛上部采石场，并提出奥古斯都回应科斯僭主尼基亚斯墓被毁的解释。
- **Related Scripture:** Matthew 27:62–28:15's sealing/stolen-body dispute only for correcting later misconceptions; also Roman burial-protection language.
  **对应经文**：马太福音 27:62–28:15 的封墓与盗尸争论，只能作为后世误传辨析；亦可照明罗马墓葬保护语言。
- **Supports:** A severe Roman Greek prohibition against moving corpses, seals, or tombstones. Marble science constrains provenance and strongly weakens direct Nazareth/early-Christian connections.
  **能支持**：罗马帝国有严厉禁止移动尸体、封墓构件和墓石的希腊文敕令；石材科学分析为重建产地提供新约束，并强烈削弱其与拿撒勒或早期基督教直接相关的旧说。
- **Does not establish:** Shipped from Nazareth is not a secure findspot; Kos marble alone cannot precisely date the decree. No Jesus, Christians, empty tomb, theft, or resurrection; not an emperor's response to resurrection news. Nikias/Augustus remains historical reconstruction despite explanatory strength.
  **不能支持**：“由拿撒勒寄来”不是可靠出土地点；石材来自科斯也不能单独给敕令精确定年。它没有提耶稣、基督徒、空墓、盗尸或复活，不能称为罗马皇帝听闻复活后颁布的诏书。科斯尼基亚斯解释与奥古斯都定年虽有较好解释力，仍是历史重建而非铭文自述。
- **Grade:** A for inscription/collection/content/isotopes; B for Kos marble; B/C for Nikias/Augustan reconstruction; D for direct resurrection linkage.
  **等级**：A（铭文、馆藏、内容及石材同位素）；B（科斯石材来源）；B／C（尼基亚斯事件与奥古斯都处境重建）；D（与耶稣复活直接相连）。
- **Sources:** [Bibliothèque nationale de France: Froehner.VIII.13](https://medaillesetantiques.bnf.fr/ws/catalogue/app/collection/record/ark%3A/12148/c33gb6sb); [Kyle Harper et al.: stable isotopes and marble provenance](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jasrep.2020.102228)
  **来源**：[法国国家图书馆：Froehner.VIII.13](https://medaillesetantiques.bnf.fr/ws/catalogue/app/collection/record/ark%3A/12148/c33gb6sb)；[Kyle Harper 等：稳定同位素与石材来源研究](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jasrep.2020.102228)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-108"></a>

### 108. Tacitus, Annals 15.44: Execution and Expansion, Not Resurrection | 塔西佗《编年史》15.44：处决与运动扩展，不是复活记载

- **Date / document:** Senator/historian Tacitus wrote the relevant books around 116 CE. Amid Nero's fire narrative, 15.44 places Christus' execution under Pontius Pilate/Tiberius, the movement's spread from Judea to Rome, and Nero's later punishment of Christians.
  **年代／文献**：罗马元老、史家塔西佗约在主后 116 年写成《编年史》相关卷次；15.44 在尼禄火灾叙事中说“Christus”于提庇留年间被本丢·彼拉多处以极刑，此运动从犹太传到罗马，尼禄后来惩罚被称为基督徒的人。
- **Related Scripture:** Gospel passion accounts; Acts 1–28 expansion, only indirectly alongside resurrection proclamation's historical consequences.
  **对应经文**：四福音受难叙事；使徒行传 1–28 章所述运动扩展；只可间接放在复活宣告的历史后果旁。
- **Supports:** A hostile early-second-century Roman author connects the movement's name to an executed Christus under Pilate and its later presence in Judea/Rome: an important non-Christian source for execution and Roman Christian conditions.
  **能支持**：一位对基督徒明显不友善的二世纪初罗马作者，把运动名称连到一位在彼拉多时期被处决的 Christus，并认为该运动后来在犹太和罗马出现；它是研究耶稣被处决及早期基督徒罗马处境的重要非基督教文本。
- **Does not establish:** No empty tomb, third day, appearances, or ascension, and no specified source among official archives, hearings, Pliny, Josephus, or common knowledge. Recent debate over sources/Neronian punishment prevents claims of independent Roman archival resurrection verification.
  **不能支持**：塔西佗没有提空墓、第三日、显现或升天；也没有说明资料来自官方档案、审讯、普林尼、约瑟夫斯还是流行知识。近期研究仍争论其信息来源和尼禄惩罚基督徒叙事，不能把它写成“罗马档案独立验证复活”。
- **Grade:** A for extant text/hostility; A/B for execution/movement testimony; C for sources/independence/Neronian details; D for direct external resurrection proof.
  **等级**：A（现传《编年史》文本及其敌意语境）；A／B（罗马史家关于处决和运动存在的见证）；C（资料来源、独立程度及尼禄事件细节）；D（复活的外部直接证明）。
- **Sources:** [Perseus: Tacitus Annals 15.44, text and English translation](https://www.perseus.tufts.edu/hopper/text?doc=Tac.+Ann.+15.44); [Christopher M. Hansen: recent challenge on Tacitus' sources](https://doi.org/10.1080/2222582X.2023.2173628)
  **来源**：[Perseus：塔西佗《编年史》15.44 原文与英译](https://www.perseus.tufts.edu/hopper/text?doc=Tac.+Ann.+15.44)；[Christopher M. Hansen：塔西佗资料来源的近期异议](https://doi.org/10.1080/2222582X.2023.2173628)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-109"></a>

### 109. 4Q109/4Q110 Ecclesiastes: Early Copies and Latest Possible Existence | 4Q109／4Q110《传道书》：现存早期抄本与成书下限

- **Date / manuscript:** Cave 4 Hebrew fragments: 4Q109/4QQohᵃ partly chapters 5–7, usually around 175–150 BCE paleographically; tiny 4Q110/4QQohᵇ around 1:10–14, usually later Herodian with no overly narrow interval.
  **年代／抄本**：昆兰第 4 洞两份希伯来文《传道书》残卷。4Q109（4QQohᵃ）保存 5–7 章部分经文，古文字学通常定为约主前 175–150 年；4Q110（4QQohᵇ）只保存 1:10–14 左右的很小片段，通常归入较晚的希律时期，具体范围不宜过窄。
- **Related Scripture:** Ecclesiastes 1, 5–7; whole-book transmission/date discussion.
  **对应经文**：传道书 1、5–7 章；并可用于全书文本传播与日期讨论。
- **Supports:** 4Q109 as an earliest surviving witness shows the book formed/copied by the earlier second century BCE. Both enable Hebrew/Masoretic/ancient-version comparison.
  **能支持**：4Q109 是现存最早的《传道书》见证之一，说明本书最迟在主前二世纪前半已经成形并被抄写；两份残卷也让研究者把早期希伯来文本与马所拉本、古译本作比较。
- **Does not establish:** Latest attested existence does not establish how much earlier composition was or decide Solomonic authorship. Incomplete witnesses/variants establish neither word-for-word invariance nor absence of a recognizable Ecclesiastes text.
  **不能支持**：抄本给出的是“最迟已经存在”的界线，不说明作品究竟早了多少年；不能单凭它们证明或否定所罗门作者说。残卷不完整且有细部异文，既不能证明“每个字从未变化”，也不能据此说当时没有可辨认的《传道书》文本。
- **Grade:** A for copies/cave/language/passages/paleographic ranges; B for circulation by the early second century BCE; C for initial date/authors/layers/overly narrow 4Q110 dating.
  **等级**：A（抄本、洞穴、语言、现存经文和古文字日期范围）；B（本书在主前二世纪前半以前已流传）；C（初创年代、作者、编集阶段与 4Q110 过窄定年）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: 4Q109](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q109-1?locale=en_US); [Israel Antiquities Authority: 4Q110](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q110-1?locale=en_US); [James Muilenburg: first 4Q109 study](https://doi.org/10.2307/1355543); [Hebrew University: publication record, Qumran Cave 4 XI: Psalms to Chronicles](https://cris.huji.ac.il/en/publications/psalms-to-chronicles/)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局：4Q109](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q109-1?locale=en_US)；[以色列文物局：4Q110](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q110-1?locale=en_US)；[James Muilenburg：4Q109 首版研究](https://doi.org/10.2307/1355543)；[希伯来大学：*Qumran Cave 4 XI: Psalms to Chronicles* 出版记录](https://cris.huji.ac.il/en/publications/psalms-to-chronicles/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-110"></a>

### 110. OB VA+BM Siduri's Advice: Enjoyment Under Death and Ecclesiastes 9 | OB VA+BM 西杜丽劝告：死亡下的享受与传道书 9 章

- **Date / collection:** Old Babylonian Gilgamesh Meissner–Millard tablet, direct join of Berlin VAT 4105 and British Museum BM 96974, conventionally seventeenth century BCE. Dealer claimed Sippar, without controlled excavation.
  **年代／馆藏**：古巴比伦《吉尔伽美什》梅斯纳—米拉德泥版，由柏林 Vorderasiatisches Museum VAT 4105 与大英博物馆 BM 96974 两片直接拼合，学术缩写 OB VA+BM；常约定为主前 17 世纪。来源商称来自锡帕，但没有现代控制发掘语境。
- **Related Scripture:** Ecclesiastes 2:24–26, 3:12–14, 5:18–20, 9:7–10, especially 9:7–9's food, drink, white clothes, beloved companion.
  **对应经文**：传道书 2:24–26、3:12–14、5:18–20、9:7–10；尤其 9:7–9 的食物、饮酒、洁白衣服与所爱伴侣。
- **Supports:** Old Babylonian narrative advice to eat/drink happily, keep clothes clean, and enjoy child/partner under inevitable death: a concrete ancient literary parallel.
  **能支持**：古巴比伦智慧叙事确有在死亡不可逃避的前提下，劝人吃喝欢喜、衣服洁净、享受孩子与伴侣的段落；这一意象组合与传道书 9 章构成真正、具体的古代文学平行。
- **Does not establish:** This advice survives only in this Old Babylonian version; the Standard Babylonian passage was revised. Shared motif/contact/closer relation remains possible, not direct Ecclesiastes translation or a fixed route/date. Ecclesiastes frames enjoyment through God's allotted gift, work, reverence, and judgment.
  **不能支持**：西杜丽劝告只见于这一古巴比伦版本，标准巴比伦版已改写相关段落，不能把各版《吉尔伽美什》混为一谈。相似可能来自共同母题、文学接触或更具体关系，却不能单独证明传道书直接翻译泥版、锁定传播路线或成书年代；传道书还把享受置于神所赐的分、工作、敬畏与审判中。
- **Grade:** A for joins/Akkadian text/content; B for death–enjoyment imagery; C for dependence/routes/date; D for identical theology.
  **等级**：A（泥版拼合、阿卡德文本与段落内容）；B（死亡—享受母题和密集意象的比较）；C（直接依赖、传播路线与精确定年）；D（两部作品神学相同）。
- **Sources:** [Electronic Babylonian Library: OB VA+BM](https://www.ebl.lmu.de/corpus/L/1/4/OB/VA+BM); [A. R. Millard: first BM 96974 edition](https://doi.org/10.2307/4199768); [Matthew Suriano: kingship/carpe diem in Siduri and Ecclesiastes](https://doi.org/10.1163/15685330-12341276); [Rim Nurullin: Akkadian poetics of Siduri's advice](https://doi.org/10.31857/S032103910022122-7)
  **来源**：[电子巴比伦文库：OB VA+BM](https://www.ebl.lmu.de/corpus/L/1/4/OB/VA+BM)；[A. R. Millard：BM 96974 首版研究](https://doi.org/10.2307/4199768)；[Matthew Suriano：西杜丽与传道书的王权、及时行乐比较](https://doi.org/10.1163/15685330-12341276)；[Rim Nurullin：西杜丽劝告的阿卡德诗歌分析](https://doi.org/10.31857/S032103910022122-7)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-111"></a>

### 111. 4Q106, 4Q107, 4Q108, and 6Q6: Four Qumran Song of Songs Copies | 4Q106、4Q107、4Q108 与 6Q6：四份昆兰《雅歌》残卷

- **Date / manuscript:** Hebrew fragments 4QCantᵃ/ᵇ/ᶜ from Cave 4 and 6Q6 from Cave 6. Mostly late Second Temple paleographic dates; each range/scroll form requires separate discussion.
  **年代／抄本**：第 4 洞的 4Q106（4QCantᵃ）、4Q107（4QCantᵇ）、4Q108（4QCantᶜ），以及第 6 洞的 6Q6，均为希伯来文《雅歌》残卷；古文字日期多落在第二圣殿晚期，具体范围与各卷形态需逐件讨论。
- **Related Scripture:** Song of Songs, fragments across chapters 1–7.
  **对应经文**：雅歌，现存残片分布于第 1–7 章若干段落。
- **Supports:** Four physical copies directly establish late Second Temple copying/preservation/circulation. Text, blank spaces, and variants inform forms/possible compilation history.
  **能支持**：四份物质抄本直接证明《雅歌》在第二圣殿晚期被抄写、保存和流传；残卷内容、空白与异文可用于讨论早期文本形态和可能的编集史。
- **Does not establish:** Qumran discovery does not reveal its community's interpretation of love or universal canonical forms. Gaps may be damage, selection, or differing forms, not automatically deliberate deletion; no proof of Solomon's authorship.
  **不能支持**：在昆兰发现不等于已知昆兰群体怎样解释爱情内容，也不自动证明所有犹太群体采用同一正典形态。残片缺文可能来自损坏、节选或不同文本形态，不能未经论证就称为删改；抄本也不证明所罗门作者身份。
- **Grade:** A for four copies/caves/language/text; B for transmission/comparison; C for whole-scroll shape/layers/community exegesis/universal canon.
  **等级**：A（四份抄本、洞穴、语言和保存文本）；B（第二圣殿晚期抄传与文本比较）；C（完整卷形、编集阶段、群体解释与普遍正典地位）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: 4Q106](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q106-1?locale=en_US); [4Q107](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q107-1?locale=en_US); [4Q108](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q108-1?locale=en_US); [6Q6](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/6Q6-1?locale=en_US); [Émile Puech: new edition of four manuscripts](https://doi.org/10.2143/RBI.123.1.3132129)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局：4Q106](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q106-1?locale=en_US)；[4Q107](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q107-1?locale=en_US)；[4Q108](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q108-1?locale=en_US)；[6Q6](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/6Q6-1?locale=en_US)；[Émile Puech：四份抄本新校勘](https://doi.org/10.2143/RBI.123.1.3132129)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-112"></a>

### 112. Papyrus Harris 500 / EA 10060: Egyptian Love Songs and Song of Songs' Genre | Papyrus Harris 500／EA 10060：古埃及爱情歌与《雅歌》文类

- **Date / collection:** British Museum EA 10060, registration 1872,1101.2, Egyptian hieratic papyrus catalogued Eighteenth Dynasty. Four love-song groups and Song of the Harper from Intef's tomb on front; Capture of Joppa/Doomed Prince and other stories on back.
  **年代／馆藏**：大英博物馆 EA 10060，登记号 1872,1101.2，通称 Papyrus Harris 500；来自埃及的僧侣体纸草，博物馆目录列为第十八王朝。正面保存四组爱情歌与《安特夫墓竖琴师之歌》，背面保存《夺取约帕》和《注定的王子》等故事。
- **Related Scripture:** Song of Songs, especially alternating lovers, bodily praise, scent, garden, river, nature.
  **对应经文**：雅歌全卷，尤其恋人交替发声、身体赞美、香气、花园、河流和自然景物。
- **Supports:** Egyptian collections voiced by human lovers with body/nature imagery and desire; Harris 500 and other songs establish a real ancient Near Eastern love-poetry genre.
  **能支持**：古埃及确有以人类恋人声音、身体—自然比喻和渴望为核心的爱情诗集；Harris 500 与其他埃及爱情歌共同证明《雅歌》所处的古代近东爱情诗文类真实存在。
- **Does not establish:** No Solomon, Israel, or YHWH. Copy date, poetic formation, and Song of Songs' date differ. Shared genre/images prove neither copying nor mandatory divine-marriage ritual, wedding chronicle, or purely secular classification.
  **不能支持**：纸草没有提所罗门、以色列或耶和华；纸草抄写年代、诗歌形成时间和《雅歌》日期不能压成同一年代。共享文类和意象不能单独证明直接抄写，也不能把《雅歌》强制归为神婚仪式、婚礼实录或单纯世俗作品。
- **Grade:** A for papyrus/collection/script/content; B for love-song genre/comparison; C for routes/dependence/date; D for divine-marriage ritual solely from parallels.
  **等级**：A（纸草、馆藏、文字与文本内容）；B（古代爱情诗文类和比较背景）；C（具体传播链、直接依赖和《雅歌》精确定年）；D（仅凭平行认定神婚礼仪）。
- **Sources:** [British Museum: EA 10060 / Papyrus Harris 500](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/Y_EA10060); [Antonio Loprieno: shared backgrounds of Egyptian love poetry and Song of Songs](https://doi.org/10.1515/9783110897012.105); [J. L. Andruska: lovers in ancient Near Eastern love-song genres](https://doi.org/10.1093/jts/flab019)
  **来源**：[大英博物馆：EA 10060／Papyrus Harris 500](https://www.britishmuseum.org/collection/object/Y_EA10060)；[Antonio Loprieno：埃及爱情诗与《雅歌》的共同背景](https://doi.org/10.1515/9783110897012.105)；[J. L. Andruska：古代近东爱情歌文类中的恋人](https://doi.org/10.1093/jts/flab019)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-113"></a>

### 113. 4Q174 Eschatological Commentary A: David's Covenant and Psalms 1–2 | 4Q174《末世释经 A》：大卫之约与诗篇 1–2 的组合接收

- **Date / manuscript:** Cave 4 Hebrew parchment 4Q174, IAA's Herodian-script Pesher / Eschatological Commentary A, formerly 4QFlorilegium.
  **年代／抄本**：昆兰第 4 洞希伯来文羊皮纸残卷 4Q174；以色列文物局现题为 4Q Eschatological Commentary A，目录将字体归为希律时期，文类列为 *Pesher*（释经）。旧研究常称 4QFlorilegium。
- **Related Scripture:** 2 Samuel 7:10–14; Psalms 1:1, 2:1–2 and David/anointed king, alongside Exodus 15, Amos 9:11, others.
  **对应经文**：撒母耳记下 7:10–14；诗篇 1:1、2:1–2 及大卫／受膏君王主题；另组合出埃及记 15 章、阿摩司书 9:11 等经文。
- **Supports:** At least one Second Temple Jewish interpretive community read Davidic promise, opening Psalms, and prophets in a final/Davidic framework: pre-Christian royal-psalm rereading and context for Hebrews combining Psalm 2/2 Samuel 7.
  **能支持**：这份实体残卷直接见证，第二圣殿时期至少有一个犹太释经群体把大卫后裔应许、诗篇开篇和先知文本放进末世／大卫式框架阅读。它为“王权诗在新约以前已经发生弥赛亚或末世重读”提供接收史实例，也帮助比较希伯来书把诗篇 2 与撒母耳记下 7 章并置的释经环境。
- **Does not establish:** Damaged text, joins/paragraph relations, and links with 4Q177 remain debated. No universal Jewish messianism, equivalence with New Testament Christology, or proof Hebrews copied this scroll; no contemporary object for David, original Psalms, or Jesus' events.
  **不能支持**：残卷破碎，具体行文复原、段落关系及其与 4Q177 的关系仍有讨论。它不能证明所有犹太人共享同一弥赛亚观，不能把昆兰的“大卫苗裔”图式等同新约基督论，也不能证明希伯来书直接抄用此卷；更不是大卫之约、诗篇原作或耶稣事件的同期物证。
- **Grade:** A for copy/cave/language/genre/extant combinations; B for Davidic/final reception; C for restoration/full local doctrine/representativeness; D for direct New Testament source or original-event proof.
  **等级**：A（抄本、洞穴、语言、释经文类及保存的经文组合）；B（第二圣殿时期大卫／末世接收实例）；C（残文复原、群体内完整教义及广泛代表性）；D（直接成为新约来源或证明原事件）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: 4Q174, Eschatological Commentary A](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q174-1); [George J. Brooke: *Exegesis at Qumran: 4Q Florilegium in Its Jewish Context*](https://www.bloomsbury.com/us/exegesis-at-qumran-4q-florilegium-in-i-9780567441645/); [George J. Brooke: 2 Samuel 7 and Psalm 2 combined in 4Q174](https://doi.org/10.5325/j.ctv14gpcwm.6)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局：4Q174，4Q Eschatological Commentary A](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q174-1)；[George J. Brooke：*Exegesis at Qumran: 4Q Florilegium in Its Jewish Context*](https://www.bloomsbury.com/us/exegesis-at-qumran-4q-florilegium-in-i-9780567441645/)；[George J. Brooke：4Q174 中撒下 7 与诗 2 的组合](https://doi.org/10.5325/j.ctv14gpcwm.6)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-114"></a>

### 114. Josephus, Antiquities 4.219: Women's Testimony in Formal Legal Settings | 约瑟夫斯《犹太古史》4.219：女性证词的正式法律语境

- **Date / document:** Josephus completed Greek Antiquities around 93/94 CE. Book 4 retells/interprets Mosaic law, requiring two/three reliable witnesses; 4.219 adds exclusion of women/slaves. This is his era's legal expression, not first-century court files.
  **年代／文献**：约瑟夫斯约在主后 93/94 年完成希腊文《犹太古史》。第 4 卷以重述、解释摩西律法的方式要求至少两三名可靠见证人，4.219 又说不接纳妇女和奴仆作证；这是作者及其时代的法律表述，不是第一世纪法庭卷宗。
- **Related Scripture:** Deuteronomy 19:15; Matthew 27:55–28:10; Mark 15:40–16:8; Luke 23:49–24:12; John 19:25, 20:1–18.
  **对应经文**：申命记 19:15；马太福音 27:55–28:10；马可福音 15:40–16:8；路加福音 23:49–24:12；约翰福音 19:25、20:1–18。
- **Supports:** At least one late-first-century Jewish male elite states formal testimony as excluding women, illuminating tensions when Gospel women first connect cross, burial, and empty tomb in ancient public-credibility settings.
  **能支持**：至少一位第一世纪末犹太男性精英把正式法庭证词陈述为排除妇女；这一文本可帮助讨论福音书让妇女首先连接十字架、安葬和空墓，在古代公共可信度语境中可能具有怎样的张力。
- **Does not establish:** Deuteronomy itself requires two/three witnesses without explicit female exclusion; Josephus' addition is not verbatim Mosaic law. Court eligibility is not universal disbelief in women about family news, burial, mourning, property, religion, or daily life. Sixty years after the events, he cannot establish every locality's practice in the 30s; embarrassment alone cannot prove empty tomb/resurrection.
  **不能支持**：申命记 19:15 本身只要求两三名见证人，没有明文排除妇女，不能把约瑟夫斯的附加说法冒充摩西律法逐字原文。法庭资格也不等于妇女在家庭消息、丧葬照料、哀悼、财产事务、宗教宣告或日常生活中从无人相信；约瑟夫斯离福音书所述事件约六十年，其陈述不能证明主后 30 年代每个地方的实际执行，更不能以“尴尬准则”直接证明空墓或复活。
- **Debate:** Actual later-first-century practice, idealized legal restatement, and Greco-Roman-facing rhetoric need comparison with other law/social settings. Women's tomb visits also fit common mourning/burial roles; social restriction does not make narrative formation impossible.
  **争议**：这段话多大程度反映第一世纪后半犹太法庭惯例、作者个人理想化的法律重述，或面向希腊罗马读者的修辞，仍须与其他犹太法律材料和具体社会场景比较。妇女到墓前也符合古代女性常见的哀悼与丧葬角色，因此“社会上受限制”与“叙事绝不可能形成”不是同一命题。
- **Grade:** A for extant text/normative claim; B for late-first-century elite legal ideas; C for all-Jewish/all-region/all-life generalization; D for direct resurrection/empty-tomb proof from female testimony.
  **等级**：A（现传《犹太古史》4.219 的文本与规范性主张）；B（第一世纪末精英正式法律观念的背景）；C（外推为全体犹太人、所有地区及全部生活场景的实际规则）；D（由女性见证直接证明空墓或复活）。
- **Sources:** [Perseus: Josephus Antiquities 4.219, English translation](https://www.perseus.tufts.edu/hopper/text?doc=Perseus%3Atext%3A1999.01.0146%3Abook%3D4%3Asection%3D219); [Carolyn Osiek: women's burial, witness, and narrative roles in empty-tomb tradition](https://hts.org.za/index.php/hts/article/view/1600); [InHee C. Berg: female burial-kinship roles in the Gospels](https://doi.org/10.1177/0146107917731835)
  **来源**：[Perseus：约瑟夫斯《犹太古史》4.219 英译](https://www.perseus.tufts.edu/hopper/text?doc=Perseus%3Atext%3A1999.01.0146%3Abook%3D4%3Asection%3D219)；[Carolyn Osiek：妇女在空墓传统中的丧葬、见证与叙事角色](https://hts.org.za/index.php/hts/article/view/1600)；[InHee C. Berg：福音书中的女性丧葬亲属角色](https://doi.org/10.1177/0146107917731835)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-115"></a>

### 115. Philo, Special Laws 4.53–54: Another First-Century Restatement of Witness Numbers | 斐洛《特殊律法》4.53–54：两三见证人规则的另一种第一世纪重述

- **Date / document:** Alexandrian Philo lived around 20 BCE–50 CE. This early-first-century Greek law exposition restates Deuteronomy 17:6/19:15 against false testimony: one witness may be mistaken and does not outweigh one defendant.
  **年代／文献**：亚历山大的斐洛约生活于主前 20 年至主后 50 年，《特殊律法》属于第一世纪上半叶的希腊文犹太律法解释。4.53–54 在“不可作假见证”的讨论中重述申命记 17:6、19:15：法官不可接纳单一见证，因为一人可能误认，而且一人与被告人数相等时不足以形成证据优势。
- **Related Scripture:** Deuteronomy 17:6, 19:15; Matthew 18:16; John 5:31–40, 8:14–18; first-century background to Gospel resurrection-witness language.
  **对应经文**：申命记 17:6、19:15；马太福音 18:16；约翰福音 5:31–40、8:14–18；并可作为四福音复活见证语言的第一世纪解释背景。
- **Supports:** A roughly contemporary diaspora author uses the two/three-witness rule without adding Josephus' later explicit women/slaves exclusion, directly showing different Second Temple emphases on one text.
  **能支持**：一位与耶稣、初代教会大致同时代的散居犹太作者确实使用两三名见证人的经文规则，却没有在这段重述中附加约瑟夫斯后来明写的妇女／奴仆排除。它直接证明第二圣殿时期对同一经文可以有不同阐释重点。
- **Does not establish:** Silence does not expressly permit women in every civil/criminal court. Alexandrian philosophical exposition is not Judean court records or a direct account of Galilean, Jerusalem, or Gospel-audience evaluations of Mary Magdalene.
  **不能支持**：斐洛没有明写排除，不等于他明确准许妇女参与所有民事或刑事法庭；沉默证据不能承担这种结论。亚历山大哲学式律法解释也不是犹太地法庭实录，不能直接说明加利利、耶路撒冷或四福音受众如何评价抹大拉的马利亚。
- **Debate:** Exact composition years remain difficult. General masculine grammar, male-citizen assumptions, or intentional eligibility restriction cannot be decided from silence here. Its comparative value is differing restatement, not a complete theory of women's legal rights from missing wording.
  **争议**：《特殊律法》各篇的精确写作年份难以锁定；斐洛的男性用语属于一般语法、男性公民预设，还是有意限定资格，也不能仅由 4.53–54 的沉默决定。最重要的比较价值是“同一经文的重述不完全相同”，不是以缺文建立一套完整女性法权。
- **Grade:** A for text/rule/reasoning; B for interpretive diversity; C for formal female eligibility/local practice inferred from silence; D for Gospel reports/resurrection proof.
  **等级**：A（现传《特殊律法》4.53–54 的文本、两三见证人规则及其理由）；B（第一世纪犹太释经多样性的比较）；C（由沉默推断女性正式资格或地方实际执行）；D（直接证明福音书妇女报告或复活）。
- **Sources:** [Philo: Special Laws 4.53–54, English translation](https://www.earlyjewishwritings.com/text/philo/book30.html); [Sarah J. K. Pearce: Second Temple reception of Deuteronomic witness law](https://www.mohrsiebeck.com/en/book/the-words-of-moses-9783161507335/)
  **来源**：[斐洛：《特殊律法》4.53–54 英译](https://www.earlyjewishwritings.com/text/philo/book30.html)；[Sarah J. K. Pearce：第二圣殿时期申命记见证律接收研究](https://www.mohrsiebeck.com/en/book/the-words-of-moses-9783161507335/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-116"></a>

### 116. Mishnah Testimony and a Husband's Death: Restrictions Are Not Universal Disbelief | 《米示拿》证词通则与丈夫死亡案件：限制并不等于所有场景不可信

- **Date / document:** Compiled around 200 CE in Palestine from earlier/current rabbinic opinions. Inclusion alone cannot date a tradition to Second Temple times. Shevuot 4:1, Yevamot 15:1/4, and Eduyot 6:1 address different cases in one collection.
  **年代／文献**：《米示拿》约在主后 200 年于巴勒斯坦编定，汇集较早至当时的拉比法律意见；某条传统可能更早，但不能仅凭被收入文集就自动定到第二圣殿时期。《誓言》4:1、《耶瓦莫特》15:1、15:4 和《见证集》6:1 属于同一文集中的不同案件。
- **Related Scripture:** Deuteronomy 19:15; Matthew 28:1–10; Mark 16:1–8; Luke 24:1–12; John 20:1–18.
  **对应经文**：申命记 19:15；马太福音 28:1–10；马可福音 16:1–8；路加福音 24:1–12；约翰福音 20:1–18。
- **Supports:** Shevuot 4:1 restricts the formal testimony oath to qualified men, excluding women/relatives/others. Yevamot 15:1 lets a wife returning under peaceful conditions report her husband's death for remarriage; 15:4 accepts everyone except specified women with suspected conflicts and discusses conflicting female reports. Eduyot 6:1 also preserves remarriage on one witness.
  **能支持**：《誓言》4:1 说正式“证言的誓言”适用于男子而不适用于妇女、亲属及其他不合资格者；《耶瓦莫特》15:1 却允许在和平背景下归来的妻子以“我丈夫死了”使自己获准再婚，15:4 说在丈夫死亡报告中除数类被认为有利害冲突的女性外“众人都可信”，并讨论女性证词相冲的情况；《见证集》6:1 也保存以一个见证人准许妇女再婚的裁决。
- **Does not establish:** No proof that 30s CE courts applied the 200 CE compilation verbatim. A remarriage death-report exception is not a public resurrection announcement; formal oath restriction likewise does not erase women's credibility in mourning, cemetery discoveries, family news, or all religion.
  **不能支持**：这些段落不能证明主后 30 年代的法庭已经逐字采用约主后 200 年的编定规则，也不能把“丈夫死亡、准许再婚”的特定例外直接等同“耶稣复活”的公共宣告。反过来，《誓言》4:1 的正式限制也不能推成妇女在哀悼、墓地发现、家庭消息或一切宗教言说中都不可信。
- **Debate:** Distinguish first-/second-century formation, procedural relaxation versus different credibility categories, and later systematization. Preserved general rules, disagreements, and exceptions resist one-sentence accounts of all ancient Jewish practice.
  **争议**：个别裁决究竟形成于第一还是第二世纪、例外是对一般证据法的程序性宽免还是另一类可信度判断，以及后世解释怎样系统化这些材料，均需继续区分。文本同时保存通则、争论和例外，本身就反对“一句话概括全部古代犹太实践”。
- **Grade:** A for extant rules/disputes/exceptions; B for case-specific eligibility around 200 CE; C for exact 30s dating/universal first-century representation; D for directly proving/refuting Gospel empty tomb/resurrection.
  **等级**：A（现传《米示拿》各段的规则、争论和例外）；B（约主后 200 年拉比法按案件分类证词资格）；C（把个别传统精确定到主后 30 年代或代表全体第一世纪犹太人）；D（直接证明或否定福音书空墓与复活）。
- **Sources:** [Sefaria: Mishnah Shevuot 4:1](https://www.sefaria.org/Mishnah_Shevuot.4.1-13); [JTS: Mishnah Yevamot 15:1](https://www.jtsa.edu/torah/yevamot-151/); [Sefaria: Mishnah Yevamot 15:4](https://www.sefaria.org/Mishnah_Yevamot.15.4); [Sefaria: Mishnah Eduyot 6:1](https://www.sefaria.org/English_Explanation_of_Mishnah_Eduyot.6.1); [Martin Goodman: Mishnah's compilation around 200 CE and collection type](https://doi.org/10.1093/acrefore/9780199381135.013.4222)
  **来源**：[《米示拿·誓言》4:1，Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org/Mishnah_Shevuot.4.1-13)；[《米示拿·耶瓦莫特》15:1，JTS](https://www.jtsa.edu/torah/yevamot-151/)；[《米示拿·耶瓦莫特》15:4，Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org/Mishnah_Yevamot.15.4)；[《米示拿·见证集》6:1，Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org/English_Explanation_of_Mishnah_Eduyot.6.1)；[Martin Goodman：米示拿约主后 200 年编定及文集性质](https://doi.org/10.1093/acrefore/9780199381135.013.4222)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-117"></a>

### 117. Justin, Dialogue with Trypho 108: Second-Century Reception of the Stolen-Body Charge | 游斯丁《与特里弗对话》108：“门徒盗尸”指控的第二世纪接收

- **Date / document:** Greek Dialogue around 155–160 CE. Justin claims Jewish agents spread the story of disciples stealing the body at night and deceiving people with resurrection/ascension. The surviving object is Christian apologetic, not the accusation's Jewish original.
  **年代／文献**：殉道者游斯丁约在主后 155–160 年写成希腊文《与特里弗对话》。第 108 章由游斯丁声称，犹太方面曾差派人到各地宣称耶稣的门徒夜间从墓中偷走遗体，又以复活、升天欺骗人；现存的是第二世纪基督徒护教作品，不是该指控的犹太原件。
- **Related Scripture:** Matthew 27:62–28:15, especially 28:11–15; week 41 counter-narrative reception.
  **对应经文**：马太福音 27:62–28:15，尤其 28:11–15；并用于第 41 周空墓反叙事的接收史。
- **Supports:** By mid-second century, Christian apologetic to Jews continues the stolen-body response, showing Matthew's rival reports entering later dispute memory.
  **能支持**：至迟第二世纪中叶，基督教对犹太人的护教叙述已经继续使用“门徒盗尸”这一反驳；它说明马太福音内部的两种报告怎样进入稍后的犹太—基督徒争论记忆。
- **Does not establish:** No proof of first-century worldwide Jewish missions, independent guard/council testimony, or a historically empty tomb merely because theft was alleged. Close Matthean resemblance does not decide independent oral tradition, continuing accusation, or apologetic retelling.
  **不能支持**：第 108 章不能证明第一世纪犹太领袖确曾组织世界性使团、不能充当守卫或公会的独立口供，也不能仅因反方解释遗体被盗就演绎推出墓在历史上必为空。文本明显接近马太福音 28 章，是否另保存独立口传、延续的真实指控或主要是对马太的护教重述，不能由本段单独决定。
- **Debate:** The work may retain real/earlier debate but is literary Christian composition. Trypho's historicity and actual-conversation basis remain debated; chapter 108's claim is Justin's speech, not Trypho's admission.
  **争议**：《对话》可能吸收真实论辩记忆和较早犹太—基督徒争论，也是一部经过文学塑造、面向读者的基督徒作品；特里弗是否历史人物、对话在多大程度上记录实际交谈，学界有不同判断。第 108 章的指控出自游斯丁发言，更不能当作特里弗亲口承认。
- **Grade:** A for extant wording/charge; B for mid-second-century apologetic reception; C for independent oral/Jewish dissemination history; D for independent first-century empty-tomb/resurrection proof.
  **等级**：A（现传《对话》108 的文字及其中确有“盗尸”指控）；B（第二世纪中叶基督教护教与争论接收史）；C（另有独立口传、真实犹太传播活动及其范围）；D（第一世纪独立空墓证词或复活证明）。
- **Sources:** [Justin: Dialogue with Trypho 108, Roberts–Donaldson translation](https://www.logoslibrary.org/justin/trypho/108.html); [Riemer Roukema: Dialogue dated around 155–160 CE](https://doi.org/10.1163/18712428-bja10069); [Graham N. Stanton: Dialogue's literature, tradition, and Jewish–Christian polemic](https://www.cambridge.org/core/books/abs/tolerance-and-intolerance-in-early-judaism-and-christianity/justin-martyrs-dialogue-with-trypho-group-boundaries-proselytes-and-godfearers/B7033095143CEDF5A96FB24E7AC1E90A)
  **来源**：[游斯丁：《与特里弗对话》108，Roberts–Donaldson 英译](https://www.logoslibrary.org/justin/trypho/108.html)；[Riemer Roukema：《对话》约写于主后 155–160 年](https://doi.org/10.1163/18712428-bja10069)；[Graham N. Stanton：《对话》的文学、传统与犹太—基督徒论争性质](https://www.cambridge.org/core/books/abs/tolerance-and-intolerance-in-early-judaism-and-christianity/justin-martyrs-dialogue-with-trypho-group-boundaries-proselytes-and-godfearers/B7033095143CEDF5A96FB24E7AC1E90A)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-118"></a>

### 118. Celsus Through Origen: Gendered Criticism and Competing Resurrection Explanations | 克理索的复活批评：经俄利根《反克理索》保存的性别贬抑与竞争解释

- **Date / textual witnesses:** Celsus' lost True Doctrine is usually late second century, traditionally about 178 CE; cautious work gives its last third or even early third century. Origen quotes/responds in Against Celsus around 248 CE.
  **年代／传本**：克理索（Celsus，亦译凯尔苏斯）的《真理之道》一般放在第二世纪后期，旧说常定为约主后 178 年，较谨慎研究只定在第二世纪最后三分之一，甚至保留第三世纪初的可能。原作已经失传；现有批评由俄利根约在主后 248 年写成的《反克理索》引述和回应。
- **Related Scripture:** Matthew 28; Luke 24:9–12; John 20:1–18, 24–29, especially Mary's report, hesitant disciples, bodily appearances.
  **对应经文**：马太福音 28 章；路加福音 24:9–12；约翰福音 20:1–18、24–29；尤其抹大拉的马利亚报告、门徒迟疑及身体显现叙事。
- **Supports:** Against Celsus 2.55/59–60 preserves criticism disparaging the first woman as half-mad and proposing dreams, wish-generated imagination, or deceit: gender prejudice and psychological/fraud alternatives in later-second-century reception.
  **能支持**：《反克理索》2.55、59–60 保存一套具体的非基督徒反驳：把首位女性见证者贬称为“半疯癫的妇人”，并提出梦境、愿望所生想象和有意欺骗等解释。它说明第二世纪后期的批评者既会利用性别偏见，也已把心理经验与造假作为复活宣告的竞争模型。
- **Does not establish:** Celsus is no eyewitness and Origen no neutral copyist. These chapters prove neither Mary's invention/dream nor her report's truth. Celsus' Jewish speaker and its Jewish versus Greek-philosophical sources remain disputed, not universal first-century Jewish opinion.
  **不能支持**：克理索不是事件现场见证人，俄利根也不是中立抄写员；这几章不能证明抹大拉的马利亚必属虚构或其经验必属梦境，反过来也不能因为反方攻击她就证明她的报告真实。克理索让一名“犹太人”发言，但该角色是否代表真实犹太资料、混合多少希腊哲学批评，仍有实质争论，不能倒推成第一世纪犹太人的普遍意见。
- **Debate:** Origen is generally considered extensive/fairly faithful, but no original permits checking exact wording, omissions, or order. No contemporary bibliography fixes the work's exact year; 178 is not undisputed.
  **争议**：研究通常认为俄利根广泛且大致忠实地保存克理索论点，但我们没有原书校对，确切原句、删节范围和整体次序仍经由对手作品重建。《真理之道》的精确年份也没有同期书目信息可固定；“约 178 年”不应写成无争议日期。
- **Grade:** A for extant quotation/response; B for later-second-century criticism/gender rhetoric; C for original date/wording/Jewish persona/source/representation; D for first-century event proof or direct resurrection verification/refutation.
  **等级**：A（现传《反克理索》2.55、59–60 的引文与俄利根回应）；B（第二世纪后期非基督徒复活批评及性别化修辞的接收史）；C（原作精确年份、逐字原貌、“犹太人”角色的资料来源和代表性）；D（第一世纪事件证据或对复活的直接证明／证伪）。
- **Sources:** [Origen: Against Celsus II.55, 59–60](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/04162.htm); [Lincoln H. Blumell: Celsus' date, Origen's transmission, and the Jewish character](https://doi.org/10.1177/000842980703600206)
  **来源**：[俄利根：《反克理索》第二卷，第 55、59–60 章](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/04162.htm)；[Lincoln H. Blumell：克理索原作年代、俄利根传文与“犹太人”角色的资料批判](https://doi.org/10.1177/000842980703600206)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-119"></a>

### 119. P9 / P.Oxy. III 402: Earliest Surviving 1 John Papyrus Fragment | P9／P.Oxy. III 402：《约翰一书》的最早现存纸草残片

- **Date / collection:** Greek P9, Harvard Houghton Library SM 3736, excavated at Oxyrhynchus by Grenfell/Hunt; about 8 × 5.2 cm, six front/eleven back lines. Paleographic relative dating: first editors late fourth/fifth century; newer 4CARE follows Clarysse/Orsini at about 275–325 CE.
  **年代／馆藏**：希腊文纸草残片 P.Oxy. III 402，Gregory–Aland 编号 P9，现藏哈佛大学 Houghton Library，SM 3736。它由 Grenfell 与 Hunt 在 Oxyrhynchus 发掘，现存约 8 × 5.2 厘米，正面六行、背面十一行。书写年代只能由古文字学相对判断：原出版者曾定为四世纪末／五世纪，较新的 4CARE 数据库依 Clarysse 与 Orsini 的研究采用约主后 275–325 年。
- **Material type:** An early codex-page fragment with 56 Greek words from 1 John 4:11–12, 14–17, excluding verse 13. Irregular copying illuminates transmission/practice.
  **材料性质**：一页早期纸草册页的残片，保存约翰一书 4:11–12、14–17 的 56 个希腊文词；第 13 节不在现存文字中。抄写有不规则之处，可用于观察早期文本传递和抄写实践。
- **Related Scripture:** 1 John 4:11–17; week 51 transmission.
  **对应经文**：约翰一书 4:11–17；第 51 周约翰书信的文本流传史。
- **Supports:** Under the adopted paleographic range, Greek codex-form 1 John circulated in Egypt by the late-third/early-fourth-century transition; an early physical witness to this passage.
  **能支持**：按目前采用的古文字日期，最迟到三世纪末／四世纪初，《约翰一书》已经以希腊文册页形式在埃及流传；它也给本段经文提供了现存早期实物见证。
- **Does not establish:** No autograph, original author/place/precise late-first/early-second-century composition date, fixed complete three-letter collection, or direct evidence for internal community division.
  **不能支持**：P9 不是作者亲笔，不能决定《约翰一书》的原作者、写作地点和一世纪末／二世纪初的精确日期；一小片残页也不能证明当时已有约翰一、二、三书的固定完整文集，更不能直接证明书内群体分裂的历史细节。
- **Debate:** Paleographic ranges have repeatedly changed; not an error-free 200 CE. Errors/variants show transmission, not an unrecoverable or wholly chaotic book.
  **争议**：古文字日期是相对范围，已在研究史中多次调整；因此不应只写成无误差的“主后 200 年”。残片的抄写错误与异文说明文本确在传递，不等于整卷经文混乱或无法恢复。
- **Grade:** A for papyrus/collection/findspot/language/passages; B/C for paleographic range/revision; B for Egyptian circulation by the third/fourth-century transition; D for original authors/dates/community events.
  **等级**：A（纸草、馆藏、出土地、语文与保存经文）；B／C（古文字日期范围及其修订）；B（不晚于三／四世纪之交的埃及流传）；D（原作作者、原作日期与群体事件的直接证明）。
- **Sources:** [4CARE: Artefact 267，P.Oxy. III 402／P9](https://4care-skos.mf.no/4care-artefacts/267/); [H. A. G. Houghton: *The Text of the Johannine Literature*](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9780191060496_A35506240/preview-9780191060496_A35506240.pdf)
  **来源**：[4CARE：Artefact 267，P.Oxy. III 402／P9](https://4care-skos.mf.no/4care-artefacts/267/)；[H. A. G. Houghton：*The Text of the Johannine Literature*](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9780191060496_A35506240/preview-9780191060496_A35506240.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-120"></a>

### 120. Sinaiticus' Pastorals and 1–3 John: a Complete Fourth-Century Collection | 西奈抄本中的教牧书信与约翰一至三书：四世纪完整文集见证

- **Date / collection:** Usually mid-fourth century; British Library catalogue 325–374 CE. Shared collections/digitization: British Library, Leipzig University Library, St. Catherine's Sinai, Russian National Library. Scribe A continuously copies Pastorals at Add MS 43725 ff. 294r–298r, then Catholic letters: folio 322 links 2 Peter 3:3–18 to 1 John 1:1–6; 324b links 2 John 7–13, 3 John 1–15, Jude 1–6.
  **年代／馆藏**：西奈抄本通常定于主后四世纪中叶，大英图书馆目录给主后 325–374 年范围；现由大英图书馆、莱比锡大学图书馆、西奈圣凯瑟琳修道院和俄罗斯国家图书馆分藏并联合数字化。教牧书信在 Add MS 43725 对开页 294r–298r，由抄写员 A 连续写成；同一抄写员后来又写普通书信，相关对开页 322 由彼得后书 3:3–18 接到约翰一书 1:1–6，对开页 324b 则由约翰二书 7–13 接到约翰三书 1–15，再接犹大书 1–6。
- **Material type:** Greek parchment Bible with complete surviving New Testament. After 2 Thessalonians its Pauline order is Hebrews, 1 Timothy, 2 Timothy, Titus, Philemon, then Acts/Catholic Epistles. Folio 295b links 1 Timothy 6:9–21 to 2 Timothy 1:1–15; folio 297 links 2 Timothy 4:21–22 to Titus 1:1–2:9, physically displaying the three Pastorals together.
  **材料性质**：羊皮纸希腊文圣经大抄本，现存完整新约。其保罗书信次序在帖撒罗尼迦后书之后放《希伯来书》，再连续抄《提摩太前书》《提摩太后书》《提多书》《腓利门书》，随后才是《使徒行传》和普通书信；对开页 295b 由提前 6:9–21 接到提后 1:1–15，对开页 297 又由提后 4:21–22 接到多 1:1–2:9，物理页面直接显示三封教牧书信相连。
- **Related Scripture:** Complete 1/2 Timothy, Titus; Philemon; complete 1/2/3 John; weeks 49/51.
  **对应经文**：提摩太前书、提摩太后书、提多书全卷；腓利门书；约翰一、二、三书全卷；第 49、51 周。
- **Supports:** By the fourth century this Greek Bible fully includes all Pastorals in Paul. Earlier surviving papyri have Titus/1 Timothy portions but no 2 Timothy; Sinaiticus is the earliest currently known complete physical Greek 2 Timothy witness. It also has all Johannine letters after 2 Peter/before Jude.
  **能支持**：到四世纪，至少这一大型希腊文圣经已把三封教牧书信完整抄入保罗书信集合；在现存更早纸草只保存《提多书》《提摩太前书》片段而未保存《提摩太后书》的情况下，它提供目前所知最早的完整希腊文《提摩太后书》实物见证。它也完整抄录三封约翰书信，并按彼得后书—约翰一至三书—犹大书编排。
- **Does not establish:** No first-century author/date, post-Acts-28 itinerary, original 3 John writing order, or universal earlier scope. P46's missing ending and Vaticanus' extant Pauline absence do not establish deliberate rejection by their communities.
  **不能支持**：四世纪完整抄本不能决定一世纪原作者、写作日期、传统徒 28 后行程，或约翰三书最初写作次序；也不能证明所有地区在更早时期已有相同书卷范围。P46 末叶缺失、梵蒂冈抄本现存保罗部分不含教牧书信，都不能反向等同于那些抄本原属群体主动拒绝三卷。
- **Debate:** Earliest surviving complete does not mean fourth-century first composition/reading. Sinaiticus is not the sole fourth-century canonical form; collections, order, and reception differ by manuscripts, versions, and regions.
  **争议**：“目前最早完整希腊文见证”描述的是现存材料状态，不等于第四世纪才首次写成或首次被阅读。西奈抄本也不是唯一第四世纪正典形态；不同抄本、古译本和地区的书卷范围、次序与接收速度并不同步，应把一份大抄本的完整收录与全教会处处一致分开。
- **Grade:** A for pages/full texts/order/images; B for full fourth-century collection reception; C for universal scope/earlier absence; D for original author/date/itinerary/events.
  **等级**：A（抄本页况、三封教牧书信及约翰一至三书全文、次序和数字影像）；B（第四世纪保罗书信／普通书信集合中的完整接收）；C（外推所有地区统一书目或更早缺席的意义）；D（原作者、原作日期、传统行程或卷内事件证明）。
- **Sources:** [Codex Sinaiticus: fourth-century dating](https://www.codexsinaiticus.org/en/codex/date.aspx); [Codex Sinaiticus: complete surviving New Testament and book order](https://www.codexsinaiticus.org/en/codex/content.aspx); [British Library: Add MS 43725, ff. 294r–298r, 1 Timothy–Philemon](https://searcharchives.bl.uk/catalog/041-002169734); [Codex Sinaiticus: 1 Timothy's ending / 2 Timothy's opening](https://codexsinaiticus.org/en/manuscript.aspx?book=47&chapter=6&lid=en&side=r&verse=16&zoomSlider=0); [Codex Sinaiticus: 2 Timothy's ending / Titus' opening](https://codexsinaiticus.org/en/manuscript.aspx?book=49&lid=en&side=r&zoomSlider=0); [Codex Sinaiticus: opening page of 1 John](https://www.codexsinaiticus.org/en/manuscript.aspx?book=55); [Codex Sinaiticus: joined pages of 2/3 John and Jude](https://www.codexsinaiticus.org/en/manuscript.aspx?book=57); [H. A. G. Houghton: *The Text of the Johannine Literature*](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9780191060496_A35506240/preview-9780191060496_A35506240.pdf)
  **来源**：[Codex Sinaiticus：四世纪定年说明](https://www.codexsinaiticus.org/en/codex/date.aspx)；[Codex Sinaiticus：现存完整新约与书卷次序](https://www.codexsinaiticus.org/en/codex/content.aspx)；[大英图书馆：Add MS 43725，ff. 294r–298r《提前》至《腓利门》](https://searcharchives.bl.uk/catalog/041-002169734)；[Codex Sinaiticus：提前卷尾接提后卷首](https://codexsinaiticus.org/en/manuscript.aspx?book=47&chapter=6&lid=en&side=r&verse=16&zoomSlider=0)；[Codex Sinaiticus：提后卷尾接《提多书》卷首](https://codexsinaiticus.org/en/manuscript.aspx?book=49&lid=en&side=r&zoomSlider=0)；[Codex Sinaiticus：约翰一书开页](https://www.codexsinaiticus.org/en/manuscript.aspx?book=55)；[Codex Sinaiticus：约翰二、三书与犹大书相接页](https://www.codexsinaiticus.org/en/manuscript.aspx?book=57)；[H. A. G. Houghton：*The Text of the Johannine Literature*](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9780191060496_A35506240/preview-9780191060496_A35506240.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-27.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-27。

<a id="evidence-121"></a>

### 121. Irenaeus, Against Heresies 3.16.5–8: Late-Second-Century 1/2 John Reception | 爱任纽《反异端》3.16.5–8：二世纪后期对约翰一、二书的接收

- **Date / document:** Around 180 CE, chiefly preserved in ancient Latin with Greek fragments. 3.16.5 uses wording near 1 John 2:18–22; 3.16.8 uses 2 John 7–8, 1 John 4:1–3 and 5:1 in arguments for real incarnation.
  **年代／文献**：里昂的爱任纽大约在主后 180 年前后写《反异端》。该书完整内容主要经古代拉丁译本保存，另有希腊片段；3.16.5 引用与约翰一书 2:18–22 相近的“敌基督”“从我们中间出去”和否认基督语言，3.16.8 又在基督真实成肉身的论证中使用约翰二书 7–8、约翰一书 4:1–3 与 5:1。
- **Material type:** An anti-heretical Christian work using Johannine language in Christological debate; reception history, not the original community's archive.
  **材料性质**：第二世纪后期的基督教反异端著作；作者把约翰书信语言用于基督论争辩。它是接收史见证，不是原收信群体的档案。
- **Related Scripture:** 1 John 2:18–22, 4:1–3, 5:1; 2 John 7–8; incarnation, departure, deceivers.
  **对应经文**：约翰一书 2:18–22、4:1–3、5:1；约翰二书 7–8；道成肉身、离开群体与迷惑者主题。
- **Supports:** By late second century a Gallic church writer uses key 1/2 John wording against denial of real incarnation, establishing early interregional polemical reception.
  **能支持**：至迟二世纪后期，《约翰一书》和《约翰二书》的关键措辞已被一位高卢教会作者接收，并用来反对否认基督真实成肉身的教导；这说明约翰书信的基督论分辨很早进入跨地区教会论战。
- **Does not establish:** His letter language and continuous use do not explicitly list all three; no 3 John quotation here, certain apostolic authorship, exact original year, or identification of original secessionists with a later complete heresy system.
  **不能支持**：爱任纽在此称“他的书信”并把两封书信的材料连续使用，不能据此认定他已明确列出一、二、三书三卷书目；本段没有引用约翰三书，也不能证明三书作者必为使徒约翰、原作日期必在某一年，或书内离开者就是爱任纽时代已经成形的某一完整异端体系。
- **Debate:** Ancient translation/transmission requires Greek New Testament/Latin Irenaeus comparison. A major early recipient does not represent every second-century church/list.
  **争议**：现传文本经过古代翻译和抄传，逐词比较应同时注意希腊新约与拉丁《反异端》的传文；爱任纽是重要早期接收者，却不自动代表二世纪所有教会或所有书卷清单。
- **Grade:** A for extant quotations/argument; B for 1/2 John reception/Christological use; C for clear book distinction/representation; D for 3 John reception or original authors/community events.
  **等级**：A（现传《反异端》3.16.5–8 的引用与论证内容）；B（二世纪后期约翰一、二书的接收与基督论使用）；C（爱任纽是否清楚区分两卷及其代表范围）；D（约翰三书接收、原作者与一世纪群体事件的直接证明）。
- **Sources:** [Irenaeus: Against Heresies 3.16, especially 5 and 8](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/0103316.htm); [H. A. G. Houghton: *The Text of the Johannine Literature*](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9780191060496_A35506240/preview-9780191060496_A35506240.pdf)
  **来源**：[爱任纽：《反异端》3.16，尤其 3.16.5、8](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/0103316.htm)；[H. A. G. Houghton：*The Text of the Johannine Literature*](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9780191060496_A35506240/preview-9780191060496_A35506240.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-122"></a>

### 122. Polycarp to the Philippians: Early Reception of Paul, 1 Peter, and Johannine Letters | 坡旅甲《致腓立比人书》：保罗书信、《彼得前书》与约翰书信的早期接收

- **Date / document:** Usually early second century, here broad 110–140 CE given unity, Ignatian martyrdom, and editorial-layer debate. Greek survives to mid-9.2; later parts chiefly Latin/later quotations. The 3.2 Pauline-letter statement and 12.1 Scripture formula thus have distinct transmission layers.
  **年代／文献**：士每拿监督坡旅甲的《致腓立比人书》通常放在主后二世纪前半；因书信统一性、与伊格那丢殉道的关系及可能编辑层仍有争论，本项目采用较宽的约主后 110–140 年。现传希腊文到 9.2 中段为止，后半主要依赖古拉丁译本和较晚引文，故 3.2 的保罗书信说明与 12.1 的“经文”公式还须标明传本层次不同。
- **Material type:** Different reception actions: 1.3 resembles Ephesians 2:8–9; 4.1 follows 1 Timothy 6:7/10 with Ephesians 6 armor; 12.1 introduces Psalm 4:4/Ephesians 4:26 as scriptures. 3.2 explicitly remembers Paul's teaching and subsequent letters to Philippi for study; 11.3 remembers praise at his letter's opening. 1.3–8.2 densely use 1 Peter 1–4; 7.1 parallels 1 John 4:2–3/2 John 7's incarnation/antichrist formula.
  **材料性质**：使徒教父书信的现传文本包含数种强度不同的接收动作。1.3 接近弗 2:8–9，4.1 以弗 6 的“军装”语言接续《提前》6:7、10，12.1 又在“这些经文”引介后并列诗 4:4 与弗 4:26。3.2 明说蒙福且得荣耀的保罗曾在腓立比人中教导，离开后给他们写信，要他们仔细研读；11.3 再说保罗在“他的书信开头”称赞腓立比群体。与此同时，1.3–8.2 密集使用彼前 1–4，7.1 又与约一 4:2–3、约二 7 的“成了肉身／敌基督”公式高度平行。
- **Related Scripture:** Ephesians 2:8–9, 4:26, 6:11–14; Philippians 1:3–5, 2:10, 2:16 and local letter memory; 1 Timothy 6:6–10; 1 Peter 1–4; 1 John 4:2–3; 2 John 7; weeks 48–51.
  **对应经文**：以弗所书 2:8–9、4:26、6:11–14；腓立比书 1:3–5、2:10、2:16 及保罗写给腓立比群体的书信记忆；提摩太前书 6:6–10；彼得前书 1–4；约翰一书 4:2–3；约翰二书 7；第 48–51 周。
- **Supports:** An early-second-century Asia Minor leader uses 1 Peter/1 Timothy 6 widely and remembers/recommends Paul's Philippian letters. Multiple Ephesians uses reach Scripture introduction in 12.1. These literary/local clocks predate P46/P72 by generations, distinguishing implicit use, Scripture naming, and explicit Pauline-letter memory.
  **能支持**：二世纪前半的小亚细亚教会领袖不仅广泛使用《彼得前书》和《提前》6，也认识保罗写给腓立比群体的书信并劝当地人研读；对《以弗所书》的多处使用又在 12.1 达到“经文”引介层。它为《以弗所书》《腓立比书》《提摩太前书》《彼得前书》建立早于 P46／P72 数代的文学／地方记忆接收时钟，同时显示“隐含使用”“称为经文”“提到一封保罗来信”是三种不同动作。
- **Does not establish:** Not modern titles at every use or discussion of authorship disputes. Use proves neither traditional autographs, post-Acts-28 travels, complete Paul, nor a closed 27-book canon. 3.2/11.3 cannot decide Philippians' unity or original letter count from translated singular/plural; Scripture language does not establish today's identical list.
  **不能支持**：坡旅甲没有在每处写出现代卷名，也没有讨论作者争议；文学使用不能证明所有被使用书卷都由传统作者亲笔、徒 28 后行程、整套保罗文集或现代 27 卷正典已经封闭。3.2／11.3 不能单独决定现存《腓立比书》是一封还是数封材料的组合，也不能由译文中的单复数恢复保罗共写了几封信。12.1 称“经文”不能证明当时已有一张与现代完全相同的书目。
- **Debate:** Repeated 1 Timothy/1 Peter/Ephesians relations exceed common vocabulary; Philippian reception explicitly links Paul, recipients, and letters. Exact date/unity/Latin transmission, written texts versus memory, and individual verbal relations remain debated. Johannine parallels are weaker and separately graded.
  **争议**：《提前》6、《彼得前书》和《以弗所书》的多处关系强于普通词汇平行；《腓立比书》的本地接收又有保罗、收信群体和书信三重明示。但书信精确日期、统一性、后半拉丁传文、坡旅甲使用成文文本还是熟记传统，以及每一处逐词关系仍有讨论。约翰书信平行的具体来源更弱，必须另行分级。
- **Grade:** A for extant wording/3.2 and 11.3 memory/transmission extent; B/C for 110–140 date/unity; B+ for probable 1 Timothy/1 Peter/Ephesians use and local Philippians reception; B/C for Johannine sources/individual parallels/later transmission; D for traditional authors/full lists/Philippians composition/original events.
  **等级**：A（现传《致腓立比人书》的措辞、3.2／11.3 的保罗—腓立比书信说明及传本范围）；B／C（约 110–140 年日期与统一性）；B+（对《提前》6、《彼得前书》《以弗所书》的高概率文学使用及《腓立比书》地方接收）；B／C（约翰书信具体来源、个别平行和后半传文解释）；D（传统作者、完整书目、腓立比书信组合或原收信事件证明）。
- **Sources:** [Polycarp to the Philippians: extant English translation](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/0136.htm); [Paul Foster: Polycarp's use of Paul, 1 Peter, and 1 John](https://www.pure.ed.ac.uk/ws/portalfiles/portal/135892256/Foster_Polycarp_s_Teaching.pdf); [Michael W. Holmes: Polycarp and literature later forming the New Testament](https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/10.1177/0014524606070855); [Kenneth Berding: Polycarp's chiefly Pauline and secondary Petrine influence](https://etsjets.org/publication/polycarps-use-of-1-clement-an-assumption-reconsidered/); [David J. Downs / T. Christopher Hoklotubbe: Polycarp's 1 Timothy 6 use and grading](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0028688516000175); [Oxford: debate on Polycarp's Johannine literary relationships](https://academic.oup.com/book/50341/chapter-abstract/422712248)
  **来源**：[《坡旅甲致腓立比人书》现传英译](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/0136.htm)；[Paul Foster：坡旅甲对保罗书信、《彼得前书》及《约翰一书》的使用](https://www.pure.ed.ac.uk/ws/portalfiles/portal/135892256/Foster_Polycarp_s_Teaching.pdf)；[Michael W. Holmes：坡旅甲与后来形成新约的文献](https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/10.1177/0014524606070855)；[Kenneth Berding：坡旅甲主要受保罗、其次受《彼得前书》影响](https://etsjets.org/publication/polycarps-use-of-1-clement-an-assumption-reconsidered/)；[David J. Downs、T. Christopher Hoklotubbe：坡旅甲对《提前》6 章的使用及研究分级](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0028688516000175)；[Oxford：坡旅甲与约翰书信文学关系争论](https://academic.oup.com/book/50341/chapter-abstract/422712248)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-27.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-27。

<a id="evidence-123"></a>

### 123. Origen Through Eusebius, History 3.24–25: 2/3 John Disputed Yet Widely Recognized | 俄利根经优西比乌保存的意见与《教会史》3.24–25：约翰二、三书“有争议但被多人承认”

- **Date / transmitted text:** Origen's early-third-century John commentary V survives here in Eusebius' early-fourth-century History 6.25.10. History 3.24.17–18/3.25.2–3 describes 1 John as undisputed, 2/3 John disputed but recognized by many, potentially by the evangelist or another John.
  **年代／传文**：俄利根约在三世纪前半的《约翰福音注释》第五卷讨论约翰书信；该段今由优西比乌四世纪初《教会史》6.25.10 引述。优西比乌又在 3.24.17–18 与 3.25.2–3 总结自己的资料：约翰一书从古至今不受争议，约翰二、三书则属于有争议、却“被许多人承认”的作品，作者可能是福音书作者，也可能是另一位同名者。
- **Material type:** An historian's classification plus an earlier author's preserved fragment: reception, attribution, canon history, not council legislation.
  **材料性质**：第四世纪初教会史家的书目分类，以及他所保存的更早俄利根著作片段；这是接收、作者归属和正典史资料，不是教会会议法令。
- **Related Scripture:** 1/2/3 John; the short letters name the elder rather than John.
  **对应经文**：约翰一书、约翰二书、约翰三书；两封短书自称“长老”而不写“约翰”的作者问题。
- **Supports:** Across this chain, 1 John has broader recognition while known/accepted 2/3 John retain authenticity/John-identity disputes. Existing/used books can still face authority/authorship disagreement.
  **能支持**：至少从俄利根到优西比乌的资料链中，三封书信并非同等无争议：约翰一书广获承认，约翰二、三书已为人所知且有不少教会作者承认，却仍有人质疑真实性或作者究竟是哪一位约翰。这直接解释“书卷存在、被使用”和“归属／权威仍受争议”可以同时为真。
- **Does not establish:** Disputed does not mean fabricated, wholly rejected, or unread: Eusebius distinguishes them from heretical fabrications. Recognition by many does not mean regional unanimity, apostolic authorship, or a fourth-century list as first-century publication records.
  **不能支持**：“有争议”不等于优西比乌宣布两卷伪造、完全拒绝或无人阅读；他把它们与明确斥为异端伪作的文本分在不同类别。反过来，列为“被许多人承认”也不证明全地区已经一致、作者必为使徒，或第四世纪书目可以倒作一世纪出版记录。
- **Debate:** Eusebius composes purposefully from current/earlier sources; Origen's quoted fragment cannot represent all local use. Revelation's double placement at 3.25 underscores a disputed historical list rather than binary verdict.
  **争议**：优西比乌是有编排目的的教会史家，分类综合自己时代与所读前代资料；俄利根原段又经引述保存，不能假定每个地区的实际使用都被完整记录。3.25 对启示录的双重放置也提醒读者，这是一份呈现争论的历史清单，不是简单的二元判决。
- **Grade:** A for extant History 3.24–25/6.25 classifications/quotations; B for uneven third-/early-fourth-century reception; C for universal extrapolation/full Origen context; D for original author/date/first-century reception scope.
  **等级**：A（现传《教会史》3.24–25、6.25 的分类与引文）；B（第三至四世纪初约翰二、三书接收不均及作者争论）；C（把分类外推为所有地区、或完整恢复俄利根原文语境）；D（原作者、原作日期和一世纪接收范围的直接证明）。
- **Sources:** [Eusebius: Ecclesiastical History III.24–25](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/250103.htm); [Ecclesiastical History VI.25: Origen's preserved fragment](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/250106.htm); [Cambridge: Eusebius and the Biographical Logic of the New Testament Canon](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0028688524000055)
  **来源**：[优西比乌《教会史》第三卷 3.24–25](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/250103.htm)；[《教会史》第六卷 6.25，保存俄利根片段](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/250106.htm)；[Cambridge：Eusebius and the Biographical Logic of the New Testament Canon](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0028688524000055)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-124"></a>

### 124. P74 / Bodmer XVII: Acts and Representative Fragments of All Catholic Epistles | P74／Papyrus Bodmer XVII：保存《使徒行传》与全部普通书信代表片段的纸草册

- **Date / collection:** Gregory–Aland P74, Bibliotheca Bodmeriana, Cologny/Geneva. CSNTM seventh century; 124 Greek papyrus fragments of a single-column codex, about 30–35 lines per column.
  **年代／馆藏**：Gregory–Aland P74，Papyrus Bodmer XVII，现藏瑞士科洛尼／日内瓦的 Bibliotheca Bodmeriana。CSNTM 定为主后七世纪；现存 124 片希腊文纸草，原为单栏册页，每栏约 30–35 行。
- **Material type:** Incomplete Apostolos with Acts and all seven Catholic letters. Many 1 John fragments, about three 2 John verses, 3 John 6/12; only Acts' closing title survives. Do not invent missing titles for each letter.
  **材料性质**：一部残缺的 *Apostolos* 册，包含《使徒行传》和七封普通书信的代表片段。约翰书信部分保存《约翰一书》多处残文、《约翰二书》三节左右和《约翰三书》6、12 节；现存书名题记只有《使徒行传》卷尾，不能为每封书信补造未保存的题名。
- **Related Scripture:** Acts/Catholic letters including 1–3 John; weeks 42/51 textual history.
  **对应经文**：《使徒行传》及约翰一、二、三书等普通书信的文本史；第 42、51 周。
- **Supports:** Physical Acts fragments/closing title; one of only two Johannine-letter papyri and the only one preserving all three. Seventh-century collection of Acts and all seven Catholic letters, beyond only longer 1 John.
  **能支持**：P74 直接保存《使徒行传》片段及卷尾题名，也是现存仅有的两份约翰书信纸草之一和其中唯一保存三封约翰书信片段者；它以实物证明，七世纪抄写者把《使徒行传》与全部七封普通书信编入同一纸草文集，而不是只保存较长的约翰一书。
- **Does not establish:** Centuries later than fourth-century Sinaiticus, not the earliest combined Acts/John witness or full surviving text. No original authors/dates/order, second-century reception, local first recognition, or author proof from Acts' title; no universal identical title format.
  **不能支持**：P74 晚于四世纪西奈抄本约数世纪，不能称为《使徒行传》或三封约翰书信合编的最早见证；现存只是残片，不等于各卷全文都保留下来。它也不能解决《使徒行传》或普通书信的原作者、初写年代、最初书序、二世纪接收范围或某一地区何时首次承认这些书；《使徒行传》卷尾题名不能证明作者，也不能外推成每封书信原有同样格式。
- **Debate:** Material form/scope are relatively secure, but joins, codex size, and variants require reconstruction. Papyrus does not automatically mean early: this one is seventh century.
  **争议**：P74 的物质形态和内容范围较稳，但残片如何复位、原册页规模与个别异文仍依赖抄本学重建。“纸草”也不自动等于“年代很早”；这一件明确属于七世纪。
- **Grade:** A for object/material/language/collection/date/fragments/contents; B for seventh-century collection/continued 1–3 John copying; C for full codex/earlier local reception; D for apostolic authors/original dates/earliest canon status.
  **等级**：A（物件、材料、语言、馆藏、七世纪日期、残片和文集内容）；B（七世纪普通书信合编与三封约翰书信持续传抄）；C（完整原册重建和较早地区接收）；D（使徒作者、原作日期与最早正典地位的证明）。
- **Sources:** [CSNTM: P74 / Bodmer XVII images and manuscript record](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/View/GA_P74); [Garrick V. Allen: P74 collection and titles](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0028688521000370); [Jean de Savignac: *Les papyrus Bodmer XVII*](https://doi.org/10.3406/scrip.1963.3164)
  **来源**：[CSNTM：P74／Bodmer XVII 数字影像与抄本资料](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/View/GA_P74)；[Garrick V. Allen：P74 的文集与题名资料](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0028688521000370)；[Jean de Savignac：*Les papyrus Bodmer XVII*](https://doi.org/10.3406/scrip.1963.3164)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-125"></a>

### 125. Old Syriac Gospels: Surviving Scope Cannot Decide 2/3 John Reception | 古叙利亚文福音书见证：现存范围不能回答约翰二、三书是否被接收

- **Date / manuscript:** Pre-Peshitta separated-Gospel tradition: Sinai palimpsest usually fourth/fifth century, fifth-century Curetonian, plus recently recognized sixth-century undertext leaves from Sinai/Vatican. The 2023 Vat. iber. 4 discovery, two leaves of Matthew 11:30–12:26 dated to early sixth century, is recognized as a fourth Old Syriac Gospel manuscript.
  **年代／抄本**：古叙利亚文分卷福音书早于别西大译本；主要见证包括通常定于四／五世纪的西奈叙利亚文重写本、五世纪的库热顿福音书，以及近年从西奈和梵蒂冈重写本下层文字辨认出的六世纪残页。2023 年公布的 Vat. iber. 4 两叶残片保存马太福音 11:30–12:26，古文字学定于六世纪前半，被辨认为目前所知第四份古叙利亚文福音书抄本的遗存。
- **Material type:** Physical Gospel witnesses, not an extant complete Old Syriac New Testament. Houghton cautiously states no Old Syriac evidence for Catholic letters, not an explicit deletion of them.
  **材料性质**：这些是古叙利亚文**福音书**的物质见证，不是一部现存完整“古叙利亚文新约”。Houghton 的约翰文献综述因此谨慎写作“普通书信没有古叙利亚文证据”，而不是“古叙利亚文版明确删除普通书信”。
- **Related Scripture:** Four Gospels, especially Syriac John history; limits on arguments about 1–3 John reception.
  **对应经文**：四福音，尤其约翰福音的早期叙利亚文文本史；反向用于限制约翰一、二、三书接收史的论证。
- **Supports:** Early Syriac Gospel translation/copying/revision; actual extant codices center on Gospels. Define versions by surviving books/leaves, not an assumed complete modern-table-of-contents Bible.
  **能支持**：叙利亚语基督徒很早便翻译、抄写并修订四福音；现存古叙利亚文材料的书卷形态以福音书为中心。它也说明“某古译本传统”必须按实际保存的册子和残页界定，不能先假定曾有一部与现代新约目录相同的完整古叙利亚文圣经。
- **Does not establish:** Gospel codices lacking 2/3 John establish neither deliberate deletion, regional rejection, nor ignorance. Nor do they prove a lost Old Syriac Catholic-letter translation existed.
  **不能支持**：不能因为古叙利亚文福音书抄本没有约翰二、三书，就说译者主动删去、叙利亚语教会拒绝、或当地无人知道两书；这些抄本本来就是福音书册。它同样不能证明曾经存在却已失传的古叙利亚文普通书信译本。
- **Debate:** Relationships, exemplars, and formation dates remain open; new leaves change witness counts. No current physical evidence is not a canon verdict.
  **争议**：各古叙利亚文见证之间的关系、底本和译本形成日期仍有研究空间；新辨认的残页不断改变“现存几份见证”的统计。这里采用“当前没有普通书信的古叙利亚文实物证据”，不把材料沉默改写成正典判决。
- **Grade:** A for extant manuscripts/palimpsests/language/Gospels; B for pre-Peshitta tradition/circulation; C for formation/relationships/lost scope; D for rejection inferred from Gospel-only absence.
  **等级**：A（现存抄本／重写本的材料、语文和福音书内容）；B（古叙利亚文福音传统先于别西大译本及其早期流传）；C（译本的精确形成日期、各见证关系及失传范围）；D（由福音书册缺少书信推断约二、约三遭拒）。
- **Sources:** [Grigory Kessel: new Old Syriac Gospel witness, Vat. iber. 4](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0028688522000182); [Jean-Claude Haelewyck: Old Syriac Gospel research overview](https://doi.org/10.14428/babelao.vol8.2019.19703); [David G. K. Taylor: advances in Old Syriac Gospel text research](https://ora.ox.ac.uk/objects/uuid%3Aa381b5f0-1437-495e-a050-15d4418f9066); [H. A. G. Houghton: *The Text of the Johannine Literature*](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9780191060496_A35506240/preview-9780191060496_A35506240.pdf)
  **来源**：[Grigory Kessel：Vat. iber. 4 古叙利亚文福音书新见证](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0028688522000182)；[Jean-Claude Haelewyck：古叙利亚文福音书研究综述](https://doi.org/10.14428/babelao.vol8.2019.19703)；[David G. K. Taylor：古叙利亚文福音书文本研究的新进展](https://ora.ox.ac.uk/objects/uuid%3Aa381b5f0-1437-495e-a050-15d4418f9066)；[H. A. G. Houghton：*The Text of the Johannine Literature*](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9780191060496_A35506240/preview-9780191060496_A35506240.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-126"></a>

### 126. Peshitta's 22-Book New Testament: 1 John Without 2/3 John | 别西大译本的 22 卷新约范围：有约翰一书，没有约翰二、三书

- **Date / version:** Usually formed fourth/fifth century and standard by the fifth. Traditional 22: four Gospels, Acts, fourteen Pauline letters, James, 1 Peter, 1 John; absent 2 Peter, 2/3 John, Jude, Revelation.
  **年代／版本**：别西大译本（Peshitta）新约通常放在四／五世纪形成并于五世纪成为叙利亚语标准文本。传统的别西大新约为 22 卷：四福音、使徒行传、十四封保罗书信，以及雅各书、彼得前书、约翰一书；不含彼得后书、约翰二书、约翰三书、犹大书和启示录。
- **Material type:** Version scope/transmission, not an excavation or modern council's binary decision. Modern complete editions supplementing five later Syriac books must distinguish them from traditional Peshitta text.
  **材料性质**：这是古译本的书卷范围与抄传传统，不是一次出土事件，也不是一份现代教会会议的二元判决。现代印本若把后期叙利亚文的五卷补进“完整新约”，必须与传统别西大译本文本分开标明。
- **Related Scripture:** 1 John; Syriac reception of 2/3 John, 2 Peter, Jude, Revelation.
  **对应经文**：约翰一书全卷；约翰二、三书的叙利亚文接收史；并涉及彼得后书、犹大书与启示录。
- **Supports:** Standard Peshitta groups 1 John with James/1 Peter as three Catholic letters, excluding 2/3 John: an uneven regional/language comparison with fourth-century Greek Sinaiticus containing all three.
  **能支持**：在成为标准的别西大新约形态中，《约翰一书》与雅各书、彼得前书同属三封普通书信，《约翰二、三书》不在该版本书卷范围内。这给希腊文西奈抄本已经合收三书的四世纪图景提供了一个不同步的区域／语言对照。
- **Does not establish:** Omission does not mean every Syriac Christian was ignorant, rejecting, or prohibiting a book; Greek texts, patristic use, and later versions could circulate locally. No undisputed 1 John authorship or original nonexistence of short letters.
  **不能支持**：译本没有收录某书，不等于所有叙利亚语基督徒都不知道、拒绝或禁止该书；希腊文本、教父引文和后续译本仍可能在同一区域流通。它也不能证明《约翰一书》作者无争议，或两封短书原本不存在。
- **Debate:** Do not assign all translation/revision to an unverified single translator/year. Twenty-two describes traditional version scope, not later mixed/supplemented printings; version, liturgical reading, and theological canon judgments need not coincide everywhere.
  **争议**：别西大译本各部分的译修过程与确切完成时间不宜归给一个未经证实的单一译者；“22 卷”描述的是传统版本书卷范围，不应把后来混合印本或补译本倒算进去。书卷范围、礼仪诵读和神学上的正典判断也不必在所有叙利亚教会中完全重合。
- **Grade:** A for traditional scope/three Catholic letters; B for fourth/fifth-century standardization/uneven reception; C for translator/year/local use; D for universal Syriac rejection from omission.
  **等级**：A（传统别西大新约的书卷范围及三封普通书信）；B（四／五世纪标准化、与希腊大抄本不同步的接收史）；C（精确译者、完成年份和各地实际使用范围）；D（由版本缺卷推断所有叙利亚语基督徒一致拒绝）。
- **Sources:** [Nestle–Aland 28 introduction: Syriac versions, Peshitta scope, critical use](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9783438072399_A33839551/preview-9783438072399_A33839551.pdf); [H. A. G. Houghton: Peshitta's James, 1 Peter, and 1 John only](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9780191060496_A35506240/preview-9780191060496_A35506240.pdf); [Oxford Handbook: Syriac / Christian Palestinian Aramaic New Testament versions](https://academic.oup.com/edited-volume/61539/chapter-abstract/537143991)
  **来源**：[Nestle–Aland 28 导论：叙利亚文版本、别西大书卷范围与批判版使用](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9783438072399_A33839551/preview-9783438072399_A33839551.pdf)；[H. A. G. Houghton：别西大仅含雅各、彼前、约一三封普通书信](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9780191060496_A35506240/preview-9780191060496_A35506240.pdf)；[Oxford Handbook：叙利亚文与基督教巴勒斯坦亚兰文新约版本综述](https://academic.oup.com/edited-volume/61539/chapter-abstract/537143991)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-127"></a>

### 127. Philoxenian Version: 507/508 Revision and Caution on Short-Letter Attribution | 菲罗克塞努译本：507／508 年修订与“四封短普通书信”归属警语

- **Date / version:** Philoxenos of Mabbug commissions chorepiscopus Polycarp in 507/508 CE to produce/revise the Syriac New Testament toward Greek, dated in surviving Harklean Gospel colophons.
  **年代／版本**：马布格的菲罗克塞努（Philoxenos of Mabbug）委托其副主教波利卡普（Polycarp）在主后 507／508 年制作或修订叙利亚文新约，使之更贴近希腊文。现传哈克尔福音书卷尾题记把马布格的工作放在这一年份。
- **Material type:** Original not fully preserved; portions known through Philoxenos' quotations. Sixth-century Syriac witnesses to 2 Peter, 2/3 John, Jude, Revelation are traditionally assigned to it, but modern critical editions call attribution uncertain.
  **材料性质**：原菲罗克塞努新约没有完整保存；现可从菲罗克塞努著作中的引文认识其部分文字。另有一组彼得后书、约翰二书、约翰三书、犹大书和启示录的六世纪叙利亚文传本，传统上归入菲罗克塞努译本，但现代批判版明确提醒：这种归属并非确定。
- **Related Scripture:** 2/3 John, 2 Peter, Jude, Revelation; later expansion/retranslation.
  **对应经文**：约翰二、三书；彼得后书、犹大书、启示录；后期叙利亚文新约的扩充与重译。
- **Supports:** An early-sixth-century West Syriac Greek-oriented revision; Peshitta-absent 2/3 John and others present in sixth-century Syriac witnesses, showing continued reception beyond 22 books.
  **能支持**：六世纪初西叙利亚传统确有一项以希腊文为参照的新约译修工程；约二、约三等别西大未收书卷至迟在六世纪叙利亚文传本中出现。它显示叙利亚文接收不是停在 22 卷形态，而有继续翻译和校订的历史。
- **Does not establish:** Today's so-called Philoxenian short-letter text is not unconditionally Polycarp's 507/508 original or a complete preserved codex. Do not conflate later Harklean revision with it.
  **不能支持**：不能把今天所有所谓“菲罗克塞努”短书文字都无条件认作波利卡普 507／508 年的原稿，也不能说完整菲罗克塞努新约已经作为一册保存。后来的哈克尔修订更不能与它当作同一个译本。
- **Debate:** Houghton says the four short Catholic letters were probably included; Nestle–Aland calls attribution uncertain. Grade the project's existence separately from each transmitted passage's version identity.
  **争议**：学界对现存四封短普通书信和启示录是否直接保存菲罗克塞努译文持保留；Houghton 因此说四封短普通书信“很可能”已在该译本中，Nestle–Aland 则标明归属“不确定”。本项目据此把“译修工程存在”与“每段现传文字的版本归属”分级。
- **Grade:** A for documented 507/508 project/commissioner/reviser; B for sixth-century short-letter witnesses/incomplete original; B/C for attribution of four short letters/Revelation; D for full original identity with extant text.
  **等级**：A（507／508 年译修传统、委托者与译修者的文献资料）；B（六世纪后期叙利亚文短普通书信的存在、原版本未完整保存）；B／C（四封短书与启示录归入原菲罗克塞努译本）；D（把现传文字等同完整 507／508 年原稿）。
- **Sources:** [Syriaca.org: Philoxenian New Testament entry](https://syriaca.org/work/10536); [William H. P. Hatch: Harklean Gospel colophons on 507/508 and 615/616](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0017816000022203); [Nestle–Aland 28 introduction: Philoxenian preservation/attribution limits](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9783438072399_A33839551/preview-9783438072399_A33839551.pdf); [H. A. G. Houghton: 2/3 John in later Syriac versions](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9780191060496_A35506240/preview-9780191060496_A35506240.pdf)
  **来源**：[Syriaca.org：菲罗克塞努新约版本条目](https://syriaca.org/work/10536)；[William H. P. Hatch：哈克尔福音书题记所载 507／508 与 615／616 年译修史](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0017816000022203)；[Nestle–Aland 28 导论：Philoxeniana 的保存与归属边界](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9783438072399_A33839551/preview-9783438072399_A33839551.pdf)；[H. A. G. Houghton：约翰二、三书在后期叙利亚文版本中的位置](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9780191060496_A35506240/preview-9780191060496_A35506240.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
