<a id="evidence-128"></a>

### 128. Harklean Revision: 615/616 and a Full 27-Book Syriac New Testament | 哈克尔译本：615／616 年校订与完整 27 卷叙利亚文新约

- **Date / version:** Thomas of Harqel compares earlier Syriac with two/three Greek manuscripts at Enaton near Alexandria, completing revision in 615/616 CE according to ancient Gospel colophons.
  **年代／版本**：哈克尔的多马（Thomas of Harqel）在亚历山大里亚附近九里修道院（Enaton）把早期叙利亚文译本与两至三份希腊文抄本仔细比较，于主后 615／616 年完成修订；相关资料由哈克尔福音书的古代卷尾题记保存。
- **Material type:** Very literal Greek-oriented Syriac revision with asterisks, obeli, and marginal variants. Nestle–Aland 28 calls it the sole ancient Syriac version wholly preserving the New Testament, including five books absent from Peshitta.
  **材料性质**：高度逐字对应希腊文的叙利亚文新约修订本，常以星号、短横及页边异文保留不同希腊读法。Nestle–Aland 28 称它是唯一完整保存全新约的古代叙利亚文版本；其书卷范围包括别西大缺少的彼得后书、约翰二书、约翰三书、犹大书和启示录。
- **Related Scripture:** 1–3 John/full New Testament history, especially 2/3 John's clear seventh-century position.
  **对应经文**：约翰一、二、三书及全新约文本史；尤其约翰二、三书在七世纪叙利亚文中的明确位置。
- **Supports:** A complete 27-book Syriac tradition by early seventh century, with 2/3 John no longer inferred only from uncertain Philoxenian remnants. Margins demonstrate comparison of multiple Greek copies.
  **能支持**：到七世纪初，至少哈克尔修订传统已经用叙利亚文保存完整 27 卷新约，约翰二、三书不再只是从“可能属菲罗克塞努”的残余文字推知。其页边系统还实证古代译修者会比较多份希腊文抄本，而非机械复制单一底本。
- **Does not establish:** No projection of 27 books into fifth-century Peshitta or immediate uniform liturgy in all East/West Syriac churches after 616. Literalism does not yield one unambiguous Greek exemplar for every word.
  **不能支持**：哈克尔译本的 27 卷范围不能倒推五世纪别西大译本本来就有这些书，也不能证明所有东、西叙利亚教会从 616 年起立刻采用同一礼仪书目。极端直译也不表示每一个叙利亚词都能无歧义还原唯一希腊底本。
- **Debate:** Text, starred matter, and margins have different sources; critical signs vary by copy. Relations with Philoxenian/Greek Byzantine development require book-by-book work. Complete version coverage does not mean each extant Harklean manuscript contains all 27 books.
  **争议**：哈克尔正文、星号材料和页边异文来自不同层次，且各抄本的批判符号保存并不完全一致；研究它与菲罗克塞努底本及希腊拜占庭文本发展的关系，必须逐卷、逐抄本处理。“完整 27 卷版本”说的是版本传承可覆盖全新约，不等于每一现存哈克尔抄本都包含 27 卷。
- **Grade:** A for colophon place/reviser/date/extant text/signs; B for complete version/multiple-Greek comparison; C for marginal sources/development/regional liturgy; D for projecting later scope into earlier universal Syriac lists.
  **等级**：A（卷尾题记所载地点、译修者与 615／616 年；现存哈克尔各卷文本和批判符号）；B（七世纪初完整 27 卷叙利亚文版本、与希腊抄本校订的文本史意义）；C（各页边读法的具体来源、抄本间发展及地区礼仪采用范围）；D（由 616 年版本倒推更早全叙利亚教会统一书目）。
- **Sources:** [William H. P. Hatch: Chester Beatty Harklean Gospel colophon](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0017816000022203); [Nestle–Aland 28 introduction: full Harklean scope and short letters/Revelation](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9783438072399_A33839551/preview-9783438072399_A33839551.pdf); [Peter J. Williams: *The Syriac Versions of the New Testament*](https://doi.org/10.1163/9789004236554_007); [Peter Malik: Harklean and Greek textual development](https://doi.org/10.1163/15685365-12341592)
  **来源**：[William H. P. Hatch：Chester Beatty 哈克尔福音书题记](https://doi.org/10.1017/S0017816000022203)；[Nestle–Aland 28 导论：Harklensis 全新约范围、短普通书信与启示录见证](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9783438072399_A33839551/preview-9783438072399_A33839551.pdf)；[Peter J. Williams：*The Syriac Versions of the New Testament*](https://doi.org/10.1163/9789004236554_007)；[Peter Malik：哈克尔译本与希腊文本发展](https://doi.org/10.1163/15685365-12341592)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-129"></a>

### 129. BL Add. 14,528: Absence from Reading Is Not Noncanonical Status | BL Add. 14,528 早期叙利亚文读经表：没有选读不等于不属正典

- **Date / manuscript:** British Library fols. 152–191, sixth-century script. Burkitt places contents possibly late fifth century, in early-sixth-century Edessa/Euphrates surroundings. Beginning/interior leaves are missing.
  **年代／抄本**：大英图书馆藏 BL Add. 14,528 的读经索引见于 fols. 152–191，书体定于六世纪；F. C. Burkitt 认为表格内容可早至五世纪末，并把它放在六世纪初埃德萨及幼发拉底河谷的教会环境中。抄本开头和中间若干叶已经散失，现存范围并非毫无缺口。
- **Material type:** Festival table with Psalms, Old Testament, Acts/Catholic letters, Paul, Gospels; not complete Bible or council list. It reads 1 Peter/1 John but not already-Peshitta James; also lacks 2 Peter, 2/3 John, Jude, Revelation in this cycle.
  **材料性质**：礼仪读经表，按节期规定诗篇、旧约、使徒行传／普通书信、保罗书信和福音书选段；它不是一部完整圣经，也不是教会会议颁布的正典目录。现存表明确选读彼得前书、约翰一书，却没有选读雅各书；后者当时已经属于叙利亚文新约的三封普通书信。彼得后书、约翰二书、约翰三书、犹大书与启示录也未见于这一读经循环。
- **Related Scripture:** James, Petrine/Johannine letters, Jude, Revelation; distinguish version inclusion from public selection.
  **对应经文**：雅各书、彼得前后书、约翰一至三书、犹大书、启示录；并用于区分“译本收录”与“公开礼仪选读”。
- **Supports:** An organized local annual cycle by early sixth century with selected 1 Peter/1 John. Included-in-Peshitta yet unselected James directly disproves equating liturgical silence with noncanonical status.
  **能支持**：至迟六世纪初，埃德萨周边至少有一套高度编排的年度公共读经秩序；彼得前书和约翰一书确实进入若干节期读经。最重要的反例是：雅各书虽在别西大书卷范围内，却没有被该表选读，实证“礼仪沉默”不能直接等同“非正典”。
- **Does not establish:** Not universal East/West Syriac liturgy, ignorance, formal rejection, or fabrication judgment from absence. No book-by-book authorship/authority verdicts or invented fillings of missing leaves.
  **不能支持**：不能把一份地方读经表说成所有东、西叙利亚教会的统一礼仪，更不能由某卷未出现断言当地无人知道、正式拒绝或判为伪作。读经表也没有逐卷陈述作者、权威等级或正典判决；抄本残缺处不能凭猜测补入某卷。
- **Debate:** Burkitt's practical canon description also acknowledges James, 2 Thessalonians, Philemon, and some Old Testament books as scripture without readings. Later work challenges one template for all Syriac/later East Syriac systems; describe one early Edessene witness only.
  **争议**：Burkitt 曾把此表概括为五、六世纪叙利亚教会“实际用途上的正典”，但他同时指出雅各书、帖撒罗尼迦后书、腓利门书和若干旧约书虽被认为属于经书，却没有读经。后续研究又质疑把 BL Add. 14,528 当成全叙利亚教会或后来东叙利亚体系的单一蓝本。本文因此只写“早期埃德萨地区的一套读经见证”，不把选择性循环升格为正式书目。
- **Grade:** A for manuscript/table/selections; B for local early-sixth-century liturgy; B/C for formation/influence; C for local knowledge inferred from absence; D for universal formal canon verdict.
  **等级**：A（抄本、读经表结构及现存选段）；B（六世纪初埃德萨周边的地方礼仪使用）；B／C（表格形成时间与影响范围）；C（由读经缺席推测某卷在当地的实际认知）；D（把读经表当成全叙利亚教会的正式正典判决）。
- **Sources:** [F. C. Burkitt: The Early Syriac Lectionary System, full Add. 14,528 table/comparisons](https://archive.org/download/earlysyriaclecti0000fcra/earlysyriaclecti0000fcra.pdf); [Burkitt: early-sixth-century Edessene reading setting](https://biblicalstudies.org.uk/pdf/jts/024_415.pdf); [K. D. Jenner: early Syriac lectionary development/local diversity](https://doi.org/10.31826/9781463232993-002)
  **来源**：[F. C. Burkitt：*The Early Syriac Lectionary System*，含 BL Add. 14,528 全表与比较材料](https://archive.org/download/earlysyriaclecti0000fcra/earlysyriaclecti0000fcra.pdf)；[Burkitt：六世纪初埃德萨读经环境的同期研究说明](https://biblicalstudies.org.uk/pdf/jts/024_415.pdf)；[K. D. Jenner：早期叙利亚读经系统的发展与地方多样性](https://doi.org/10.31826/9781463232993-002)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-130"></a>

### 130. BL Add. 14,686: a 1255 West Syriac Cycle Reads 2 Peter but Not 2/3 John | BL Add. 14,686 西叙利亚读经本：1255 年循环选读彼后二书，却未选约二、约三

- **Date / manuscript:** 208 paper leaves, probably copied in 1255 at Deir al-Surian, Wadi al-Natrun, Egypt, per colophon. Two leaves missing; comparison with Deir al-Surian Syr. 33 yields 61 Sunday/festival/special-day groups.
  **年代／抄本**：纸本 BL Add. 14,686 共 208 叶；卷尾题记显示它很可能于主后 1255 年抄写在埃及瓦迪纳特伦的代尔叙利亚尼修道院（Deir al-Surian）。两叶已经散失，现代学者仍可按抄本和近缘的 Deir al-Surian Syr. 33 整理出 61 组主日、节期与特别日读经。
- **Material type:** Full-text Old Testament, Acts, and Catholic-letter lectionary, repeatedly using 1 John and assigning 2 Peter 3:8–15 for Transfiguration/Mount Tabor. Published full contents lack 2/3 John/Jude.
  **材料性质**：西叙利亚礼仪读经本，保存所规定经文的全文，选段来自旧约、使徒行传和普通书信。它多次选读约翰一书，并在登山变像／他泊山节期指定彼得后书 3:8–15；已发表的完整目录没有约翰二书、约翰三书或犹大书选段。
- **Related Scripture:** 2 Peter 3:8–15, 1 John; West Syriac liturgical reception of 2/3 John/Jude.
  **对应经文**：彼得后书 3:8–15；约翰一书多处；约翰二、三书与犹大书的西叙利亚礼仪接收史。
- **Supports:** At least one thirteenth-century network publicly reads Peshitta-absent 2 Peter while using 1 John widely. Expanded versions can enter practice without all five supplementary books entering every cycle simultaneously/completely.
  **能支持**：到十三世纪，至少这一西叙利亚读经传统已经把别西大未收的彼得后书用于公共节期，同时继续大量选用约翰一书。这说明后期叙利亚文完整译本的扩展可能进入实际礼仪，却不是五封后补书卷整齐、同时、全面进入每套循环。
- **Does not establish:** No universal West Syriac practice across centuries or proof of ignorance/noncanonicity/prohibition for absent letters. A selective annual cycle with two missing leaves: absence describes this reconstructed contents list.
  **不能支持**：不能由这一卷读经本推断所有西叙利亚教会、所有世纪都选读彼得后书；也不能因目录没有约翰二、三书便说两书在当地未知、非正典或禁止阅读。年度循环本来就是选择性的，且抄本缺两叶；“未见选段”只描述这份已重建目录。
- **Debate:** Related copies partly restore missing material but do not permit invention; the two codices overlap strongly without full identity. This corrects never-reading-short-letters claims without creating another universal rule.
  **争议**：个别缺叶内容可借近缘抄本部分复原，但不能任意填补；BL Add. 14,686 与 Deir al-Surian Syr. 33 虽高度重叠，也并非处处相同。该抄本因此能纠正“叙利亚礼仪从不读四封短普通书信”的绝对说法，却不能反向建立另一套全地区通则。
- **Grade:** A for manuscript/1255 colophon/61 groups/2 Peter selection; B for one thirteenth-century network; C for regional extrapolation/silence; D for formal canon scope.
  **等级**：A（抄本、1255 年题记、已发表的 61 组读经和彼后 3:8–15）；B（十三世纪一个西叙利亚礼仪网络的实际选读）；C（由一份循环外推全地区、或解释目录沉默）；D（据此宣布正式正典范围）。
- **Sources:** [Korsvoll, Lied, Lund: Add. 14,686 introduction, full contents, translated colophon](https://doi.org/10.31826/hug-2018-190110); [Hugoye 19.2 original publication](https://hugoye.bethmardutho.org/article/hv19n2korsvoll_lied_lund)
  **来源**：[Korsvoll、Lied、Lund：BL Add. 14,686 导论、完整读经目录与卷尾题记译文](https://doi.org/10.31826/hug-2018-190110)；[Hugoye 19.2 原刊入口](https://hugoye.bethmardutho.org/article/hv19n2korsvoll_lied_lund)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-131"></a>

### 131. Bar Salibi's Catholic-Letter Commentary: Exegesis Is Not a Fixed Liturgical Cycle | 巴尔·萨利比普通书信注释：注释约二、约三不等于礼仪已固定选读

- **Date / manuscript:** Dionysius bar Ṣalibi died in 1171; Birmingham Mingana Syriac 343 copied around 1350. Fols. 1–93r contain Acts/Catholic-letter commentary, followed by Paul.
  **年代／抄本**：西叙利亚主教、注释家狄奥尼修斯·巴尔·萨利比（Dionysius bar Ṣalibi）卒于 1171 年；伯明翰 Mingana Syriac 343 约抄于 1350 年。该抄本在 fols. 1–93r 保存其《使徒行传》与普通书信注释，随后保存保罗书信注释。
- **Material type:** Later exegetical copy, not version, lectionary, or council list. Order: James, 1/2 Peter, 1 John, 2 John (89v–90r), 3 John (90v–91r), Jude; different use from Add. 14,686 festival readings.
  **材料性质**：释经著作的后期抄本，不是经文译本、礼仪读经本或会议书目。其普通书信部分依次包括雅各书、彼得前后书、约翰一书、约翰二书（fols. 89v–90r）、约翰三书（fols. 90v–91r）和犹大书；这与 BL Add. 14,686 的节期选段属于不同使用场景。
- **Related Scripture:** West Syriac commentary on full 2/3 John and the seven-letter collection.
  **对应经文**：约翰二、三书全卷的西叙利亚释经接收；并涉及七封普通书信作为注释集合。
- **Supports:** Twelfth-century tradition explicitly knows/explains 2/3 John. Absence from one annual cycle does not mean local ignorance; grouped commentary shows continuous exegetical textual objects.
  **能支持**：十二世纪西叙利亚学术／教会注释传统明确知道并解释约翰二、三书；约三不因未见于某一年度读经目录就可以被称为“当地无人认识”。约二、约三与其余普通书信按卷成组，也显示它们已经成为可供连续释经的文本对象。
- **Does not establish:** Commentary does not establish every congregation's Sunday reading or universal canon verdict. A 1350 copy is not a twelfth-century autograph and cannot settle first-century authors/dates.
  **不能支持**：巴尔·萨利比为某卷作注释，不等于每个地方堂会在主日公开诵读，也不自动等于所有叙利亚教会已作同一正式正典判决。约 1350 年抄本证明作品的传抄，却不能把现存书页本身改定为十二世纪原稿；注释内容也不能解决一世纪作者与日期。
- **Debate:** Distinguish twelfth-century author and fourteenth-century carrier; sources, exemplars, and Greek/Syriac predecessors need passage-specific study. Comparison with 1255 shows commentary and cycle selection proceeding unevenly.
  **争议**：必须区分注释作品的十二世纪作者层与现存抄本的十四世纪物质层；个别注释资料来源、文本底本及其与前代希腊／叙利亚注释的关系仍须逐段研究。它与 1255 年读经本相邻比较时，只能说明“可注释”与“被某循环选读”并不同步。
- **Grade:** A for manuscript/date/folios/contents; B for twelfth-century explicit use; C for inferred local liturgy/universal lists; D for original authors/dates/unanimity.
  **等级**：A（Mingana Syriac 343 的年代、叶码和现存内容）；B（十二世纪西叙利亚注释传统明确使用约二、约三）；C（由注释推断具体地方礼仪或普遍书目）；D（由中世纪注释证明原作者、一世纪日期或全教会统一）。
- **Sources:** [syri.ac: Mingana Syriac 343 folios, date, contents](https://syri.ac/mingana-syriac-343); [A. Mingana: Catalogue of the Mingana Collection, volume I](https://archive.org/details/MinganaCatalogueOfTheMinganaCollection.I); [Bar Salibi's Revelation, Acts, and Catholic Epistles commentary: CSCO bibliography](https://catalogo.upsa.es/cgi-bin/koha/opac-detail.pl?biblionumber=119274)
  **来源**：[syri.ac：Mingana Syriac 343 的叶码、年代与内容目录](https://syri.ac/mingana-syriac-343)；[A. Mingana：*Catalogue of the Mingana Collection of Manuscripts* 第一卷](https://archive.org/details/MinganaCatalogueOfTheMinganaCollection.I)；[巴尔·萨利比《启示录、使徒行传与普通书信注释》CSCO 版书目记录](https://catalogo.upsa.es/cgi-bin/koha/opac-detail.pl?biblionumber=119274)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-132"></a>

### 132. Urmi 1889 East Syriac Table: Reading Paul Does Not Mean Recognizing Only Paul | 乌尔米 1889 东叙利亚读经表：只读保罗书信不等于只承认保罗书信

- **Date / version:** Canterbury's mission printed the Urmi table in 1889. Maclean's 1894 East Syrian Daily Offices explains sources, calendar/table pp. 264–281, passage index 284–290: late-nineteenth-century arrangement, not ancient manuscript pages.
  **年代／版本**：英国坎特伯雷大主教差会于 1889 年在乌尔米（Urmi）印行东叙利亚文读经表；A. J. Maclean 在 1894 年出版的 *East Syrian Daily Offices* 中说明资料来源，并把“历书与读经表”列于印刷页 264–281、逐段索引列于 284–290。Maclean 的表不是古代手抄本原页，而是十九世纪末对当时东叙利亚礼仪循环的整理。
- **Material type:** Printed liturgical catalogue: Law, Prophets, Acts, Apostle, Gospel columns. Glossary says daily Apostle comes from Paul; passage index includes Hebrews, not Philemon, and no Catholic letters/Revelation. A commemoration of James the Lord's brother is not a James reading.
  **材料性质**：历史印本与礼仪目录。表格依次设“律法、先知、使徒行传、使徒书信、福音”栏；书末术语表明确说，每日 “The Apostle” 都取自保罗书信。逐段索引只列保罗书信传统中的选段（含《希伯来书》，但未列《腓利门书》），没有雅各书、彼得书信、约翰书信、犹大书或启示录。历书虽有“主的兄弟雅各”纪念日，纪念对象的名字仍不能冒充《雅各书》选段。
- **Related Scripture:** East Syriac reception of Catholic letters/Revelation; the Pauline column itself does not use every Pauline letter.
  **对应经文**：雅各书、彼得前后书、约翰一至三书、犹大书、启示录的东叙利亚礼仪接收史；并反向说明保罗书信栏也没有覆盖每一封保罗书信。
- **Supports:** The recorded Urmi cycle reserves Apostle for Paul, unlike West Syriac Add. 14,686's 2 Peter. Versions, commentaries, and cycles can differ. Brock distinguishes monastic/cathedral systems, the former becoming usual; Maclean is that later usual system, not ancient Kokhe.
  **能支持**：十九世纪末乌尔米所记录的这一东叙利亚循环，把“使徒书信”位置限定在保罗书信传统，普通书信和启示录没有进入表中。这给西叙利亚 BL Add. 14,686 选读彼后 3:8–15 的做法提供了真实的地区对照，也再次证明：某版本可有一卷、某注释家可解释一卷，而具体礼仪循环仍可能不选它。Brock 的综合研究进一步说明，东叙利亚传统原有修道院与主教座堂两套系统，后来以前者为常规；Maclean 所载属于这一后期常规系统，不是古科赫表。
- **Does not establish:** Do not project 1889/1894 into sixth-century Kokhe/Upper Monastery originals or every East Syriac community/century. No reading does not establish ignorance, absent translation, unused commentary, or formal noncanonicity; James commemoration does not supply a James reading.
  **不能支持**：不能把 1889／1894 年印本倒作六世纪科赫主教座堂或上修道院的原始读经表，也不能代表每个东叙利亚社群、每个世纪的实际做法。普通书信或启示录没有选段，不等于当地无人知道、译本没有、注释家不用或教会已经正式宣布“非正典”；表中“雅各纪念日”也不能证明礼仪当天诵读《雅各书》。
- **Debate:** Maclean reports no full-text manuscript for direct printing: editors reconstructed from Khudhra incipits/endings/older section numbers, sometimes erroneous, with print mistakes. His 1894 corrections may retain Khudhra errors. Verses remain documented but checkable; distinct from Macomber's ancient Kokhe reconstruction.
  **争议**：Maclean 明说，当时没有一部保存全文、可直接照排的手抄读经本；1889 年编者须从多地《胡德拉》（Khudhra）卷所记段首、段尾和旧式段号反查印本圣经，原段号偶有错误，初版也有排印失误。Maclean 希望在 1894 年英文表中订正，但仍承认可能保留来自《胡德拉》的错误。因此，具体节号以“有文献记录但仍可复核”处理；它与 Macomber 重建的古科赫系统不可合并。
- **Grade:** A for print/columns/index/source notes; B for late Urmi selections/later monastic norm; C for projecting earlier practice/error-free verses; D for formal noncanonical verdict or ancient-Kokhe equivalence.
  **等级**：A（1889／1894 印本、表格栏位、逐段索引及 Maclean 的资料说明）；B（十九世纪末乌尔米所记录的东叙利亚礼仪选择、后期修道院系统成为常规的学术综合）；C（由晚期表重建更早各地做法、每一节号的无误传承）；D（由礼仪缺席宣布正式非正典，或把晚期表倒作古科赫系统）。
- **Sources:** [A. J. Maclean: East Syrian Daily Offices (1894), pp. xxx–xxxi, 264–290, glossary 291](https://archive.org/details/eastsyriandailyo00macluoft); [Assyrian Church of the East Western European Diocese: full scan](https://acote.church/media/rg63b2n/east-syrian-daily-offices); [Sebastian P. Brock: Syriac liturgical versions and two East Syriac reading systems](https://syri.ac/bibliography/1307970569); [Brill: collected scholarly volume](https://brill.com/display/title/13677)
  **来源**：[A. J. Maclean：*East Syrian Daily Offices* 1894 年扫描本，导论 pp. xxx–xxxi、附录 pp. 264–290、术语表 p. 291](https://archive.org/details/eastsyriandailyo00macluoft)；[亚述东方教会西欧教区提供的全书扫描入口](https://acote.church/media/rg63b2n/east-syrian-daily-offices)；[Sebastian P. Brock：叙利亚文礼仪中的圣经版本与东叙利亚两套读经系统](https://syri.ac/bibliography/1307970569)；[Brill：论文所收学术论文集](https://brill.com/display/title/13677)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-133"></a>

### 133. BL Add. 14,491: Cathedral Attribution and Philemon as a Counterexample | BL Add. 14,491 东叙利亚保罗书信读经本：古科赫用法归属与《腓利门书》反例

- **Date / collection:** British Library Add MS 14491, 132 parchment leaves, missing end; Wright 1870/2023 Peshitta Paul edition date ninth/tenth century, current BL summary tenth/eleventh. Faded ink retraced; Samuel the stylite repaired/rebound around 1089.
  **年代／馆藏**：大英图书馆 Add MS 14491，羊皮纸 132 叶，卷末有缺叶。Wright 1870 年目录和 2023 年保罗书信别西大校勘本定为九／十世纪；大英图书馆当前简目写十／十一世纪。部分墨迹褪色后被重描，约 1089 年又经柱修士撒母耳修补装订。
- **Material type:** Specialized East Syriac Pauline lectionary, not continuous New Testament/canon list. Wright has 66 annual occasions; Juckel/Parlevliet-Flesseman's 2023 Appendix 2 lists 130 selections including Hebrews. Item 22 joins Philemon 23–25 after Hebrews 6:1–8; item 128 is Philemon 4–25.
  **材料性质**：东叙利亚保罗书信读经本，不是连续抄写整部新约，也不是正式正典书目。Wright 按全年节期列出 66 组礼仪场合；Juckel 与 Parlevliet-Flesseman 2023 年附录进一步逐项列出 130 个选段，范围属于保罗传统并包括《希伯来书》。其中第 22 项把门 23–25 接在来 6:1–8 后，第 128 项为门 4–25。
- **Related Scripture:** Romans–Philemon/Hebrews reception; absence from a specialized Pauline carrier cannot settle Catholic-letter/Revelation canon status.
  **对应经文**：罗马书至腓利门书、希伯来书的东叙利亚礼仪接收史；也为普通书信与启示录为何不能从“保罗读经本的缺席”推出正典结论提供方法反例。
- **Supports:** By ninth–eleventh century an annual physical Pauline cycle uses Hebrews/Philemon. Brock, following Macomber, assigns cathedral usage, advancing Kokhe study beyond nineteenth-century Urmi to a medieval witness. Philemon's two readings versus Urmi's absence disprove an invariant East Syriac omission rule.
  **能支持**：至少到九至十一世纪，东叙利亚社群已有按全年节期编排的实体保罗书信读经本；《希伯来书》和《腓利门书》都实际进入这份循环。Brock 的抄本清单依据 Macomber 的研究把本卷列为主教座堂用法见证之一，因此它能把古科赫系统的研究从十九世纪乌尔米印本向前推进到一件中世纪抄本。更关键的是，1889 年乌尔米表没有《腓利门书》，本卷却两次使用该书：后期资料的缺卷不能倒作东叙利亚历代不变规则。
- **Does not establish:** Later than a proposed sixth-century Kokhe system, it cannot recover the whole earliest table, unchanged transmission, or every congregation. Pauline specialization is not formal rejection of other books.
  **不能支持**：本卷的物质年代晚于 Macomber 所推测的六世纪科赫系统，不能单独恢复最早科赫原表、证明每一选段从六世纪无变化传承，或代表所有东叙利亚堂会。它本来就是保罗书信专门读经本，因而没有雅各书、彼得书信、约翰书信、犹大书或启示录，不能把这种载体限定改写成正式拒绝。
- **Debate:** Ninth/tenth versus tenth/eleventh dating differs by catalogue. Kokhe-cathedral attribution is modern reconstruction, not the colophon's self-label. Macomber proposes sixth-century or earlier origin; Baumstark/Kannookadan disagree on priority of Kokhe versus Upper Monastery. Rouwhorst links likely sixth/seventh-century convergence with reform without absolute evidence assigning all unification to Ishoʿyahb III.
  **争议**：抄本年代在九／十世纪与十／十一世纪之间有馆藏定年差异；“科赫主教座堂系统”的归属属于现代礼仪史重建，不等于卷尾自称“科赫”。Macomber 认为科赫系统可能形成于六世纪或更早，Baumstark、Kannookadan 对科赫与上修道院系统孰先孰后的判断却相反；Rouwhorst 进一步提醒，东叙利亚礼仪在六、七世纪趋向统一很可能与改革有关，但尚无绝对证据把全部统一工作明确归给伊书亚布三世。
- **Grade:** A for material/condition/type/selections/two Philemon readings; B for cathedral/Kokhe relation; C for full sixth-century reconstruction/invariance; D for noncanonicity inferred from specialized scope.
  **等级**：A（抄本材质、保存状况、保罗书信读经本性质、现存选段与《腓利门书》两处）；B（主教座堂用法归属及与古科赫系统的关系）；C（由中世纪抄本完整复原六世纪系统或证明逐段无变化传承）；D（由专门读经本的载体范围宣布普通书信或启示录非正典）。
- **Sources:** [British Library: current Add MS 14491 catalogue](https://searcharchives.bl.uk/?f%5Broot_ancestor_reference_ssi%5D%5B%5D=Add+MS+14425-14741&per_page=100&sort=hierarchy); [William Wright: Catalogue of Syriac Manuscripts, volume I, pp. 179–181](https://archive.org/details/cataloguesyriac01wriggoog/page/n228/mode/2up); [Juckel / Parlevliet-Flesseman: 2023 Peshitta Pauline edition, Appendix 2, Gorgias DOI](https://doi.org/10.31826/9781463244804); [Sebastian P. Brock: published British Library Syriac texts list, Gorgias DOI](https://doi.org/10.31826/9781463242152); [W. F. Macomber: Kokhe cathedral lectionary bibliography](https://syri.ac/bibliography/817347434); [Gerard Rouwhorst: Syriac reading/two-system reconstruction debate, Vandenhoeck & Ruprecht DOI](https://doi.org/10.13109/9783666567292.153)
  **来源**：[大英图书馆 Add MS 14491 当前简目](https://searcharchives.bl.uk/?f%5Broot_ancestor_reference_ssi%5D%5B%5D=Add+MS+14425-14741&per_page=100&sort=hierarchy)；[William Wright：*Catalogue of Syriac Manuscripts in the British Museum* 第一卷，pp. 179–181](https://archive.org/details/cataloguesyriac01wriggoog/page/n228/mode/2up)；[Juckel、Parlevliet-Flesseman：2023 年别西大保罗书信校勘本，Appendix 2（Gorgias Press DOI）](https://doi.org/10.31826/9781463244804)；[Sebastian P. Brock：大英图书馆叙利亚文抄本已刊文本清单（Gorgias Press DOI）](https://doi.org/10.31826/9781463242152)；[W. F. Macomber：科赫主教座堂读经系统书目记录](https://syri.ac/bibliography/817347434)；[Gerard Rouwhorst：叙利亚教会的礼仪读经及两种系统重建争议（Vandenhoeck & Ruprecht DOI）](https://doi.org/10.13109/9783666567292.153)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-134"></a>

### 134. BL Add. 17,923: Copying Location Is Not Liturgical-System Attribution | BL Add. 17,923 福音读经本：抄写地点与礼仪系统归属必须分开

- **Date / collection:** British Library Add MS 17923, eleventh-century parchment, 172 leaves, some damage and gaps after fols. 16/25/30. Colophon names ʿEbed-yeshuaʿ copying for priest Mari at Mosul's Hormizd monastery. Kannookadan gives 1074 from the catalogue; Wright says the year is damaged.
  **年代／馆藏**：大英图书馆 Add MS 17923，十一世纪羊皮纸抄本，现存 172 叶；若干叶轻微污损、撕裂，fols. 16、25、30 后有缺叶。卷尾题记说，抄写员 ʿEbed-yeshuaʿ 在摩苏尔的霍尔米兹德修道院为祭司 Mari 抄写此书；Kannookadan 依目录作 1074 年，但 Wright 认为具体年份所在处受损。
- **Material type:** Annual East Syriac Gospel lectionary. Wright lists Advent, Christmas, Epiphany, Lent, Holy Week, Easter, Ascension, Pentecost, Apostles, Elijah, Cross, Dedication, saints, drought, famine, earthquake, plague, dead. Kannookadan calls it UM11, one of two Gospel core witnesses for Upper Monastery usage, based on passage comparison, not a colophon locating copying there.
  **材料性质**：供全年使用的东叙利亚福音读经本。Wright 的目录从将临期、圣诞、主显、四旬期、圣周、复活、升天、五旬节，继续列到使徒期、以利亚期、十字架、献堂节、圣徒纪念及旱灾、饥荒、地震、瘟疫、亡者等特别场合。Kannookadan 又把它编号为 UM11，作为“上修道院礼仪系统”的两部福音核心见证之一。这是按选段比较所得的系统归属，不表示题记把抄写地点写成上修道院。
- **Related Scripture:** East Syriac monastic reception of all four Gospels; distinguish place, system, carrier, formation.
  **对应经文**：马太、马可、路加、约翰四福音的东叙利亚修道院礼仪接收史；并帮助区分抄写地点、礼仪系统、载体类型和形成年代。
- **Supports:** An actual annual Gospel lectionary at named eleventh-century Hormizd monastery; full comparative tables assign its cycle to Upper Monastery tradition. Both statements can be true because they answer different questions.
  **能支持**：十一世纪摩苏尔一所具名的霍尔米兹德修道院实际拥有全年福音读经本；Kannookadan 的逐段表又显示，本卷所保存的循环属于上修道院礼仪传统。由此可同时说“抄于霍尔米兹德修道院”和“见证上修道院系统”，两句话回答的是不同问题，并不矛盾。
- **Does not establish:** Hormizd and Upper Monastery are not one location. This is not a seventh-century original table or self-described Upper Monastery codex. Gospel specialization makes missing letters/Revelation canonically irrelevant.
  **不能支持**：不能把霍尔米兹德修道院与摩苏尔的上修道院当成同一地点，也不能把这部十一世纪抄本说成七世纪“上修道院原表”或卷尾自署的上修道院本。它是福音专门读经本，本来不负责保存保罗书信、普通书信或启示录；这些书的缺席没有正典意义。
- **Debate:** Modern structural attribution does not project every UM11 reading into the seventh century. Baumstark, Macomber, and Kannookadan differ on systems' priority/Ishoʿyahb III; Rouwhorst cautions against one reconstruction as established unique history.
  **争议**：本卷的系统归属是现代礼仪史依据选段结构作出的分类；上修道院系统本身又有发展和增补，不能把 UM11 的每一处读法都投射回七世纪。Baumstark、Macomber、Kannookadan 对修道院／主教座堂两制的先后及伊书亚布三世改革作用并不一致，Rouwhorst 因而提醒不要把一套重建写成已证实的唯一历史。
- **Grade:** A for object/material/eleventh-century date/colophon/annual contents; B for full-selection system comparison; C for seventh-century reform/invariance; D for rejection inferred from Gospel specialization.
  **等级**：A（抄本、材质、十一世纪定年、霍尔米兹德修道院题记、全年福音节期目录）；B（依据完整选段比较归入上修道院礼仪系统）；C（由此复原七世纪改革或不变的上修道院原表）；D（由福音专门读经本缺少书信／启示录推出正典拒绝）。
- **Sources:** [British Library: current Add MS 17923 catalogue](https://searcharchives.bl.uk/?f%5Bcollection_area_ssi%5D%5B%5D=Oriental+Manuscripts&f%5Blanguage_ssim%5D%5B%5D=Syriac&f%5Burl_non_blank_si%5D%5B%5D=No&search_field=all_fields&sort=title_for_sort_si+asc%2C+scope_and_content_for_sort_si+asc); [William Wright: Syriac Manuscripts catalogue I, pp. 182–188](https://archive.org/details/cataloguesyriac01wriggoog/page/n231/mode/2up); [Princeton Theological Commons: Kannookadan, East Syrian Lectionary, pp. 13, 28, 30–62](https://commons.ptsem.edu/id/eastsyrianlectio0000paul); [Sebastian P. Brock: East Syriac Lectionary, monastic usage, Gorgias DOI](https://doi.org/10.31826/9781463242152); [Gerard Rouwhorst: Kokhe, Upper Monastery, Ishoʿyahb III reconstruction differences](https://doi.org/10.13109/9783666567292.153)
  **来源**：[大英图书馆 Add MS 17923 当前简目](https://searcharchives.bl.uk/?f%5Bcollection_area_ssi%5D%5B%5D=Oriental+Manuscripts&f%5Blanguage_ssim%5D%5B%5D=Syriac&f%5Burl_non_blank_si%5D%5B%5D=No&search_field=all_fields&sort=title_for_sort_si+asc%2C+scope_and_content_for_sort_si+asc)；[William Wright：*Catalogue of Syriac Manuscripts in the British Museum* 第一卷，pp. 182–188](https://archive.org/details/cataloguesyriac01wriggoog/page/n231/mode/2up)；[Princeton Theological Commons：Pauly Kannookadan，*The East Syrian Lectionary*，pp. 13、28、30–62 馆藏入口](https://commons.ptsem.edu/id/eastsyrianlectio0000paul)；[Sebastian P. Brock：大英图书馆抄本清单所列“East Syriac Lectionary, monastic usage”（Gorgias Press DOI）](https://doi.org/10.31826/9781463242152)；[Gerard Rouwhorst：科赫、上修道院及伊书亚布三世改革重建的分歧（Vandenhoeck & Ruprecht DOI）](https://doi.org/10.13109/9783666567292.153)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.

<a id="evidence-135"></a>

### 135. Kannookadan's Upper Monastery Table / Vat. Syr. 23–24: an Annual System, Not a Canon List | Kannookadan 上修道院逐段表／Vat. Syr. 23–24：年度选读系统不是正典目录

- **Date / material:** Vat. Syr. 23: twelfth-century, 142-leaf Syriac–Arabic letter lectionary explicitly naming Upper Monastery order. Vat. Syr. 24: thirteenth-century, 153-leaf Old Testament lectionary naming Mar Abraham/Mar Gabriel usage. Kannookadan 1991 compares eleven accessible lectionaries UM1–11, three Khudhras UM12–14, printings, and Macomber's Kokhe transcript with Mosul/Beth ʿAbhe.
  **年代／材料**：Vat. Syr. 23 为十二世纪、142 叶的叙利亚文—阿拉伯文对照书信读经本，卷尾明说选段依上修道院系统；Vat. Syr. 24 为十三世纪、153 叶的旧约读经本，题名明说依 Mar Abraham 与 Mar Gabriel 上修道院礼制。Kannookadan 1991 年研究以十一部可取得的读经抄本（UM1–UM11）、三部《胡德拉》（UM12–UM14）和印本互校，整理出完整年循环，并在第三章用 Macomber 的科赫转录与摩苏尔、Beth ʿAbhe 材料逐段比较。
- **Material type:** Modern critical/liturgical reconstruction from physical witnesses. Main days have A(1)/A(2) Old Testament/Acts, B Pauline exhortation, C Gospel, with variants. End-verse notation is exclusive: Romans 5:1–6 actually ends at 5:5, unlike usual inclusive modern references.
  **材料性质**：实体抄本群的现代校勘／礼仪史重建。表中每个主要礼仪日以 A(1)、A(2)、B、C 排列两段旧约或《使徒行传》材料、保罗传统劝勉和福音；不同抄本的异文另列。作者在本书采用东方传统“末节号不含在内”的标法，例如表中的罗 5:1–6 实际读到 5:5，不能直接按现代常见的含末节标法抄用。
- **Related Scripture:** Selected Old Testament, Sirach, Acts, Gospels, Romans–Titus/Hebrews; strict limits on Philemon/Catholic-letter/Revelation absence.
  **对应经文**：上修道院年度表实际使用的多卷旧约、《便西拉智训》、《使徒行传》、四福音、罗马书至提多书及《希伯来书》；并为《腓利门书》、普通书信和《启示录》的礼仪缺席提供严格边界。
- **Supports:** Self-identified physical witnesses and full annual comparison establish more than a vague later printed label. Pauline exhortations use Hebrews heavily; full review finds no Philemon, James, Peter, John letters, Jude, or Revelation. Camb. 3291 binds Acts/three Catholic letters in part one but labels part two annual Pauline readings: shared carrier is not shared liturgical column.
  **能支持**：上修道院系统不再只是后期印本的泛称，而有明确自署系统的旧约、书信读经本和覆盖全年节期的逐段表。表中“使徒性劝勉”取自保罗传统并大量使用《希伯来书》；对全文表作卷名复核，没有找到《腓利门书》、雅各书、彼得书信、约翰书信、犹大书或《启示录》选段。另有 Camb. 3291 把《使徒行传》与三封普通书信置于第一部分，第二部分题名仍明确是“圣保罗书信的年度读经”；这正好说明合订在同一载体与进入同一礼仪栏不是一回事。
- **Does not establish:** Unselected is not formally noncanonical, unknown, or prohibited for private/exegetical use. A complete cycle records selections, not every Bible book; absent Psalms/wisdom/minor prophets counter omission-equals-rejection. Medieval Vat. Syr. 23–24 cannot assign every reading unchanged to Ishoʿyahb III.
  **不能支持**：表中未选某卷，不等于上修道院正式宣布该卷非正典、不认识该卷或禁止私人／注释使用。完整“年循环”是完整记录这一套选择规则，不是把整部圣经逐卷读完；旧约智慧书、诗篇及多位小先知也未进入这张主表，足以否定“未选＝拒绝”的推法。Vat. Syr. 23–24 都晚于七世纪，不能把现存每一选段无变化地归给伊书亚布三世。
- **Debate:** Kannookadan connects four uses to Ishoʿyahb III, seeing Upper Monastery/Mosul/Beth ʿAbhe as close variants versus Kokhe. Tables support closeness, not certain reformer/date/priority. Mixed witnesses include Add. 14,688 switching from Upper Monastery before New Sunday to Kokhe afterward, and Leningrad XXI varying by festival: systems are comparable traditions, not sealed boxes.
  **争议**：Kannookadan 主张四套用法可追溯至伊书亚布三世改革，并认为上修道院、摩苏尔、Beth ʿAbhe 三套只是同一修道院系统的轻微变体，科赫主教座堂系统差异较大；逐段表确实支持“前三者更接近、科赫差异较多”，但改革者、形成时点和先后关系仍属历史重建。作者自己也记录混合见证：BL Add. 14,688 在新主日以前依上修道院、其后依科赫；Leningrad XXI 也按节期混用两制。因此“系统”是可比较的传统类型，不是每卷抄本绝无交叉的密封盒。
- **Grade:** A for physical dates/leaf counts/system labels/published selections/variants; B for multi-copy annual reconstruction/UM11/close monastic uses; C for seventh-century projection/all reform attribution; D for noncanonical verdict from omission.
  **等级**：A（Vat. Syr. 23–24 的叶数、年代、题记／卷尾系统名称；出版表中的具体选段与异文）；B（依据多抄本比较重建上修道院年度系统、归类 UM11 及前三套修道院用法的近似关系）；C（把现存表逐项倒推七世纪或把全部改革归给伊书亚布三世）；D（由礼仪未选直接判非正典）。
- **Sources:** [Princeton Theological Commons: Kannookadan, The East Syrian Lectionary](https://commons.ptsem.edu/id/eastsyrianlectio0000paul); [W. F. Macomber: original full Kokhe transcription bibliography](https://syri.ac/bibliography/817347434); [Gerard Rouwhorst: limits on Ishoʿyahb III / single-template reconstruction](https://doi.org/10.13109/9783666567292.153)
  **来源**：[Princeton Theological Commons：Pauly Kannookadan，*The East Syrian Lectionary: An Historico-Liturgical Study* 馆藏入口](https://commons.ptsem.edu/id/eastsyrianlectio0000paul)；[W. F. Macomber：科赫主教座堂完整转录的原始论文书目](https://syri.ac/bibliography/817347434)；[Gerard Rouwhorst：对伊书亚布三世改革与单一蓝本重建的限制（Vandenhoeck & Ruprecht DOI）](https://doi.org/10.13109/9783666567292.153)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-26.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-26。

<a id="evidence-136"></a>

### 136. Doctrine of Addai's Edessene Public Reading: Explicit Exclusion Is Still Not an Apostolic Archive | 《阿代教义》的埃德萨公开诵读规则：明确排他语句仍不是使徒时代档案

- **Date / text:** Story set in first-century Edessa, composition usually late fourth/early fifth. Phillips' full Syriac edition/translation (1876) uses St. Petersburg N.S. Syr. 4; further fragments/later witnesses survive.
  **年代／文本**：《阿代教义》（*Doctrine／Teaching of Addai*）把故事设置在主后一世纪的埃德萨，现有研究却通常把作品成文放在四世纪后期或五世纪初。George Phillips 1876 年首次完整刊行叙利亚文并附英译，所据主抄本为圣彼得堡 N.S. Syr. 4；作品另有多个残片和后期见证。
- **Material type:** Literary apostolic-foundation narrative, not speech shorthand or royal archives. Its ending commands public Law, Prophets, Gospel, Paul, Acts of Twelve, excluding other books read alongside them. Stronger normative scope than a silent lectionary, yet within later composition.
  **材料性质**：假托埃德萨使徒奠基史的教会叙事，不是阿代讲话的现场速记，也不是第一世纪王室档案。故事末段让阿代吩咐教会公开诵读律法、先知、“福音”、保罗书信和十二使徒行传，并明确说不要与这些书一同诵读其他书。这比“某读经表没有选某卷”更接近规范性书目陈述，但仍须按作品的晚四／早五世纪文学处境使用。
- **Related Scripture:** Law/Prophets/Gospel/Acts/Paul; absence of Catholic letters/Revelation in Edessene reception.
  **对应经文**：旧约律法与先知、福音传统、《使徒行传》、保罗书信；并从缺席角度涉及普通书信与《启示录》的埃德萨接收史。
- **Supports:** A late-fourth/early-fifth-century author's explicit public-reading ideal, distinguishable from limited later festival silence. Singular Gospel invites comparison with long Syriac harmony use.
  **能支持**：晚四／早五世纪一位埃德萨叙事作者认为，教会公开诵读应围绕律法、先知、福音、保罗书信和《使徒行传》，并以排他语气保护这组文本。它提供了真正的“诵读范围明文”，不能再与后期有限节期表的沉默混为一谈；“福音”用单数也适合放在叙利亚合参福音长期使用的语境中研究。
- **Does not establish:** Not a first-century apostolic canon decree or individual-book list. Paul is not enumerated; singular Gospel cannot alone distinguish Diatessaron, collective four Gospels, or one text. This scope does not prove universal Syriac ignorance/private nonuse/noncommentary.
  **不能支持**：不能把文中阿代之口直接改写为第一世纪使徒的正式定典令，也不能把类别名称扩写成逐卷清单。“保罗书信”没有逐封列名，“福音”单数也不能单凭本句决定作者指《四福音合参》、四卷福音的集合称呼或某一具体福音文本。普通书信和《启示录》没有列入，能说明这份文本的公开诵读边界，却不能证明各地所有叙利亚基督徒从未认识、私人阅读或注释这些书。
- **Debate:** Earlier traditional elements differ from final composition. Link with harmony requires independent Ephrem/other evidence; existing practice versus ideal prescription cannot be decided merely from narrative format.
  **争议**：作品包含较早传统的可能性与现存叙事的最终成文年代必须分开；“每日在百姓面前所读的福音”与《四福音合参》的具体关系仍须结合以法莲等独立材料判断。排他语句究竟描述既有实践、提出理想规范，还是两者兼有，也不能只靠叙事形式解决。
- **Grade:** A for extant Syriac categories/exclusion; B for later Edessene ideals/local reception; C for exact books/universal execution inferred from literary command; D for first-century or timeless universal rule.
  **等级**：A（现存叙利亚文本及明确列出的诵读类别／排他语句）；B（晚四／早五世纪埃德萨公开诵读理想与地方接收史）；C（由类别恢复逐卷范围、由文学命令推定所有堂会实际一致执行）；D（倒作第一世纪定典令或全叙利亚历代通则）。
- **Sources:** [George Phillips: Syriac edition, English translation, notes (1876)](https://archive.org/details/doctrineofaddaia00phil); [syri.ac: Phillips edition bibliography](https://syri.ac/bibliography/967999565); [Syriaca.org: work, editions, manuscripts](https://syriaca.org/work/921); [Phillips translation: searchable text, passage on printed p. 44](https://www.tertullian.org/fathers/addai_2_text.htm); [Ilaria Ramelli: work's date and possible historical traces](https://www.degruyterbrill.com/document/doi/10.31826/hug-2011-090104/pdf)
  **来源**：[George Phillips：1876 年叙利亚文校本、英译与注释](https://archive.org/details/doctrineofaddaia00phil)；[syri.ac：Phillips 版书目信息](https://syri.ac/bibliography/967999565)；[Syriaca.org：作品、版本与抄本记录](https://syriaca.org/work/921)；[Phillips 英译可检索文本，相关段落在印刷页 44](https://www.tertullian.org/fathers/addai_2_text.htm)；[Ilaria Ramelli：作品年代与可能历史痕迹的讨论](https://www.degruyterbrill.com/document/doi/10.31826/hug-2011-090104/pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-27.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-27。

<a id="evidence-137"></a>

### 137. Anonymous List in Sinai Syr. 10: Text Date and Carrier Date Differ | Sinai Syr. 10 的匿名叙利亚正典表：清单年代与承载抄本年代必须拆开

- **Date / manuscript:** Miscellaneous patristic Syriac codex, syri.ac around 700 CE versus Lewis 1894's ninth century. Gallagher/Meade date the appended list text fourth century, probably Edessa: formation and carrier answer different questions.
  **年代／抄本**：圣凯瑟琳修道院 Sinai Syr. 10 是一部包含多位教父讲道与摘录的叙利亚文杂集。syri.ac 当前把实体抄本约定为主后 700 年，Agnes Smith Lewis 1894 年旧目录则定为九世纪；卷末所抄书目文本本身被 Gallagher 与 Meade 判为四世纪、很可能来自埃德萨。两组年代回答“这张表何时形成”和“现存载体何时抄写”两个不同问题。
- **Material type:** Anonymous canon/stichometric catalogue, with book-line counts. Old attribution alongside apostolic biographies to Irenaeus is rejected by modern research, which calls it an anonymous Syriac Christian list.
  **材料性质**：匿名的正典／行数字数书目，旧目录题为“正典书目录”并记载旧约、新约各书行数。抄本把它连同使徒简传等材料归于爱任纽名下，现代研究不接受这种作者归属，而称其为匿名“叙利亚基督教清单”。
- **Related Scripture:** Old Testament/protocanonical and secondary groups; four Gospels, Acts, fourteen Paul including Hebrews; no seven Catholic letters/Revelation.
  **对应经文**：旧约正典与次经分组；新约四福音、《使徒行传》和十四封保罗书信（包括《希伯来书》）；并涉及七封普通书信与《启示录》的缺席。
- **Supports:** A representative earliest-known Syriac canon list: grouped Old Testament, explicitly four Gospels/Acts/fourteen Paul, no Catholic letters/Revelation. Comparable early Syriac setting to Addai but actual classified list rather than selections or narrative command.
  **能支持**：这是现知最早的叙利亚正典目录代表。旧约先列原正典书，再把第二组书分开；新约明确列四福音、《使徒行传》和十四封保罗书信，却不列普通书信或《启示录》。它与《阿代教义》大致同一早期叙利亚接收环境，却提供另一种材料类型：不是节期选段，也不是叙事命令，而是实际的分类书目。
- **Does not establish:** Do not date text to the carrier's 700 or call the physical codex fourth century. Without Catholic letters, it differs from later Peshitta's James/1 Peter/1 John 22-book scope. A list's limits do not establish every fourth-century Syriac community's nonuse.
  **不能支持**：不能因现存抄本约主后 700 年就把清单本身定到七世纪，也不能因清单文字可能来自四世纪就把这件实体 codex 称为四世纪抄本。它没有普通书信，因此也不能与后来含雅各、彼前、约一的别西大 22 卷范围混称为同一张“叙利亚正典表”；清单未列某卷只能说明这一书目所表达的范围，不能宣布四世纪每一个叙利亚群体从未使用该卷。
- **Debate:** Fourth-century/Edessene origin is modern textual judgment, not signed date/place; carrier dates also differ. Old catalogue OCR cannot securely recover every count/damaged reading; use critical editions.
  **争议**：四世纪成文与埃德萨来源属于现代文本史判断，不是卷尾自署日期、地点；Lewis 的“九世纪”和 syri.ac 的“约 700 年”显示实体抄本仍有定年差异。旧目录的 OCR 不足以精确恢复每卷行数或残损读法，具体转录应以批判版而非搜索文本为准。
- **Grade:** A for extant codex/list/categories; B for fourth-century text/probable Edessa/Old Testament grouping; C for universal/community-practice extrapolation; D for accepted false Irenaean attribution, date conflation, or ignorance from omission.
  **等级**：A（现存抄本、书目形态及所列／未列类别）；B（清单的四世纪年代、可能埃德萨来源及两组旧约书的分类解释）；C（由一张清单代表所有叙利亚地区或逐项恢复社群实践）；D（把伪托爱任纽当真、混淆文本与载体年代，或由缺卷推定无人知晓）。
- **Sources:** [Gallagher / Meade: Biblical Canon Lists, chapter 5 summary/analysis](https://academic.oup.com/book/25501/chapter/192737586); [syri.ac: Sinai Syr. 10 record, around 700 CE](https://syri.ac/sinai-syr-10); [Agnes Smith Lewis: Sinai Syriac manuscripts catalogue (1894)](https://archive.org/details/SmithLewisCatalogueOfTheSyriacMssInTheConventOfSaintCatherineOnMountSinai)
  **来源**：[Gallagher、Meade：*The Biblical Canon Lists from Early Christianity* 第五章摘要与清单分析](https://academic.oup.com/book/25501/chapter/192737586)；[syri.ac：Sinai Syr. 10 当前抄本资料与约 700 年定年](https://syri.ac/sinai-syr-10)；[Agnes Smith Lewis：1894 年圣凯瑟琳修道院叙利亚文抄本目录](https://archive.org/details/SmithLewisCatalogueOfTheSyriacMssInTheConventOfSaintCatherineOnMountSinai)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-27.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-27。

<a id="evidence-138"></a>

### 138. ʿAbdishoʿ bar Brikha's 1298 Catalogue: a Named List Within a Larger Church Library | ʿAbdishoʿ bar Brikha 1298 年书目：具名东叙利亚清单与“更大教会书库”不是一回事

- **Date / author:** Nisibis metropolitan ʿAbdishoʿ/Ebedjesu, died 1318, compiled in 1298; Badger's 1852 translation now supplemented by Stadel's critical edition/translation/notes.
  **年代／作者**：尼西比斯都主教 ʿAbdishoʿ bar Brikha（又作 Ebedjesu，卒于 1318 年）在 1298 年编成《教会书籍目录》。G. P. Badger 1852 年刊出旧英译；现代已有 Seth M. Stadel 的批判性校本、英译与注释。
- **Material type:** Dated named East Syriac verse bibliography: Old/New Testament divisions followed by Western/Eastern fathers/other works. Biblical sections attest scope; the whole is not a modern binary canon list.
  **材料性质**：有作者、有年代的东叙利亚韵文书目及说明，先列“旧约经书”“新约经书”，随后继续列西方教父、东方教父和其他教会著作。因此其中的圣经分部可作明确书目见证，整部目录却不是一张只有“正典／非正典”两格的现代清单。
- **Related Scripture:** Four Gospels, Acts, James, 1 Peter, 1 John, fourteen Paul including named Philemon/Hebrews; absent 2 Peter, 2/3 John, Jude, Revelation.
  **对应经文**：四福音、《使徒行传》、雅各书、彼得前书、约翰一书、十四封保罗书信（包括《腓利门书》和《希伯来书》）；并涉及彼得后书、约翰二至三书、犹大书和《启示录》的缺席。
- **Supports:** Named 1298 bishop explicitly lists the traditional 22-book structure, including individually Philemon/Hebrews. A later named-catalogue layer alongside earlier anonymous lists/medieval liturgy.
  **能支持**：1298 年一位具名东叙利亚主教把新约经书说明为四福音、《使徒行传》、三封“大公书信”——雅各、彼得、约翰各一封——及十四封保罗书信。《腓利门书》和《希伯来书》均逐名出现，彼后、约二、约三、犹大与《启示录》没有进入这一新约分部。这与传统别西大 22 卷结构相合，也给早期匿名清单和中世纪礼仪表增加一个后期、具名、明确书目层。
- **Does not establish:** Do not count all later fathers, harmonies, histories, and lost titles as biblical canon or assume knowledge of a title means possession of a physical copy. Badger's outdated interpretations cannot replace critical text; Gospel/letter location traditions are not first-century proof.
  **不能支持**：不能把目录后续所有教父、合参福音、历史和已失传题名都算作“圣经正典”，也不能因为作者知道某书或书名就推断他拥有可供查阅的实体抄本。Badger 的旧英译对书名、作者与作品关系偶有过时解释，不能替代现代校本；书中附带的福音写作地点、书信寄送地点等传统也不能直接当成第一世纪历史证明。
- **Debate:** Lied notes known-only titles, duplicate names, and items challenging modern book categories. Cleaning unruly books for a closed canon misreads manuscript knowledge catalogues. Compare only the explicit New Testament division, without leveling all ecclesiastical literature.
  **争议**：Liv Ingeborg Lied 特别指出，ʿAbdishoʿ 的大目录包含仅知书名、同书异名及不符合现代“旧约书卷”想象的条目；若只为寻找一个封闭正典而清洗这些“不守规矩的书”，会误读手抄文化中的知识目录。这里因此只把作者明确划出的新约分部用于卷书范围比较，不把全部教会著作压成同一等级。
- **Grade:** A for dated catalogue/division/named books; B for later East Syriac/Peshitta comparison; C for possessed books/universal practice/earlier projection; D for whole-library closed canon or first-century author/place proof.
  **等级**：A（1298 年目录、新约分部及逐名书卷）；B（作为后期东叙利亚书目与别西大范围的比较）；C（由作者知识推定实体藏书、全地区一致实践或更早世纪状态）；D（把整部教会文学目录当封闭圣经正典，或用后期传统证明第一世纪作者与地点）。
- **Sources:** [Badger's 1852 translation: biblical and ecclesiastical catalogue](https://www.tertullian.org/fathers/abdisho_bar_brika_syriac_writers_01_text.htm); [Liv Ingeborg Lied: catalogues, canon discourse, and unruly books](https://www.openbookpublishers.com/books/10.11647/obp.0375/chapters/10.11647/obp.0375.03); [Seth M. Stadel: modern critical edition, translation, notes](https://brill.com/display/title/71976?language=en)
  **来源**：[Badger 1852 年旧英译：圣经与教会著作目录](https://www.tertullian.org/fathers/abdisho_bar_brika_syriac_writers_01_text.htm)；[Liv Ingeborg Lied：书目、正典话语与“难归类书籍”的方法警告](https://www.openbookpublishers.com/books/10.11647/obp.0375/chapters/10.11647/obp.0375.03)；[Seth M. Stadel：现代校本、英译与注释书目信息](https://brill.com/display/title/71976?language=en)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-27.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-27。

<a id="evidence-139"></a>

### 139. Ignatius, Smyrnaeans 1–3: Second-Century Bodily Resurrection Reception | 伊格那丢《致士每拿人书》1–3：身体复活认信的二世纪接收

- **Date / text:** Antiochene Ignatius writes en route to Roman execution. The seven-letter middle recension is usually early second century, with proposals around 110, 130s, or later. Here broad early–mid-second century rather than a settled single year.
  **年代／文本**：安提阿的伊格那丢在赴罗马受刑途中写给士每拿教会；七封伊格那丢书信的中卷本通常被放在二世纪早期，但精确年代与历史处境仍有约主后 110 年前后、130 年代乃至更晚的不同方案。本项采用“二世纪早至中期”的宽限，不把传统的单一年份当作已定事实。
- **Material type:** Bishop's exhortative/polemical letter, using the usual scholarly middle recension rather than later long additions. Chapters 1–2 emphasize real suffering/crucifixion/resurrection; chapter 3 bodily presence, touching, shared eating/drinking.
  **材料性质**：基督徒监督写给另一地方教会的劝勉／反论书信。这里使用学界通常接受的中卷本；后来的长卷本含扩写，不能反向混入较早文本。第 1–2 章反复强调耶稣“真实”受苦、被钉和复活，不只是表象；第 3 章又说复活后的耶稣仍在肉身中，邀请门徒触摸，且与他们同吃同喝。
- **Related Scripture:** Luke 24:36–43; John 20:24–29; Acts 10:40–41; 1 Corinthians 15; Pilate-era suffering/bodily reception.
  **对应经文**：路加福音 24:36–43；约翰福音 20:24–29；使徒行传 10:40–41；哥林多前书 15 章；耶稣在彼拉多治下受难与身体复活的早期教会接收。
- **Supports:** An early–mid-second-century Antioch–Asia Minor network counters merely apparent suffering through real, touchable, eating/drinking resurrection. Bodily belief shapes martyrdom, Eucharist, unity, embodiment: early cross-local reception beyond abstract soul survival.
  **能支持**：到二世纪早至中期，安提阿—小亚细亚教会网络已经以“真实受苦、真实复活、可触摸、同吃同喝”的身体语言反驳把耶稣受难化为幻象的主张。它显示复活认信并非只停在抽象灵魂存续，而会塑造殉道、圣餐、教会合一和对肉身的理解；这是一项年代很早、地理上跨地方的接收史见证。
- **Does not establish:** No 30s eyewitness or non-Christian independent record, and no standalone empty-tomb/resurrection proof. Luke 24 resemblance does not establish verbatim canonical quotation or an unargued independent eyewitness chain.
  **不能支持**：伊格那丢不是主后 30 年代的现场见证人，也不是非基督徒独立档案；书信不能单独证明空墓或复活事件。第 3 章与路加福音 24 章非常接近，但相似不能自动证明作者逐字引用现行正典《路加福音》，更不能由此建立一条未经论证的独立目击传承。
- **Debate:** Middle-recension authenticity and precise dating are separate. Chapter 3.2 may use Luke, shared oral/written tradition, or wording later associated with Hebrews' Gospel/Petrine tradition; one parallel cannot decide.
  **争议**：七封中卷本的真实性与每封信的精确年代是两个应分别讨论的问题；现代研究虽多以中卷本为工作文本，仍对主后 110 年前后或更晚定年有争论。第 3.2 节究竟直接使用《路加福音》、使用与路加共有的口传／书面传统，还是保存后来另归于《希伯来人福音》或彼得传统的语句，也不能只靠一句平行文决定。
- **Grade:** A for middle-recension wording/bodily language; B for early–mid-second-century reception/anti-docetism; C for exact year/Luke dependence/routes; D for independent first-century event/tomb/non-Christian evidence.
  **等级**：A（中卷本《致士每拿人书》1–3 的现传文字及其明确身体语言）；B（二世纪早至中期跨地方教会的身体复活接收与反幻影论处境）；C（精确年份、直接引用《路加福音》及传统传递路线）；D（第一世纪独立事件证词、空墓证明或非基督徒旁证）。
- **Sources:** [Lightfoot: Smyrnaeans 1–3, English translation](https://www.earlychristianwritings.com/text/ignatius-smyrnaeans-lightfoot.html); [New Advent: middle recension and comparison with long recension](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/0109.htm); [Princeton Theological Commons: Kirsopp Lake's Apostolic Fathers (1912)](https://commons.ptsem.edu/id/apostolicfathers01lake); [Jonathon Lookadoo: recent review of Ignatian date/authenticity](https://doi.org/10.1177/1476993X20914798)
  **来源**：[Lightfoot 英译：《致士每拿人书》1–3](https://www.earlychristianwritings.com/text/ignatius-smyrnaeans-lightfoot.html)；[New Advent：中卷本英译与长卷本对照入口](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/0109.htm)；[Princeton Theological Commons：Kirsopp Lake 1912 年《使徒教父》校本与英译](https://commons.ptsem.edu/id/apostolicfathers01lake)；[Jonathon Lookadoo：伊格那丢书信年代与真实性近期讨论综述](https://doi.org/10.1177/1476993X20914798)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-27.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-27。

<a id="evidence-140"></a>

### 140. Dura-Europos Women's Procession: a Resurrection Candidate, Not a Settled Empty-Tomb Image | 杜拉欧罗普斯洗礼堂女性行列壁画：复活图像候选，不是已定空墓画

- **Date / object:** Christian building in M8 remodeled with baptistery mid-third century. Yale 1932.1201c pigment-on-plaster Procession of Women dated about 240–245 CE. Burial before Sasanian siege in 254–256 secures pre-Constantinian limit.
  **年代／器物**：叙利亚幼发拉底河畔杜拉欧罗普斯 M8 街区的基督教建筑在三世纪中叶改建并设洗礼堂；耶鲁大学美术馆现把“女性行列”壁画（馆藏号 1932.1201c，颜料绘于灰泥）定为约主后 240–245 年。建筑在主后 254–256 年萨珊围城前被填封，为整组材料提供稳固的前君士坦丁时代年代上限。
- **Material type:** Actual ritual-space image, not Gospel manuscript. Women carry lights/vessels, possibly a longer original procession. A 2024 study says remodeled space ceased being residential, qualifying unexamined house-church/domus ecclesiae labels.
  **材料性质**：洗礼堂墙面的残存基督教图像，现存片段显示数名持火炬和器皿前行的妇女，原行列可能更长。它属于实际的教会礼仪空间和视觉文化，不是文字福音书抄本；2024 年建筑研究又提醒，改建后空间已明显脱离住宅功能，传统“家中教会／domus ecclesiae”标签不宜未经说明继续使用。
- **Related Scripture:** Matthew 25:1–13; Gospel tomb women; Romans 6:3–5's baptism/death/resurrection.
  **对应经文**：马太福音 25:1–13 的十童女；马太福音 28 章、马可福音 16 章、路加福音 24 章、约翰福音 20 章的墓前妇女；罗马书 6:3–5 的洗礼—受死—复活关联。
- **Supports:** A securely excavated mid-third-century community uses female/light/vessel/procession imagery among healing/salvation/Bible scenes. Tomb-women identification would be early resurrection reception; wise-virgin or baptismal-candidate readings still establish pre-Constantinian ritual/identity imagery.
  **能支持**：三世纪中叶一个有明确考古语境的基督徒群体，以妇女、光源、器皿和行列图像布置洗礼空间，并把洗礼置于更广的医治、救恩和圣经叙事图像循环中。若“墓前妇女”解释成立，它会是很早的复活叙事视觉接收；即使采用“聪明童女”或洗礼入门者解释，材料仍能说明福音意象怎样进入前君士坦丁时代的群体仪式与身份塑造。
- **Does not establish:** No unambiguous surviving tomb, angel, or risen Jesus. Not a settled empty-tomb scene, earliest Mary Magdalene portrait, or event proof. Third-century worship is not 30s Jerusalem archaeology.
  **不能支持**：残片没有保留可无歧义辨认的坟墓、天使或复活耶稣，不能称为“已证实的空墓图”“最早抹大拉马利亚画像”，也不能证明福音书所叙事件。建筑和绘画的存在只证明三世纪群体的敬拜与图像接收，不是主后 30 年代耶路撒冷的考古旁证。
- **Debate:** Yale retains tomb women as possible but disputed; ISAW suggests wise virgins, Peppard wedding/baptism. Lights, vessels, star signs, count, destination allow different reconstructions. Building phase 232/233 is not automatically the painting's present 240–245 date.
  **争议**：耶鲁的整体说明仍把墓前妇女列为可能释读，却明确承认图像有争论；ISAW 以“聪明童女？”为题，Michael Peppard 则把行列解释为迎接新郎的婚礼／洗礼场景。持灯、持器皿、星形符号、原有人数及行列目的地都可支持不同重建；约主后 232／233 年常被用作建筑第一阶段年代，却不能未经说明直接等同当前约主后 240–245 年壁画定年。
- **Grade:** A for object/ID/M8/baptistery/pre-256 limit; B for ritual/Bible-salvation imagery; C for scene/reconstruction; D for empty-tomb/resurrection proof or certain Magdalene portrait.
  **等级**：A（器物、馆藏号、M8 出土语境、洗礼堂位置及主后 256 年前的年代上限）；B（三世纪基督徒礼仪空间与圣经—救恩视觉文化）；C（墓前妇女、十童女或洗礼入门者的具体图像辨认及原有场面重建）；D（空墓／复活事件证明或无争议的抹大拉马利亚肖像）。
- **Sources:** [Yale University Art Gallery: Procession of Women, 1932.1201c](https://artgallery.yale.edu/collections/objects/34496); [Yale Dura-Europos Project: Christian building and baptistery images](https://duraeuropos.artgallery.yale.edu/christian-building); [ISAW: Wise Virgins? alternative interpretation](https://isaw.nyu.edu/exhibitions/edge-of-empires/highlights/procession); [Michael Peppard: The World's Oldest Church, wedding/baptism imagery](https://academic.oup.com/yale-scholarship-online/book/13063); [Cambridge 2024: Dura rebuilding and nonresidential reassessment](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/journal-of-roman-archaeology/article/debating-the-domus-ecclesiae-at-duraeuropos-the-christian-building-in-context/E76ED3AD86D09A74893368840DEDFA6A)
  **来源**：[耶鲁大学美术馆：女性行列壁画，1932.1201c](https://artgallery.yale.edu/collections/objects/34496)；[耶鲁杜拉欧罗普斯项目：基督教建筑与洗礼堂图像概览](https://duraeuropos.artgallery.yale.edu/christian-building)；[ISAW：“聪明童女？”替代释读](https://isaw.nyu.edu/exhibitions/edge-of-empires/highlights/procession)；[Michael Peppard：*The World’s Oldest Church*，重释婚礼与洗礼图像](https://academic.oup.com/yale-scholarship-online/book/13063)；[Cambridge 2024：杜拉基督教建筑的改建年代与非住宅化复核](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/journal-of-roman-archaeology/article/debating-the-domus-ecclesiae-at-duraeuropos-the-christian-building-in-context/E76ED3AD86D09A74893368840DEDFA6A)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-27.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-27。

<a id="evidence-141"></a>

### 141. Irenaeus, Against Heresies 1.10.1–3: Rule of Faith as a Whole Salvation Story | 爱任纽《反异端》1.10.1–3：“信仰／真理规则”把整条救恩史连成一线

- **Date / text and manuscripts:** Late second century, generally 174–189 CE, Book I around 180. Greek original chiefly Latin transmission, plus Greek/Syriac/Armenian witnesses. P.Oxy. III 405, Cambridge MS Add. 4413, around second/third century preserves III.9.3, demonstrating early Egyptian circulation, not I.10.1's rule.
  **年代／文本与抄本**：里昂的爱任纽在二世纪后期写《反异端》，全书通常放在约主后 174–189 年之间，第 1 卷常约定为主后 180 年前后。作品原用希腊文写成，完整内容主要由古代拉丁译本保存，另有希腊、叙利亚和亚美尼亚见证。约二／三世纪的 P.Oxy. III 405（Cambridge University Library, MS Add. 4413）保存第 3 卷 9.3 的希腊文片段，证明作品很早已传到埃及；它并不保存本项第 1 卷 10.1 的“信仰规则”段落。
- **Material type:** Anti-Valentinian/other polemic, not conciliar creed or regional survey. I.10.1 summarizes received faith: Creator, incarnation, prophetic plan, virgin birth, passion, resurrection, bodily ascension, return, recapitulation, universal bodily resurrection. I.10.2–3 claims transregional common content with expandable teaching within its framework.
  **材料性质**：反瓦伦廷派等对手的基督教论战著作，不是会议通过的信经文本或各地堂会问卷。1.10.1 把教会所“领受”的信仰概括为一位创造主、基督道成肉身、先知所宣告的计划、童女生子、受难、从死者中复活、在肉身中升天、荣耀再来、万有归一和全人类身体复活；1.10.2–3 又宣称不同语言和地区守住同一内容，并允许教师在不改变“信仰骨架”的前提下展开解释。
- **Related Scripture:** Genesis 1; law/prophets; Gospels; Acts 1; Ephesians 1:10; Philippians 2:10–11; 1 Corinthians 15:42–54; Revelation 20–22.
  **对应经文**：创世记 1 章；律法与先知；四福音的道成肉身、受难与复活；使徒行传 1 章；以弗所书 1:10；腓立比书 2:10–11；哥林多前书 15:42–54；启示录 20–22 章。
- **Supports:** A late-second-century Lyon leader links creation/prophets, Jesus' birth–passion–resurrection–ascension, return, and all humanity's bodily resurrection. Both Jesus' past rising and future embodied hope make bodies/creation objects of renewal, not discarded shells.
  **能支持**：到二世纪后期，里昂一位重要教会领袖已把旧约创造与先知、耶稣的降生—受难—复活—升天、将来再来和全人身体复活理解为一条不可拆散的公开故事线。这里的复活既是耶稣已经发生的“从死者中复活”，又是将来“使全人类的肉身重新起来”的盼望；它显示身体与受造界不是等待丢弃的外壳，而是救赎故事要更新的对象。
- **Does not establish:** No verbatim first-century apostolic record or identical regional written creeds from polemical claims of German/Spanish/Gallic/Eastern/Egyptian/Libyan unity. P.Oxy. 405 attests III.9.3 in Egypt, not every word of I.10.1 around 200 or resurrection itself.
  **不能支持**：不能把爱任纽自称从使徒领受的纲要当作一世纪逐字记录，也不能从“德国、西班牙、高卢、东方、埃及、利比亚都一样”的论战修辞推出每个地区已经留下完全相同的书面信经。P.Oxy. III 405 证明《反异端》第 3 卷很早在埃及流传，不证明第 1 卷 10.1 的每个词已有约主后 200 年实物见证，更不证明复活事件本身。
- **Debate:** Baptismal, catechetical, scriptural formula, and polemical arrangement origins remain debated. Partly fixed content/flexible wording better describes it than today's Apostles' Creed. Claims of unity need other groups' own texts for comparison.
  **争议**：“信仰／真理规则”究竟多大程度来自洗礼问答、要理教导、经文内传统公式或作者在论战中的重新编排，仍有讨论。现有研究较适合把它理解为内容部分固定、措辞可以伸缩的叙事纲要；不应直接等同后来定型的《使徒信经》。爱任纽对广泛一致性的陈述可证明他提出这一主张，却仍须与其他群体自己的文本比较。
- **Grade:** A for extant I.10.1–3 and P.Oxy. 405's object/Greek/III.9.3 identity; B for Lyon's whole-story rule/early Egyptian circulation; C for layers/baptism/regional agreement/Tertullian dependence; D for verbatim first-century creed/eyewitness/event proof.
  **等级**：A（现传 1.10.1–3 的内容；P.Oxy. III 405 的物件、希腊文及第 3 卷 9.3 身份）；B（二世纪后期里昂教会领袖用信仰规则串联创造、基督事件和身体复活；作品很早传至埃及）；C（精确成书分层、规则与洗礼信条的关系、各地区实际一致程度及特土良的依赖方式）；D（一世纪逐字使徒信经、独立目击证词或复活事件证明）。
- **Sources:** [Irenaeus: Against Heresies 1.10.1–3, English translation](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/0103110.htm); [John Behr: Irenaeus, rule of faith, and nonuniform catholic identity](https://academic.oup.com/book/2593); [Brill 2026: rule of faith, storyline, baptism, canon formation](https://doi.org/10.1163/9789004751071_006); [University of Aberdeen: partly fixed / partly flexible rule of faith](https://abdn.elsevierpure.com/en/publications/rule-of-faith-and-the-emerging-symbolum-apostolorum-origin-functi/); [H. A. G. Houghton: P.Oxy. III 405, MS Add. 4413, passage/quotation signs](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9780192573018_A42908571/preview-9780192573018_A42908571.pdf); [Trismegistos: P.Oxy. III 405 textual identity](https://www.trismegistos.org/text/61317)
  **来源**：[爱任纽：《反异端》1.10.1–3 英译](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/0103110.htm)；[John Behr：爱任纽的处境、信仰规则与非单一化的“大公”身份](https://academic.oup.com/book/2593)；[Brill 2026：信仰规则与圣经故事线、洗礼及正典形成](https://doi.org/10.1163/9789004751071_006)；[University of Aberdeen：部分固定、部分灵活表述的信仰规则研究](https://abdn.elsevierpure.com/en/publications/rule-of-faith-and-the-emerging-symbolum-apostolorum-origin-functi/)；[H. A. G. Houghton：P.Oxy. III 405／MS Add. 4413、所存段落与引文符号](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9780192573018_A42908571/preview-9780192573018_A42908571.pdf)；[Trismegistos：P.Oxy. III 405 文本身份记录](https://www.trismegistos.org/text/61317)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-27.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-27。

<a id="evidence-142"></a>

### 142. Tertullian, Prescription Against Heretics 13: North African Rule and Bodily Restoration | 特土良《驳异端者的抗辩》13：北非拉丁“信仰规则”与肉身复原

- **Date / text and witnesses:** Carthage, around 200 CE, with title/year debated. Latin chapter 13 names regula fidei. Ninth-century Agobardinus and medieval families transmit it; modern text uses Refoulé/other critical editions. Composition and surviving-carrier dates differ.
  **年代／文本与传本**：迦太基的特土良约在主后 200 年写成拉丁文《驳异端者的抗辩》（*De praescriptione haereticorum*，精确题名和年份仍有讨论）。第 13 章保存一段明确称为 *regula fidei* 的信仰规则。现存传本包括九世纪 Codex Agobardinus 及几组中世纪抄本；现代拉丁文本以 Refoulé 等批判版为基础，因此“约主后 200 年成文”与“现存抄本年代”不能混写。
- **Material type:** Christian polemic using Roman procedural defense metaphor, defining interpretive story boundaries: Creator through Word; Son foretold in patriarchs/prophets, incarnate through Mary, kingdom/miracles/cross/third-day resurrection/ascension/Spirit/return; righteous/wicked resurrection and restoration of flesh.
  **材料性质**：以罗马法律“程序抗辩”为比喻的基督教反异端论著。作者不是逐节注释某一卷书，而是先说明解释经文所需守住的故事边界：独一创造主借祂的道创造；圣子在先祖和先知中被预告，由马利亚成为肉身；耶稣宣讲天国、行异能、被钉、第三日复活、升天、差圣灵，将来荣耀再来；义人与恶人都要复活，并有“肉身的复原”。
- **Related Scripture:** Genesis 1; law/prophets; Matthew 28/Luke 24/other Gospels; Acts 1–2; 1 Corinthians 15; 1 Thessalonians 4–5; Revelation 20–22.
  **对应经文**：创世记 1 章；律法与先知；马太福音 28 章、路加福音 24 章及其他福音复活传统；使徒行传 1–2 章；哥林多前书 15 章；帖撒罗尼迦前书 4–5 章；启示录 20–22 章。
- **Supports:** Around-200 Latin North Africa has a sequence similar but not identical to Irenaeus, from creation through universal embodied restoration. Resurrection enters teaching/identity boundaries while final hope remains bodily, not mere disembodied immortality.
  **能支持**：约主后 200 年的北非拉丁基督徒著作已经用与爱任纽相近而不完全相同的顺序，把整本圣经压成“创造—预告—成肉身—天国—十架—第三日复活—升天—圣灵—再来—普遍复活”的教导纲要；末句明确说到两类人的复活与肉身复原。这既显示复活从事件宣告进入拉丁教会的教导和身份边界，也表明终末盼望仍包含身体，而非只剩无肉身的灵魂不朽。
- **Does not establish:** Not today's Apostles' Creed text or universal agreement from a normative polemical claim. Irenaeus/Tertullian relationships prevent simply counting two independent votes. Reliable medieval transmission is not a 200 CE autograph or first-century event proof.
  **不能支持**：不能把本章直接称作现代《使徒信经》原文，也不能由作者说这规则没有争议就推出约主后 200 年所有基督徒完全一致。特土良的摘要是论战中的规范性主张，不是独立调查；他与爱任纽思想和写作的关系意味着两份材料不能简单按“两票”计算。现存中世纪抄本可靠传递较早作品，不等于手中有主后 200 年的原稿，更不证明第一世纪复活事件。
- **Debate:** Exact date, legal background, dependence, and actual baptismal relation remain open. Other formulations support stable framework/variable expression, not one identical local creed. Later ecclesiastical changes cannot be projected indiscriminately into this earlier work.
  **争议**：作品精确年代、法律术语的具体背景、特土良使用爱任纽的程度，以及第 13 章与实际洗礼问答的关系均可继续研究。特土良在其他著作中也用不同措辞复述信仰规则，较适合说明“故事骨架稳定、表达可变”，不宜重建一份所有地方逐字相同的固定信经；其后来教会立场的变化也不能未经分期倒灌进这部约主后 200 年的作品。
- **Grade:** A for Latin chapter/order/third-day resurrection/restoration; B for North African bodily hope around 200; C for year/title/dependence/baptism; D for modern Creed original/universal survey/first-century event proof.
  **等级**：A（第 13 章拉丁文本、规则顺序及“第三日复活／肉身复原”明文）；B（约主后 200 年北非拉丁教会的复活与身体盼望接收）；C（精确年份、标题、对爱任纽的依赖程度及洗礼礼文关系）；D（现代《使徒信经》原文、各地一致问卷、第一世纪事件证词或复活证明）。
- **Sources:** [Tertullian: Prescription Against Heretics 13, English translation](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/0311.htm); [Refoulé's critical Latin text transcription, chapter 13](https://www.tertullian.org/latin/de_praescriptione_haereticorum.htm); [Tertullian Project: work, witnesses, editions, pseudonymous appendix](https://tertullian.org/works/de_praescriptione_haereticorum.htm); [Altaner / Graef: around-200 date and legal-theological structure](https://www.tertullian.org/articles/altaner_patrology_eng.htm); [Jorunn Økland et al.: Irenaeus/Tertullian and scriptural narrative structure](https://doi.org/10.1080/0039338X.2021.2019829); [Cambridge: catechetical/initiation setting of Tertullian's rule](https://www.cambridge.org/core/books/knowledge-faith-and-early-christian-initiation/simplicity-and-power/C418A79963A4AE8A60D7E68882DE1142)
  **来源**：[特土良：《驳异端者的抗辩》13 英译](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/0311.htm)；[Refoulé 批判版拉丁文本转录，第 13 章](https://www.tertullian.org/latin/de_praescriptione_haereticorum.htm)；[Tertullian Project：作品、传本、版本与伪托附录说明](https://tertullian.org/works/de_praescriptione_haereticorum.htm)；[Altaner／Graef：作品约主后 200 年及法律—神学结构概述](https://www.tertullian.org/articles/altaner_patrology_eng.htm)；[Jorunn Økland 等：爱任纽与特土良怎样以信仰规则维持经文故事结构](https://doi.org/10.1080/0039338X.2021.2019829)；[Cambridge：特土良信仰规则的要理／入门处境](https://www.cambridge.org/core/books/knowledge-faith-and-early-christian-initiation/simplicity-and-power/C418A79963A4AE8A60D7E68882DE1142)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-27.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-27。

<a id="evidence-143"></a>

### 143. Apostolic Tradition 21: Triple Baptismal Questions and Composite Text | 《使徒传统》21：三重洗礼问答与复合文本问题

- **Date / text and witnesses:** Traditionally Hippolytus/Rome/about 215 CE; modern research cannot safely assign the whole to one author/place/year. Sparse Greek; chapter 21 reconstructed from fourth-century Latin in a sixth-century Verona palimpsest and later Sahidic Coptic, Arabic, Ethiopic, related orders. Bradshaw/Johnson/Phillips identify varied sources/layers, including core, bishop instructions, priest/deacon details here. The 215 label is research history, not an extant original rite.
  **年代／文本与传本**：这部教会规程传统上归给罗马的希坡律陀，并常标作约主后 215 年；现代研究却不能安全地把整部作品交给一位作者、一个地点或一年。希腊文原作只存少量片段，第 21 章主要须由四世纪拉丁译本所传的六世纪维罗纳重写羊皮卷，以及较晚的萨希迪科普特文、阿拉伯文、埃塞俄比亚文版本和相关教会规程互校重建。Bradshaw、Johnson 与 Phillips 认为全书含不同来源、年代和编辑层；第 21 章本身也至少可分析出核心、监督指令和司祭／执事细则等层次。因此“传统约主后 215 年”是研究史标签，不等于手中有一份当年罗马礼仪原稿。
- **Material type:** Candidates answer I believe to Almighty Father; Jesus Christ born through Spirit/virgin Mary, crucified under Pilate, dead/buried, raised third day, ascended/seated at Father's right, returning judge; then Spirit, holy church, resurrection of flesh. Each answer accompanies baptism, placing story into embodied communal initiation.
  **材料性质**：第 21 章描写洗礼候选人在水中逐题回答“我信”。现传重建的三题依次指向全能的父；由圣灵和童贞女马利亚所生、在本丢·彼拉多下被钉、死而埋葬、第三日从死者中复活、升天、坐在父右边并将审判活人死人的基督耶稣；以及圣灵、圣教会和肉身复活。每题回答后施洗一次，显示信仰故事不是只供书房讨论，而被置入群体入门与身体行动。
- **Related Scripture:** Matthew 28:19; Gospel birth/passion/resurrection; Acts 1:9–11, 10:42; Romans 6:3–5; 1 Corinthians 15; 2 Timothy 4:1; 1 Peter 3:21–22.
  **对应经文**：马太福音 28:19；四福音的降生、受难与复活叙事；使徒行传 1:9–11、10:42；罗马书 6:3–5；哥林多前书 15 章；提摩太后书 4:1；彼得前书 3:21–22。
- **Supports:** Multilingual transmission attests Father–Son–Spirit questions, continuous Christ story, and bodily hope, showing how the framework of flexible faith rules becomes spoken, embodied initiation in a transmitted church order.
  **能支持**：现存多语传统共同见证一种父—子—圣灵的三重洗礼问答，基督题把降生、彼拉多、十架、埋葬、第三日复活、升天、掌权与再临审判压成连续故事，第三题又把耶稣的复活接到信徒所盼望的肉身复活。与爱任纽、特土良的灵活“信仰规则”相比，这里进一步显示同一故事骨架怎样在传下来的教会规程中成为受洗者亲口回答并以身体进入的身份告白。
- **Does not establish:** No apostolic authorship from title or verbatim 215 Roman original. Later versions/rewriting postdate proposed original; Latin resumes only at question two, with question one mainly reconstructed from Eastern versions. Similarity does not prove direct word-for-word descent to Old Roman/modern Apostles' Creed. Later reception, not 30s baptism/eyewitness/event records.
  **不能支持**：不能只凭书名就断言使徒亲写，也不能把重建文本的每个词都定为主后 215 年罗马原礼文。现存版本年代、地域和改写历史均晚于假定原作；拉丁文本在入门段落长期缺文，直到第二题开头才恢复，第一题主要须靠东方版本重建。相似内容也不能证明它逐字产生了古罗马信条或现代《使徒信经》。它是较晚的礼仪接收史，不是主后 30 年代的洗礼记录、复活目击档案或事件证明。
- **Debate:** Hippolytan authorship, Rome/elsewhere, second–fourth-century layers/final editing, and chapter details remain disputed. Bradshaw favors composite long formation; Stewart-Sykes retains more Hippolytan/early-third-century setting. Attested structure/story order differs from dating every word.
  **争议**：作者是否为希坡律陀、材料应放在罗马还是其他地区、最早层与最终编辑分别属于二至四世纪何时，以及第 21 章哪些细则属于早期核心，均无定论。Bradshaw 等主张复合来源和长时段编纂；Stewart-Sykes 等仍较多保留希坡律陀和早期三世纪背景。研究可确认“现传规程中的问答结构与故事次序”，却必须把“最早层可能较早”与“整段逐字可定年”分开。
- **Grade:** A for multilingual questions/story/bodily wording/carriers/version relations; B for baptismal use; C for author/215 Rome/original clauses/direct Old Roman genealogy; D for apostolic original/first-century rite/independent resurrection eyewitness or proof.
  **等级**：A（多语传本所见三重问答、基督故事次序及肉身复活明文；现存载体和版本关系）；B（教会规程传统把复活纲要用于洗礼入门）；C（希坡律陀归属、约主后 215 年罗马定位、各句原始年代及与古罗马信条的直接谱系）；D（使徒原稿、第一世纪礼文、独立复活目击或事件证明）。
- **Sources:** [Burton Scott Easton: chapter 21 baptismal questions and older reconstruction](https://www.gutenberg.org/files/61614/61614-h/61614-h.htm); [Bradshaw / Johnson / Phillips: versions, composite sources, chapter 21 layers](https://www.jstor.org/stable/j.ctvb9371z); [John F. Baldovin: uncertain author and third-century Roman attribution](https://doi.org/10.1177/004056390306400303); [Notre Dame: 2002 commentary, multilingual/composite reconstruction](https://news.nd.edu/news/two-theologians-publish-new-book-on-apostolic-tradition/); [Wolfram Kinzig 2024: baptismal questions, creeds, Apostolic Tradition research limits](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9783110318531_A30252850/preview-9783110318531_A30252850.pdf)
  **来源**：[Burton Scott Easton 英译：第 21 章三重洗礼问答及其旧式希坡律陀重建](https://www.gutenberg.org/files/61614/61614-h/61614-h.htm)；[Bradshaw、Johnson、Phillips：多版本对读、复合来源与第 21 章分层](https://www.jstor.org/stable/j.ctvb9371z)；[John F. Baldovin：作者与三世纪罗马归属均不能安全确定](https://doi.org/10.1177/004056390306400303)；[Notre Dame：2002 年评注本的多语重建与复合文本结论](https://news.nd.edu/news/two-theologians-publish-new-book-on-apostolic-tradition/)；[Wolfram Kinzig 2024：洗礼问答、固定信条与《使徒传统》重建的研究史边界](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9783110318531_A30252850/preview-9783110318531_A30252850.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-27.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-27。

<a id="evidence-144"></a>

### 144. Marcellus to Julius: Earliest Continuous Old Roman Creed and Limits of Projection | 马尔凯鲁斯致犹流书中的认信：古罗马信条最早连续文本与倒推边界

- **Date / text and witnesses:** Deposed Marcellus of Ancyra submits a defense to Roman bishop Julius I around 340/341 CE, preserved by Epiphanius' Panarion 72.2–3 around 375. Its Greek first-person confession is usually identified as the earliest complete continuous Old Roman Creed R, an ancestor of later Western Apostles' Creed. First surviving continuous text is not first appearance of content or a surviving second-century original.
  **年代／文本与传本**：安居拉的马尔凯鲁斯被废黜后到罗马为自己的正统性申辩，约主后 340／341 年向罗马主教犹流一世递交书信；该信后来由撒拉米的埃皮法纽约主后 375 年写成的《药箱集》／《驳异端》72.2–3 保存。书末的希腊文第一人称认信通常被识别为古罗马信条（简称 R）最早完整、连续的现存形式，也是后来西方《使徒信经》文本史的重要祖本；“最早现存连续文本”却不等于“内容首次在 340／341 年出现”或“二世纪原稿已经保存”。
- **Material type:** Scripturally framed defense amid doctrinal/Roman synodal disputes: Almighty God; only Son born through Spirit/virgin Mary; Pilate/crucifixion/burial; third-day resurrection/ascension/Father's right/judgment; Spirit/church/forgiveness/flesh resurrection/eternal life. Greek opening lacks Father and ending includes eternal life; later Latin forms must not silently erase differences.
  **材料性质**：马尔凯鲁斯在教义争议和罗马会议处境中陈明自己从圣经所领受的信仰。认信从全能的神开始，接着讲祂的独生子耶稣基督由圣灵和童贞女马利亚所生、在本丢·彼拉多下被钉并埋葬、第三日从死者中复活、升天、坐在父右边、将审判活人死人；末段列圣灵、圣教会、罪得赦免、肉身复活和永生。现传希腊文首句没有“父”，末尾有“永生”；这些差异不可被后来的拉丁形式静默抹平。
- **Related Scripture:** Gospel birth/passion/burial/third-day resurrection; Acts 1:9–11, 10:42; 1 Corinthians 15:3–4, 42–54; 2 Timothy 4:1; Revelation 20–22.
  **对应经文**：四福音的降生、受难、埋葬与第三日复活；使徒行传 1:9–11、10:42；哥林多前书 15:3–4、42–54；提摩太后书 4:1；启示录 20–22 章。
- **Supports:** By 340/341 a continuous first-person confession combines Jesus' history, church life, and future bodily life. Shared framework with faith rules/questions, but more fixed wording; Christ's third-day rising grounds future flesh resurrection, linking Gospels, Corinthians, Revelation in the route.
  **能支持**：到主后 340／341 年，一份连续、第一人称的希腊文认信已经把耶稣的历史叙事、现今教会生活和将来身体复活／永生压缩在同一条线上。它与前一阶段的信仰规则和洗礼问答共享故事骨架，却比爱任纽、特土良的可伸缩摘要更接近固定文本；同时它明确让耶稣“第三日复活”成为将来“肉身复活”的根据，帮助 52 卷路线从福音书接到哥林多前书和启示录。
- **Does not establish:** The Old Roman name does not project every clause into second-century Rome or supply today's complete Apostles' Creed. Marcellus uses/adapts a defense, not a verbatim live baptism record, transmitted through later Epiphanius. Fourth-century text/reception, not apostolic coauthorship, first-century survey, or resurrection external proof.
  **不能支持**：不能因这份文本后来被称为“古罗马信条”，就把每句无缝倒推到二世纪罗马，也不能说现传文字就是今天通行《使徒信经》的完整原文。马尔凯鲁斯是在申辩中使用或调整这份认信，并非留下罗马洗礼礼仪现场逐字记录；他的文本又经四世纪后期埃皮法纽传递。它证明四世纪可见文本和接收，不是使徒合写信经、第一世纪群体问卷或复活事件外证。
- **Debate:** Older research places baptismal prehistory late second/early third century. Kinzig/Vinzent 1999 favor fourth-century fixed declaration linked to Marcellus/340–341; Westra argues an earlier Roman precursor from broad Western successors. Kinzig 2024 recognizes more complex prehistory. Safest: full attestation in 340/341; earlier questions/precursors merit reconstruction, exact words/dates grade C.
  **争议**：传统研究常把 R 的罗马洗礼前史推到二世纪末或三世纪初；Kinzig 与 Vinzent 1999 年曾主张固定宣告式信条不早于四世纪，并把 R 的形成与马尔凯鲁斯及主后 340／341 年罗马会议联系起来。Westra 等反对把它视为马尔凯鲁斯新作，依据西方后继文本的广泛分布主张更早的罗马前身；Kinzig 2024 年也明确承认 R 的前史比早先晚定年模型更复杂。因此最稳妥的表述是：“340／341 年首次完整见证；更早问答或罗马前身很可能值得重建，但具体措辞和年代属 C 级。”
- **Grade:** A for continuous Panarion Greek/defense context/latest 340–341; B for fixed resurrection/life reception/later ancestry; C for Marcellus' role/exact Roman precursor/second-third-century dating; D for twelve-apostle coauthorship/modern verbatim creed/first-century events.
  **等级**：A（《药箱集》72.2–3 所传连续希腊文、马尔凯鲁斯致犹流的申辩处境及 340／341 年最迟年代）；B（四世纪固定认信中的第三日复活、肉身复活和永生接收；其为后来西方信经的重要祖本）；C（马尔凯鲁斯是否引用、改写或促成 R，罗马礼仪前身的精确文字和二／三世纪年代）；D（十二使徒共同写成、现代《使徒信经》逐字原文、第一世纪事件证明）。
- **Sources:** [Epiphanius Panarion 72.2: Marcellus to Julius, Frank Williams excerpt](https://tertullian.org/rpearse/epiphanius.html); [Schaff / CCEL: Greek confession, translation, and differences from Rufinus](https://ccel.org/ccel/s/schaff/creeds2/cache/creeds2.pdf); [Glen L. Thompson: Marcellus' letter, Greek/translation/introduction/commentary](https://www.jstor.org/stable/j.ctt130h994); [Kinzig / Vinzent 1999: late model of fixed-creed origins](https://doi.org/10.1093/jts/50.2.535); [Westra: Old Roman prehistory, regional variants, Apostles' Creed](https://www.brepolsonline.net/doi/abs/10.1484/M.IPM-EB.5.112152); [Wolfram Kinzig 2024: revised synthesis of R's 340/341 first attestation](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9783110318531_A30252850/preview-9783110318531_A30252850.pdf)
  **来源**：[Epiphanius《药箱集》72.2：马尔凯鲁斯致犹流书的申辩处境，Frank Williams 英译摘录](https://tertullian.org/rpearse/epiphanius.html)；[Schaff／CCEL：马尔凯鲁斯希腊文认信、英译及与鲁非努拉丁形式的差异](https://ccel.org/ccel/s/schaff/creeds2/cache/creeds2.pdf)；[Glen L. Thompson：马尔凯鲁斯致犹流书的希腊文、英译、导论与评注](https://www.jstor.org/stable/j.ctt130h994)；[Kinzig、Vinzent 1999：固定信条起源的晚定年模型](https://doi.org/10.1093/jts/50.2.535)；[Westra：古罗马信条前史、区域变体与后来《使徒信经》](https://www.brepolsonline.net/doi/abs/10.1484/M.IPM-EB.5.112152)；[Wolfram Kinzig 2024：旧争论、新证据与“R 首见于 340／341 年”的更新综合](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9783110318531_A30252850/preview-9783110318531_A30252850.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-27.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-27。

<a id="evidence-145"></a>

### 145. Rufinus' Creed Commentary: Roman/Aquileian Variants Around 404 and Apostolic Legend | 鲁非努《信经评注》：约主后 404 年罗马／阿奎莱亚文本、地方变体与使徒传说

- **Date / text and witnesses:** Rufinus of Aquileia writes Latin Expositio symboli around 404 for bishop Laurentius, expounding his baptismal sequence and comparing Rome: key Western initiation/variation evidence. One old online translation introduction misprints 307–309, about forty years before his birth; modern editions/research support around 404.
  **年代／文本与传本**：阿奎莱亚的鲁非努约在主后 404 年应劳伦提乌斯主教之请写成拉丁文《信经评注》（*Expositio symboli*）。作品按作者自己在阿奎莱亚受洗时所领受的次序逐条解释，又屡次拿罗马形式比较；它是研究四、五世纪之交西方信经、入门教导和地区差异的核心文本。网上一部旧英译导言把年代误排成“307–309”，早于鲁非努出生约四十年；这里依据现代校本与研究采用“约 404 年”。
- **Material type:** Aquileian clauses add invisible, not subject to suffering after Father Almighty, descent to the dead between burial/resurrection, and this flesh's resurrection. Rufinus says descent absent from contemporary Roman/Eastern creeds, implicit in burial. He reports Roman public baptismal recitation resisting a word's addition, versus local anti-heretical additions elsewhere.
  **材料性质**：从逐条评注可恢复的阿奎莱亚形式，在“全能的父”后加“不可见、不能受苦”，在埋葬与第三日复活之间有“降在阴间”，并以“这肉身复活”收尾。鲁非努明说“降在阴间”不在当时罗马及东方诸教会的信经中，认为其意思已隐含在“埋葬”内；他还报告罗马受洗者在会众面前公开背诵，信徒的耳朵不容增加一字，而其他地方为排除异端教导有所增补。
- **Related Scripture:** Gospel birth/Pilate/cross/burial/third-day resurrection; Acts 1:9–11, 2:22–36, 10:42; 1 Peter 3:18–22; 1 Corinthians 15; Revelation 20–22.
  **对应经文**：四福音的降生、彼拉多、十架、埋葬与第三日复活；使徒行传 1:9–11、2:22–36、10:42；彼得前书 3:18–22；哥林多前书 15 章；启示录 20–22 章。
- **Supports:** Shared framework but differing wording around 404; fixed public recitation and dispute-specific additions coexist. Pilate–burial–descent–third-day rising links Christ's body, baptismal identity, and this flesh's future resurrection.
  **能支持**：约主后 404 年，罗马和阿奎莱亚虽共享古罗马信条的基本故事骨架，却不是逐字相同；地方教会会以固定背诵保护文本，也会为具体争论增加限定语。阿奎莱亚把“彼拉多—埋葬—降在阴间—第三日复活”接到“这肉身复活”，清楚显示基督的身体故事、受洗者身份与信徒将来的身体盼望被读成一线。
- **Does not establish:** His inherited story of apostles each contributing before dispersal establishes an origin legend circulating around 400, not first-century writing of the modern Creed. Descent was not then universal Roman/Eastern wording or all later interpretations; no independent resurrection testimony.
  **不能支持**：鲁非努转述“众使徒在分赴各国前共同拟定、每人贡献一句”的先辈传统，只能证明这项起源传说约主后 400 年已流通，不能证明十二使徒在一世纪写下现代《使徒信经》。阿奎莱亚的“降在阴间”既不是当时罗马或东方普遍条款，也不能把后来关于该句的所有教义解释倒灌给鲁非努；这份评注同样不是复活事件的独立外证。
- **Debate:** Approximate 404 is not a signed month/place. His no-Roman-heresy explanation, public-recitation history, and regional additions are participant interpretation, not a Western census. Earlier clauses/exact Roman wording require later comparative witnesses.
  **争议**：作品可约定到主后 404 年，精确月份和写作地点仍无自署日期。鲁非努对罗马“从未产生异端”的解释、对公开背诵古制的概括及对其他地区增补原因的说明，是一位参与者的历史解释，不等于经过独立调查的全西方统计；阿奎莱亚每一特殊条款何时加入、鲁非努所比较的罗马底本精确到哪一字，也须由后继文本共同重建。
- **Grade:** A for Latin/explicit comparison/local clauses; B for initiation/public recitation/variants/bodily hope; C for clause origins/exact forms/universality; D for apostolic modern-Creed composition/first-century local clauses/event proof.
  **等级**：A（《评注》现传拉丁文字、罗马／阿奎莱亚明示比较、“降在阴间”与“这肉身复活”等地方条款）；B（四、五世纪之交西方入门教导、公开背诵和区域变体；身体复活的信经接收）；C（每项条款的更早年代、罗马／阿奎莱亚底本逐字复原及鲁非努历史解释的普遍性）；D（十二使徒合写现代信经、地方条款的一世纪来源或复活事件证明）。
- **Sources:** [Rufinus: Commentary on the Creed, translation/Aquileian Latin](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/2711.htm); [Wolfram Kinzig: 404 dating, Roman public recitation, rejection of added words](https://publikationen.uni-tuebingen.de/xmlui/bitstream/handle/10900/123818/Kinzig_122.pdf?isAllowed=y&sequence=1); [Liuwe H. Westra: Old Roman/local/Apostles' Creed textual history](https://doi.org/10.1484/M.IPM-EB.5.112152); [Cambridge: Rufinus' pro-Nicene initiation in late-antique Italy](https://www.cambridge.org/core/books/knowledge-faith-and-early-christian-initiation/catechesis-in-late-antique-italy/FC5CC4CD0033C64BA7519125301EDFA4); [Wolfram Kinzig 2024: R, Rufinus RR, and received text T](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9783110318531_A30252850/preview-9783110318531_A30252850.pdf)
  **来源**：[鲁非努：《信经评注》英译与阿奎莱亚拉丁形式](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/2711.htm)；[Wolfram Kinzig：约 404 年定年、罗马公开背诵及拒绝加字的原文讨论](https://publikationen.uni-tuebingen.de/xmlui/bitstream/handle/10900/123818/Kinzig_122.pdf?isAllowed=y&sequence=1)；[Liuwe H. Westra：古罗马信条、地方变体与后来《使徒信经》的文本史](https://doi.org/10.1484/M.IPM-EB.5.112152)；[Cambridge：鲁非努在晚期意大利的亲尼西亚入门教导](https://www.cambridge.org/core/books/knowledge-faith-and-early-christian-initiation/catechesis-in-late-antique-italy/FC5CC4CD0033C64BA7519125301EDFA4)；[Wolfram Kinzig 2024：R、鲁非努形式 RR 与通行文本 T 的区别](https://api.pageplace.de/preview/DT0400.9783110318531_A30252850/preview-9783110318531_A30252850.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-27.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-27。

<a id="evidence-146"></a>

### 146. Nicene Creed of 325: Conciliar Definition, Transmission, and Modern Liturgy | 325 年尼西亚信经：会议定义、传文差异与现代礼仪文本的区别

- **Date / text and transmission:** Constantine's council issued Greek definition/anathemas in 325. Signed original/full daily acts are lost; compare contemporary Eusebius' letter, Athanasius, Socrates, Philostorgius, and 451 Chalcedon. Stable body, but created in anathemas varies; modern print is not an unvariant original parchment.
  **年代／文本与传递**：罗马皇帝君士坦丁召集的尼西亚会议于主后 325 年发布希腊文信仰定义及附加谴责语。会议签署原件和完整逐日记录没有保存下来；现传文本须由同年与会者该撒利亚的优西比乌致本教会书信，以及亚他那修、苏格拉底、菲洛斯托尔吉乌和 451 年迦克墩会议档案等见证校合。正文高度稳定，附加谴责中的“受造”一词却并非所有传文都有，提醒读者不要把现代排印本误当作一张无异文的原始羊皮纸。
- **Material type:** A definition for Christological disputes, not a complete local baptismal life-story. one almighty Father, Creator of all visible/invisible things; one Lord Jesus Christ, begotten from the Father, not created, of one essence with the Father (homoousios), creation through him; descent/incarnation/humanity/passion/third-day resurrection/ascension/judgment; brief Spirit clause. Anathemas reject once nonexistent, from nothing, different essence, mutability.
  **材料性质**：这是一份为特定基督论争议发布的会议性定义，不是完整叙述耶稣生平的地方洗礼问答。正文认信一位创造可见与不可见万有的全能父；一位由父所生、不是受造、与父“同质／同一本质”（*homoousios*）的主耶稣基督，万有借祂而成；祂为人的救恩降下、成肉身、成为人、受难、第三日复活、升天并将审判活人死人；最后仅简短说“并信圣灵”。附文谴责“曾有一时祂不存在”“从无而有”、不同本质及可改变等说法。
- **Related Scripture:** Genesis 1; John 1:1–18; 1 Corinthians 8:6; Philippians 2:6–11; Colossians 1:15–20; Hebrews 1:1–4; Gospel passion/resurrection; Acts 1:9–11, 10:42; 1 Corinthians 15:3–4.
  **对应经文**：创世记 1 章；约翰福音 1:1–18；哥林多前书 8:6；腓立比书 2:6–11；歌罗西书 1:15–20；希伯来书 1:1–4；四福音受难与第三日复活；使徒行传 1:9–11、10:42；哥林多前书 15:3–4。
- **Supports:** A transregional council defines creation, Father–Son relation, saving incarnation, passion/rising/ascension/judgment with disciplinary force. Resurrection belongs to Creator/Son's saving identity; homoousios/anathemas exclude classifying Son among creatures.
  **能支持**：到主后 325 年，一次跨地区主教会议已把“创造—圣子与父的关系—为救恩成肉身—受难—第三日复活—升天—再临审判”写成具有纪律作用的共同定义。它显示复活并非与“耶稣是谁”分开的孤立神迹：若万有借圣子而成，祂的成肉身、受难与复活就处在同一创造主的救赎行动中；“同质”及谴责语又表明会议试图排除把圣子归入受造物的读法。
- **Does not establish:** Not today's Niceno-Constantinopolitan liturgical text. It lacks later full clauses on Spirit/virgin Mary, Pilate, burial, according to Scriptures, one holy catholic apostolic church, one baptism, dead rising, coming-age life; no filioque. No biblical book-list decision in this creed or independent archaeological resurrection proof.
  **不能支持**：325 年文本不是今天主日礼仪通常诵读的尼西亚—君士坦丁堡信经。它没有“由圣灵和童贞女马利亚成肉身”“本丢·彼拉多”“埋葬”“按圣经”“一圣大公使徒教会”“一次洗礼”“死人复活”和“来世生命”等后来文本的完整条款；也没有西方的 *filioque*。会议没有在这份信经中决定圣经目录，信经本身也不能作为一项独立考古证据证明复活事件。
- **Debate:** Eusebius' preceding confession as fixed Caesarean baptismal text/draft source, drafting sequence, and signers' identical understanding of homoousios remain debated. Early attendance counts around 250/270/300 differ from later 318, with Genesis 14:14 symbolic reception, not a precise verifiable roll.
  **争议**：优西比乌信中先引的认信究竟是否该撒利亚的固定地方洗礼信经、在多大程度上成为会议草案，以及会议如何逐句形成最终文本，仍有争论；“同质”在不同签署者那里是否完全同义也不可假定。与会主教数的早期资料约为 250、270 或 300，后来固定的“318 位教父”兼有创世记 14:14 的象征接收史，不宜当成可逐名核验的精确点名册。
- **Grade:** A for collated 325 body/main anathemas/context; B for conciliar doctrine/discipline/story; C for drafts/Eusebian source/variants/signatures/counts; D for modern verbatim identity/immediate universal use/canon decision/event proof.
  **等级**：A（多源校合的 325 年正文、主要谴责语及尼西亚会议处境）；B（跨地区会议以信经作为教义／纪律准绳，并把第三日复活嵌入创造—救赎故事）；C（草拟过程、优西比乌底本性质、个别异文、签署名单和精确人数）；D（与今天礼仪文本逐字相同、当年立刻全球一致使用、圣经定典决定或复活事件证明）。
- **Sources:** [Nicene Creed of 325 and anathemas, English translation](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/3801.htm); [Greek, Latin, and Kelly's English translation](https://earlychurchtexts.com/main/nicaea/creed_of_nicaea.shtml); [Athanasius: Decrees of Nicaea, essence/homoousios](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/2809.htm); [Eusebius to Caesarea: transmission and contemporary value](https://biblehub.com/library/athanasius/select_works_and_letters_or_athanasius/epistola_eusebii.htm); [Mark J. Edwards: purpose, variants, origin models](https://doi.org/10.1017/9781108613200.007); [Multi-author study (2025): contemporary sources and the 318 tradition](https://www.degruyterbrill.com/de/document/isbn/9783657797868/html); [Wolfram Kinzig 2024: Nicaea through Chalcedon](https://doi.org/10.3917/commun.296.0032)
  **来源**：[325 年尼西亚信经及谴责语英译](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/3801.htm)；[希腊文、拉丁文与 Kelly 英译对照](https://earlychurchtexts.com/main/nicaea/creed_of_nicaea.shtml)；[亚他那修《论尼西亚会议决议》及其对“从本质／同质”的说明](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/2809.htm)；[优西比乌致该撒利亚教会书信的传递与会议当年价值](https://biblehub.com/library/athanasius/select_works_and_letters_or_athanasius/epistola_eusebii.htm)；[Mark J. Edwards：信经目的、传文异读与起源方案](https://doi.org/10.1017/9781108613200.007)；[2025 年多作者研究：优先重审当代材料及“318”形成史](https://www.degruyterbrill.com/de/document/isbn/9783657797868/html)；[Wolfram Kinzig 2024：尼西亚信经及其至迦克墩的接收史](https://doi.org/10.3917/commun.296.0032)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-27.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-27。

<a id="evidence-147"></a>

### 147. Niceno-Constantinopolitan C2: Traditional 381 Attribution and First Full 451 Attestation | 尼西亚—君士坦丁堡信经 C2：传统 381 年归属、451 年首份明文与全故事压缩

- **Date / text and transmission:** 381 reaffirmed Nicaea; its doctrinal tome/anathemas are lost and 382 bishops' letter summarizes Trinity/incarnation. Modern C2 first appears completely/explicitly in 451 Chalcedonian acts, read by a Constantinopolitan deacon as Nicene-compatible faith of the 150 fathers. State both traditional 381 attribution and full extant attestation by 451.
  **年代／文本与传递**：君士坦丁堡会议于主后 381 年重申尼西亚信仰；会议本身的教义长卷和谴责书没有保存，382 年主教书信只作三一与成肉身摘要。今天通常称为“尼西亚—君士坦丁堡信经”的希腊文本（现代研究常记作 C2），现存首次完整且明确的会议见证出现在主后 451 年迦克墩会议记录：一位君士坦丁堡执事从书卷读出，会议称其为与尼西亚一致的“150 位教父的信仰”。因此“传统归于 381 年”和“现有连续文本最迟在 451 年可见”必须同时写明。
- **Material type:** Creation; eternally begotten Son, of one essence with the Father; incarnation through Spirit/virgin Mary, Pilate/cross/suffering/burial/scriptural third-day rising, ascension/right hand/return/endless kingdom; Spirit as Lord and life-giver, proceeding from the Father and speaking through prophets; one holy catholic apostolic church, one forgiving baptism, dead rising, coming-age life.
  **材料性质**：C2 从创造天地、可见与不可见万有开始；宣告永恒受生、与父同质的圣子，接着展开祂由圣灵和童贞女马利亚成肉身、在本丢·彼拉多下被钉、受难、埋葬、按圣经第三日复活、升天坐在父右边、荣耀再来和国度无穷；又称圣灵为主、赐生命者、从父而出、借先知说话；末段接一圣大公使徒教会、一次赦罪的洗礼、死人复活和来世生命。
