- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: 4Q175 / Testimonia, material/script/DJD V](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q175-1?locale=en_US); [USC West Semitic Research Project: state, combinations, dating](https://dornsife.usc.edu/wsrp/testimonia/)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局：4Q175／4QTestimonia 数字档案、载体、书体与 DJD V 出版资料](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q175-1?locale=en_US)；[USC West Semitic Research Project：短卷状况、经文组合与常见断年](https://dornsife.usc.edu/wsrp/testimonia/)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-186"></a>

### 186. 11Q13 / Melchizedek: Jubilee, Remission, Good News, and Final Atonement | 11Q13／11QMelchizedek：禧年、债务释放、好消息与末世赎罪

- **Date / discovery and carrier:** Cave 11 fragmentary Hebrew parchment exegesis, IAA Hasmonean square-script nonbiblical work; DJD XXIII 221–241. Often late-second/early-first century BCE, not first-conception year.
  **年代／出土与载体**：11Q13 出自昆兰第 11 洞，是残碎的希伯来文羊皮释经作品。以色列文物局把方体字宽归哈斯摩尼时期，列为非圣经“释经文本”；正式出版为 DJD XXIII, pp. 221–241。学术窄断年常落在主前二世纪末至一世纪前半，但不能由此推出作品初次构思的精确年。
- **Material type:** Column two combines Leviticus 25:13 land return, Deuteronomy 15:2 remission, Isaiah 61:1–2 release/favor and 52:7 good news, Psalm 82 council/judgment, Daniel 9 periods/anointed one as tenth Jubilee. Melchizedek releases/atones/judges against Belial. Separate visible letters, restored quotations, interpretive supplements.
  **材料性质**：保存较多的第二栏把利 25:13 禧年归回、申 15:2 债务豁免、赛 61:1–2 释放／“恩年”、赛 52:7 报好信息、诗 82 的天上议会与审判、但 9 的受膏者和时期语言组合成“第十个禧年”的末世解释；麦基洗德在其中执行释放、赎罪与审判，并与彼列阵营对立。大量文字残缺，现代译本必须把可见字母、经文复原和解释性补字分开。
- **Related Scripture:** Leviticus 25; Deuteronomy 15; Psalm 82; Isaiah 52:7, 61:1–2; Daniel 9:24–25; Luke 4:16–21; Hebrews 7; weeks 8, 10, 19, 26, 32, 34, 37, 50.
  **对应经文**：利 25；申 15；诗 82；赛 52:7、61:1–2；但 9:24–25；路 4:16–21；来 7；禧年、豁免、好消息、赎罪和麦基洗德接收史；第 8、10、19、26、32、34、37、50 周。
- **Supports:** Pre-Christian combined Jubilee/debt/Isaianic release/good news/atonement/judgment through an exalted agent, illuminating Luke 4/Hebrews 7 and broader New Testament interpretive language.
  **能支持**：基督教以前已有犹太释经者把禧年土地归回、安息年债务豁免、以赛亚的释放／好消息、赎罪日和末世审判组合起来，并以一位高举的麦基洗德作为拯救与审判代理。这使路 4 的“主的恩年／释放”、来 7 的麦基洗德论证和更广的新约“好消息—赦免—末世”语言处于可理解的第二圣殿解释世界。
- **Does not establish:** No Jesus/Nazareth or direct Genesis 14/Psalm 110 citation; no verbatim Hebrews 7 argument, authors' access, Jesus' Qumran calendar, or identical Christ identification.
  **不能支持**：11Q13 没有提耶稣或拿撒勒，也没有直接引用创 14 或诗 110；虽然麦基洗德名称及若干主题使三者可以比较，不能写成卷轴已经逐字拥有《希伯来书》7 章的论证。它也不能证明路加或《希伯来书》作者读过本卷、耶稣采用昆兰历法，或昆兰麦基洗德就是基督教所认的基督。
- **Debate:** Heavenly/angelic/messianic/other identity and elohim/atonement/Psalm 82/Daniel relations debated; the work does not directly call him priest. Jubilee calculation/start/gaps uncertain. Shared exegesis stays distinct from direct dependence without further evidence.
  **争议**：麦基洗德在残文中应称天上／天使性代理、弥赛亚人物还是其他高举身份，及其与 `elohim`、赎罪日、诗 82 和但 9 的精确关系，仍有讨论；作品本身没有直接称他为“祭司”。利 25 与申 15 的协调可视为末世化法律释经，但“第十禧年”的年代起点和缺行复原不稳。与路 4、来 7 的关系宜评为共同经文与解释环境，除非另有文本证据，不升级为直接文学依赖。
- **Grade:** A for object/cave/material/language/Hasmonean class/visible combinations/edition; B for combined interpretation; C for date/restoration/identity/Jubilee/New Testament relationship; D for Jesus/Nazareth event/calendar/direct Hebrews copying.
  **等级**：A（器物、洞穴、羊皮、希伯来文、哈斯摩尼宽断代、可见经文组合和 DJD 出版）；B（前基督教时期禧年—豁免—好消息—赎罪—末世审判的组合式释经）；C（窄断年、缺行复原、麦基洗德身份、禧年计算及与新约的直接关系）；D（耶稣身份、拿撒勒讲道事件、昆兰历法使用或《希伯来书》直接抄用证明）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: 11Q13 / Melchizedek, material/script/DJD XXIII](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/11Q13-1?locale=en_US); [University of Minnesota: Leviticus 25 / Deuteronomy 15 legal exegesis](https://experts.umn.edu/en/publications/this-is-the-manner-of-the-remission-implicit-legal-exegesis-in-11/); [Gard Granerød: no direct Genesis 14 / Psalm 110 quotation, Hebrews comparison](https://www.bsw.org/biblica/vol-90-2009/melchizedek-in-hebrews-7/15/article-p198.html); [Logan Williams: Jubilee, Isaiah 61, forgiveness in 11Q13](https://doi.org/10.1177/0142064X231191176)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局：11Q13／11QMelchizedek 数字档案、载体、书体与 DJD XXIII 出版资料](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/11Q13-1?locale=en_US)；[明尼苏达大学研究目录：利 25 与申 15 豁免法规的组合式法律释经](https://experts.umn.edu/en/publications/this-is-the-manner-of-the-remission-implicit-legal-exegesis-in-11/)；[Gard Granerød：11Q13 没有直接引用创 14／诗 110，及与来 7 的比较边界](https://www.bsw.org/biblica/vol-90-2009/melchizedek-in-hebrews-7/15/article-p198.html)；[Logan Williams：11Q13 的禧年、赛 61 与赦罪解释](https://doi.org/10.1177/0142064X231191176)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-187"></a>

### 187. Elephantine P. 13464 / TAD A4.1: the So-Called Passover Letter | Pap. Berlin P. 13464／TAD A4.1：象岛“逾越节书信”与散居节期实践

- **Date / discovery and collection:** Excavated by German expedition, Berlin Egyptian Museum/Papyrus Collection P.13464, also TAD A4.1/Cowley 21. Darius year five, Berlin project's 419–418 BCE.
  **年代／出土与馆藏**：这件帝国亚兰文纸草由德国发掘队在埃及象岛正式考古发掘取得，现藏柏林国家博物馆埃及博物馆与纸草收藏，馆藏号 Pap. Berlin P. 13464；标准编号另有 TAD A4.1、Cowley 21。书信以大流士王第五年纪年，柏林 Elephantine 数字项目给出的公历范围为主前 419–418 年。
- **Material type:** Hananiah to Yedaniah/Jewish garrison, involving Nisan 14–21, seven unleavened days, rest at ends, restrictions on eating or storing leavened matter, royal instruction to Arsames. Important dates/verbs/Passover wording reconstructed. Berlin calls it so-called, with calendar, unleavened-bread, soldiers' furlough alternatives.
  **材料性质**：书信由哈拿尼亚写给耶大尼亚及象岛“犹太军队／驻军”同伴。残文涉及尼散月第十四至二十一日、七日无酵、两端停止工作、禁食或收藏发酵物，并提到王向埃及总督亚沙姆所发命令。传统因此称它为“逾越节书信”，但纸草残损，若干关键日数、动词和“逾越节”读法依赖复原；柏林现行数据库特意称“所谓的逾越节书信”，并注明近期研究争论它是否实际在规定逾越节，另有历法调整、无酵节或军人宗教休假等解释。
- **Related Scripture:** Exodus 12:1–20, 23:15; Leviticus 23:4–8; Numbers 28:16–25; Deuteronomy 16:1–8; Ezra 6:19–22; Nehemiah diaspora; Matthew 26; Mark 14; Luke 22; John 2, 6, 11–13, 18–19; weeks 6, 8, 10, 29, 40.
  **对应经文**：出 12:1–20、23:15；利 23:4–8；民 28:16–25；申 16:1–8；拉 6:19–22；尼希米记的波斯帝国与散居网络；太 26；可 14；路 22；约 2、6、11–13、18–19；第 6、8、10、29、40 周。
- **Supports:** Late-fifth-century southern Egyptian Judean garrison used Nisan/unleavened/rest/leaven instructions, connecting festival memory with Persian diaspora, correspondence/local leaders/administration. Specific evidence for its own surviving words/community, beyond a broad memory claim.
  **能支持**：主前五世纪后期，埃及南境的犹大／犹太驻军群体确实使用尼散月日期、七日无酵、停工和除酵等节期指令；这个实物把出埃及传统中的节期记忆接到波斯帝国散居生活，也显示宗教日历会通过书信、地方领袖和帝国行政网络实际协调。它比笼统说“散居者仍记得逾越节”更具体，却首先证明自己的残文和这一群体。
- **Does not establish:** No Moses/plagues/lamb blood/sea/Exodus event or Exodus manuscript. No complete Pentateuch, invariant later law, universal calendar/procedure, Last Supper date, Johannine sequence, or Eucharistic theology.
  **不能支持**：纸草没有叙述摩西、十灾、羔羊血、过海或出埃及事件，不能成为历史出埃及的同期外证；它也不是《出埃及记》抄本，不能证明当时象岛社群已经拥有完整五经、使用与后来马所拉本逐字相同的律法，或所有犹太群体都按同一日历和程序守节。主前五世纪文书更不能直接证明耶稣最后晚餐的具体日期、第四福音的节期次序或圣餐神学。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** Traditional restorations of Nisan fourteen/`פסחא` compare Pentateuch; newer readings reconsider traces/calendar/purpose. Keep A visible object/date/instructions, B local practice, C restoration/Passover label/calendar/Jerusalem-Pentateuch relation, D Exodus/Moses/Last Supper.
  **争议与解读边界**：传统复原把尼散月十四日和 `פסחא` 读作逾越节，又把七日无酵与五经节期法规比较；较新解释则重新讨论残字、波斯历法行政和命令功能。项目固定区分三层：A 级为器物、馆藏、可见亚兰文及明确日期／无酵／停工内容；B 级为波斯时期象岛犹太群体的节期—历法实践；C 级为缺行复原、是否明确提“逾越节”、具体日历改革及与耶路撒冷／五经文本的关系；D 级为出埃及事件、摩西作者或新约最后晚餐的直接证明。
- **Grade:** A for excavated object/collection/language/correspondents/regnal year/visible instructions; B for diaspora festival practice; C for title/restoration/exact days/purpose/Pentateuch/uniformity; D for events/full Pentateuch/Moses/Gospel dates/Eucharist.
  **等级**：A（正式发掘的纸草、馆藏、语言、通信双方、王年及可见节期指令）；B（波斯时期散居犹太驻军以亚兰文协调无酵节／逾越相关实践）；C（题名、关键复原、精确公历日、命令目的、五经接触与跨地区统一程度）；D（出埃及事件、完整五经、摩西作者、福音书日期或圣餐神学证明）。
- **Sources:** [Berlin Elephantine: P. 13464 object, excavation, transcription, translation, date, debate](https://elephantine.smb.museum/objects/object.php?o=100463); [Berlin museums' Elephantine exhibition: P. 13464, 419–418 BCE, excavation](https://www.smb.museum/fileadmin/website/Presse/Pressematerial/2024/04/03_AEMP_240424_Elephantine_Exponate.pdf); [Idan Dershowitz: postponed unleavened-bread festival/calendar model](https://www.thetorah.com/article/darius-ii-delays-the-festival-of-matzot-in-418-bce); [Tamara Cohn Eskenazi: soldiers' religious furlough/response](https://www.thetorah.com/article/the-passover-papyrus-orders-a-religious-furlough-for-judean-soldiers)
  **来源**：[柏林 Elephantine 项目：Pap. Berlin P. 13464 器物、出土、转录、译文、日期与研究争议](https://elephantine.smb.museum/objects/object.php?o=100463)；[柏林国家博物馆 Elephantine 展览器物表：P. 13464、419–418 BCE 与正式发掘资料](https://www.smb.museum/fileadmin/website/Presse/Pressematerial/2024/04/03_AEMP_240424_Elephantine_Exponate.pdf)；[Idan Dershowitz：无酵节延后／历法解释](https://www.thetorah.com/article/darius-ii-delays-the-festival-of-matzot-in-418-bce)；[Tamara Cohn Eskenazi：驻军宗教休假解释及对前说的回应](https://www.thetorah.com/article/the-passover-papyrus-orders-a-religious-furlough-for-judean-soldiers)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-188"></a>

### 188. P104 / P.Oxy. LXIV 4404: Matthew 21 Fragment | P104／P.Oxy. LXIV 4404：《马太福音》21 章纸草残片

- **Date / discovery and collection:** Oxyrhynchus publication, Oxford Sackler Library, CSNTM second-century paleographic classification, not dated signature.
  **年代／出土与馆藏**：P104 由俄克喜林库斯出土纸草出版计划正式编号为 P.Oxy. LXIV 4404，现藏牛津大学 Sackler Library。CSNTM 目录按古文字学归入二世纪；这是书体比较所得的宽断代，不是纸草自署年月。
- **Material type:** Small Greek single-column codex piece, originally about 31 lines; Matthew 21:34–37, faint reverse traces/restorations associated with 21:43/45. Tenants-parable text, not full page/Gospel.
  **材料性质**：这是一小片希腊文纸草 codex 页，原页单栏、约 31 行；一面保存太 21:34–37，另一面有与 21:43、45 相联系的淡痕和复原。它直接接触葡萄园园户比喻的文字，却不足以重建整页，更不是一卷完整《马太福音》。
- **Related Scripture:** Matthew 21:33–46; Jerusalem/temple conflict/tenants; week 39.
  **对应经文**：太 21:33–46；上耶路撒冷、圣殿冲突与园户比喻；第 39 周。
- **Supports:** Under current dating, Greek Matthew 21 copied in an Egyptian codex by second century, earlier than complete fourth-century witnesses.
  **能支持**：按现行目录断代，最迟到二世纪，《马太福音》21 章的希腊文已经以 codex 页形式在埃及抄传；可辨文字使本卷的文本时钟早于四世纪完整大抄本。
- **Does not establish:** No author/initial year/verbatim speech day/conflict process/theology, autograph, or automatic original readings.
  **不能支持**：P104 不能证明马太作者、福音书初写日期、耶稣确曾在某日讲出逐字相同的比喻、圣殿冲突的具体过程或经文的神学结论；残片较早也不等于它就是作者原稿或每个读法都最原始。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** Flexible paleographic interval; reverse faint letters/layout/restorations less secure than clear front. Catalogue second-century fragment, not fully visible reconstructed page.
  **争议与解读边界**：二世纪断代依靠书体比较，应保留年代弹性；背面淡痕的字母识别、行数和缺文复原也低于正面清楚文字。引用时宜说“目录断为二世纪的残片”，不把可能的页版复原写成肉眼完整可见。
- **Grade:** A for object/collection/material/Greek/visible text; B for second-century copying; C for exact date/layout/reverse restoration; D for author/date/Jesus events/theology.
  **等级**：A（器物、馆藏、纸草 codex、希腊文及太 21 可辨文字）；B（二世纪《马太福音》21 章在埃及抄传）；C（窄断年、原页版面和背面淡痕／缺文复原）；D（作者、初写日期、耶稣言行事件或神学宣告证明）。
- **Sources:** [CSNTM: P104 / P.Oxy. LXIV 4404, date/collection/material/images](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/View/GA_P104); [Oxford Sustainable Digital Scholarship: P.Oxy. LXIV 4404 bibliography/passages](https://portal.sds.ox.ac.uk/articles/online_resource/P_Oxy_LXIV_4404_Matthew_XXI_34-37_43_and_45_/21178702)
  **来源**：[CSNTM：P104／P.Oxy. LXIV 4404 的馆藏、年代、材质与数字图像](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/View/GA_P104)；[牛津大学 Sustainable Digital Scholarship：P.Oxy. LXIV 4404 书目与经文范围](https://portal.sds.ox.ac.uk/articles/online_resource/P_Oxy_LXIV_4404_Matthew_XXI_34-37_43_and_45_/21178702)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-189"></a>

### 189. P137 / P.Oxy. LXXXIII 5345: Mark 1 and First-Century Rumors | P137／P.Oxy. LXXXIII 5345：《马可福音》1 章与“一世纪马可”传闻

- **Date / discovery and collection:** EES Oxyrhynchus collection; formal edition late-second/early-third-century hand. Earlier first/second-century inventory estimate predates identification as Mark.
  **年代／出土与馆藏**：P137 是俄克喜林库斯纸草 P.Oxy. LXXXIII 5345，现由 Egypt Exploration Society 的 Oxyrhynchus Papyri 收藏管理。正式出版把书手归于二世纪末至三世纪初；早期库存曾给出一／二世纪的暂定范围，但那是在残片被识别为《马可福音》以前的初步目录判断。
- **Material type:** Greek codex corner, Mark 1:7–9/16–18: Baptist/baptism/call, not whole narrative.
  **材料性质**：这是一片希腊文纸草 codex，正反两面保存可 1:7–9、16–18 的部分文字，涉及施洗约翰宣告、耶稣受洗和呼召门徒。残片只是一页的一角，不能恢复完整叙事。
- **Related Scripture:** Mark 1:7–9, 16–18; baptism/temptation/call setting; week 36.
  **对应经文**：可 1:7–9、16–18；受洗、试探与门徒蒙召；第 36 周。
- **Supports:** Direct late-second/early-third-century Greek witness, stopping popular first-century-Mark publicity unsupported by formal paleography.
  **能支持**：二世纪末至三世纪初，《马可福音》1 章已经有直接希腊文纸草见证。正式出版也为广传的“发现第一世纪《马可福音》纸草”说法设置清楚停止线：现有书体比较不支持该宣传。
- **Does not establish:** Not first-century copy, author/initial year, baptismal voice/Spirit/call event, or invariant whole text.
  **不能支持**：P137 不能称为第一世纪抄本，也不能证明马可作者、初写年份、耶稣受洗时的天声／圣灵、门徒蒙召事件或福音书全部文字从未改变。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** No exact year; preliminary unidentified inventory/secrecy rumors do not outweigh edition. Rejecting first-century dating does not imply no earlier Mark existed.
  **争议与解读边界**：古文字学无法把它钉在单一年份；“二世纪末至三世纪初”仍是比较范围。早期保密传闻和未识别库存断代不能凌驾于正式出版，也不应因否定“一世纪”就反向声称本卷此前不存在。
- **Grade:** A for object/collection/form/language/text; B for Egyptian copying; C for narrower date/layout/gaps; D for first-century copy/author/date/events/theology.
  **等级**：A（器物、馆藏、纸草 codex、希腊文及可 1 可辨文字）；B（二世纪末至三世纪初《马可福音》在埃及抄传）；C（更窄断年、原页版面和缺文复原）；D（第一世纪抄本、作者、初写日期、受洗／呼召事件或神学宣告证明）。
- **Sources:** [Egypt Exploration Society: P.Oxy. LXXXIII 5345, late-second/early-third date](https://www.ees.ac.uk/resource/p-oxy-lxxxiii-5345.html); [EES statement: first-century Mark claim and provenance](https://www.ees.ac.uk/static/87896159-4184-47b8-884a38ad012986e9/18-06-04-EES-Statement-Mark5345.pdf); [P.Oxy. LXXXIII 5345 published text/images](https://www.ees.ac.uk/static/af98dfae-bd9d-4490-8a9971f05a78d610/POxy-LXXXIII-5345-text-and-image.pdf)
  **来源**：[Egypt Exploration Society：P.Oxy. LXXXIII 5345 正式说明与二世纪末至三世纪初断代](https://www.ees.ac.uk/resource/p-oxy-lxxxiii-5345.html)；[EES 正式声明：所谓“第一世纪马可”与馆藏来源边界](https://www.ees.ac.uk/static/87896159-4184-47b8-884a38ad012986e9/18-06-04-EES-Statement-Mark5345.pdf)；[P.Oxy. LXXXIII 5345 正式文本与图像](https://www.ees.ac.uk/static/af98dfae-bd9d-4490-8a9971f05a78d610/POxy-LXXXIII-5345-text-and-image.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

### 190. P4 and P75: Third-Century Luke from Opening to Resurrection | P4 与 P75：《路加福音》从开场到受难复活叙事的三世纪纸草

- **Date / discovery and collection:** P4 BnF Suppl. Gr.1120, six fragments; P75 Vatican Pap. Hanna.1, about fifty leaves. CSNTM paleographically third century.
  **年代／出土与馆藏**：P4 现藏法国国家图书馆，馆藏号 Suppl. Gr. 1120；P75 现藏梵蒂冈宗座图书馆，馆藏号 Pap. Hanna. 1。CSNTM 均按古文字学列为三世纪：P4 由六块纸草残片组成，P75 约存 50 叶。
- **Material type:** Greek codices: P4 portions of Luke 1–6; P75 Luke 3–24/John 1–12, together from birth/genealogy/Galilee through Jerusalem/passion/resurrection. P75 joins Luke/John in one codex.
  **材料性质**：两者都是希腊文纸草 codex。P4 保存路 1–6 的多处文字；P75 保存路 3–24 及约 1–12 的大量段落。二者合看可直接追踪《路加福音》从降生／家谱／加利利事工到耶路撒冷、受难与复活叙事的早期抄传；P75 又把路加与约翰置于同一现存 codex。
- **Related Scripture:** Luke 1–24; John 1–12 in P75; weeks 35–41.
  **对应经文**：路 1–24；约 1–12 作为 P75 同册材料；四福音开场至复活叙事；第 35–41 周。
- **Supports:** Broad third-century Luke coverage and a physical Luke–John codex, beyond reliance on complete fourth-century copies/later lists.
  **能支持**：三世纪已有多处、跨全卷范围的《路加福音》希腊文实物；P75 还直接证明至少这一册把《路加福音》与《约翰福音》连续保存。它使本卷文本时钟不必只依赖四世纪完整大抄本或更晚书目。
- **Does not establish:** No Luke authorship/shared Acts author/initial dates/events/miracles/resurrection or universal Gospel order. Adjacency is insufficient for dependence/final canon.
  **不能支持**：P4／P75 不能证明路加作者、《路加福音》与《使徒行传》的同一作者、两卷初写日期、耶稣言行／神迹／复活事件，或所有早期福音 codex 都采用相同次序；同册相邻也不是文学依赖或正典最终定型的充分条件。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** Paleographic dates, P4 page reconstruction/P75 missing leaves distinguished from surviving text. This codex's pairing A; universal order/formation extrapolation C.
  **争议与解读边界**：两件的三世纪归属都是书体断代；P4 各碎片的页版复原和 P75 原册缺叶须与现存文字区分。P75 的路加—约翰组合是该件 codex 的 A 级事实，由此外推整个地区、全部福音次序或形成过程则降为 C。
- **Grade:** A for objects/collections/material/language/text/pairing; B for broad third-century copying; C for date/layout/universality; D for author/date/Luke–Acts relationship/events.
  **等级**：A（两件器物、馆藏、纸草 codex、希腊文及可辨经文；P75 的路加—约翰同册）；B（三世纪《路加福音》广泛篇幅抄传）；C（窄断年、缺页／版面复原及同册安排的普遍性）；D（作者、初写日期、路加—使徒行传作者关系或卷内事件证明）。
- **Sources:** [CSNTM: P4 / BnF Suppl. Gr. 1120, date/collection/material/passages](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/Group/GA_P4); [CSNTM: P75 / Pap. Hanna. 1, date/collection/fifty leaves/Luke–John](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/View/GA_P75?OSIS=John.3.16)
  **来源**：[CSNTM：P4／BnF Suppl. Gr. 1120 的年代、馆藏、材质与经文范围](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/Group/GA_P4)；[CSNTM：P75／Pap. Hanna. 1 的年代、馆藏、50 叶及路加—约翰内容](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/View/GA_P75?OSIS=John.3.16)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-191"></a>

### 191. P52/P66/P75: Early John Papyri and P52's Dating Limits | P52、P66 与 P75：《约翰福音》早期纸草链及 P52 断代边界

- **Date / discovery and collection:** P52 Rylands Gr.P.457, CSNTM second century; P66 Fondation Martin Bodmer P.Bodmer II, late-second/early-third; P75 Vatican Hanna.1, third. Primarily paleographic dates.
  **年代／出土与馆藏**：P52 现藏曼彻斯特 John Rylands Library，馆藏号 Gr. P. 457，CSNTM 目录列为二世纪；P66 现藏 Fondation Martin Bodmer，馆藏号 P.Bodmer II，通常断为二世纪末至三世纪初；P75 现藏梵蒂冈宗座图书馆 Pap. Hanna. 1，断为三世纪。三者年代都主要来自古文字比较。
- **Material type:** Tiny P52 John 18 recto/verso; P66 about 75 identifiable leaves/fragments across John; P75 about fifty leaves, John 1–12 after Luke 3–24. Small fragment, extensive text, paired codex are distinct layers.
  **材料性质**：P52 是一片很小的希腊文纸草 codex，正反两面保存约 18 章部分文字；P66 约有 75 片可辨叶及碎片，广泛保存《约翰福音》；P75 约存 50 叶，保存约 1–12，并在同册前半保存路 3–24。它们构成“极小残片—大部经文—与另一福音同册”的多层文本实物链。
- **Related Scripture:** John 1–21, especially 18; incarnation/signs/passion/resurrection; weeks 35–36, 38–41.
  **对应经文**：约 1–21，尤其约 18；道成肉身、记号、受难与复活叙事；第 35–36、38–41 周。
- **Supports:** Multiple Greek witnesses around late-second/third century, P66/P75 far broader than P52. Each dates attested circulation, avoiding reliance on one tiny fragment.
  **能支持**：到二世纪末／三世纪前后，《约翰福音》已有不止一件希腊文纸草，且 P66、P75 保存范围远大于 P52。P52 至少表明约 18 的文字在其抄本年代已经流传；P66、P75 则使判断不必压在一片极小纸草上。
- **Does not establish:** No certain 125 CE P52, precise composition, apostle John, pre-Egypt route, Pilate trial/theology, or autographs.
  **不能支持**：P52 不能固定称为“主后 125 年的确定抄本”，更不能由其年代倒推出《约翰福音》的精确初写年、使徒约翰作者、在埃及以前的传播路线、彼拉多审讯事件或第四福音神学宣告；三件也不是作者原稿。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** New script comparisons span second/third century, exceeding old precise publicity. Retain catalogue second-century label with open third-century possibility; earliest surviving/classified does not mean no prior copying.
  **争议与解读边界**：P52 曾被宣传为约主后 125 年，较新的古文字学复核指出可比书手跨二至三世纪，因而精确度不应超过材料允许。项目保留目录的“二世纪”标签，同时在解释中公开三世纪可能性；“最早”只能指当前幸存与目录分类，不能表示此前没有抄传。
- **Grade:** A for objects/collections/material/language/passages; B for multiple early copies; C for exact dates/routes/codex/reading priority; D for author/composition/events/theology.
  **等级**：A（器物、馆藏、纸草 codex、希腊文及各自可辨范围）；B（二世纪末／三世纪前后《约翰福音》的多件抄传见证）；C（P52 及其余抄本的窄断年、传播路线、原册复原与读法先后）；D（作者、精确初写年、审讯／复活事件或神学宣告证明）。
- **Sources:** [CSNTM: P52 / Rylands Gr. P. 457, material/date/John 18](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/View/GA_P52); [Brent Nongbri, New Testament Studies: P52 dating precision/second–third-century parallels](https://www.cambridge.org/core/services/aop-cambridge-core/content/view/1D4E56BF0E9D4DDDA313D0C2754E2F28/S0028688520000089a.pdf/palaeography_precision_and_publicity_further_thoughts_on_pryliii457_p52.pdf); [CSNTM: P66 / Bodmer II, late-second/early-third date and broad John text](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/View/GA_P66_Bodmer); [CSNTM: P75 / Hanna 1, third-century Luke–John codex](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/View/GA_P75?OSIS=John.3.16)
  **来源**：[CSNTM：P52／Rylands Gr. P. 457 的馆藏、材质、目录年代与约 18 内容](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/View/GA_P52)；[Brent Nongbri，*New Testament Studies*：P52 古文字断代的精度与二至三世纪比较范围](https://www.cambridge.org/core/services/aop-cambridge-core/content/view/1D4E56BF0E9D4DDDA313D0C2754E2F28/S0028688520000089a.pdf/palaeography_precision_and_publicity_further_thoughts_on_pryliii457_p52.pdf)；[CSNTM：P66／P.Bodmer II 的二世纪末至三世纪初断代及大范围《约翰福音》内容](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/View/GA_P66_Bodmer)；[CSNTM：P75／Pap. Hanna. 1 的三世纪路加—约翰 codex](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/View/GA_P75?OSIS=John.3.16)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-192"></a>

### 192. P29 and P45: Third-Century Acts and a Four-Gospel–Acts Codex | P29 与 P45：《使徒行传》的三世纪纸草及四福音—使徒行传 codex

- **Date / discovery and collection:** P29/P.Oxy.XIII1597, Bodleian Gr.bib.g.4(P); P45 Chester Beatty CBL BP I and Vienna Pap.G.31974. CSNTM third-century paleography.
  **年代／出土与馆藏**：P29 是 P.Oxy. XIII 1597，现藏牛津 Bodleian Library，馆藏号 Gr. bib. g. 4 (P)；P45 分藏都柏林 Chester Beatty Library（CBL BP I）和维也纳 Austrian National Library（Pap. G. 31974）。CSNTM 均按古文字学列为三世纪。
- **Material type:** Tiny P29 Acts 26:7–8/20; P45 about thirty single-column Greek papyrus leaves, all four Gospels/Acts, Acts portions across 4–17.
  **材料性质**：P29 是一片很小的希腊文纸草 codex，保存徒 26:7–8、20 的部分文字。P45 约存 30 叶，原为单栏希腊文纸草 codex，现存《马太福音》《马可福音》《路加福音》《约翰福音》和《使徒行传》多处残文；《使徒行传》可辨范围跨徒 4–17。
- **Related Scripture:** Gospel fragments; Acts 4–17/26; Jerusalem/mission/Paul's defense; weeks 37–40, 42–45, 48.
  **对应经文**：四福音片段；徒 4–17、26；耶路撒冷群体、宣教旅程与保罗申辩；第 37–40、42–45、48 周。
- **Supports:** Two third-century direct Acts witnesses before seventh-century P74; P45 physically joins Gospels/Acts, P29 separately preserves chapter 26.
  **能支持**：三世纪已有两件直接《使徒行传》希腊文纸草，时间早于七世纪 P74；P45 还直接证明至少一册曾把四福音与《使徒行传》装入同一 codex，P29 则独立保存徒 26 的微小片段。两件共同补出本卷早期文本时钟。
- **Does not establish:** No author/Luke–Acts link/date/speeches/miracles/journeys/defense proof or full original contents/order/ending from surviving leaves.
  **不能支持**：P29／P45 不能证明路加作者、《路加福音》与《使徒行传》的作者关系、写作日期、使徒讲章、神迹、旅程细节或保罗申辩事件；P45 残缺，也不能凭现存叶断定原册所有卷、完整次序和卷尾形态。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** P29 too small for whole-book textual typing; P45 missing many leaves. Extant five works are fact, further contents/binding order remain reconstructed. P74 is broader/later, not earliest Acts papyrus.
  **争议与解读边界**：两件断代依赖书体比较。P29 过小，不适合单独建立稳定“文本类型”或整卷读法；P45 缺失大量页叶，现存“五卷”是实物事实，原册是否还含其他作品及精确装订次序仍须保留。P74 是更晚但范围更广的见证，不应再误称为最早《使徒行传》纸草。
- **Grade:** A for objects/collections/material/language/text/combination; B for copying/multi-book codex; C for dates/P29 type/P45 extent-order/reconstruction; D for author/date/people/events/theology.
  **等级**：A（两件器物、馆藏、纸草 codex、希腊文及可辨经文；P45 的四福音—使徒行传组合）；B（三世纪《使徒行传》抄传和一册多卷福音—使徒行传形式）；C（窄断年、P29 文本归类、P45 原册范围／次序和缺页复原）；D（作者、初写日期、卷内人物／事件或神学宣告证明）。
- **Sources:** [CSNTM: P29 / P.Oxy. XIII 1597, date/collection/material/images](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/View/GA_P29); [Bodleian Libraries: P29 / Gr. bib. g. 4 (P), digital record](https://digital.bodleian.ox.ac.uk/objects/c44d9cdc-e710-4b7f-a52e-05e2fa8175d2/); [CSNTM: P45, third century, collections, thirty leaves, Gospels–Acts](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/Manuscript/Group/GA_P45); [Chester Beatty: Frederic Kenyon's P45 edition and leaf descriptions](https://chesterbeatty.ie/assets/uploads/2018/11/Biblical-Papyri-Descriptions-and-Texts-of-Twelve-Manuscripts-on-Papyrus-of-The-Greek-Bible-II-text-Opt.pdf)
  **来源**：[CSNTM：P29／P.Oxy. XIII 1597 的馆藏、年代、材质与数字图像](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/manuscript/View/GA_P29)；[Bodleian Libraries：P29／Gr. bib. g. 4 (P) 数字藏品](https://digital.bodleian.ox.ac.uk/objects/c44d9cdc-e710-4b7f-a52e-05e2fa8175d2/)；[CSNTM：P45 的三世纪断代、分藏地点、30 叶及四福音—使徒行传内容](https://manuscripts.csntm.org/Manuscript/Group/GA_P45)；[Chester Beatty：Frederic Kenyon 对 P45 的原始出版与逐叶说明](https://chesterbeatty.ie/assets/uploads/2018/11/Biblical-Papyri-Descriptions-and-Texts-of-Twelve-Manuscripts-on-Papyrus-of-The-Greek-Bible-II-text-Opt.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-193"></a>

### 193. 4Q1/4Q7 Genesis: Hasmonean Hebrew Fragments | 4Q1／4Q7《创世记》：哈斯摩尼时期的希伯来文创世残卷

- **Date / discovery and carrier:** Cave 4 square-Hebrew leather, IAA Hasmonean, 4Q7 about 100–1 BCE.
  **年代／出土与载体**：4Q1（4QGen-Exodᵃ）与 4Q7（4QGenᵍ）都出自昆兰第 4 洞，是方体希伯来字母写成的皮质经文抄本；以色列文物局宽归哈斯摩尼时期，并把 4Q7 列为约主前 100–1 年。
- **Material type:** 4Q7 Genesis 1–2 including first three creation days; 4Q1 editorially groups Genesis 8:20–21 with Exodus fragments as Genesis–Exodus. Ordinary scripture fragments, not commentary/rewriting/tablet parallels.
  **材料性质**：4Q7 的两组残片保存创 1–2 章、包括创造前三日的文字；4Q1 的正式归类则把创 8:20–21 与《出埃及记》多段残片归入同一件“创世记—出埃及记”抄本。两件都是真正承载经文的卷轴残片，不是创世释经、改写作品或后期泥版平行。
- **Related Scripture:** Genesis 1–2, 8; whole-book physical-text clock; weeks 1–5.
  **对应经文**：创 1–2、8 章；《创世记》全卷的文本实物时钟；第 1–5 周。
- **Supports:** Late Second Temple Hebrew creation/post-flood sacrifice text and a proposed cross-book Pentateuch roll, adding direct MS beyond comparative ancient Near Eastern background.
  **能支持**：到第二圣殿后期，《创世记》的创造与洪水后献祭文字已有希伯来文皮质实物；4Q1 的编辑归类还提供一件跨《创世记》《出埃及记》的五经卷轴案例。它把本项目原先只有古代近东比较背景的《创世记》补上直接 MS 层。
- **Does not establish:** No creation/Eden/flood/Babel/patriarch/Joseph events, Moses, initial/final date, identical fifty chapters, or full survival of every 4Q1 chapter.
  **不能支持**：残片不能考古证明创造、伊甸、洪水、巴别、列祖或约瑟事件，也不能证明摩西作者、全卷初写／定稿日期、所有 50 章当时逐字一致，或 4Q1 原卷的每一章都实际保存下来。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** Hasmonean is broad script dating; common-roll attribution/length/cross-book form need material reconstruction. Tiny 4Q7's early status is survival, not proximity to creation/autograph.
  **争议与解读边界**：哈斯摩尼时期是古文字学宽断代，不是抄写日；4Q1 的残片同属一卷、原卷长度及卷内跨书安排依赖物质和书体重建。4Q7 只存极少文字，“最早之一”描述幸存材料，不等于离创世事件最近或等于作者原稿。
- **Grade:** A for fragments/cave/material/language/script/text; B for copying/cross-book attribution; C for date/extent/joins/unsurviving passages; D for events/Moses/composition/theology.
  **等级**：A（两件残片、洞穴、材质、语言、字形、经文身份及可辨文字）；B（第二圣殿后期《创世记》希伯来文抄传及 4Q1 的跨卷归类）；C（窄断年、4Q1 原卷范围／残片组合和未存章节复原）；D（卷内事件、摩西作者、初写／定稿年或神学宣告证明）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: 4Q1 / Genesis–Exodus, metadata/DJD XII](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q1-1?locale=en_US); [Israel Antiquities Authority: 4Q7 / Genesis metadata](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q7-1?locale=en_US); [Israel Antiquities Authority: 4Q7 date/language/first three creation days](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/featured-scrolls)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局：4Q1／4QGen-Exodᵃ 的馆藏元数据与 DJD XII 出版信息](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q1-1?locale=en_US)；[以色列文物局：4Q7／4QGenᵍ 的馆藏元数据](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q7-1?locale=en_US)；[以色列文物局精选古卷：4Q7 年代、语言与创造前三日内容](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/featured-scrolls)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-194"></a>

### 194. 4Q22 / Paleo-Exodus: Chapters 6–37 in Paleo-Hebrew | 4Q22／4QpaleoExodᵐ：《出埃及记》6–37 章的古希伯来字母卷轴

- **Date / discovery and carrier:** Cave 4 Hebrew leather in paleo-Hebrew script, IAA Hasmonean; published skin radiocarbon around 100–25 BCE. Copying also needs script/material comparison.
  **年代／出土与载体**：4Q22 出自昆兰第 4 洞，是用古希伯来字母写在皮质材料上的希伯来文经卷；以色列文物局宽归哈斯摩尼时期，已发表碳十四结果给出约主前 100–25 年范围。科学测年针对皮革，抄写时间仍须与书体和物质重建合看。
- **Material type:** Many fragments reconstructed as about 44 columns, extant/locatable Exodus 6:25–37:16, plagues/Passover/sea/Sinai/covenant/calf/tabernacle. Additions, reordered/harmonized readings: pre-Samaritan or expanded Pentateuch form.
  **材料性质**：正式出版把大量残片复原为约 44 栏，现存或可定位文字从出 6:25 延伸到 37:16，跨十灾、逾越、过海、西奈、约书、金牛犊和会幕段落。该卷有多处增补、调序与协调读法，常被称为“前撒玛利亚型”或更中性地称为扩展型五经文本。
- **Related Scripture:** Exodus 6–37; whole-book physical-text clock; weeks 6–7.
  **对应经文**：出 6–37 章；《出埃及记》全卷的文本实物时钟；第 6–7 周。
- **Supports:** Extensive first-century-BCE-era narrative/law, overlapping yet differing from Masoretic/Samaritan/versions. Neither universal identity nor arbitrary corruption from any variant.
  **能支持**：到主前一世纪前后，《出埃及记》大量叙事和律法已经有一件古希伯来字母经卷实物；其与后来的马所拉本、撒玛利亚五经和古译本之间既有高度重合也有可观察差异，证明第二圣殿文本史不能简化成“每份抄本完全相同”或“发现异文就是任意篡改”。
- **Does not establish:** Not Moses/contemporary archive or proof of pharaoh/plagues/sea/route/Sinai/tabernacle/divine law; surviving endpoints do not mean every verse survives.
  **不能支持**：4Q22 不是摩西亲笔或出埃及同期档案，不能证明法老身份、十灾、过海、路线、西奈显现、会幕建造或律法神授，也不能由现存始末宣称整卷每节都保存。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** Pre-Samaritan describes shared expansion/harmonization, not later Samaritan-community origin or one cause for every variant. Skin dates carrier, not composition; distinguish actual letters, assigned columns, modern restoration.
  **争议与解读边界**：“前撒玛利亚型”描述与撒玛利亚五经共享的扩展／协调特征，不等于卷轴由后来撒玛利亚社群制作，也不代表每个差异都同一来源。碳十四测的是载体而非作品形成；栏位、缺文和未存经句须区分实见文字与现代复原。
- **Grade:** A for object/cave/material/language/script/passages/radiocarbon; B for extensive expanded transmission; C for exact copying/length/restoration/model; D for events/Moses/composition/revelation.
  **等级**：A（残卷、洞穴、材质、语言、古希伯来字母、可辨经文及碳十四范围）；B（第二圣殿后期的大范围《出埃及记》抄传与扩展型文本传统）；C（窄抄写年、原卷总长、逐处复原及“前撒玛利亚型”的形成模型）；D（卷内事件、摩西作者、初写／定稿年或启示证明）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: 4Q22 / paleo-Exodus, metadata/DJD IX](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q22-1?locale=en_US); [Bonani et al., Radiocarbon 37/1: 4Q22 radiocarbon](https://citeseerx.ist.psu.edu/document?doi=e37e8081602a084267006e2f81e743cedd95d700&repid=rep1&type=pdf); [Emanuel Tov: 4Q22 expanded/pre-Samaritan form](https://www.emanueltov.info/docs/books/biqoret-hanusah.2hebrew-book2.pdf); [Hila Dayfani: 4Q22 and Samaritan Pentateuch textual history](https://academic.oup.com/edited-volume/61539/chapter/537125938)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局：4Q22／4QpaleoExodᵐ 的馆藏元数据与 DJD IX 出版信息](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q22-1?locale=en_US)；[Bonani 等：4Q22 的碳十四测年，*Radiocarbon* 37/1](https://citeseerx.ist.psu.edu/document?doi=e37e8081602a084267006e2f81e743cedd95d700&repid=rep1&type=pdf)；[Emanuel Tov：4Q22 的扩展型／前撒玛利亚文本说明](https://www.emanueltov.info/docs/books/biqoret-hanusah.2hebrew-book2.pdf)；[Hila Dayfani：4Q22 与撒玛利亚五经文本史的新复核](https://academic.oup.com/edited-volume/61539/chapter/537125938)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-195"></a>

### 195. 4Q27 / 4QNumᵇ: Extensive Hebrew Fragments of Numbers 11–36 | 4Q27／4QNumᵇ：《民数记》11–36 章的大范围希伯来文残卷

- **Date / discovery and carrier:** Cave 4 square-Hebrew biblical leather scroll; IAA broadly Herodian, DJD XII 205–267. Hundreds of photographs do not equal original fragments, columns, or completeness.
  **年代／出土与载体**：4Q27 出自昆兰第 4 洞，是方体希伯来字母写在皮质材料上的经文抄本；以色列文物局宽归希律时期，正式出版为 DJD XII 205–267 页。馆藏现以数百幅照片记录大量残片，照片数不能直接等同原始碎片、栏数或完整度。
- **Material type:** Many passages across Numbers 11:31–36:7: scouts/wilderness crisis, Korah, red heifer, itinerary, Balaam, census, offerings, vows, war, land laws; some red ink. Reordering, harmonization, variants relate to expanded Pentateuch, Samaritan, or Old Greek traditions.
  **材料性质**：可辨经文跨民 11:31–36:7 的许多段落，包括探子与旷野危机、可拉事件、红母牛条例、行程、巴兰神谕、人口清单、节期献祭、许愿、战争与分地法规；若干行使用红色墨水。卷中又有与扩展型五经、撒玛利亚五经或古希腊传统相关的调序、协调和异文。
- **Related Scripture:** Numbers 11–36, especially 13–14, 16–24, 26–36; the book's material-text clock; week 9.
  **对应经文**：民 11–36 章，尤其 13–14、16–24、26–36 章；《民数记》全卷的文本实物时钟；第 9 周。
- **Supports:** Direct Hebrew scroll of much of later Numbers by late Second Temple period; supplements Ketef Hinnom, Deir Alla, 4Q175 with MS evidence and comparable ancient readings.
  **能支持**：到第二圣殿后期，《民数记》后半大范围文字已有直接希伯来文经卷实物；它把基特夫欣嫩祝福片、德尔阿拉巴兰铭文和 4Q175 选集之外的 MS 层补齐，也显示本卷文本在古代抄传中已有可比较读法。
- **Does not establish:** No proof of forty-year itinerary, population, Korah's judgment, bronze serpent, Balaam, war/land events; not complete Numbers, Moses' autograph, or final-edition certificate.
  **不能支持**：4Q27 不能证明四十年旷野行程、人口数字、可拉审判、铜蛇、巴兰事件、争战或分地发生，也不能把后半残卷写成一份完整《民数记》、摩西亲笔或全卷定稿日期证书。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** Herodian is a paleographic range; red ink, joins, columns, longer/shorter or reordered readings need individual arguments. A shared Samaritan/Greek reading does not make the whole scroll one fixed text type or identify the earliest reading.
  **争议与解读边界**：希律时期仍是古文字学范围；红墨功能、残片位置、栏位复原和某些长短／调序读法须逐处论证。它与撒玛利亚五经或希腊传统共享某读法，不等于整卷属于单一固定“文本类型”，也不自动判定哪一读法最早。
- **Grade:** A for scroll/cave/material/language/script/identity/visible scope; B for extensive later-Numbers copying/textual diversity; C for narrow dating/red ink/reconstruction/genealogy; D for wilderness events/numbers/Moses/composition/finalization/theology.
  **等级**：A（残卷、洞穴、材质、语言、字形、经文身份和可辨范围）；B（第二圣殿后期《民数记》大范围希伯来抄传及古代文本多样性）；C（窄断年、红墨功能、栏位／缺文复原和文本谱系）；D（旷野人物事件、人口数字、摩西作者、初写／定稿年或神学宣告证明）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: 4Q27 / 4QNumᵇ metadata, images, DJD XII](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q27-1?locale=en_US); [Emanuel Tov: 4Q27 and expanded Pentateuchal texts](https://www.emanueltov.info/docs/books/biqoret-hanusah.2hebrew-book2.pdf)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局：4Q27／4QNumᵇ 的馆藏元数据、图像与 DJD XII 出版信息](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q27-1?locale=en_US)；[Emanuel Tov：4Q27 与扩展型五经文本的比较](https://www.emanueltov.info/docs/books/biqoret-hanusah.2hebrew-book2.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-196"></a>

### 196. 8ḤevXIIgr and Mur88: Ordinary Twelve Scrolls Witnessing All Three Habakkuk Chapters | 8ḤevXIIgr 与 Mur88：《哈巴谷书》三章的普通《十二先知》经卷见证

- **Date / discovery and carrier:** 8ḤevXIIgr / Rahlfs 943, Cave of Horror at Nahal Hever, Greek leather Twelve scroll, IAA broadly Herodian 50–1 BCE. Mur88 / MurXII, Wadi Murabbaʿat, Roman-period Hebrew leather Twelve. Ordinary Scripture, not verse-by-verse pesher.
  **年代／出土与载体**：8ḤevXIIgr（Rahlfs 943）来自拿哈·赫维尔“恐怖洞”，是希腊文皮质《十二先知》卷；以色列文物局把它宽列主前 50–1 年的希律时期。Mur88／MurXII 来自 Wadi Murabbaʿat，是罗马时期希伯来文皮质《十二先知》卷。两件都是普通经文卷，不是逐节附解释的 pesher。
- **Material type:** Greek Habakkuk 1:5–11, 1:14–2:8, 2:13–20, 3:8–15, revised toward contemporary Hebrew and writing divine name in paleo-Hebrew; Mur88 has Hebrew passages of chapters 1–3. Together supplement 1QpHab's quotation/exegesis of only 1–2.
  **材料性质**：8ḤevXIIgr 保存哈 1:5–11、1:14–2:8、2:13–20 及 3:8–15，希腊文多处向当时希伯来文本校订，并在希腊正文中以古希伯来字母写神名；Mur88 则保存哈 1–3 章多段希伯来文。两件共同补上 1QpHab 只抄引／解释 1–2 章的局限。
- **Related Scripture:** Habakkuk 1–3, especially 2:4/20 and chapter 3 prayer; later Romans 1:17, Galatians 3:11, Hebrews 10:37–38; weeks 23, 44, 47, 50.
  **对应经文**：哈 1–3 章，尤其 2:4、2:20 与第 3 章诗祷；罗 1:17、加 3:11、来 10:37–38 的后期重读；第 23、44、47、50 周。
- **Supports:** Around Christianity's formation, all three chapters circulated in ordinary Greek/Hebrew Twelve scrolls; chapter 3 is not supplied only by medieval witnesses. Pre-Christian Greek translation/revision can be compared with Paul and Hebrews.
  **能支持**：在基督教形成前后，《哈巴谷书》三章已进入普通《十二先知》卷的希腊文和希伯来文抄传；第 3 章并非只由中世纪抄本补出。8ḤevXIIgr 还提供前基督教时期的希腊译文／修订层，可与保罗及《希伯来书》所用的希腊传统比较。
- **Does not establish:** No prophet's biography, precise invasion dialogues, fulfillment mechanism, first incorporation of chapter 3, or direct use by Paul/Hebrews. Earlier texts do not dictate only one New Testament interpretation.
  **不能支持**：两卷不能证明哈巴谷本人、迦勒底入侵的具体对话、神谕应验机制、第三章最初编入时间，或保罗／《希伯来书》作者直接使用这两件卷轴；文本早于新约也不等于新约解释只有一种可能。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** Two Greek scribes, original extent, date, precise kaige/Hebrew-revision phase debated; broad Roman dating of Mur88 is no copying day. 4Q82's possible Habakkuk 2:4 cannot count as equally strong.
  **争议与解读边界**：8ḤevXIIgr 的两位书手、原始卷幅、断代以及它属于 kaige／向希伯来文修订的精确阶段仍有讨论；Mur88 的罗马时期宽年也不能缩成抄写日。4Q82 只被疑似定位到哈 2:4，不能计为与这两件同强度的本卷残卷。
- **Grade:** A for objects/provenance/carrier/language/ordinary Twelve/visible text; B for late-Second-Temple/Roman Greek-Hebrew copying of three chapters; C for narrow dates/revisions/scribes/exemplars/restoration; D for prophet/events/first compilation/direct New Testament use/theology.
  **等级**：A（两件经卷、出土地、载体、语言、普通《十二先知》身份及可辨经文）；B（第二圣殿末期至罗马时期《哈巴谷书》三章的希腊／希伯来抄传）；C（窄断年、修订层、书手关系、逐处底本及残片复原）；D（先知人物、卷内事件、第三章最初编集、新约作者直接依赖或神学解释证明）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority featured scrolls: 8ḤevXIIgr provenance, date, language, Twelve](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/featured-scrolls); [Göttingen Septuagint catalogue: Rahlfs 943 collection and Habakkuk passages](https://septuaginta.uni-goettingen.de/catalogue/Ra_943/); [Gunnar M. Eidsvåg: two scribes, dates, Habakkuk 2:20/3:2, paleo-Hebrew divine name](https://septuaginta.uni-goettingen.de/static/ioscs/pdf/volumes/jscs46.pdf); [Israel Antiquities Authority: Mur88 / MurXII](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/MUR88-1?locale=en_US)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局精选古卷：8ḤevXIIgr 的出土地、年代、语言与《十二先知》身份](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/featured-scrolls)；[哥廷根七十士译本目录：Rahlfs 943 的馆藏与《哈巴谷书》残存范围](https://septuaginta.uni-goettingen.de/catalogue/Ra_943/)；[Gunnar M. Eidsvåg：8ḤevXIIgr 的两位书手、年代范围、哈 2:20／3:2 与古希伯来字母神名](https://septuaginta.uni-goettingen.de/static/ioscs/pdf/volumes/jscs46.pdf)；[以色列文物局：Mur88／MurXII 馆藏页](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/MUR88-1?locale=en_US)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-197"></a>

### 197. Sinaiticus 2 Esdras 9:9–23:31: Direct Fourth-Century Greek Nehemiah | 《西奈抄本》2 Esdras 9:9–23:31：《尼希米记》的四世纪希腊文直接见证

- **Date / carrier and collection:** Mid-fourth-century Greek parchment codex, divided among London, Leipzig, St Petersburg, St Catherine's Sinai. After a misplaced/duplicated 1 Chronicles section, surviving Old Testament continues 2 Esdras 9:9–23:31 then Esther.
  **年代／载体与馆藏**：《西奈抄本》是约主后四世纪中叶的大型希腊文羊皮 codex，现分藏伦敦、莱比锡、圣彼得堡及西奈圣凯瑟琳修道院。现存旧约部分在一段误装／重复的《历代志上》之后，从 2 Esdras 9:9 接续到 23:31，再进入《以斯帖记》。
- **Material type:** Greek 2 Esdras is not the other work often bearing that name in English Apocrypha: chapters 1–10 = Ezra 1–10, 11–23 = Nehemiah 1–13. Surviving text thus directly carries Nehemiah as well as later Ezra.
  **材料性质**：希腊传统的 2 Esdras 不是现代英语次经中常称 “2 Esdras” 的另一作品；它的 1–10 章对应《以斯拉记》1–10 章，11–23 章对应《尼希米记》1–13 章。因此《西奈抄本》现存 2 Esdras 9:9–23:31 不只保存《以斯拉记》末段，也直接承载整段希腊文《尼希米记》。
- **Related Scripture:** Ezra 9–10; Nehemiah 1–13; wall/covenant/lists/Temple service/reform material-text clock; week 29.
  **对应经文**：拉 9–10；尼 1–13；城墙、盟约、名单、圣殿服事与改革叙事的文本实物时钟；第 29 周。
- **Supports:** By mid-fourth century continuous Greek Ezra–Nehemiah entered a large Christian codex; thirteen Nehemiah chapters can be checked leaf by leaf. Fills MS gap, not a Persian archive.
  **能支持**：到四世纪中叶，希腊文《以斯拉—尼希米》已作为连续的 2 Esdras 进入大型基督教圣经 codex，《尼希米记》十三章有可逐叶核对的直接文本实物。这补的是目前项目 MS 缺口，不是把四世纪抄本变成波斯时期档案。
- **Does not establish:** No Nehemiah's person, 445/444 BCE wall, Sanballat conflict, lists/covenant/reform, Hebrew work's first boundaries/author/final compilation, or equally complete Second Temple scroll.
  **不能支持**：该抄本不能证明尼希米本人、主前 445／444 年建墙、参巴拉冲突、名单、盟约签署或改革，也不能单凭合卷形态决定希伯来作品的最初边界、作者、最后编集年，或证明第二圣殿时期已经存在同样完整的《尼希米记》卷轴。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** Names/chapter-verse numbering differ, with local omissions/variants: convert before citation. Duplicated Chronicles affects surviving beginning, but Nehemiah in 11–23 remains. Combined form is this codex's fact, not proof of every earlier Jewish manuscript.
  **争议与解读边界**：2 Esdras 的名称、章节和节数与现代《以斯拉记》《尼希米记》不同，个别段落也有缺文／异文；必须先换算再引用。现存开头受重复《历代志上》叶影响，但 2 Esdras 11–23 的《尼希米记》范围实际保留；“合在一卷”是该希腊 codex 的事实，不是所有更早犹太抄本形态的证明。
- **Grade:** A for codex/date/material/leaves/Greek/9:9–23:31 scope; B for fourth-century direct Greek Nehemiah/continuous Ezra–Nehemiah; C for earlier combination/missing leaves/numbering/variants; D for Persian people/events/authorship/first boundaries/composition/finalization/reform.
  **等级**：A（codex、年代、材质、现存叶、希腊文及 2 Esdras 9:9–23:31 范围）；B（四世纪《尼希米记》希腊文直接抄传及《以斯拉—尼希米》连续编排）；C（早期合卷史、缺叶复原、章节换算和个别异文）；D（波斯时期人物事件、作者、最初边界、初写／定稿日期或改革证明）。
- **Sources:** [Codex Sinaiticus Project: surviving contents, fourth-century parchment codex](https://www.codexsinaiticus.org/en/codex/content.aspx); [Codex Sinaiticus Project: 2 Esdras 23:15–31 manuscript page](https://www.codexsinaiticus.org/en/manuscript.aspx?book=8&chapter=23&verse=21); [Codex Sinaiticus Project: original sequence and missing-leaf reconstruction](https://codexsinaiticus.org/en/project/transcription_reconstructing.aspx); [NETS / University of Pennsylvania: 2 Esdras 1–10 = Ezra, 11–23 = Nehemiah](https://ccat.sas.upenn.edu/nets/edition/16-2esdras-nets.pdf); [Emanuel Tov: surviving 2 Esdras 9:9–23:31 in Sinaiticus](https://www.emanueltov.info/docs/varia/272.sinaiticus.pdf?v=1.0)
  **来源**：[Codex Sinaiticus Project：现存内容及四世纪羊皮 codex 概况](https://www.codexsinaiticus.org/en/codex/content.aspx)；[Codex Sinaiticus Project：2 Esdras 23:15–31 实物页](https://www.codexsinaiticus.org/en/manuscript.aspx?book=8&chapter=23&verse=21)；[Codex Sinaiticus Project：原 codex 卷序与缺叶重建](https://codexsinaiticus.org/en/project/transcription_reconstructing.aspx)；[NETS／University of Pennsylvania：2 Esdras 1–10＝以斯拉、11–23＝尼希米](https://ccat.sas.upenn.edu/nets/edition/16-2esdras-nets.pdf)；[Emanuel Tov：《西奈抄本》现存 2 Esdras 9:9–23:31 范围](https://www.emanueltov.info/docs/varia/272.sinaiticus.pdf?v=1.0)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-198"></a>

### 198. Sinaiticus 1 Chronicles: In-Place Fragments and an Erroneous Duplicate | 《西奈抄本》《历代志上》：原位残片与误抄重复段的双重见证

- **Date / carrier and collection:** Mid-fourth-century Greek parchment codex in four collections. St Catherine's 1975 New Finds preserve parts of 17:14–18:11; reconstructed original book begins near quire 28's end and ends about quire 30. A duplicate 9:27–19:17 was copied into 2 Esdras; ancient corrector marked three crosses.
  **年代／载体与馆藏**：《西奈抄本》是约主后四世纪中叶的大型希腊文羊皮 codex，现分藏伦敦、莱比锡、圣彼得堡及西奈圣凯瑟琳修道院。1975 年圣凯瑟琳修道院“新发现”残片保存代上 17:14–18:11 的部分文字；重建又显示本卷原来从第 28 帖末段开始，约在第 30 帖结束。另有代上 9:27–19:17 被误抄进 2 Esdras 中段的重复区块，古代校订者已以三个十字记号标出错误。
- **Material type:** Ordinary Greek biblical codex fragments, continuous leaves, correction traces. New Finds belong to original Chronicles position; overlapping text also survives in the erroneous duplicate, giving two witnesses within one codex. British Library and other holdings permit checking most duplicate text.
  **材料性质**：希腊文普通经文 codex 的残片、连续叶和抄写校正痕迹。代上 17:14–18:11 的新发现残片属于重建的《历代志上》原位；重叠经文又在误抄重复段中幸存，所以同一 codex 对部分《历代志上》文字保留两次见证。英国图书馆所藏叶与其他分藏合起来可核对误抄段的大部分范围。
- **Related Scripture:** 1 Chronicles 9:27–19:17, especially 17:14–18:11; Levites, Saul's death, David's accession/warriors/covenant/wars material-text clock; week 14.
  **对应经文**：历代志上 9:27–19:17，尤其 17:14–18:11；利未人职任、扫罗之死、大卫登基、勇士、大卫之约与征战叙事的文本实物时钟；第 14 周。
- **Supports:** Direct Greek Chronicles in a large Christian codex by mid-fourth century; in-place fragments/duplicate give MS access and show error detection/marking. Fills dedicated 1 Chronicles witness gap in 66-book navigation.
  **能支持**：到四世纪中叶，《历代志上》的希腊文本已经进入大型基督教圣经 codex；现存原位残片和误抄重复段共同为本卷提供可直接核对的 MS 入口，也让读者观察古代抄写错误怎样被发现和标记。这补上的是 66 卷导航中《历代志上》的专属直接文本缺口。
- **Does not establish:** No genealogies, Davidic covenant, Temple preparations, warriors/wars, author, first Hebrew form, final compilation; duplicate is not a different ancient book order. Does not settle 4Q118 column one's identity or turn 2 Chronicles in 4Q118 into 1 Chronicles.
  **不能支持**：四世纪希腊抄本不能证明亚当至大卫的家谱、大卫之约、建殿预备、勇士名单或征战事件，也不能决定《历代志》作者、希伯来底本的最初形态、最后编集年，或把误抄段当成另一种古代卷序。它也不能解决 4Q118 第一栏是否属于《历代志》、或把只保存《历代志下》的 4Q118 横向扩成《历代志上》抄本。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** Distinguish mostly missing original Chronicles leaves, small in-place fragments recovered in 1975, and copied duplicate in 2 Esdras. Erroneously copied/inserted duplicate is safer than merely later misbinding; sequence reconstruction strong, exemplar's role interpretive.
  **争议与解读边界**：需要区分三件事：原 codex 中已经缺失的大部分《历代志上》叶、1975 年寻回的原位小残片，以及抄入 2 Esdras 的重复段。重复段最安全的称呼是“误抄／插入的重复区块”，不宜只说“后来装订错页”；页序重建很强，但原范本怎样导致错误仍属解释。
- **Grade:** A for codex/date/material/Greek/original fragments/duplicate; B for fourth-century direct copying/duplication/correction; C for full sequence/error cause/missing extent; D for book's people/events/author/first exemplar/compilation/theology.
  **等级**：A（codex、年代、材质、现存希腊文字、原位残片与误抄重复段）；B（四世纪《历代志上》直接抄传、重复段及古代校正）；C（完整原卷叶序复原、错误成因与缺失范围细节）；D（卷内人物事件、作者、最初底本、编集日期或神学宣告证明）。
- **Sources:** [Codex Sinaiticus Project: original sequence, in-place fragments, duplicated text](https://codexsinaiticus.org/en/project/transcription_reconstructing.aspx); [Codex Sinaiticus Project: misplaced 1 Chronicles, manuscript and transcription](https://www.codexsinaiticus.org/en/manuscript.aspx?book=7); [British Library: Add MS 43725 and 1 Chronicles holdings](https://searcharchives.bl.uk/catalog/032-002169711); [Emanuel Tov: surviving 1 Chronicles in Sinaiticus](https://www.emanueltov.info/docs/varia/272.sinaiticus.pdf?v=1.0)
  **来源**：[Codex Sinaiticus Project：原 codex 卷序、原位残片与误抄重复段重建](https://codexsinaiticus.org/en/project/transcription_reconstructing.aspx)；[Codex Sinaiticus Project：《历代志上》误抄段实物与转写](https://www.codexsinaiticus.org/en/manuscript.aspx?book=7)；[British Library：Add MS 43725 及《历代志上》分藏范围](https://searcharchives.bl.uk/catalog/032-002169711)；[Emanuel Tov：《西奈抄本》现存《历代志上》范围](https://www.emanueltov.info/docs/varia/272.sinaiticus.pdf?v=1.0)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-199"></a>

### 199. 4Q252 / Commentary on Genesis A: Selective Rereading from Flood Calendar to Judah's Kingship | 4Q252／《创世记释义 A》：从洪水历法到犹大王权的选择性重读

- **Date / carrier and discovery:** Cave 4 Hebrew parchment fragments; IAA Herodian script, classified exegesis rather than ordinary Genesis scroll.
  **年代／载体与出土**：昆兰第 4 洞出土的希伯来文羊皮残卷；以色列文物局按希律时期字形归类，作品类型列为“释经文本”，而不是普通《创世记》经卷。
- **Material type:** Six-column selective exegesis: Genesis 6–9 flood dates, then Abraham, Sodom, Isaac's blessing, Esau/Amalek, Genesis 49 blessings. Rewriting, quotation/explanation, and פשרו (its interpretation is); not continuous chapter-by-chapter commentary.
  **材料性质**：六栏选择性释经，现存内容从创 6–9 的洪水日期，跳到亚伯拉罕、所多玛、以撒祝福、以扫／亚玛力和创 49 的雅各祝福。不同段落有改写、引文加说明及 `פשרו`（“其解释是”）等形式，不宜把它设想为逐章连续注释。
- **Related Scripture:** Genesis 6:3, 7:10–8:18, 9:24–27, 11:31; portions of 15, 18, 22, 28, 36, 49; weeks 2–5.
  **对应经文**：创 6:3；7:10–8:18；9:24–27；11:31；15、18、22、28、36、49 章若干语句；第 2–5 周。
- **Supports:** Late Second Temple interpreters calculate flood timetable, retell patriarchs, relate Judah's Genesis 49:10 blessing to Davidic throne, righteous anointed one/Davidic branch, community law-observance: direct RX of calendar/kingship/end-time rereading.
  **能支持**：到第二圣殿末期，犹太释经者会精算洪水日程、重述列祖材料，并把创 49:10 的犹大祝福连到大卫王位、“公义的受膏者／大卫的苗裔”和群体守律法。这是一件把《创世记》故事带入后来历法、王权与末世期待的直接 RX 实物。
- **Does not establish:** No flood/Abraham/Sodom/blessing events, Genesis author/final form/first meanings, or named New Testament fulfillment merely from messianic rereading.
  **不能支持**：它不能证明洪水、亚伯拉罕、所多玛或雅各祝福的事件，也不能给《创世记》作者、定稿和各段原始含义定年；创 49 的弥赛亚式重读也不能单独证明某位新约人物已经被卷中作者指名。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** Joins, missing lines, Genesis 18/Deuteronomic law combination, 364-day calendar and commentary/rewritten-Bible genre debated. Safest: selective Herodian Genesis exegesis, not complete Qumran commentary.
  **争议与解读边界**：残片接合、缺行复原、创 18 与申命记法律的组合、364 日历法细节及“释义／重写圣经”的文类称呼仍可讨论。最安全的结论是“有一件希律时期的选择性《创世记》释经”，不是“昆兰保存了完整创世记注释”。
- **Grade:** A for fragments/cave/language/carrier/passages/catalogue class; B for calendar/royal/community reception; C for date/lines/joins/genre/restoration; D for creation-patriarch events/author/finalization/Christological fulfillment.
  **等级**：A（残卷、洞穴、语言、载体、可辨经文及馆藏分类）；B（第二圣殿末期《创世记》的历法、王权与群体式接收）；C（窄断年、缺行、接合、文类及逐句复原）；D（创世—列祖事件、作者、定稿或基督论应验证明）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: 4Q252 / Commentary on Genesis A](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q252-1?locale=en_US); [Qumran-Digital: 4Q252 transcription and Genesis 49 kingship interpretation](https://lexicon.qumran-digital.org/transcriptions/4Q252/2022-10-14/index.html); [Journal of Hebrew Scriptures: genre survey, selective Genesis 6–49 in 4Q252–254a](https://jhsonline.org/index.php/jhs/article/download/7318/6019?inline=1)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局：4Q252／Commentary on Genesis A](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q252-1?locale=en_US)；[Qumran-Digital：4Q252 转写及创 49 王权释义](https://lexicon.qumran-digital.org/transcriptions/4Q252/2022-10-14/index.html)；[Journal of Hebrew Scriptures：4Q252–254a 选择性处理创 6–49 的文类综述](https://jhsonline.org/index.php/jhs/article/download/7318/6019?inline=1)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-200"></a>

### 200. 4Q166 / 4Q167 Hosea Pesharim: Applying Covenant, Festivals, and Northern Oracles | 4Q166／4Q167《何西阿书释义》：婚约、节期与北国神谕的现实化解释

- **Date / carrier and discovery:** Cave 4 Hebrew parchment exegesis fragments, IAA Herodian script, nonbiblical Pesher Hosea A/B.
  **年代／载体与出土**：两件均出土于昆兰第 4 洞，是希伯来文羊皮释经残卷；以色列文物局按希律时期字形归类，并将它们列为非经卷类的 `Pesher Hosea A／B`。
- **Material type:** 4Q166 quotes/explains Hosea 2:8–14; small 4Q167 fragments cover portions of 5:13–15, 6, 8:6–14. Its interpretation is applies prophetic wording to interpreters' community, opponents, festivals, final age.
  **材料性质**：4Q166 保存何 2:8–14 的经文引句与释义，4Q167 的小残片覆盖何 5:13–15、6 章及 8:6–14 的若干语句。作品在经文引句后以“其解释是”把先知语言应用于释经者自己的群体、对手、节期和末世处境。
- **Related Scripture:** Hosea 1–3, 5–6, 8, 11, 14 storyline, especially 2:8–14, 5:13–15, 6:4; week 18.
  **对应经文**：何 1–3、5–6、8、11、14 的主线，尤其何 2:8–14；5:13–15；6:4 等；第 18 周。
- **Supports:** A late Second Temple community not only copies Hosea but studies marriage/covenant, idols, foreign aid, festivals, judgment to interpret calendar disputes/community conflict. Adds named-commentary RX beyond ordinary Twelve scrolls.
  **能支持**：第二圣殿末期的一个犹太群体不只抄写《何西阿书》，还持续研读其婚约、偶像、外援、节期和审判语言，并用来解释自己的历法争论与群体冲突。这补的是《何西阿书》从普通《十二先知》经卷到具名注释的 RX 层。
- **Does not establish:** No Hosea's person/marriage/eighth-century speech scenes or exclusive original meaning; commentary opponents cannot simply equal ancient Israel in Scripture.
  **不能支持**：两卷不能证明何西阿本人、婚姻经历、主前八世纪的具体讲论场景，或昆兰解释就是先知唯一原意；释义中的群体对手也不能无说明地等同经文中的古代以色列。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** Fragmentary joins/line order/passages/opponent terms require restoration. 4Q166 festival criticism witnesses Qumran calendar controversy, not proof Hosea 2 originally concerned a 364-day calendar.
  **争议与解读边界**：两卷残破，碎片接合、行次、可辨经文范围及若干对手称谓都有复原问题；4Q166 的节期批评可显示昆兰式历法争论，不能据此断言何 2 原先就在谈 364 日历法。
- **Grade:** A for two objects/cave/language/carrier/quotes/pesher class; B for continued Hosea exegetical reception; C for joins/lines/opponents/calendar; D for prophet/marriage/original whole-book meaning/fulfillment.
  **等级**：A（两件残卷、洞穴、语言、载体、可辨引文及 `pesher` 分类）；B（第二圣殿末期《何西阿书》的连续释经接收）；C（碎片接合、缺行、具体对手和历法细节）；D（先知生平、婚姻事件、全卷原意或应验证明）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: 4Q166 / Pesher Hosea A](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q166-1?locale=en_US); [Israel Antiquities Authority: 4Q167 / Pesher Hosea B](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q167-1?locale=en_US); [Qumran-Digital: 4Q166 transcription and Hosea 2 quotations](https://lexicon.qumran-digital.org/transcriptions/4Q166/2025-03-11/index.html); [Qumran-Digital: 4Q167 transcription and Hosea 5–8 quotations](https://lexicon.qumran-digital.org/transcriptions/4Q167/2025-11-11/index.html)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局：4Q166／Pesher Hosea A](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q166-1?locale=en_US)；[以色列文物局：4Q167／Pesher Hosea B](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q167-1?locale=en_US)；[Qumran-Digital：4Q166 转写与何 2 引句](https://lexicon.qumran-digital.org/transcriptions/4Q166/2025-03-11/index.html)；[Qumran-Digital：4Q167 转写与何 5–8 引句](https://lexicon.qumran-digital.org/transcriptions/4Q167/2025-11-11/index.html)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-201"></a>

### 201. 1Q14 / Pesher Micah: Samaria–Jerusalem Judgment Reread as Community History | 1Q14／《弥迦书释义》：撒马利亚—耶路撒冷审判被读入昆兰群体史

- **Date / carrier and discovery:** Cave 1 Hebrew parchment fragments, IAA Herodian script, Pesher Micah.
  **年代／载体与出土**：昆兰第 1 洞出土的希伯来文羊皮残卷；以色列文物局以希律时期字形归类，作品列为 `Pesher Micah`。
- **Material type:** Broken groups mainly quote/explain Micah 1. Clearest quotes Jacob's transgression/Judah's high places, Samaria/Jerusalem, then interprets Jerusalem through Teacher of Righteousness, community council, final-judgment survivors; much linking text restored in brackets.
  **材料性质**：多组破碎残片主要保存弥 1 章引句与释义。最清楚的一组先引用“雅各的罪过／犹大的邱坛”及撒马利亚、耶路撒冷，再把“耶路撒冷”解释到“公义教师”、群体议会及末后审判中得救者；大部分连接文字依赖括号复原。
- **Related Scripture:** Micah 1:2–9, especially 1:5–6; city judgment/remnant/community fidelity; week 19.
  **对应经文**：弥 1:2–9，尤其 1:5–6；弥迦全卷的城邑审判、余民与群体忠信主题；第 19 周。
- **Supports:** Late Second Temple community treats Micah as authoritative prophecy interpreting its leaders, opponents, Jerusalem, final age; RX beyond ordinary 4Q81 Twelve fragments.
  **能支持**：到第二圣殿末期，《弥迦书》已被一个犹太群体当作能够解释自身领袖、对手、耶路撒冷和末后处境的权威先知文本。这是普通 4Q81《十二先知》文本残片之外的 RX 证据。
- **Does not establish:** No eighth-century destruction details, Micah's biography, Christian Bethlehem fulfillment, or Teacher projected into Micah's setting. Later pesher cannot replace chapter one's Assyrian context.
  **不能支持**：它不能证明主前八世纪撒马利亚／耶路撒冷的毁灭细节、弥迦生平、伯利恒预言的基督教实现，或把“公义教师”反投回弥迦原处境；后期释义也不能取代弥 1 的亚述时代历史语境。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** Extremely fragmentary order, bracketed supplements, interpretive targets debated. Teacher of Righteousness stronger in legible lines; full syntax/community terms still separate visible text from editorial restoration.
  **争议与解读边界**：1Q14 极度残破，碎片次序、括号内补字和若干释义对象均有争论；“公义教师”在可读行中较强，但完整句法和群体称谓仍须把可见字与编辑复原分开。
- **Grade:** A for object/cave/language/Micah 1 quotes/class; B for community exegetical reception; C for order/lines/opponents/syntax; D for prophet/eighth-century events/original meaning/New Testament fulfillment.
  **等级**：A（残卷、洞穴、语言、可辨弥 1 引文与馆藏分类）；B（第二圣殿末期对《弥迦书》的群体式释经接收）；C（碎片次序、缺行、对手及完整句法）；D（先知人物、八世纪事件、原意或新约应验证明）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: 1Q14 / Pesher Micah](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/1Q14-1?locale=en_US); [Qumran-Digital: 1Q14 transcription and Micah 1:2–6 quotations](https://lexicon.qumran-digital.org/transcriptions/1Q14/2025-03-11/index.html); [Pontifical Biblical Institute: institutional bibliography for Dominic S. Irudayaraj's 2017 article](https://www.biblico.it/archivio/presentazione/pres_corsi_sem_24-25.pdf)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局：1Q14／Pesher Micah](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/1Q14-1?locale=en_US)；[Qumran-Digital：1Q14 转写及弥 1:2–6 引句](https://lexicon.qumran-digital.org/transcriptions/1Q14/2025-03-11/index.html)；[教皇圣经学院：Dominic S. Irudayaraj 该篇 2017 年论文的机构书目记录](https://www.biblico.it/archivio/presentazione/pres_corsi_sem_24-25.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-202"></a>

### 202. 4Q169 / Pesher Nahum: Nineveh's Oracles Reread through Hasmonean Conflict | 4Q169／《那鸿书释义》：尼尼微神谕被重读为哈斯摩尼时代冲突

- **Date / carrier and discovery:** Cave 4 Hebrew parchment exegesis, IAA Herodian script, Pesher Nahum.
  **年代／载体与出土**：昆兰第 4 洞出土的希伯来文羊皮残卷；以色列文物局按希律时期字形归类，作品列为 `Pesher Nahum`。
- **Material type:** Quotes/explains 1:3–6, 2:12–14, 3:1–5/6–9/10–12/14. Names Greek King Demetrius; Lion of Wrath/Seekers of Smooth Things interpret prophetic images through first-century BCE Jewish and Seleucid–Hasmonean conflict.
  **材料性质**：连续引释鸿 1:3–6、2:12–14、3:1–5、6–9、10–12、14 等段。残卷明确提“希腊王德米特里”，又以“忿怒之狮”“寻求圆滑之事者”等代称解释先知图像，把原指尼尼微的神谕应用到主前一世纪犹太内部和塞琉古—哈斯摩尼冲突。
- **Related Scripture:** Nahum 1–3; Nineveh's violence/imperial judgment/later communal rereading; week 22.
  **对应经文**：那鸿书 1–3 章；尼尼微暴力、帝国受审及后来群体重读；第 22 周。
- **Supports:** Material witness to continued Nahum study about five centuries after Nineveh's fall, applied to Demetrius/Jerusalem/factions. Makes time gap between Assyrian events and Second Temple reception concrete.
  **能支持**：这件实物证明《那鸿书》在尼尼微陷落约五百年后仍被逐段研究，并被当作解释德米特里、耶路撒冷和犹太派系斗争的活文本。它把“亚述事件史”与“第二圣殿接收史”之间的时间距离具体展示出来。
- **Does not establish:** No every poetic detail of 612 BCE fall, prophet's identity/date, or first-century targets originally named by Nahum. Violent narrative does not automatically authorize later violence theologically.
  **不能支持**：它不能证明主前 612 年尼尼微陷落的每个诗性细节、那鸿的身份和写作年，也不能把主前一世纪对象说成那鸿原先逐名预告的人物。释义中的暴力叙述更不能自动成为对后来暴力的神学认可。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** Demetrius is visible; identifying Demetrius III, Lion as Alexander Jannaeus, Seekers as Pharisees is common reconstruction, not three explicit names. Sequence, composition year, factions debated.
  **争议与解读边界**：`德米特里` 是残卷明文；把他具体认作德米特里三世、把“忿怒之狮”认作亚历山大·詹纳乌斯、把“寻求圆滑之事者”认作法利赛人，是常见历史重建，却不是三个人名都逐字写在卷上。事件次序、成书年及派系身份仍有争论。
- **Grade:** A for object/cave/language/quotes/visible Demetrius; B for applied exegesis/Hasmonean context; C for epithets/identities/joins/date; D for all Nineveh details/prophet/original meaning/theological verdict.
  **等级**：A（残卷、洞穴、语言、经文引句及“德米特里”等可见文字）；B（第二圣殿末期对《那鸿书》的现实化释经与哈斯摩尼冲突背景）；C（代称身份、精确人物、碎片接合及写作年）；D（尼尼微事件全部细节、先知身份、原意或神学裁决）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: 4Q169 / Pesher Nahum](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q169-1?locale=en_US); [Qumran-Digital: 4Q169 transcription and Nahum 1–3 exegesis](https://lexicon.qumran-digital.org/transcriptions/4Q169/2023-12-08/index.html); [Brill: Shani Berrin，《The Pesher Nahum Scroll from Qumran》](https://brill.com/display/book/9789047404248/BP000001.xml)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局：4Q169／Pesher Nahum](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q169-1?locale=en_US)；[Qumran-Digital：4Q169 转写及鸿 1–3 引释](https://lexicon.qumran-digital.org/transcriptions/4Q169/2023-12-08/index.html)；[Brill：Shani Berrin，《The Pesher Nahum Scroll from Qumran》](https://brill.com/display/book/9789047404248/BP000001.xml)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-203"></a>

### 203. 1Q15 / 4Q170 Zephaniah Pesharim: Two Fragmentary Day-of-the-Lord Witnesses | 1Q15／4Q170《西番雅书释义》：两件极残破的“耶和华之日”接收见证

- **Date / carrier and discovery:** Caves 1 and 4 respectively; Hebrew parchment exegesis, IAA broad Hellenistic–Roman and related paleographic classes, not one identical year.
  **年代／载体与出土**：1Q15 出土于昆兰第 1 洞，4Q170 出土于第 4 洞；均为希伯来文羊皮释经残片。以色列文物局分别采用较宽的希腊化—罗马时期及相关古文字分类，不能据此给两件定到同一年。
- **Material type:** 1Q15 quotes Zephaniah 1:18–2:2, applying after its interpretation is to all Judah's inhabitants. 4Q170 preserves 1:12–13 wording about neither good nor harm, wealth/houses; commentary almost entirely lost. Separate portions/copies, not one torn-apart sheet.
  **材料性质**：1Q15 保存番 1:18–2:2 的连续引句，并在“其解释是”后把信息应用于“犹大地所有居民”；4Q170 只残存番 1:12–13 关于“耶和华必不降福，也不降祸”及财物、房屋的引文，原释义几乎全部遗失。两件应视为不同段落／抄件，不是同一张卷被拆开。
- **Related Scripture:** Zephaniah 1:12–2:2; Lord's day, complacent Jerusalem, Judah's judgment, repentance; week 22.
  **对应经文**：番 1:12–2:2；耶和华的日子、耶路撒冷自满、犹大受审与悔改召唤；第 22 周。
- **Supports:** At least two caves preserve named Zephaniah exegesis: late Second Temple quotation/application to interpreters' Judah/judgment expectations beyond ordinary Twelve copying.
  **能支持**：至少两个昆兰洞穴保存了《西番雅书》释义作品，显示这卷短先知书在第二圣殿后期不仅进入普通《十二先知》经卷，也被逐句引用并应用到释经者的“犹大地”与审判期待中。
- **Does not establish:** No prophet, sequence of Josiah's reforms, precise seventh-century fulfillment, or unique Qumran referent. 4Q170's surviving quotation does not permit inventing lost interpretation.
  **不能支持**：残卷不能证明西番雅本人、约西亚改革先后、主前七世纪神谕的具体应验，或昆兰群体就是经文唯一指涉；4Q170 尤其只保留引文，不能补写已经失落的解释。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** Both highly fragmentary: 1Q15 missing lines/full Judah context, 4Q170 extent, mutual relationship cannot be over-restored. Named pesher fragments, not a known complete Qumran commentary.
  **争议与解读边界**：两件都极残破；1Q15 的缺行与“犹大地”完整语境、4Q170 的作品范围和两件之间的关系都不能过度复原。最强结论是“存在具名释义残片”，不是“已知完整昆兰《西番雅书》注释”。
- **Grade:** A for fragments/caves/language/quotes/pesher class; B for exegetical reception; C for dating/lines/extent/relationship; D for prophet/Josianic events/original meaning/fulfillment.
  **等级**：A（两件残片、洞穴、语言、可辨番 1–2 引句与 `pesher` 分类）；B（第二圣殿后期《西番雅书》的释经接收）；C（断代、缺行、完整作品范围及两件关系）；D（先知人物、约西亚时代事件、原意或应验证明）。
- **Sources:** [Israel Antiquities Authority: 1Q15 / Pesher Zephaniah](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/1Q15-1?locale=en_US); [Israel Antiquities Authority: 4Q170 / Pesher Zephaniah](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q170-1?locale=en_US); [Qumran-Digital: 1Q15 transcription and Zephaniah 1:18–2:2](https://lexicon.qumran-digital.org/transcriptions/1Q15/2025-08-25/index.html); [Qumran-Digital: 4Q170 transcription and Zephaniah 1:12–13](https://lexicon.qumran-digital.org/transcriptions/4Q170/2026-02-11/index.html)
  **来源**：[以色列文物局：1Q15／Pesher Zephaniah](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/1Q15-1?locale=en_US)；[以色列文物局：4Q170／Pesher Zephaniah](https://www.deadseascrolls.org.il/explore-the-archive/manuscript/4Q170-1?locale=en_US)；[Qumran-Digital：1Q15 转写及番 1:18–2:2](https://lexicon.qumran-digital.org/transcriptions/1Q15/2025-08-25/index.html)；[Qumran-Digital：4Q170 转写及番 1:12–13](https://lexicon.qumran-digital.org/transcriptions/4Q170/2026-02-11/index.html)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-204"></a>

### 204. Melito of Sardis: A Named Late-Second-Century Old Testament Catalogue | 撒狄的梅利托旧约书目：第二世纪后期把律法、历史、智慧与先知逐名连成一组

- **Date / textual witnesses:** Late-second-century Eklogai preface to Onesimus describes eastern inquiry into Old Testament books' number/order where events were proclaimed/occurred. Work lost; Eusebius' early-fourth-century Church History 4.26.13–14 transmits passage: statement and surviving carrier have distinct dates.
  **年代／传本**：撒狄主教梅利托（Melito of Sardis）在第二世纪后期写给俄尼西母的《摘录集》（*Eklogai*）序言中，说明自己曾往“东方／这些事宣讲和发生之地”查考“旧约书卷”的数目与次序。原书已经失传，现段由优西比乌约第四世纪初写成的《教会史》4.26.13–14 引录；因此必须把梅利托陈述的年代与现存承载文字的年代分开。
- **Material type:** Named Christian catalogue, not manuscript, Jewish council decree, or excavated list. Pentateuch, Joshua, Judges, Ruth, four Kings, two Chronicles, Psalms, Proverbs also Wisdom, Ecclesiastes, Song, Job, Isaiah, Jeremiah, one Twelve, Daniel, Ezekiel, Ezra.
  **材料性质**：古代基督徒作者所作的具名旧约书目，不是经卷抄本、犹太会议决议或考古出土清单。名单依次列摩西五卷、约书亚、士师、路得、“列王四卷”、历代志两卷、诗篇、箴言（“也称智慧”）、传道书、雅歌、约伯、以赛亚、耶利米、《十二先知》一卷、但以理、以西结和以斯拉。
- **Related books / weeks:** Old Testament storyline except Esther and not separately named Lamentations; named CAT RX for Judges, Ruth, 1 Samuel, 1–2 Kings, Proverbs, Ecclesiastes, Amos, Jonah, Jeremiah, Ezekiel, Ezra, Zechariah, Malachi, Joel, plus bridge 1–2 Chronicles, Song, Obadiah, Haggai; weeks 1–27, 29–33.
  **对应书卷／周次**：除《以斯帖记》及未被单独写出的《耶利米哀歌》外，覆盖本项目旧约主线；尤其为《士师记》《路得记》《撒母耳记上》《列王纪上下》《箴言》《传道书》《阿摩司书》《约拿书》《耶利米书》《以西结书》《以斯拉记》《撒迦利亚书》《玛拉基书》《约珥书》以及桥接层《历代志上下》《雅歌》《俄巴底亚书》《哈该书》提供明确 CAT（书目列名）型 RX；第 1–27、29–33 周。
- **Supports:** One late-second-century Asia Minor leader researches named Old Testament books and combined naming: four Greek Kingdoms = modern 1–2 Samuel/1–2 Kings; two Chronicles/one Twelve show modern counts cannot simply map backward. Explicit catalogue reception, not thematic resemblance.
  **能支持**：到第二世纪后期，小亚细亚一位基督徒领袖会把这些书卷当作“旧约书卷”逐名查考，并知道若干古代合卷／命名习惯：“列王四卷”对应希腊传统的王国书一至四，即现代《撒母耳记上、下》与《列王纪上、下》；“历代志两卷”和“《十二先知》一卷”又显示现代分卷数不能直接倒套古代计数。它为多卷提供的是明确书目接收，不是只有主题相似。
- **Does not establish:** No all Jews/Christians' identical scope/order/text or modern Protestant 39 separately bound books; no authors/composition/finalization/events/theology. Eastern trip is no documented Caesarean-library official canon certificate.
  **不能支持**：名单不能证明第二世纪所有犹太人与基督徒使用完全相同的范围、次序或文本形态，也不能直接称为今天新教 39 卷旧约已经逐卷装订完成。它不证明任何卷的作者、初写／定稿日期、卷内人物事件或神学宣告；“梅利托赴东方”也不能在无进一步证据下扩写成他在凯撒利亚某座图书馆取得官方正典证书。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** Esther's absence intentional/transmission/source difference unresolved; cannot add unnamed Lamentations from later combining. Ezra likely Ezra–Nehemiah, precise Greek text separate question. Proverbs also Wisdom usually alternate title, not automatically extra Wisdom of Solomon.
  **争议与解读边界**：《以斯帖记》的缺席究竟是有意、抄传遗漏还是资料来源差异，仍不能决定；《耶利米哀歌》没有被单独点名，不能仅凭后来合卷习惯替梅利托补写。“以斯拉”很可能按古代习惯涵盖以斯拉—尼希米，但具体采用哪一种希腊《以斯拉》文本仍须另论。“箴言，也称智慧”通常理解为同一书的别称，不宜未经论证拆成另一本《所罗门智训》。
- **Grade:** A for transmitted list/order/Eusebian frame; B+ for Melito's circle's named reception; C for full lost context/exact date/inquiry site/omissions/name mappings; D for first-century/universal closed canon/authors/events/original meaning/theology.
  **等级**：A（现传《教会史》4.26.13–14 的名单、次序和优西比乌引文框架）；B+（第二世纪后期梅利托圈子的具名旧约书目接收）；C（原《摘录集》的完整语境、精确年代、考察地点、遗漏原因和若干古代书名的现代对应）；D（第一世纪或全地区封闭正典、作者、事件、原意及神学正确性的证明）。
- **Sources:** [Eusebius, Church History 4.26.13–14: transmitted Melito catalogue](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/250104.htm); [Wikisource / NPNF: catalogue and notes on one Twelve scroll and Ezra](https://en.wikisource.org/wiki/Nicene_and_Post-Nicene_Fathers:_Series_II/Volume_I/Church_History_of_Eusebius/Book_IV/Chapter_26); [Oxford: Gallagher and Meade, Greek catalogues' texts, translations, analysis](https://academic.oup.com/book/25501/chapter/192729013)
  **来源**：[优西比乌《教会史》4.26.13–14：梅利托书目现传全文](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/250104.htm)；[Wikisource／NPNF：逐项名单及“十二先知一卷”“以斯拉”注释](https://en.wikisource.org/wiki/Nicene_and_Post-Nicene_Fathers:_Series_II/Volume_I/Church_History_of_Eusebius/Book_IV/Chapter_26)；[Oxford：Gallagher、Meade 对梅利托、俄利根等希腊书目的原文、翻译与分析](https://academic.oup.com/book/25501/chapter/192729013)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="evidence-205"></a>

### 205. Origen's Hebrew Twenty-Two: Combined Counting, Esther, and Lamentations | 俄利根“按希伯来人二十二卷”书目：合卷计数、以斯帖与哀歌的明确接收

- **Date / textual witnesses:** Early-third-century Psalm 1 commentary says Hebrew-transmitted canon has twenty-two books like alphabet. Original passage lost, quoted in early-fourth-century Eusebius, Church History 6.25.1–2: statement and carrier are two clocks.
  **年代／传本**：俄利根在第三世纪前半的《诗篇第一篇注释》中说明“按希伯来人传下”的正典书卷共有二十二卷，与希伯来字母数对应。该注释的此段原本已经失传，现由优西比乌第四世纪初《教会史》6.25.1–2 引录；它是“第三世纪陈述—第四世纪引文载体”的双时钟资料。
- **Material type:** Hebrew/Greek titles combine Judges–Ruth, Samuel pair, Kings pair, Chronicles pair, first/second Ezra; explicitly Jeremiah with Lamentations and Epistle in one and names Esther. Maccabees outside these is not silently another internal twenty-two item.
  **材料性质**：带希伯来／希腊书名对照的书目说明。现传次序把《士师记》与《路得记》合一，把《撒母耳记》上下合一，把《列王纪》上下合一，把《历代志》上下合一，把“以斯拉第一、第二”合一；又明确写“耶利米连同哀歌和书信为一卷”，并逐名列《以斯帖记》。名单末尾另说“这些以外”还有《马加比书》，不能把这句话抹成二十二卷内部的下一卷。
- **Related books / weeks:** Named CAT RX for Judges, Ruth, 1 Samuel, 1–2 Kings, Proverbs, Ecclesiastes, Jeremiah, Ezekiel, Ezra, Esther, bridge Chronicles pair/Song/Lamentations; weeks 1–17, 19–29, 31–33. Transmitted enumeration omits Twelve; anchor 204, not 205, provides those explicit names.
  **对应书卷／周次**：明确补强《士师记》《路得记》《撒母耳记上》《列王纪上下》《箴言》《传道书》《耶利米书》《以西结书》《以斯拉记》《以斯帖记》以及桥接层《历代志上下》《雅歌》《耶利米哀歌》的 CAT 型 RX；第 1–17、19–29、31–33 周。现传枚举没有写《十二先知》，故本项目不借锚点 205 给小先知补列名，而由锚点 204 的明文承担。
- **Supports:** Third-century scholar knows Hebrew-style twenty-two counting combining modern books and supplies Hebrew titles. Ancient22/modern39 can largely reflect combined counting; Esther/Lamentations explicitly in his bibliographical knowledge.
  **能支持**：第三世纪基督徒学者已经知道一种把现代多卷合计为“二十二卷”的希伯来式计数，并能逐项给出多个希伯来标题。它直接说明“现代 39 卷”与“古代 22 卷”可以主要因合卷方式不同而指向大范围相同书卷；也明确证明《以斯帖记》和《哀歌》进入俄利根所记录的书目知识。
- **Does not establish:** Not bound twenty-two, Jewish council decree, uniform church list, or universal settled boundaries; no Esther court events/Lamentations author/Kings events/visions/traditional authors. According to Hebrews does not guarantee every item matches current Masoretic form.
  **不能支持**：这不是二十二卷实体合订本、犹太公会法令或所有教会统一目录，不能用来宣布当时所有群体的正典边界已经完全一致。它同样不能证明以斯帖宫廷事件、耶路撒冷哀歌作者、列王叙事、先知异象或任何卷的传统作者；“按希伯来人”也不保证名单每项都只反映现行马所拉文本形态。
- **Debate and interpretive boundaries:** Claims twenty-two but enumerates twenty-one, missing Twelve. Commonly treated as omission by Origen/Eusebius/later transmission; existing wording must not be silently repaired. Epistle with Jeremiah/Lamentations usually Letter of Jeremiah, outside later Hebrew24: numbers/Greek use/Christian cataloguing do not fully coincide. Twenty-two is no mechanical code for modern39.
  **争议与解读边界**：现传名单自称二十二卷，实际只枚举二十一项，因为《十二先知》整卷缺失；多数研究把它视为俄利根、优西比乌或后来抄传中的遗漏，但现有文字仍不可静默改写。另一方面，“耶利米＋哀歌＋书信”中的“书信”通常指《耶利米书信》，它不在后来的希伯来 24 卷中，说明希伯来式数字、希腊文本使用与基督徒书目实践并非完全重合。二十二这个数字不能当作现代 39 卷边界的机械密码。
- **Grade:** A for transmitted 6.25.1–2 claim/combinations/Esther/Lamentations/Epistle/outside wording; B+ for Origen's early-third-century reception/counting; C for omission stage/lost context/Hebrew transliterations/Epistle history; D for universal closed canon/authors/events/theology.
  **等级**：A（现传《教会史》6.25.1–2 的二十二卷声明、逐项合卷、以斯帖／哀歌／书信及“这些以外”措辞）；B+（第三世纪前半俄利根对希伯来式书卷计数和具体书名的接收知识）；C（《十二先知》遗漏发生环节、原注释完整语境、各希伯来转写及“书信”的集合史）；D（普世封闭正典、原作者、卷内事件或神学宣告的证明）。
- **Sources:** [Eusebius, Church History 6.25.1–2: transmitted Origen catalogue](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/250106.htm); [Wikisource / NPNF: Church History 6.25 catalogue](https://en.wikisource.org/wiki/Nicene_and_Post-Nicene_Fathers:_Series_II/Volume_I/Church_History_of_Eusebius/Book_VI/Chapter_25); [Oxford: Gallagher and Meade, early Greek Christian catalogues](https://academic.oup.com/book/25501/chapter/192729013); [University of Edinburgh: Timothy H. Lim on the transmitted omission of the Twelve](https://www.research.ed.ac.uk/files/77111228/Lim2018TheWritingsInTheHellenistic.pdf)
  **来源**：[优西比乌《教会史》6.25.1–2：俄利根书目现传全文](https://www.newadvent.org/fathers/250106.htm)；[Wikisource／NPNF：《教会史》6.25 逐项名单](https://en.wikisource.org/wiki/Nicene_and_Post-Nicene_Fathers:_Series_II/Volume_I/Church_History_of_Eusebius/Book_VI/Chapter_25)；[Oxford：Gallagher、Meade，早期希腊基督徒书目分析](https://academic.oup.com/book/25501/chapter/192729013)；[爱丁堡大学：Timothy H. Lim 对现传名单漏《十二先知》的讨论](https://www.research.ed.ac.uk/files/77111228/Lim2018TheWritingsInTheHellenistic.pdf)。
- **Last recorded check:** 2026-07-28.
  **最后核查**：2026-07-28。

<a id="storyline"></a>

## Placing Evidence Back into the Storyline | 把旁证放回故事线

| Biblical stage / 圣经阶段 | Available anchors / 可用锚点 | Connecting role / 串联作用 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Creation, flood, Babel | Enuma Elish; Atrahasis; Gilgamesh flood tablet; ziggurat cylinder; Genesis 4Q1/4Q7; 4Q252 Genesis commentary | First four give ancient comparative context; 4Q1/4Q7 give late Second Temple Hebrew manuscripts; 4Q252 selectively interprets flood dates and patriarchs. None proves events, authorship, or initial composition. |
| 创造、洪水与巴别 | 《埃努玛·埃利什》《阿特拉哈西斯》《吉尔伽美什》洪水泥版、神塔圆柱、4Q1／4Q7《创世记》、4Q252《创世记释义 A》 | 前四项给古代比较背景，4Q1／4Q7 给第二圣殿后期希伯来经卷实物，4Q252 则显示洪水日期与后续列祖故事怎样被选择性解释；三层都不证明事件、作者或初写年代 |
| Patriarchs and Lower Egypt | Beni Hasan Asiatic procession; Brooklyn Papyrus; Hammurabi; 4Q252 | First three attest movement into Egypt, West Asian names, and family law; 4Q252 witnesses later selective interpretation of Abraham, Jacob, and Davidic branch. Neither background nor later exegesis is contemporary proof of patriarchal persons/events. |
| 列祖与下埃及 | 贝尼哈桑西亚人图、布鲁克林纸草、汉谟拉比法律碑、4Q252《创世记释义 A》 | 前三项说明人口进入埃及、西亚姓名和古代家庭法律背景真实存在；4Q252 则见证第二圣殿时期如何选释亚伯拉罕、雅各祝福与大卫苗裔。两类材料都不能把图像、名单或后期释经倒作列祖人物与事件的同期证明 |
| Exodus, labor, festival memory | Pi-Ramesses; Rekhmire brickmaking; Brooklyn Papyrus; Merneptah; Elephantine P.13464; 4Q22 Exodus | Background links royal city, labor, West Asian population, Israel in Canaan before about 1208 BCE. P.13464 concerns Persian-period festivals; 4Q22 late Second Temple copying of Exodus 6–37. Neither is contemporary proof of Moses, plagues, crossing, or route. |
| 出埃及、埃及劳役与节期记忆 | 皮兰塞、雷克米拉墓制砖图、布鲁克林纸草、梅尔内普塔碑、象岛 P. 13464、4Q22《出埃及记》 | 背景材料连接王城、劳动、西亚人口和主前 1208 年前已在迦南的“以色列”；P. 13464 只证明波斯时期节期实践，4Q22 只证明第二圣殿后期出 6–37 的大范围抄传。后两者都不能倒作摩西、十灾、过海或路线的同期物证 |
| Sinai, wilderness, Moab plains | Esarhaddon succession treaty; Ketef Hinnom; Deir Alla Balaam; Soleb yhwꜣ; 4Q27 Numbers | Treaty form, priestly blessing, Balaam tradition, possible early divine name; 4Q27 adds later Hebrew Numbers 11–36. Text history cannot become a contemporary record of wilderness populations/routes/Balaam events. |
| 西奈、旷野与摩押平原 | 以撒哈顿继承条约、基特夫欣嫩银片、德尔阿拉巴兰铭文、索莱布 `yhwꜣ`、4Q27《民数记》 | 前四项分别说明条约文体、祭司祝福、巴兰传统和可能的早期神名线索；4Q27 补上民 11–36 的后期希伯来文经卷实物。无一项可以把文本史变成旷野人口、路线或巴兰事件的同期记录 |
| Leviticus, sacrifice, sanctuary | Ugarit KTU1.41; EmarVI373; Arad altars; Tel Moza; 11Q1; En-Gedi scroll | Distinguish Late Bronze ritual background, Iron Age Judah practice, later copying. Local sanctuary is not tabernacle; manuscripts do not prove ritual events. |
| 利未记、祭祀与圣所 | 乌加列 KTU 1.41、埃马 Emar VI 373、亚拉得祭坛、特尔摩撒、11Q1、隐基底焦卷 | 区分晚青铜时代礼仪背景、铁器时代犹大实践和后期文本传抄；不把地方圣所当会幕，也不以抄本证明礼仪事件 |
| After Exodus to judges | Merneptah; highland settlements; Jericho/Ai/Hazor; Joshua4Q47/4Q48 | Israel already in Canaan; complex formation, not uniform city sequence. Two later manuscripts concern text history/altar order, not conquest proof. |
| 出埃及后至士师 | 梅尔内普塔碑、山地聚落、耶利哥／艾城／夏琐、4Q47／4Q48《约书亚记》 | 确认“以色列”已在迦南，同时呈现复合而非逐城整齐的形成过程；两份残卷另处理后期文本史与祭坛段落次序，不证明征服事件 |
| Judges to Saul | Medinet Habu; Ashkelon ancient DNA; Judges1Q6/4Q49–50; Ruth2Q16/17,4Q104/105; Samuel1Q7/4Q51–53 | Philistine materials illuminate migration/highland–coast conflict. Three manuscript groups concern later copying/local longer-shorter readings, not judges/Ruth/Samuel/Saul events. |

| 东叙利亚全年礼仪接收 | BL Add. 17,923、Vat. Syr. 23–24 与 Kannookadan 上修道院逐段表 | 区分“抄于霍尔米兹德修道院”与“归入上修道院礼仪系统”，并显示两段旧约／使徒行传、保罗传统与福音怎样构成年度主表；既不把中世纪抄本冒充七世纪原表，也不把未选卷书误判为非正典 |
| Revelation/seven churches | Patmos tradition; seven city sites; imperial cult;P115 | Urban/imperial context; caution about church identification, local puns, excessive beast-number decoding. |
| 启示录与七教会 | 拔摩传统、七城遗址、帝国崇拜材料、P115 | 恢复城市与帝国背景，同时限制教堂认定、地方双关和兽数过度解码 |

<a id="expanded-studies"></a>

## Expanded Studies and Research Queue | 已展开与待研究清单

These topics now have dedicated studies and cannot be summarized merely as archaeologically proven:<br>
已经另设专题、不能再只用一句“已被考古证实”概括的内容：

- [Pentateuch: historical background/evidence boundaries](./摩西五经_历史背景与证据边界.md): Creation/flood parallels, patriarchs, Exodus dates/routes, Leviticus rituals/sanctuaries/manuscripts.
  [摩西五经：历史背景与证据边界](./摩西五经_历史背景与证据边界.md)：创世与洪水平行文本、列祖背景、出埃及年代和路线，以及利未记的祭祀、圣所与抄本史。
- [Joshua to kingdom formation](./约书亚至王国形成_考古模型与证据边界.md): Jericho, Ai, Hazor, highlands, Philistines, Judges/Samuel text history, united kingdom/Solomonic construction.
  [约书亚至王国形成：考古模型与证据边界](./约书亚至王国形成_考古模型与证据边界.md)：耶利哥、艾城、夏琐、早期山地聚落、非利士人、《士师记》《撒母耳记》文本史、联合王国与所罗门工程。
- [Wisdom and poetry](./智慧与诗歌书_古代文本与敬拜背景.md): Job suffering literature/Hebrew-Aramaic witnesses; Psalms Qumran compilation/Egyptian hymns/Ugaritic storm-sea poetry; Proverbs fragments/Amenemope/Ahiqar; Ecclesiastes/Song manuscripts/comparisons/love poetry.
  [智慧与诗歌书：古代文本、敬拜背景与证据边界](./智慧与诗歌书_古代文本与敬拜背景.md)：《约伯记》的受苦文学、希伯来抄本与亚兰文译本，《诗篇》的昆兰编集、埃及自然颂歌和乌加列风暴—海洋诗语，《箴言》的直接残卷及与《阿蒙尼莫普教诲》／象岛《阿希卡尔》的分层比较，以及《传道书》《雅歌》的早期抄本、比较文本与爱情诗背景。
- [Five Psalter books and New Testament quotations](./诗篇五卷结构与新约引用导航.md): Psalm titles, five-book boundaries, compilation models, Second Temple reception, actual New Testament quotations.
  [诗篇五卷结构、王权诗与新约引用导航](./诗篇五卷结构与新约引用导航.md)：区分诗题功能、五卷文本边界、编集故事模型、第二圣殿接收和新约实际引用。
- [Ancient Old Testament catalogue bridge](./古代旧约书目_22卷24卷39卷与52卷路线桥接.md): Melito/Origen lists and combined counts; CAT naming, use, commentary, liturgy, translations/codices versus the 52-book course.
  [古代旧约书目：22／24／39 卷与 52 卷路线桥接](./古代旧约书目_22卷24卷39卷与52卷路线桥接.md)：用梅利托与俄利根两份传世清单解释古代合卷计数，区分 CAT 书目列名、实际使用、注释、礼仪选读、译本／合订本和本项目的 52 卷课程数字。
- [Divided kingdom to return](./分裂王国至归回_帝国史料与逐事件对照.md): Kings/prophets, Assyrian/Babylonian anchors, exile/Persian diaspora; Lamentations/Ezekiel/Chronicles/Ezra manuscripts, Murashu, Sanballat petitions, Persepolis names, Greek Esther.
  [分裂王国至归回：帝国史料与逐事件对照](./分裂王国至归回_帝国史料与逐事件对照.md)：两国君王、先知书位置、亚述与巴比伦固定点、被掳生活、波斯归回与侨民，以及《哀歌》《以西结书》《历代志》《以斯拉记》早期残卷、穆拉舒档案、参巴拉请愿、波斯堡同名辨伪和《以斯帖记》希腊文本史。
- [Between the Testaments to four Gospels](./两约之间至四福音_第二圣殿与罗马背景.md): Septuagint, Maccabees, Hasmoneans, groups, Herods, Roman governors, Temple, synagogues, Gospel dates.
  [两约之间至四福音：第二圣殿、希腊化与罗马世界](./两约之间至四福音_第二圣殿与罗马背景.md)：七十士译本、马加比、哈斯摩尼、宗派、希律家族、罗马长官、圣殿、会堂与四福音年代问题。
- [Resurrection and early witness](./复活与早期见证_文本史与历史研究边界.md): Four Gospels’ observation/report/check/appearance/commission sequences, women, tomb models,1Corinthians15, Mark endings, Holy Sepulchre, Roman material, second–fifth-century confession/debate/images/rules/baptismal catechesis/local and council creeds; limits of historical method.
  [复活与早期见证：文本史、墓葬背景与历史研究边界](./复活与早期见证_文本史与历史研究边界.md)：四福音各自的观察—报告—核查—显现—差遣链、妇女见证语境、空墓研究模型、林前 15 的早期传统、马可结尾抄本、圣墓教堂遗存、罗马材料，以及第二至五世纪的认信、争论、图像、信仰规则、洗礼问答、地方与会议信经，并说明历史方法能判断到哪一步。
- [Acts and Paul’s letters](./使徒行传与保罗书信_旅程年代与城市史料对照.md): Jerusalem–Rome, letter chronology, Gallio, Roman officials, Corinth/Ephesus.
  [使徒行传与保罗书信：旅程、年代与城市史料对照](./使徒行传与保罗书信_旅程年代与城市史料对照.md)：耶路撒冷至罗马、保罗书信时间表、迦流锚点、罗马官员、哥林多与以弗所城市材料。
- [Hebrews to Revelation](./希伯来书至启示录_晚期新约与七教会背景.md): Author/date matrix,70CE background, Pliny, P72, Patmos, seven cities, imperial cult, P115, new creation.
  [希伯来书至启示录：晚期新约、普通书信与七教会背景](./希伯来书至启示录_晚期新约与七教会背景.md)：作者／年代矩阵、主后 70 年后景、小普林尼、P72、拔摩、七城、帝国崇拜、P115 与新创造。
- [52-week Bible storyline](./52周圣经故事线_查经与史料阅读计划.md): Places these anchors in 52 study units, each with Scripture, evidence limits, interpretive notes, and questions.
  [52 周圣经故事线：查经、史料与考古阅读计划](./52周圣经故事线_查经与史料阅读计划.md)：把上述证据锚点嵌入 52 个研读单元，并为每周配置经文、证据边界、解读注释和查经问题。

<a id="source-rules"></a>

## Source-Recording Rules | 来源记录规则

For every added evidence item, record at least:<br>
以后每加入一条旁证，至少记录：

1. Object/text name and collection identifier;
   文物或文本名称、馆藏编号；
2. Findspot and reliability of excavated context;
   发现地点与是否有可靠出土语境；
3. Date of manufacture and date of events described;
   制作年代与所述事件年代；
4. Original language and reading of key words;
   原文语言及关键字释读；
5. Related Scripture;
   对应经文；
6. What it supports and cannot establish;
   能支持与不能支持的内容；
7. Mainstream views and important dissent;
   主流观点与重要异议；
8. Official object page, formal publication, or peer-reviewed research;
   官方藏品页、正式出版物或同行评议研究；
9. Date this project last checked it.
   本项目最后核查日期。

For update steps, changes in evidence grade, current baseline, and open questions, see the [Research Maintenance Log](./研究维护日志.md).<br>
具体更新流程、证据升降级条件、当前基线和开放问题，见[《圣经时间线研究维护日志》](./研究维护日志.md)。
